# Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot

ברטנורא על משנה אהלות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Tahorot

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): On Your Way (Segments: 852, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2022 (Segments: 843)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:1:1)


**שְׁנַיִם טְמֵאִים בְּמֵת אֶחָד טָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה.** כֻּלַּהּ מַתְנִיתִין מְפָרֵשׁ לַהּ וְאָזֵיל:


שנים טמאים במת אחד טמא טומאת שבעה -the entire Mishnah is explained further on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:1:2)


**אָדָם הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּמֵת, טָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּמֵת לְכָל נֶפֶשׁ אָדָם וְטָמֵא שִׁבְעַת יָמִים, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא נַעֲשֶׂה אַב הַטֻּמְאָה, שֶׁהַמֵּת אֲבִי אֲבוֹת הַטֻּמְאָה הוּא:


אדם הנוגע במת, טמא טומאת שבעה – as it is written (Numbers 19:11): “He who touches the corpse of any human being shall be impure for seven days.” Because he has become a primary source of Levitical uncleanness/ritual impurity, as the corpse is a progenitor of the primary sources of ritual impurity/אבי אבות הטומאה.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:1:3)


**אָדָם הַנּוֹגֵעַ בּוֹ טָמֵא טֻמְאַת עָרֶב.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְהַנֶּפֶשׁ הַנּוֹגַעַת תִּטְמָא עַד הָעָרֶב, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא נַעֲשֶׂה וְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה, וְכָל וְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה אֵין בּוֹ אֶלָּא טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב. אֲבָל חֲכָמִים גָּזְרוּ עַל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בִּטְמֵא מֵת בְּעוֹדוֹ מְחֻבָּר לַמֵּת שֶׁיִּהְיֶה טָמֵא שִׁבְעָה:


אדם הנוגע בו טמא טומאת ערב – as it is written (Numbers 19:22): “[Whatever that impure person touches shall be impure] and the person who touches him shall be impure until evening.” Because he became a secondary source of impurity/ולד הטומאה, has only has ritual impurity until nightfall/טומאת ערב. But the Sages decreed on a person who has contact with a טמא מת/one made unclean through a corpse while he is still connected to the corpse, that he will be ritually impure seven [days] (see Talmud Avodah Zarah 37b).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:2:1)


**כֵּלִים הַנּוֹגְעִים בְּמֵת וֽכוּ'.** בֵּין כְּלֵי מַתָּכוֹת בֵּין כְּלֵי שֶׁטֶף וּבְגָדִים מִטַּמְּאִין כַּמֵּת עַצְמוֹ, דִּכְתִיב (שם) בַּחֲלַל חֶרֶב אוֹ בְמֵת, חֶרֶב הֲרֵי הוּא כַּמֵּת, וְהוּא הַדִּין לִשְׁאָר כָּל הַכֵּלִים. הִלְכָּךְ כֵּלִים הַנּוֹגְעִים בְּמֵת נַעֲשִׂים אֲבִי אֲבוֹת, וְכֵלִים בְּכֵלִים אַב הַטֻּמְאָה, דִּלְעוֹלָם אֵין הַכֵּלִים שְׁנִיִּים נַעֲשִׂים כָּרִאשׁוֹנִים:


כלים הנוגעים במת וכו' – whether metal vessels or vessels or clothing which require only rinsing in order to be restored to Levitical cleanness and clothing, are defiled like the corpse itself, as it is written (Numbers 19:16): “[And in the open, anyone who touches a person] who was killed or died naturally, [or human bone or a grave, shall be impure seven days].” The person who was killed is like a corpse, and this is the law for all the rest of the vessels. Therefore, vessels that come in contact with the corpse become a progenitor of the primary sources of ritual impurity/אבי אבות הטומאה, but vessels [that come in contact] with vessels ae a primary source of ritual impurity for always second-degree of ritual impurity never become like [those vessels] that are first-degree of ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:2:2)


**הַשְּׁלִישִׁי בֵּין אָדָם בֵּין כֵּלִים.** נַעֲשִׂים רִאשׁוֹנִים וּמִטַּמְּאִין טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב:


השלישי בין אדם בין כלים – become first-degree of ritual impurity, and are defiled until nightfall.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:3:1)


**כֵּיצַד אַרְבָּעָה.** כֵּלִים הַנּוֹגְעִים בְּמֵת, אֲבִי אֲבוֹת כְּמוֹתוֹ. וְאָדָם בַּכֵּלִים, אַב הַטֻּמְאָה. וְכֵלִים בָּאָדָם, נַמִּי אַב הַטֻּמְאָה כָּאָדָם עַצְמוֹ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר לא) וְכִבַּסְתֶּם בִּגְדֵיכֶם בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי וּטְהַרְתֶּם, הָא לָמַדְתָּ שֶׁכָּל אָדָם הַטָּמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה מְטַמֵּא הַכֵּלִים טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה:


כיצד ארבעה – vessels that come in contact with a corpse are progenitors of the primary sources of ritual impurity like it (i.e., a corpse). But a person [who is defiled] with utensils [which came in contact with a corpse becomes] a primary source of ritual impurity/אב הטומאה. And vessels [that were defiled by a corpse that came in contact with a person, is also a primary source of ritual impurity, like the person himself, as it is written (Numbers 31:24): “On the seventh day you shall wash your clothes and be pure, [and after that you may enter the camp],” hence you learned that every person who is defiled for seven days of defilement defiles vessels for a defilement of seven days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:3:2)


**הָרְבִיעִי בֵּין אָדָם בֵּין כֵּלִים.** נַעֲשִׂים רִאשׁוֹנִים. וְהוּא הַדִּין דִּבְכֵיצַד שְׁלֹשָׁה הֲוָה מָצֵי לְמִתְנֵי אָדָם הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּמֵת וְכֵלִים בָּאָדָם טְמֵאִים טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, הַשְּׁלִישִׁי בֵּין אָדָם בֵּין כֵּלִים טְמֵאִין טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב. אֶלָּא מִסֵּיפָא אַרְבָּעָה טְמֵאִין בְּמֵת שָׁמְעִינַן לַהּ, דַּאֲפִלּוּ אָדָם בְּכֵלִים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בְּמֵת וְכֵלִים בָּאָדָם קָתָנֵי טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן אָדָם בַּמֵּת עַצְמוֹ וְכֵלִים בְּאָדָם דְּאִיכָּא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה:


הרביעי בין אדם בין כלים -become first-degree of ritual impurity. And the say law applies with “How so- for three?” for one is able to teach that a person who comes in contact with a corpse or vessels with a person are defiled with ritual impurity for seven days; the third, whether a person or vessels are defiled with ritual impurity until nightfall, but from the concluding clause [of the Mishnah] for the fourth who are defiled through contact with a corpse, we understand it, that even a person with utensils that came in contact with a corpse and/or vessels that came in contact with a person is taught that there is a period of defilement for seven days. All the more so, a person with the dead himself, or vessels with a [defiled] person, where there is a defilement of ritual impurity for seven days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:3:3)


**שַׁפּוּד.** כְּמִין עַמּוּד שֶׁל מַתֶּכֶת תָּקוּעַ בְּאֶמְצַע הָאֹהֶל וּבְרֹאשׁוֹ קוֹשְׁרִין הָאֹהֶל:


שפוד (a spit) – like a kind of column of metal that is fastened in the tent and upon its end they tie the tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:3:4)


**אֵין הָאֹהֶל מִתְחַשֵּׁב.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין הָאֹהֶל מְטַמֵּא הַשַּׁפּוּד, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינוֹ נֶחֱשָׁב בְּמִנְיַן סֵדֶר הַטֻּמְאוֹת, אֶלָּא כָּל הַמֻּנָּח בְּאֹהֶל הַמֵּת כְּנוֹגֵעַ בַּמֵּת עַצְמוֹ, וְאֵין כָּאן אֶלָּא אַרְבָּעָה. אִי נַמִּי, אֲפִלּוּ אֵין הַשַּׁפּוּד בְּתוֹךְ הָאֹהֶל אֶלָּא שֶׁנּוֹגֵעַ בָּאֹהֶל מִבַּחוּץ אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אֵין הָאֹהֶל מִתְחַשֵּׁב בְּמִנְיַן סֵדֶר מִשְׁנָתֵנוּ. וְהָכִי מוּכָח לְקַמָּן בְּפֶרֶק סָגוֹס עָבֶה בְּמִשְׁנַת טַבְלָיוֹת שֶׁל עֵץ:


אין האוהל מתחשב (the tent does not come into consideration)- because the tent does not defile the spit, therefore it is not considered in the number of the order of the defilements, but rather, all that is placed in the tent of the corpse is like it is touching the corpse itself, and there are only four here. Alternatively, even if the spit is not within the tent, but rather that it touches the tent from outside, nevertheless, the tent does not come into consideration in the number of the order of our Mishnah. And this proved further on/ahead in the Chapter "סכום עבה"/The Thick Coarse Woolen Blanket (Chapter 15), in the Mishnah "טבליות של עץ"/Tables of Wood (Mishnah 2).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:4:1)


**שֶׁהַכֵּלִים שְׁלֹשָׁה.** כְּגוֹן כֵּלִים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בְּמֵת וְכֵלִים בַּכֵּלִים וְעוֹד כֵּלִים שְׁלִישִׁים בַּשְּׁנִיִּים, כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִין:


שהכלים שלשה – as for example vessels that came in contact with a corpse and vessels [that came in contact] with vessels. And further, vessels are third [degree of ritual uncleanness [which came in contact’ with vessels that are second [degree of ritual uncleanness], all are impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:4:2)


**וְהָאָדָם שְׁנָיִם.** כְּגוֹן אָדָם בְּמֵת וְאָדָם שֵׁנִי בְּזֶה הָאָדָם שֶׁנִּטְמָא בַּמֵּת. אֲבָל אָדָם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי בַּשֵּׁנִי, טָהוֹר:


והאדם שנים – as for example, a person [who came in contact] with a corpse and a second person [came in contact] with that person that was defiled [through contact] with a corpse. But a third person who has third [who came in contact] with [a person] with second [degree of uncleanness] is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:4:3)


**שֶׁהוּא בָאֶמְצַע.** כְּגוֹן כֵּלִים וְאָדָם וְכֵלִים, דְּהָרְבִיעִי בֵּין אָדָם בֵּין כֵּלִים מִטַּמֵּא טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב, כִּדְשָׁמְעִינַן מִמַּתְנִיתִין דִּלְעֵיל:


שהוא באמצע – as for example, vessels and a person and vessels. For the fourth, whether it is a person or vessels defiles until nightfall, as we learned from our Mishnah above (see Mishnah 1).



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:5:1)


**מְטַמֵּא בְגָדִים.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא פֵרַשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְהַנֹּגֵעַ בִּבְשַׂר הַזָּב יְכַבֵּס בְּגָדָיו, אַלְמָא שֶׁהוּא מְטַמֵּא בְּגָדִים בִּשְׁעַת מַגָּעוֹ בַּזָּב:


מטמא בגדים – all of this that is not explained [in the Mishnah] as it is written (Leviticus 15:7): “Whoever touches he the body of the one with the discharge shall wash his clothes, [bathe in water, and remain impure until evening],” so we see that he defiles [his] clothing at the time of his contact with the one with the discharge/the person who has gonorrhea.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:5:2)


**וְאֵין בְּגָדִים הַנּוֹגְעִים בְּזָב מְטַמְּאִין בְּגָדִים.** לְפִי שֶׁהַזָּב אַב הַטֻּמְאָה וְהַבְּגָדִים הַנּוֹגְעִים בּוֹ נַעֲשִׂים רִאשׁוֹן וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין בְּגָדִים אֲחֵרִים, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם וְכֵלִים מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא מֵאַב הַטֻּמְאָה:


ואין הבגדים הנוגעים בזב מטמאין בגדים – because the person with the discharge/gonorrhea is a primary source of Levitical uncleanness/אב הטומאה, and the clothes that touch it become first [degree of ritual uncleanness]. But they do not defile other clothing, for a person and vessels do not become susceptible to receiving defilement other than from a primary source of ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:5:3)


**שֶׁהַבְּגָדִים הַנּוֹשְׂאִין אֶת הַזָּב מְטַמְּאִין אָדָם.** כְּגוֹן מִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב שֶׁל זָב מְטַמְּאִין אָדָם, לְפִי שֶׁהֵן נַעֲשִׂים אַב הַטֻּמְאָה כַּזָּב עַצְמוֹ, דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְכָל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה תַּחְתָּיו יִטְמָא עַד הָעָרֶב:


והבגדים הנושאין את הזב מטמאין אדם – as for example, lying and sitting of a person with a discharge/gonorrhea which defile a person because they become a primary source of ritual impurity like the person with a discharge/gonorrhea himself As it is written (Leviticus 15:10): “Whoever touches anything that was under him shall be impure until evening.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:5:4)


**וְאֵין אָדָם הַנּוֹשֵׂא אֶת הַזָּב מְטַמֵּא אָדָם.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בְשָׁעָה שֶׁהוּא נוֹשֵׂא אוֹתוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁפֵרַשׁ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֶלָּא רִאשׁוֹן לַטֻּמְאָה, וְאֵין רִאשׁוֹן מְטַמֵּא אָדָם:


ואין אדם הנושא את הזב מטמא את האדם – and even at the time that he carries him prior to his separating. For he is aught other than first degree of ritual impurity and a nothing that is first [degree of ritual impurity] can defile a person.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:1)


**עַד שֶׁתֵּצֵא נַפְשׁוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) כָּל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּמֵת בְּנֶפֶשׁ הָאָדָם אֲשֶׁר יָמוּת. לִמֶּדְךָ הַכָּתוּב שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא עַד שֶׁיָּמוּת:


עד שתצא נפשו – as it is written (Numbers 19:13): “Whoever touches a corpse, the body of a person who has died, [and he does not purify himself, defiles the LORD’s Tabernacle; that person shall be cut off from Israel. Since the water of lustration was not dashed on him, he remains impure; his impurity is still upon him],” that Scripture comes to teach that a person does not defile until he dies.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:2)


**מְגֻיָּד.** מְחֻתָּךְ. לְשׁוֹן גֹּדּוּ אִילָנָא (דניאל ד):


מגוייד (he who had his arteries opened, bled to death) – cut piecemeal, the language of (Daniel 4:11): “Hew down the tree.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:3)


**זוֹקֵק לַיִּבּוּם.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא גּוֹסֵס יְבִמְתּוֹ אֲסוּרָה לְהִנָּשֵׂא:


זוקק ליבום – all the while that he is rapidly passing away/lying on his deathbed, his sister-in-law (i.e., widow of his brother who died without issue) is forbidden to get married


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:4)


**וּפוֹטֵר מִן הַיִּבּוּם.** אִם מֵת וְהִנִּיחַ בֵּן גּוֹסֵס, אִשְׁתּוֹ פְּטוּרָה מִן הַחֲלִיצָה וּמִן הַיִּבּוּם:


ופוטר מן היבום – if he (i.e., the brother-in-law) died, and he left a son who is rapidly passing away. His wife is exempt from Halitzah/the ceremony that frees the widow of a man who died without children from the obligation to marry one of her deceased husband’s brothers and allows her to remarry [through the removal of a special sandal from the foot of one of her deceased husband’s brothers].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:5)


**וּמַאֲכִיל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** אֶת אִמּוֹ, אִם הִיא בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת לְכֹהֵן:


ומאכיל תרומה – if the mother of his mother is an Israelite who was married to a Kohen (see also Mishnah Tractate Yevamot, Chapter 9, Mishnah 5).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:6)


**וּפוֹסֵל.** אֶת אִמּוֹ מִן הַתְּרוּמָה, אִם בַּת כֹּהֵן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא:


ופוסל – he invalidates his mother from [partaking of] the heave-offering/priest’s due if she is the daughter of a Kohen married to an Israelite.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:7)


**וְכֵן בְּהֵמָה וְחַיָּה אֵין מְטַמְּאִין.** טֻמְאַת נְבֵלוֹת, עַד שֶׁתֵּצֵא נַפְשָׁם:


וכן בהמה וחיה אין מטמאין- the ritual impurity imparted by an unslaughtered carcass, until their souls depart


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:6:8)


**כִּזְנַב הַלְּטָאָה.** כְּלוֹמַר לְמָה פִּרְכּוּס זֶה דוֹמֶה, לִזְנַב הַלְּטָאָה:


כזנב הלטאה (like the tail of a species of lizard) – meaning to say, what is this spasmatic movement (jerking motion) similar to? To the tail of a species of lizard.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:7:1)


**הָאֵבָרִים.** צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בָּהֶם בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת, וּבְכָךְ הוּא נֶחֱשָׁב אֵבָר. וּמַיְרֵי בֵּין בְּאֵבָר שֶׁנִּתְלַשׁ מִן הַחַי בֵּין בְּאֵבָר מִן הַמֵּת:


האיברים – there must be upon them flesh and sinews and bones and through this it is considered a limb. And we are speaking of whether the limb was detached from the living or whether a limb from the dead.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:1)


**פִּסַּת הָרֶגֶל.** כַּף הָרֶגֶל הִיא הַפַּרְסָה:


פיסת הרגל (the foot to the ankle) – the sole of the foot is the foot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:2)


**שִׁשָּׁה בְכָל אֶצְבַּע.** לְחָמֵשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת, הַיְנוּ שְׁלֹשִׁים:


ששה בכל אצבע -for five fingers, which are thirty.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:3)


**קֻרְסָל.** תַּרְגּוּם כְּרָעַיִם, קַרְסוּלִין. מְקוֹם חִבּוּר הָרֶגֶל וְהַשּׁוֹק:


קרסול (ankle) – It is the Aramaic translation of כרעיים/leg, (see Leviticus 11:21): “[But these you may eat among the all the winged swarming things that walk on all fours:] all that have, above their feet, jointed legs [to leap with on the ground,” קרסולי. It is the place of the connection of the foot and the foreleg.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:4)


**קַטְלִית.** לְמַעְלָה בְּרֹאשׁ הַיָּרֵךְ:


קטלת ([club-shaped], the thigh with the knee-pan)- above the top of the knee.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:5)


**בַּקָּנֶה.** עֶצֶם הַמְחֻבָּר לַיָּד. מִלְּשׁוֹן קְנֵה הַמִּדָּה, שֶׁבּוֹ מוֹדְדִים הָאַמָּה:


בקנה (forearm) – he bone that is connection to the hand. From the language (Ezekiel 40:5): “The rod that the man held [was six cubits long, plus one handbreadth for each cubit],” that with it they measure the cubit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:6)


**מַרְפֵּק.** קוד"ו בְּלַעַ"ז:


מרפק (elbow) – KODO in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:7)


**מֵאָה וְאֶחָד מִזֶּה.** שֶׁכָּל מַה שֶּׁמָּנָה עַד עַכְשָׁיו מִצַּד אֶחָד מֵהַגּוּף הוּא, כְּגוֹן יָד אֶחָד וְרֶגֶל אֶחָד וְדֹפֶן אַחַת:


מאה ואחד מזה – that all that he counted until now was from one side from the body, such as for example, one hand and one leg and one rib.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:8)


**מַפְתֵּחַ שֶׁל לֵב.** הוּא הֶחָזֶה. שֶׁמִּפְּנֵי תְנוּעַת הֶחָזֶה מְנַשֵּׁב הָרֵאָה עַל הַלֵּב, נִמְצָא הֶחָזֶה פּוֹתֵחַ דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ יִכָּנֵס הָאֲוִיר וְיֵצֵא לַלֵּב:


מפתח של לב (valve of the heart) -this is the chest. That because of the movement of the chest, the lung causes to blow on the heart, it is found that the chest opens the path that from it the air enters in and goes out to the heart.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:9)


**בִּנְקָבָיו.** הַבֵּיצִים וְגִיד הָאַמָּה:


בנקוביו (the perforations/organs of the extremities) – the testicles and the membrum virile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:10)


**בָּשָׂר כָּרָאוּי.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה אֲרוּכָה וְיַבְרִיא, אִם הָיָה מְחֻבָּר בְּאָדָם חַי:


בשר כראוי (when there is an appropriate amount of flesh) – in order that it will rise up in length and be healthy (see Tractate Kelim, Chapter 1, Mishnah 5), if it is connected to a living person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 1:8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/1:8:11)


**וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין בְּאֹהֶל.** לְפִי שֶׁטֻּמְאַת אֹהֶל אֵינָהּ אֶלָּא אוֹ בְאָדָם שָׁלֵם כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר יט) אָדָם כִּי יָמוּת בְּאֹהֶל, אוֹ בְּאֵבָר הַדּוֹמֶה לְאָדָם כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) בְּעֶצֶם אָדָם, מָה אָדָם יֵשׁ בּוֹ בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת וּמְטַמֵּא בְאֹהֶל, אַף אֵבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת מְטַמֵּא בְאֹהֶל:


ואין מטמאין באוהל – for defilement from a tent is not other either with a complete person, as it is written (Numbers 19:14): “When a person dies in a tent,” or with a limb that is similar to a person, as it states (Numbers 19:16): “or human bone,” just as a human being that has flesh and sews and bones and defiles in a tent, even a limb that has flesh and sinews and bones defiles in a tent.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:1)


**אֵלּוּ מְטַמְּאִין בְּאֹהֶל, הַמֵּת וְכַזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת.** וְאִי קַשְׁיָא, כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת מְטַמֵּא מֵת עַצְמוֹ לֹא כָל שֶׁכֵּן, הָא מִלְּתָא מִתָּרְצָה בַּגְּמָרָא בְּנָזִיר [דַּף נט], לֹא נִצְרְכָא אֶלָּא לְנֵפֶל שֶׁלֹּא נִתְקַשְּׁרוּ אֵבָרָיו בְּגִידִין שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ כַזַּיִת בָּשָׂר:


אלו מטמאין באוהל, המת וכזית מן המת -and if this is difficult, an olive’s bulk of a corpse defiles, from that which is defiled from contact with the dead, all the more so, this matter is resolved in the Gemara in [Tractate] Nazir [50a, it is needed only for [the case of] a premature/non-viable birth whose limbs were not connected with sinews as it lacks an olive’s bulk of flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:2)


**נֶצֶל.** בְּשַׂר הַמֵּת שֶׁנִּמּוֹחַ וְנַעֲשָׂה כְמִין לֵחָה סְרוּחָה:


נצל (decayed matter – liquid and coagulated portions of a corpse) – the flesh of a corpse that swelled up like a kind of ill-smelling secretion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:3)


**מְלֹא תַרְוָד.** כַּף. וְשִׁעוּרוֹ מְלֹא חָפְנַיִם:


מלוא תרוד (A tarvad/spoon-full of dust) – a spoon, and its measurement is two handfuls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:4)


**רָקָב.** גּוּפוֹ שֶׁל מֵת כְּשֶׁכָּלָה לַחוּתוֹ וְנַעֲשָׂה כְמִין עָפָר. וְאֵין מְלֹא תַרְוַד רָקָב מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא מִן הַמֵּת שֶׁנִּקְבַּר עָרֹם בְּאָרוֹן שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ וּמְכֻסֶּה בְּכִסּוּי שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ, עַד שֶׁנּוֹדַע בְּוַדַּאי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ תַּעֲרֹבֶת רִקָּבוֹן שֶׁל בֶּגֶד אוֹ שֶׁל עֵץ אוֹ עָפָר אַחֵר. אֲבָל מֵת שֶׁנִּקְבַּר בִּכְסוּתוֹ אוֹ בְאָרוֹן שֶׁל עֵץ אוֹ בֶעָפָר, אֵין לוֹ רָקָב. וְכֵן מֵת שֶׁנִּקְבַּר חֲסַר אֵבָר אֵין לוֹ רָקָב:


רקב – the body of a corpse when its moisture has departed and it becomes like a kind of dust. But a handful of a a mass of earth from a grave containing parts of a decaying human body doesn’t defile other than from a corpse that was buried naked in a coffin/bier of marble and covered with a covering of marble, until it is known with certainty that there is combination of decayed matter of cloth or wood or other dirt. But a corpse that was buried in its covering or in a wooden ark or in dust, has no decayed matter. And similarly, a corpse that was buried missing a limb has no decayed matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:5)


**הַשִּׁדְרָה וְהַגֻּלְגֹּלֶת.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם בָּשָׂר מְטַמְּאִים בְּאֹהֶל, לְפִי שֶׁצּוּרַת אָדָם נִכֶּרֶת בָּהֶם, דִּכְתִיב בְּעֶצֶם אָדָם, עֶצֶם שֶׁנִּכָּר בּוֹ שֶׁהוּא שֶׁל אָדָם:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:6)


**אֵבָר מִן הַמֵּת וְאֵבָר מִן הַחַי.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) בַּחֲלַל חֶרֶב אוֹ בְמֵת, אֵבָר שֶׁהִבְדִּילַתּוּ הַחֶרֶב מִן הַחַי הֲרֵי הוּא כְמֵת:


אבר מן המת ואבר מן החי – as it is written (Numbers 19:16): “[And in the open, anyone who touches a person who was killed or who died naturally,” a limb that the sword separated from the living is like the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:7)


**וְרֹבַע עֲצָמוֹת.** רֹבַע הַקַּב שֶׁל עֲצָמוֹת:


ורובע עצמות – one quarter of a Kab (i.e., 24 egg-bulks) of bones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:8)


**מֵרֹב הַמִּנְיָן אוֹ מֵרֹב הַבִּנְיָן.** רַבּוֹתַי פֵרְשׁוּ, שֶׁהֵן מֻעָטִין מִהְיוֹת בָּהֶן רֹב מִנְיָן וְרֹב בִּנְיָן, דְּאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶן לֹא רֹב הַמִּנְיָן וְלֹא רֹב הַבִּנְיָן מְטַמְּאִין:


מרוב המנין או מרוב הבנין – My Rabbis/Teachers explained, that they are small/minute from having in them the majority of the number [of bones] and/or the majority of the frame, for even though that they don’t have the majority of the number [of bones] nor the majority of the frame, they defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:1:9)


**וְרֹב בִּנְיָנוֹ וְרֹב מִנְיָנוֹ.** רֹב בִּנְיַן הַגּוּף, אוֹ רֹב מִנְיַן הָעֲצָמוֹת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ רֹב בִּנְיַן הַגּוּף, מְטַמְּאִין, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶן רֹבַע. וְרֹב בִּנְיָנוֹ, כְּגוֹן הַשּׁוֹקַיִם וְהַיְרֵכַיִם וְהַצְּלָעוֹת [וְהַשִּׁדְרָה]. וְרֹב מִנְיָנוֹ, כְּגוֹן רָאשֵׁי אֶצְבְּעוֹת הַיָּדַיִם וְהָרַגְלַיִם וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהֶן, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מֵאָה עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֵבָרִים, מְטַמְּאִים:


ורוב בנינו ורוב מנינו (the greater portion of a corpse as to the size of limbs and the greater number of joints/limbs)– the majority of the frame of the body, or the majority of the number of the bones even though it is not the majority of the frame of the body, they defile, and even though there isn’t a quarter [of a Kab] in them. And the majority of the frame, such as the legs and the thighs/haunches, and the ribs [and the spine]. And the greater number of joints/limbs, such as the tops of the fingers of the hands and the feet, and similar kinds of things to them, and provided that there will be one-hundred and twenty-five limbs, they defile.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:1)


**רְבִיעִית דָּם.** שֶׁיָּצָא כֻּלּוֹ לְאַחַר מִיתָה:


רביעית דם (a quarter-LOG of blood) – that all of it left [the body] after death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:2)


**וּרְבִיעִית דַּם תְּבוּסָה.** שֶׁיָּצָא מִקְצָת רְבִיעִית מֵחַיִּים וּמִקְצָת לְאַחַר מִיתָה. וּלְקַמָּן בְּפֶרֶק ג'‏ [מִשְׁנָה ה'] מְפָרֵשׁ לַהּ:


ורביעית דם תבוסה (a quarter LOG of blood that flows from a person at the time of his death or afterwards – which has the status of a corpse) – that part of a quarter [of a LOG] of blood while living and after death. And further ahead in Chapter 3 [Mishnah 5], it explains this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:3)


**תְּבוּסָה.** לְשׁוֹן מִתְבּוֹסֶסֶת בְּדָמָיִךְ (יחזקאל טז). וּרְבִיעִית דָּם שֶׁיָּצָא כֻלּוֹ לְאַחַר מִיתָה, דְּאוֹרַיְתָא, דִּכְתִיב בְּמֵת בְּנֶפֶשׁ. אֲבָל דַּם תְּבוּסָה אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן. וְתָנֵי דְּאוֹרַיְתָא וְתָנֵי דְרַבָּנָן:


תבוסה – it is the language of (Ezekiel 16:6): “[When I passed by you and saw you] wallowing in your blood, [I said to you: [Live in spite of your blood’].” And the quarter [of a LOG] of blood all of which exited [the body] after death, is from the Rabbis, as it is written (Numbers 19:13): “[Whoever touches] a corpse, the body of person who has died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:4)


**מִמֵּת אֶחָד.** וְלֹא מִשְּׁנֵי מֵתִים. קָסָבְרֵי רַבָּנָן יֵשׁ אֵם לַמָּסֹרֶת וְעַל כָּל נַפְשׁוֹת מֵת לֹא יָבֹא (ויקרא כא), נַפְשֹׁת כְּתִיב חָסֵר:


But [the concept] of דם תבוסה/that part of a quarter [of a LOG] that flows from a person at the time of his death or afterwards. For the Rabbis hold that there is "יש אם למסורת" /the consonantal text of the Torah is authoritative [when looking at Leviticus 21:11): "ועל כל נפשות מת לא יבא"/He shall not go in where there is any dead body,” [the word] נפשת/dead, is written defectively (i.e., without the letter “Vav” In it).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:5)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר מִשְּׁנֵי מֵתִים.** חֲצִי רְבִיעִית מִמֵּת זֶה וַחֲצִי רְבִיעִית מִמֵּת אַחֵר, מְטַמֵּא בְּאֹהֶל. דְּסָבַר יֵשׁ אֵם לַמִּקְרָא, נַפְשׁוֹת מֵת, מַשְׁמַע שְׁתֵּי נְפָשׁוֹת וְשִׁעוּר אֶחָד, מִדְּלֹא קָאָמַר נַפְשׁוֹת מֵתִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


רבי עקיבא אומר משני מתים – one-half of a quarter [of a LOG of blood] from this dead person and one-half of a quarter [of a LOG of blood] from another dead person, defiles in the tent, for he (i.e., Rabbi Akiba) holds, "יש אם למקרא"/the vocalized text of the Torah is authoritative, [as the Biblical verse from Leviticus 21:11) is pronounced: "נפשות מת"/any dead body, implying two souls and one measurement, for since it does not state [in Scripture] "נפשות מתים"/dead bodies. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiba.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:6)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** כְּשֵׁם שֶׁטָּמֵא בָּעֲצָמוֹת רֹב מִנְיָן וְרֹב בִּנְיָן אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ רֹבַע הַקַּב שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר הַמְיֻחָד לָעֲצָמוֹת, כָּךְ טָמֵא בְדָם הַקָּטָן שֶׁיָּצָא כֻלּוֹ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ רְבִיעִית שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר הַמְיֻחָד לַדָּם:


ר' עקיבא אומר כל שהוא – just bones defile with the majority of the number of bones and the majority of the frame, even though one-quarter of a Kab which is the designated measurement for bones, so also, it is defiled if the blood of minor – all of which exited the body, even though it doesn’t have the quarter [of a LOG] which the designated measure for blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:7)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים רְבִיעִית.** אִם אָמַרְתָּ בָּעֲצָמוֹת שֶׁבְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ לְפָנֶיךָ, תֹּאמַר בַּדָּם שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר נִשְׁתַּיְּרָה מִמֶּנָּה טִפָּה כָּל שֶׁהִיא וְלֹא יָצָא כֻלּוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים רביעית – if you were to say that with the bones that it is known that all of them are in front of you, shall you say regarding the blood that I state that there remains a drop of any amount and that not all of it has come out? But the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:8)


**כַּזַּיִת רִמָּה.** שֶׁנּוֹלְדָה בִּבְשַׂר הַמֵּת:


כזית רמה –[a olive’s bulk of a worm] that was born in the flesh of the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:9)


**מְטַמֵּא כִבְשָׂרוֹ.** דְּרִמָּה הַיּוֹצְאָה מִן הַמֵּת אֲפִלּוּ חַיָּה, כִּבְשַׂר הַמֵּת הִיא חֲשׁוּבָה, מִשּׁוּם דְּאָדָם מֵחַיִּים אִקְּרֵי רִמָּה, דִּכְתִיב (איוב כח) אַף כִּי אֱנוֹשׁ רִמָּה:


מטמא כבשרו – that a room that comes out from the corpse even while living, is considered to be like the flesh of a corpse, because, a person while alive is called a worm, as it is written (Job 25:6): “How much less man, a worm, [the Son of man, a maggot].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:10)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרים – And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:11)


**אֵפֶר שְׂרוּפִים.** אָדָם שֶׁנִּשְׂרַף וְנַעֲשָׂה אֵפֶר וְלֹא נִתְעָרֵב בּוֹ מֵאֵפֶר עֵצִים,


אפר שרופים – a person that was burned and became ash, and the ash of wood was not combined in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:12)


**שִׁעוּרוֹ בְּרֹבַע.** קַב אֵפֶר, מְטַמֵּא:


שעורו ברובע – [a quarter] of a Kab of ash, defiles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:13)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרי – And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:14)


**עֲפַר קְבָרוֹת.** עָפָר שֶׁמְּעֹרָב בּוֹ דָּם וְלֵחָה שֶׁל מֵת. וּמְלֹא תַרְוָד וְעוֹד טָמֵא, לְפִי שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לִמְלֹא תַרְוָד וְעוֹד שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה בוֹ מְלֹא תַרְוַד רָקָב דְּאָמַר לְעֵיל שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא. וְהָכִי נַמִּי מַיְרֵי בְמֵת שֶׁנִּקְבַּר עָרֹם בְּאָרוֹן שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ שֶׁאֵין עָפָר אַחֵר מְעֹרָב בּוֹ:


עפר קברות – dirt that the blood and moistness of the corpse is mixed in. And a spoonful of dust and a bit more is impure, because it is impossible to fill a spoon and a bit more so that there would not be in it a spoonful (i.e., two handfuls) of the body of a corpse when its moisture has departed, that it stated above (i.e., in Mishnah 1 of this chapter) that this is impure, But here, it also speaks of a corpse that is buried naked in a marble coffin/bier which has other dirt mixed into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:15)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מְטַהֵר.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר' שמעון מטהר – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:2:16)


**אֵינוֹ חִבּוּר לְטֻמְאָה.** וְאִם הֶאֱהִיל עַל מִקְצָתוֹ אֵינוֹ כְאִלּוּ הֶאֱהִיל עַל כֻּלּוֹ. וְכֵן לְעִנְיַן מַגָּע:


אימנו חיבור לטומהא – for if it had overshadowed over part of it, it is not as if it overshadowed over all of it. And similarly, regarding the matter of contact/touching.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:1)


**עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה.** מְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא. דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְעַל הַנּוֹגֵעַ וְגוֹ', וְשָׁם לֹא נֶאֱמַר בְּעֶצֶם אָדָם. לְלַמֵּד עַל עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה שֶׁמְּטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִכָּר שֶׁהוּא שֶׁל אָדָם. אֲבָל בְּאֹהֶל, אָדָם כִּי יָמוּת בְּאֹהֶל כְּתִיב. וּבְעֶצֶם לְעִנְיַן טֻמְאַת אֹהֶל, בָּעִינַן שֶׁיְּהֵא נִכָּר שֶׁהוּא שֶׁל אָדָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר הוּא אוֹמֵר בְּעֶצֶם אָדָם:


עצם שעורה – defiles through contact and through carrying, as it is written (Numbers 19:18): “[A person who is clean shall take hyssop, dip it in the water, and sprinkle on the tent and on all the vessels and people who were there,] or on him who touched/ועל הנגע [the bones or the person who was killed or died naturally or the grave,” but there it does not state, "בעצם אדם"/human bone (as we find in Numbers 19:16). [This comes] to teach about the bone the size of a barleycorn that defiles through contact even though it is not recognized that it of a human being. But in the tent, it is written (Numbers 19:14): “When a person dies in a tent,” and with regard to a bone for the matter of defilement through overshadowing in a tent, we require that it is recognized that it (i.e., the bone) is of a human being, for in another place (Numbers 19:16) it (i.e., the Torah) states, “or human bone”/או-בעצם אדם.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:2)


**וְאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים.** כָּל חוּץ לָאָרֶץ קָרֵי אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים. וְהָכָא מַיְרֵי בְגוּשׁ עָפָר שֶׁבָּא מִחוּץ לָאָרֶץ שֶׁהוּא מְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְאֹהֶל. אֲבָל הַנִּכְנָס לְאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים, אֲפִלּוּ לֹא נָגַע וְלֹא נָשָׂא, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה רוֹכֵב עַל סוּס, טָמֵא, דְּעַל אֲוִירָה שֶׁל אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים נַמִּי גָזְרוּ:


וארץ העמים – Everything that is outside of the Land [of Israel] is called ארץ העמים/the land of the nations [which is ritually impure]. And here we are speaking of a clod/lump of dust that comes from outside the Land which defiles through contact and through carrying but does not defile though overshadowing in a tent. But a person who enters the land of the nations, even if he did not touch nor carry anything, as for example, that he was riding upon a horse, is impure, but on the airspace of the land of the nations they (i.e., the Rabbis) also decreed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:3)


**בֵּית הַפְּרָס.** שָׂדֶה שֶׁנֶּחֱרַשׁ בָּהּ קֶבֶר וְנִשְׁבְּרוּ עַצְמוֹת הַמֵּת וְנָדוֹשׁוּ בַעֲפַר אוֹתוֹ שָׂדֶה. וּפְרָס מִלְּשׁוֹן פְּרוּסָה. הֲלֹא פָּרֹס לָרָעֵב לַחְמֶךָ (ישעיה נח):


בית הפרס (area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse) – a field in which a grave was plowed and the bones of the corpse were broken and threshed in the dirt of that field. [And the word] פרס from the language of פרוסה/a broken piece (Isaiah 58:7): “It is to share your bread with the hungry.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:4)


**כַּמָּה הוּא חֶסְרוֹן בַּשִּׁדְרָה.** וְלֹא יְטַמֵּא בְאֹהֶל, אֶלָּא בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא:


כמה חסרון בשדרה (how much is a sufficient lack in the backbone) -that it will not be defiled [through overshadowing] in the tent, but rather through contact and through carrying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:5)


**כִּמְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ.** מְלֹא רֹחַב הַנֶּקֶב שֶׁנּוֹקְבִין בְּמַרְצֵעַ:


כמלא מקדח (as large as the hole made with the large carpenters’ borer/drill) – as wide as the incision that they make with an awl.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:6)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּנָּטֵל מִן הַחַי וְיָמוּת.** וְשִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים בְּחֶסְרוֹן כְּסֶלַע:


כדי שינטל מן החי וימות – and the Sages estimated the loss as equivalent to a Sela.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:7)


**בַּקָּטָן שֶׁל רוֹפְאִים.** בְּמַרְצֵעַ קָטָן שֶׁהָרוֹפְאִים פּוֹתְחִים בּוֹ הַנְּגָעִים:


בקטן של רופאים – with a small awl that the doctors open up the wounds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:3:8)


**בַּגָּדוֹל שֶׁל לִשְׁכָּה.** וְשִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים שֶׁהוּא נֶקֶב כְּפוּנְדְיוֹן:


בגדול של לשכה (concerning the large one of the chamber) – that the Sages estimated as an incision like the size of a Pundiyon.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:1)


**הַגּוֹלֵל.** אֶבֶן גְּדוֹלָה וּרְחָבָה שֶׁסּוֹתְמִים בָּהּ פִּי הַקֶּבֶר מִלְּמַעְלָה. מִלְּשׁוֹן וְגָלֲלוּ אֶת הָאֶבֶן (בראשית כט):


הגולל (the stone placed on top of a burial cave/top-stone) – a large and wide stone that they seal/stop up the mouth of the grave from above. [This is] from the language of (Genesis 29:3): “[When the flocks were gathered there,] the stone would be rolled [from the mouth of the well and the sheep watered.]”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:2)


**וְהַדּוֹפֵק.** שְׁתֵּי אֲבָנִים גְּדוֹלוֹת אַחַת מִצַּד זֶה וְאַחַת מִצַּד זֶה שֶׁהַגּוֹלֵל נִשְׁעָן עֲלֵיהֶן:


דופק (the frame supporting the movable stone of a tomb -against which the turning body knocks) -two large stones, one from this side and the another from the other side, so that the stone placed on top of a burial cave rests upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:3)


**מְטַמְּאִין בְּמַגָּע וּבְאֹהֶל.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְעַל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּעֶצֶם אוֹ בֶחָלָל אוֹ בַמֵּת אוֹ בַקָּבֶר, מַה מֵּת מְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְאֹהֶל, אַף קֶבֶר מְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְאֹהֶל:


מטמאין במגע ובאהל – as it is written (Numbers 19:18): “or on him who touched the bones or the person who was killed or died naturally or the grave,” just as the corpse defiles through contact and through overshadowing in a tent, even the grave defiles through contact and through overshadowing in a tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:4)


**וְאֵינָן מְטַמְּאִין בְּמַשָּׂא.** דְּדָרְשִׁינַן וְכָל אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע עַל פְּנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה (שם), לְרַבּוֹת גּוֹלֵל וְדוֹפֵק, וּכְתִיב וְכָל אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע, מִשּׁוּם מַגָּע הוּא מְטַמֵּא וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם מַשָּׂא:


ואינן מטמאין במשא that we expound (Numbers 19:16): “And in the open, anyone who touches a person,” to include the stone placed on top of the burial cave and the frame supporting the movable stone of a tomb (i.e., גולל ודופק), and it is written: (Numbers 19:16): “anyone who touches,” because of contact he defiles, but he does not defile because of carrying


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:5)


**אִם יֵשׁ תַּחְתֵּיהֶן עֲפַר קְבָרוֹת.** מְטַמֵּא כְּשֶׁמְּסִיטָן, מִשּׁוּם עָפָר הַנִּסָּט עִמָּהֶן דִּמְטַמֵּא בְּמַשָּׂא מִשּׁוּם תְּפוּסָה שֶׁל מֵת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


אם יש תחתיהן עפר קברות – which defiles through movement, because of the dirt that is moved with them when it defiles through carrying, because of the holding of the corpse. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:4:6)


**דּוֹפֵק דּוֹפְקִין.** אֵצֶל אֶבֶן הַדּוֹפֵק מַנִּיחִין לִפְעָמִים אֶבֶן אַחֶרֶת לְהַחֲזִיקָהּ וְאֵין הַגּוֹלֵל נִשְׁעָן עָלֶיהָ, וְהִיא נִקְרֵאת דּוֹפֵק דּוֹפְקִין:


דופק דופקין (but the frame supporting the frame stones) – near the frame supporting the movable stone of a tomb, they sometimes place another stone to support it and the stone placed on top of the burial cave is not leaning upon it, and this called דופק דופקין/the buttress/frame stone of buttressing stones.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:5:1)


**וְאֵלּוּ אִם חָסְרוּ טְהוֹרִים.** דְּלֵית בָּהֶן כַּשִּׁעוּר:


ואלו אם חסרו טהורים – for they lack the according to the measurement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:5:2)


**כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת.** שֶׁכֵּן הוּא תְחִלַּת יְצִירָתוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם, בְּכַזַּיִת:


כזית מן המת – for such is the beginning of the creation of a human with an olive’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:5:3)


**וּרְבִיעִית דָּם.** הוֹאִיל וּבִתְחִלַּת בְּרִיָּתוֹ שֶׁל קָטָן אֵין בּוֹ פָּחוֹת מֵרְבִיעִית דָּם:


ורביעית דם – since but at the beginning of the creation of a minor, he has less than a quarter [of a LOG] of blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:5:4)


**וְאֵבָר מִן הַחַי שֶׁחָסַר עַצְמוֹ.** אֲפִלּוּ קוּלִית גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁחָסַר מִמֶּנָּה עֶצֶם כָּל שֶׁהוּא לֹא מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם אֵבָר מִן הַחַי. אֲבָל אִם חָסַר מִבְּשָׂרוֹ, לְעוֹלָם הוּא מְטַמֵּא עַד שֶׁיֶּחֱסַר מִכְּדֵי לְהַעֲלוֹת אֲרוּכָה. וְאֵבָר מִן הַמֵּת, כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ, טָמֵא. וְעֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ נַמִּי טָמֵא:


ואבר מן החי שחסר עצמו (the limb of a living person that is missing his bone) – even a marrow-bone/thigh-bone that is missing from him any part of it does not defile because a limb from a living being, but if is missing from his flesh, it always defiles until he is lacking enough to produce new flesh [on a healing wound]. But a limb from a corpse, is an olive-bulk’s worth of flesh that separates from him, is impure, and a wone like that of a barley-corn that separates from him is also impure.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:6:1)


**הַשִּׁדְרָה וְהַגֻּלְגֹּלֶת מִשְּׁנֵי מֵתִים.** חֲצִי חֻלְיוֹת הַשִּׁדְרָה מִמֵּת זֶה וַחֲצִי חֻלְיוֹת הַשִּׁדְרָה מִמֵּת אַחֵר. וְכֵן הַגֻּלְגֹּלֶת:


השדרה והגולגולת משני מתים – half of the vertebrae of the spine from this corpse and half of the vertebrae of the spine from another corpse, the skull.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:6:2)


**וְאֵבָר מִן הַחַי מִשְּׁנֵי אֲנָשִׁים.** חֲצִי אֵבָר נִתְלַשׁ מֵחַי זֶה וַחֲצִי אֵבָר כָּזֶה נִתְלַשׁ מֵחַי אַחֵר, וְנִרְאִין כְּאֵבָר אֶחָד:


ואבר מן החי משני אנשים – half of a limb that is torn off from this living person and half of a limb that is torn off rom another living person, and they appear as one limb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:6:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין.** מִטֻּמְאַת אֹהֶל. אֲבָל מְטַמְּאִין בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא מִשּׁוּם עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרין -from defilement by overshadowing in a tent. But they defile through contact and carrying because of a bone the size of a barleycorn. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:1)


**שֶׁנִּדַּקְדְּקוּ.** שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲקוּ. לְשׁוֹן וְשָׁחַקְתָּ מִמֶּנָּה הָדֵק (שמות ל):


שנדקדקו (that were crushed/powdered) – that were ground. It is the language of (Exodus 30:36): “Beat some of it into powder, [and put some before the Pact in the Tent of Meeting].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:2)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מְטַהֵר.** מִכָּל טֻמְאָה. וְאֵינָם מְטַמְּאִין לֹא בְמַגָּע וְלֹא בְמַשָּׂא וְלֹא בְאֹהֶל:


ר' שמעון מטהא – from all defilement. And they do not defile neither through contact nor through carrying nor through overshadowing in a tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִין.** בִּשְׁלָשְׁתָּן, דְּהָא אִיכָּא רֹבַע:


וחכמים מטמאין – with all of them [they defile], as there is a quarter [of a LOG].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:4)


**אֵבָר מִן הַחַי שֶׁנֶּחֱלַק לִשְׁנַיִם טָהוֹר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ חָזַר וְחִבְּרוֹ. דְּאֵין חִבּוּרֵי אָדָם חִבּוּר:


אבר מן החי שנחלק לשנים טהור – and even if he went back and reconnected them, but the connections of a man are not a connection/attachment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 2:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/2:7:5)


**נִטַּל חֲצָאִים.** שֶׁנִּטַּל מִן הַחַי לַחֲצָאִים וְלֹא נִטַּל מִמֶּנּוּ אֵבָר שָׁלֵם כְּאֶחָד, דְּלֹא הֲוָה בֵּיהּ שִׁעוּרָא מֵעוֹלָם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


שניטל חצאים (if it was taken away by halves) – that it was taken from the living person by halves, but not taken from him a complete limb as one, for there was never a measurement for it. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:1)


**כָּל הַמְטַמְּאִין בְּאֹהֶל.** הָאֲמוּרִים לְעֵיל בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק ב':


כל מטמאין באוהל – that are stated above at the beginning of chapter two [Mishnah 1].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:2)


**שֶׁנֶּחְלְקוּ.** שִׁעוּרֵיהֶן לַחֲצָאִין:


שנחלקו – their measurements [were divided] in half.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:3)


**וְהִכְנִיסָן.** כְּשֶׁהֵן מְחֻלָּקִים:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:4)


**לְתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת.** דְּהַיְנוּ לְתוֹךְ אֹהֶל אֶחָד:


לתוך הבית – that is within one tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:5)


**רַבִּי דוֹסָא בֶן הַרְכִּינַס מְטַהֵר.** דְּסָבַר לֹא מִצְטָרְפֵי לְכַשִּׁעוּר:


רבי דוסא בן הרכינס מטהר – for he holds that they do not combine to make up according to the measurement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:6)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכִשְׁנֵי חֲצָאֵי זֵיתִים מִן הַנְּבֵלָה.** פָּתַח בְּאֹהֶל וּמְפָרֵשׁ בִּנְבֵלָה, לוֹמַר לָךְ דְּכִי הֵיכִי דִסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי דוֹסָא שֶׁאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין לְטֻמְאַת אֹהֶל, הָכִי נַמִּי סְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין לְטֻמְאַת מַגָּע וּמַשָּׂא, בֵּין בְּמֵת בֵּין בִּנְבֵלָה:


הנוגע בכשני חצאי זיתים מן הנבילה – at the opening of the tent, and separates them from the carrion, to state to you, that just as that Rabbi Dosa holds that they don’t combine for defilement through the overshadowing of the tent, here also he holds that they don’t combine for defilement of contact and for carrying, whether regarding a corpse or whether regarding a carrion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:7)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכַחֲצִי זַיִת וּמַאֲהִיל עַל כַּחֲצִי זַיִת.** שֶׁהַמַּאֲהִיל עַל הַמֵּת, טָמֵא כְּאִלּוּ הָיָה עִם הַמֵּת בָּאֹהֶל. וּבַגְּמָרָא בְּפֶרֶק הָעוֹר וְהָרֹטֶב מוֹקֵי הָנָךְ מַאֲהִיל דְּרֵישָׁא בְטֻמְאָה רְצוּצָה, שֶׁאֵין בֵּין הַטֻּמְאָה וּבֵין הָאֹהֶל פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי חֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִין, דְּכֻלָּהּ נְגִיעָה הִיא. וְסֵיפָא דְּאָמַר אֲבָל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכַחֲצִי זַיִת וְדָבָר אַחֵר מַאֲהִיל עָלָיו וֽכוּ'‏ דְּמוֹדוּ רַבָּנָן דְּטָהוֹר, מַיְרֵי כְשֶׁיֵּשׁ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח בֵּין הַטֻּמְאָה וּבֵין הָאֹהֶל, דִּבְהָא מוֹדוּ רַבָּנָן דְּאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין, דִּנְגִיעָה וְאֹהֶל שְׁנֵי שֵׁמוֹת הֵן, וְכָל שֶׁהֵן מִשְּׁנֵי שֵׁמוֹת אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין. וְרַבִּי דוֹסָא סָבַר דַּאֲפִלּוּ בְטֻמְאָה רְצוּצָה אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין, וְלֹא אֲפִלּוּ לְמַגָּע וּמַשָּׂא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי דוֹסָא:


הנוגע בחצי זית ומאהיל על חצי זית (he who touches half an olive’s bulk and overshadows over half an olive’s bulk) – that the person who overshadows over corpse, is impure as if he was with the corpse in the tent. And in the Gemara [Tractate Hullin, chapter 9] in the chapter העור והרוטב/The Hide and the Broth [of the Meat], folio 125b, it maintains that the overshadowing of the tent in the first clause [of the Mishnah] are pressed ritual impurity (I.e., a corpse or part of a corpse located beneath a roof or a covering imparts ritual impurity only to objects beneath that covering; anything above the covering remains ritually pure if there is a handbreadth between the corpse and the roof or covering), for there isn’t between the defiling agent/defilement less than a handbreadth, and because of this the Sages declare it impure, for all of it is touching/contact. But the concluding clause [of the Mishnah] that stated: “But a person who is in contact with half an olive’s bulk and another thing overshadows him”/אבל הנוגע בכחצי זית ודבר אחר מאהיל עליו וכ' -that the Rabbis agree that it is pure, we are speaking of when there is an opening of a handbreadth between the defilement and between the tent. And in this the Rabbis agree that they do not combine, for touching and the tent are two categories, and those things that are from two categories do not combine. But Rabbi Dosa holds that even with pressed ritual impurity/טומאה רצוצה they don’t combine, and not even for contact [with one half of an olive’s bulk] and the contact of the tent [with one half of an olive’s bulk]. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Dosa.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:8)


**אָמַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר אַף בְּזוֹ רַבִּי דוֹסָא מְטַהֵר וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִין.** רַבִּי מֵאִיר פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר דִּכְשֶׁיֵּשׁ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח בֵּין הַטֻּמְאָה וְאֹהֶל מוֹדוּ רַבָּנָן דְּטָהוֹר וְאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין, וְאָמַר אִיהוּ דְּאַף בְּזוֹ פְלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עַל רַבִּי דוֹסָא וְאָמְרֵי דִּנְגִיעָה וְאֹהֶל חַד שְׁמָא הוּא, הִלְכָּךְ מִצְטָרְפִין לְכַשִּׁעוּר וּמְטַמְּאִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


אמר ר' מאיר אף בזו ר' דוסא מטהר וחכמים מטמאין – Rabbi Meir disputes with the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] who stated that when there is an opening between the defilement and the tent of a handbreadth, the Sages admit that it is pure, and that they don’t combine, but he himself said, that even in this the Rabbis dispute with Rabbi Dosa who state that the contact/touching and the tent are one category, hence they combine to make up the measurement and they defile But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:9)


**חוּץ מִן הַמַּגָּע עִם הַמַּשָּׂא.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנָּגַע בַּחֲצִי זַיִת וְהֵסִיט חֲצִי זַיִת:


חוץ מן המגע עם המשא – as for example, that he touched half of the olive’s bulk and he moved a half an olive’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:10)


**וְהַמַּשָּׂא עִם הָאֹהֶל.** שֶׁהֵסִיט חֲצִי זַיִת וְהֶאֱהִיל עַל חֲצִי זַיִת:


והמשא עם האוהל- that he moved half an olive’s bulk and overshadowed with a tent over half an olive’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:1:11)


**מִשֵּׁם אֶחָד.** כְּגוֹן מַגָּע וּמַגָּע, מַשָּׂא וּמַשָּׂא, אֹהֶל וְאֹהֶל:


משם אחד – as for example, touching and touching, carrying and carrying, tent and tent.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:2:1)


**מְלֹא תַרְוָד רָקָב.** דְַּאֲמַרַן לְעֵיל בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק ב'‏ שֶׁהוּא מְטַמֵּא בְאֹהֶל:


מלא תרווד רקב (ladleful of corpse mold) – that we stated above at the beginning of Chapter Two [Mishnah 1] that it defiles in a tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:2:2)


**שֶׁנִּתְפַּזֵּר לְתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת.** וְנִתְעָרֵב בּוֹ עָפָר כָּל שֶׁהוּא:


שנתפזר לתוך הבית – and it was combined with whatever an amount of dust.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:2:3)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מְטַהֵר.** דְּסָבַר סוֹפוֹ כִּתְחִלָּתוֹ, מַה תְּחִלָּתוֹ אִם נִקְבַּר הַמֵּת בְּתַכְרִיכִים אוֹ בְאָרוֹן שֶׁל עֵץ, אֵין לוֹ רָקָב, דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה דָבָר נִגְלָל עִמּוֹ, אַף סוֹפוֹ לְאַחַר שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה רָקָב אִם נִתְעָרֵב עִמּוֹ דָּבָר אַחֵר אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ור' שמעון מטהר – he holds that its end is like its beginning, that just as at its outset if the corpse is buried in burial shrouds or in a wooden casket/bier, it has no mass of earth from a grave containing parts of a decayed human body/רקב, for we require that there will be nothing folded/rolled with him, even its end, after it had become became a mass of earth from a grave containing parts of a decayed human bod, if something else combined with hi it doesn’t defile. But the Halakah is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:2:4)


**אִם מִתְכַּבֶּסֶת הַכְּסוּת וְיוֹצֵא מִמֶּנָּה רְבִיעִית דָּם.** וְכֵיצַד מְשַׁעֲרִין אוֹתָהּ, מֵבִיא מַיִם בְּמִדָּה וּמְכַבְּסָהּ בָּהֶן, וּמֵבִיא מַיִם אֲחֵרִים כְּמִדָּתָן וְנוֹתֵן בָּהֶן רְבִיעִית דָּם, אִם הָיָה מַרְאֵיהֶן שָׁוֶה הֲרֵי זוֹ טְמֵאָה, וְאִם לָאו טְהוֹרָה:


אם מתכבסת הכסות ויוצא ממנה רביעית דם – and how do we measure it? He brings water in a measure and washes it, and he brings other water according to that measure and places in it a quarter [of a LOG] of blood. If their appearance is equivalent, this is defilement, but if not it is ritually pure.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:1)


**נִשְׁפַּךְ בָּאֲוִיר.** בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת:


נשפך באויר – within the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:2)


**אִם הָיָה מְקוֹמוֹ קְטַפְרֵס.** אִם הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁנִּשְׁפַּךְ בּוֹ הַדָּם הָיָה מִדְרוֹן:


אם היה מקומו קטפרס (if the place where he was standing was sloping) – if the place where the blood was split was a declivity/slope.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:3)


**וְהֶאֱהִיל.** אָדָם עַל מִקְצָתוֹ:


והאהיל – and a person [overshadowed] over a part of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:4)


**טָהוֹר.** דִּקְטַפְרֵס אֵינוֹ חִבּוּר:


טהור – for a slope is not a connection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:5)


**הָיָה אַשְׁבֹּרֶן.** מָקוֹם הַמְכֻנָּס כְּמוֹ בְתוֹךְ גֻּמָּא:


היה אשבורן (a cavity for the reception of water) -a place where it is gathered, like within a hole/indentation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:6)


**אוֹ שֶׁקָּרַשׁ.** הַדָּם וַאֲפִלּוּ קְטַפְרֵס, הָוֵי חִבּוּר וְטָמֵא, וַאֲפִלּוּ הֶאֱהִיל עַל קְצָתוֹ:


או שקרש (or it congealed/became solid) – the blood and even the sloping place, it is a combination and it is impure, and even if it overshadowed over part of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:7)


**נִשְׁפַּךְ עַל הָאַסְקֻפָּה.** כְּמִין אִצְטַבָּא שֶׁלִּפְנֵי פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת:


נשפך על האסקופה (if it was poured on the threshold/lintel) – like a kind of balcony/portico that is in front of the opening of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:8)


**וְהִיא קְטַפְרֵס בֵּין מִבִּפְנִים.** שֶׁמְּשַׁפֵּעַ לְצַד פְּנִים:


והיא קטפרס בין מפנים that tilts towards the inside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:9)


**בֵּין מִבַּחוּץ.** שֶׁמְּשַׁפֵּעַ לְצַד חוּץ:


בין מבחוץ – that tilts towards the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:10)


**וְהַבַּיִת מַאֲהִיל.** עַל מִקְצָתוֹ: **טָהוֹר.** כָּל מַה שֶּׁבַּבַּיִת מִטֻּמְאַת אֹהֶל, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ שֶׁל דָּם לֵירֵד לִפְנִים, דְּאֵינוֹ חִבּוּר:


והבית מאהיל – upon part of it. טהור – everything that is in the house from the defilement of the overshadowing of the tent, even though that ultimately the blood will descend inside, it is not a connection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:3:11)


**חוּץ מִן הַשִּׁנַּיִם וְהַשֵּׂעָר וְהַצִּפֹּרֶן.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) בְעֶצֶם, מָה עֶצֶם שֶׁנִּבְרָא עִמּוֹ וְאֵין גִּזְעוֹ מַחֲלִיף, אַף כָּל שֶׁנִּבְרָא עִמּוֹ וְאֵין גִּזְעוֹ מַחֲלִיף. יָצְאוּ שִׁנַּיִם שֶׁלֹּא נִבְרְאוּ עִמּוֹ, יָצְאוּ שֵׂעָר וְצִפֹּרֶן שֶׁגִּזְעָן מַחֲלִיף:


חוץ מן השינים והשער והצפורן – as it is written (Numbers 19:16): “or human bone, or a grave, shall be impure seven days],” just as the bone which was created with it, and its stump/that which is cut does not grow again, even all that are created with it, and its stump/that which is cut does not grow again. They excluded teeth which sere not created with, they excluded hair and fingernails that when they are cut, do grow again.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:4:1)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ טָמֵא, שֶׁהֲרֵי מַאֲהִיל עַל הַשֵּׂעָר הַמְחֻבָּר לַמֵּת:


הבית טמא – all that is inside [the house] is impure, for it overshadows on the hair that is connected to the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:4:2)


**עֶצֶם שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלָיו כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר.** הָעֶצֶם נֶחֱשָׁב יָד לַבָּשָׂר:


עצם שיש עליו כזית בשר – the bone is considered a handle for the flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:4:3)


**תְּחוּבִים בִּידֵי אָדָם.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה הַבָּשָׂר מְחֻבָּר לָעֶצֶם מֵעִקָּרוֹ, אֶלָּא אָדָם תְּחָבוֹ לְשָׁם כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁתּוֹחֲבִים הַבָּשָׂר בַּשַּׁפּוּד:


תחובים בידי אדם – for the flesh is not connected to the bone from its essence, but rather a person inserted it thee in the manner that we insert the flesh on a spit.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:1)


**אֵיזֶהוּ דַם תְּבוּסָה.** אַמַּתְנִיתִין דִּלְעֵיל דְּפֶרֶק ב'‏ [מִשְׁנָה ב'] קָאֵי, דְּקָתָנֵי רְבִיעִית דַּם תְּבוּסָה טָמֵא, וְקָמְפָרֵשׁ הָכָא אֵיזֶהוּ דַּם תְּבוּסָה:


איזהו דם תבוסה - it is referring to our Mishnah above in Chapter 2 [Mishnah 2], which teaches that a quarter [of a LOG] of blood that flows from a person at the time of his death of afterwards is ritually impure, and it explains here what is “mixed blood”/דם תבוסה/blood that flows at the time of death or after


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:2)


**שְׁמִינִית בְּחַיָּיו וּשְׁמִינִית בְּמוֹתוֹ.** מִצְטָרְפִים לִרְבִיעִית:


שמינית בחייו ושמינית במותו – they combine to [form] a quarter [of a LOG of blood].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:3)


**רְבִיעִית בְּחַיָּיו וּרְבִיעִית בְּמוֹתוֹ.** שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ וְנָטַל מִן הַתַּעֲרֹבֶת רְבִיעִית, זֶהוּ דַם תְּבוּסָה. אֲבָל שְׁמִינִית בְּחַיָּיו וּשְׁמִינִית בְּמוֹתוֹ, לֹא:


רביעית בחייו ובריבית במותו – that they combined and he took from the mixture a quarter [of a LOG] of blood, this is “mixed blood.” But not one-eighth [of a LOG] during his life and one-eighth [of a LOG] after his death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:4)


**זֶה וָזֶה.** דְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וּדְרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:5)


**כַּמָּיִם.** וְאֵינָן מְטַמְּאִין:


כמים – and they do not defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:6)


**וְאֵיזֶהוּ דַם תְּבוּסָה.** שֶׁטָּמֵא:


ואיזהו דם תבוסה – that is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:7)


**צָלוּב שֶׁדָּמוֹ שׁוֹתֵת.** וְיוֹרֵד בְּלֹא הֶפְסֵק, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁטִּפָּה שֶׁל מִיתָה מְעֹרֶבֶת בּוֹ. אֲבָל נוֹטֵף, שֶׁיּוֹרֵד טִפָּה אַחַר טִפָּה, קַמָּא קַמָּא בָּטֵל וְטָהוֹר:


צלוב שדמו שותת (a crucified person whose blood flows gently/gushes forth) – and falls without any interruption, because the drop of death is mixed in it. But dripping, that if falls drop by drop, little by little is nullified and it is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:8)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר לֹא כִי.** כְּלוֹמַר לֹא כֵן הַדָּבָר:


רבי יהודה אומר לא כי – meaning to say, the matter is not like this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:9)


**אֶלָּא הַשּׁוֹתֵת טָהוֹר.** שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר אוֹתָהּ טִפָּה שֶׁהַנְּשָׁמָה תְלוּיָה בָהּ עָמְדָה לָהּ עַל גַּבֵּי הָעֵץ שֶׁהוּא תָלוּי בּוֹ, וְאֵין בְּדָם זֶה אוֹתָהּ טִפָּה אַחֲרוֹנָה שֶׁל מִיתָה:


אלא השותת טהור – that I state that drop which the soul is dependent upon stood on the wood that it is suspended from, but there isn’t in this drop the last drop of death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:5:10)


**וְהַמְנַטֵּף טָמֵא.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן קַמָּא קַמָּא בָּטֵל, דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אֵין דָּם מְבַטֵּל דָּם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


והמנטף טמא – we don’t say that little by little is nullified, for Rabbi Yehuda holds that blood does not nullify blood. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:6:1)


**כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת פִּתְחוֹ בְטֶפַח.** הַמֵּת בְּבַיִת וְלוֹ פְּתָחִים הַרְבֵּה, כָּל הַכֵּלִים הַמֻּנָּחִים בַּחֲלַל הַפְּתָחִים תַּחַת הַמַּשְׁקוֹף שֶׁל פֶּתַח מִבַּחוּץ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַפְּתָחִים נְעוּלִים וְיֵשׁ הֶפְסֵק בֵּין אֹהֶל הַמֵּת לְאֹהֶל הַכֵּלִים, כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִין. דְּגָזְרוּ חֲכָמִים טֻמְאָה עַל הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁהוּא דֶרֶךְ יְצִיאַת טֻמְאָה שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ שֶׁל מֵת לָצֵאת דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם, וּכְשֶׁאֵין יוֹדְעִים בְּאֵיזֶה מָקוֹם יוֹצִיאוּהוּ, כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִין. וְאִם יֵשׁ כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת בְּבַיִת וְחָשַׁב לְהוֹצִיאוֹ בְּחַלּוֹן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, מַצִּיל עַל כָּל הַפְּתָחִים הַנְּעוּלִים, כֵּיוָן שֶׁיּוֹדֵעַ הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁסּוֹפָהּ שֶׁל טֻמְאָה לָצֵאת דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם. וְאִם הוּא מֵת שָׁלֵם וְחָשַׁב לְהוֹצִיא דֶּרֶךְ חַלּוֹן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה, מַצִּיל עַל כָּל הַפְּתָחִים הַנְּעוּלִים, דִּבְשִׁעוּר אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים שִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים שֶׁרָאוּי לְהוֹצָאַת הַמֵּת:


כזית מן המת פתחו בטפח – the corpse that is in the house and it has many openings, all the vessels that are placed in the cavity of the openings underneath the lintel/cross-piece of the opening from the outside, even though the openings are locked, and there is an interruption between the tent of the corpse and the tent of the vessels, all of them are impure. For the Sages decreed that defilement on a place which is the exit path of defilement that ultimately is of a corpse to exit there, and when we don’t know which path he will remove it, all of them are impure. But if there is an olive’s bulk from a corpse in the house and he intended to remove it through the window that has an opening of a handbreadth, he protects all of the locked openings since he knows the place that ultimately the defilement will leave in that path, but if it is entire corpse and he intended to remove it through the window that has four [handbreadths], he protects all of the locked openings for with the measure of four handbreadths, the Sages estimated that it is appropriate for the removal of the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:6:2)


**אֲבָל לְהוֹצִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** אֲפִלּוּ מֵת שָׁלֵם טֻמְאָה יוֹצְאָה דֶרֶךְ חַלּוֹן כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא פָּתוּחַ, וּמְטַמֵּא הַכֵּלִים שֶׁבַּבַּיִת שֶׁבְּצִדּוֹ אִם יֵשׁ בַּחַלּוֹן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


אבל להוציא את הטומאה בפותח טפח – even a complete corpse, its defilement departs through the window all the time that it is open, and defiles the vessels that are in the house that are at its side if there is a window that is open a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:6:3)


**גָּדוֹל מִכַּזַּיִת כַּמֵּת.** דַּוְקָא כַּזַּיִת מְצֻמְצָם פִּתְחוֹ בְטֶפַח. אֲבָל יוֹתֵר מִכַּזַּיִת דִּינוֹ כְּאִלּוּ הָיָה מֵת שָׁלֵם, וְאֵין מַצִּיל עַל הַפְּתָחִים הַנְּעוּלִים עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה:


גדול מכזית כמת – specifically like a pressed-in olive’s bulk, its opening is with a handbreadth. But more than an olive’s bulk, its law as is it was a complete corpse, and it doesn’t protect over the locked openings until there would be four [handbreadths].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:6:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר הַשִּׁדְרָה וְהַגֻּלְגֹּלֶת.** שֶׁבַּבַּיִת, דִּינוֹ כְמֵת שָׁלֵם, וְאִם חָשַׁב לְהוֹצִיאָן דֶּרֶךְ חַלּוֹן, אֵין מַצִּיל עַל הַפְּתָחִים הַנְּעוּלִים עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בוֹ אַרְבָּעָה. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


רבי יוסי אומר השדרה והגולגולת - that in his Bet Din/court, it is like a complete corpse, and if he intended to remove them through a window, it does not protect the locked openings until there would be four [handbreadths]. And such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:1)
