**טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח עַל רוּם טֶפַח.** דַּף שֶׁהוּא רָחָב טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח וְגָבֹהַּ מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח, וּמַאֲהִיל עַל כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת וְעַל הַכֵּלִים, מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה עַל הַכֵּלִים:


טפח על טפח על רום טפח – a board/plank which is a handbreadth wide by a handbreadth [long] and above from the ground a handbreadth, and overshadows over an olive’s bulk of a corpse or on vessels, brings the defilement on to the vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:2)


**מְרֻבָּע.** שֶׁאִם הָיָה רָחְבּוֹ פָּחוֹת מִטֶּפַח אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיָה אָרְכּוֹ שְׁנֵי טְפָחִים, לֹא אָמְרִינַן שִׂים מַה שֶּׁיָּתֵר עַל אָרְכּוֹ בְרָחְבּוֹ וְרַבְּעֵהוּ, לְהָכִי קָתָנֵי מְרֻבָּע, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁיְּהֵא בְרָחְבּוֹ טֶפַח וּבְאָרְכּוֹ טֶפַח:


מרובע – that if its width was less than that of a handbreadth, even though its length was two handbreadths, we don’t say – place what remains from its length upon its width and make it quadrilateral. For this, it (i.e., the Mishnah) teaches "מרובע"/cubic, that there must be in its width a handbreadth and in its length a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:3)


**כֵּיצַד.** כְּלוֹמַר כֵּיצַד חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


כיצד – meaning to say, how does it interpose before the defilement/uncleanness?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:4)


**בִּיב שֶׁהוּא קָמוּר.** חֲפִירָה בַּקַּרְקַע וּמְקֹרָה, וְהַיְנוּ קָמוּר, וְשָׁם יוֹרְדִים שְׁפָכִים שֶׁבַּבַּיִת וּמִשָּׁם יוֹצְאִים לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


ביב שהוא קמור (a drain/pipe/gutter which is arched) – digging in the ground and its foundation, that is arched and there the waste-water (i.e., that which is poured in the gutter) that is in the house descends and from there exits to the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:5)


**יֶשׁ בּוֹ פוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** דְּהַיְנוּ טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח בַּבִּיב עַצְמוֹ:


יש בו פוחת טפח – that is a handbreadth by a handbreadth at the depth of a handbreadth in the drain/gutter itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:6)


**וּבִיצִיאָתוֹ.** בְּפֶתַח הַבִּיב שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ יוֹצְאִין הַמַּיִם לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


וביציאתו – in the opening of the drain/gutter that from it the waters go out to the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:7)


**טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ.** כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת בְּתוֹךְ הַבִּיב. דְּכַזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת פִּתְחוֹ בְּטֶפַח:


טומאה בתוכו – if there is an olive’s bulk from the corpse within the drain, for the olive’s bulk from the corpse its opening is with a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:8)


**הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר.** שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה יוֹצְאָה דֶּרֶךְ פֶּתַח הַבִּיב הַיּוֹצֵא לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָתָנֵי חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


הבית טהור – that the defilement/uncleanness leaves through the opening of the drain that goes out to the public domain. And this is what is taught [in the Mishnah]: "חוצץ בפני הטומאה"/interposes before the uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:9)


**טֻמְאָה בַבַּיִת מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹךְ הַבִּיב טָהוֹר.** דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ טֻמְאָה לְהַכְנִיסָהּ דֶּרֶךְ פֶּתַח הַבִּיב, שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַטֻּמְאָה לְהוֹצִיאָהּ דֶּרֶךְ פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת וְלֹא לְהַכְנִיסָהּ לַבִּיב:


טומאה בתוך הבית הביב טהור – for there is no path for the uncleanness to bring it in through the opening of the drain, for the path of the uncleanness is to remove it through the opening of the house but not to bring it into the drain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:10)


**וְאֵין בִּיצִיאָתוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** דְּטֻמְאָה עוֹלָה מִן הַבִּיב לַבַּיִת כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין לָהּ דֶּרֶךְ לָצֵאת דֶּרֶךְ פֶּתַח הַבִּיב הַיּוֹצֵא לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, דְּכָל פָּחוּת מִטֶּפַח אֵין הַטֻּמְאָה יוֹצְאָה:


ואין ביציאתו פותח טפח טומאה בתוכו הבית טמא – for the uncleanness/defilement goes up from the drain to the house since it lacks a path to leave through the opening of the drain that goes out to the public domain, for all that is less than a handbreadth the defilement/uncleanness does not leave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:11)


**אֵין בּוֹ פוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח וְאֵין בִּיצִיאָתוֹ פוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** לְחִנָּם נָקַט וְאֵין בִּיצִיאָתוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, דְּכֵיוָן דְּאֵין בּוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, עַל יְצִיאָתוֹ לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן, דִּכְאִלּוּ אֵין שָׁם חָלָל כְּלָל דָּמֵי וַהֲרֵי הַבִּיב נֶחְשָׁב כְּקַרְקַע הַבַּיִת, וְאָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים כָּל אֲשֶׁר בָּאֹהֶל יִטְמָא, לְרַבּוֹת קַרְקָעִיתוֹ שֶׁל בַּיִת כָּמוֹהוּ עַד הַתְּהוֹם:


אין פו פותח טפח ואין ביציאתו פוחת טפח – it is is taken for naught for there isn’t in its outlet an opening of a handbreadth, for since it lacks an opening of a handbreadth, on its outlet we are not concerned,, for it is as if there is like there is no cavity at all and the drain is considered like the ground of the house, and the Sages stated: All that is in the tent will be defiled, including the bottom/ground of the house which is like it until the depth/interior of the earth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:12)


**אֶחָד חוֹר שֶׁחֲרָרוּהוּ מַיִם.** הַאי פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח שֶׁמֵּבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, לֹא שְׁנָא אִם נַעֲשָׂה הַחוֹר הַזֶּה עַל יְדֵי מַיִם אוֹ שֶׁנִּקְּבוּהוּ שְׁרָצִים:


אחד חור שחררוהו מים – this that is open a handbreadth that brings the defilement/uncleanness, there is no difference if this hole was made through the water or that reptiles/insect perforated it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:13)


**אוֹ שֶׁאֲכָלַתּוּ מַלַּחַת.** לְשׁוֹן אֶרֶץ מְלֵחָה. כְּמִין עָפָר לָבָן דּוֹמֶה לְמֶלַח וּמִתְאַכֵּל וְנוֹפֵל וְנַעֲשָׂה חוֹר מֵאֵלָיו:


או שאכלתו מלחת (or a cave formed through saline corrosion) – the language of )Jeremiah 17:6): “In a barren land without inhabitant.” It is like white dust similar to salt and it consumes and falls and makes a hole on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:14)


**מִרְבָּךְ שֶׁל אֲבָנִים.** אֲבָנִים גְּדוֹלוֹת עֲרוּכוֹת וּסְדוּרוֹת זוֹ עַל זוֹ, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהָאַחַת סְדוּרָה עַל גַּבֵּי שְׁתַּיִם וְהַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת מְפֹרָדוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ וְיֵשׁ שָׁם אֹהֶל טֶפַח:


מרבך של אבנים (an irregular pile of large stones) – large stones that are arranged and set up one on the other. But sometimes it is arranged on two [stones] and the lower ones are separated from each other and there is a tent [overshadowing] a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:15)


**סְוָאר שֶׁל קוֹרוֹת.** קוֹרוֹת עֲרוּכוֹת זוֹ עַל זוֹ וְעוֹמְדוֹת לְבִנְיָן, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בֵּינֵיהֶן אֹהֶל טֶפַח:


סואר של קורות (a pile/frame of beams) – beams that are set up on top of the other that stand ready for building, but sometimes there is between them a tent overshadowing a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:16)


**כָּל אֹהֶל שֶׁאֵינוֹ עָשׂוּי בִּידֵי אָדָם אֵינוֹ אֹהֶל.** דְּיָלֵיף אֹהֶל אֹהֶל מִמִּשְׁכָּן, מַה לְּהַלָּן בִּידֵי אָדָם אַף כָּאן בִּידֵי אָדָם:


כל שאינו עשוי בידי אדם אינו אוהל – which we derive through an analogy/Gezerah Shavah from [the comparison of the usage of the word] אוהל (Numbers 19;14: "זאת התורה אדם כי ימות באוהל"/This is the ritual: When a person dies in a tent”) and אוהל (Exodus 40:19; "ויפרש את האוהל על-המשכן"/He spread the tent over the Tabernacle”), just as there (in the Tabernacle) it was done at the hands of a person, so also here (regarding the defilement of the corpse) at the hands of man.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:17)


**בִּשְׁקִיפִים.** תַּרְגּוּם סְעִיפֵי הַסְּלָעִים, שְׁקִיפֵי כֵיפָא:


בשקיפים (clefts) – It is the Aramaic translation of (Isaiah 57:5): “Among/under the clefts of the rocks,” the clefts of the rocks


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 3:7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/3:7:18)


**וּבִסְלָעִים.** בִּנְקִיקֵי הַסְּלָעִים. וּשְׁקִיפֵי וּנְקִיקֵי הַסְּלָעִים אֶחָד הֵן, אֶלָּא שֶׁזֶּה נַעֲשָׂה עַל יְדֵי מָטָר וְזֶה נַעֲשָׂה מֵאֵלָיו. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא נַעֲשׂוּ בִידֵי אָדָם מוֹדֶה בָהֶם רַבִּי יְהוּדָה, דְּלֹא יָלֵיף גְּזֵרָה שָׁוָה דְּאֹהֶל אֹהֶל מִמִּשְׁכָּן אֶלָּא לְפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח בִּלְבַד, אֲבָל בְּיוֹתֵר מִטֶּפַח, כְּגוֹן מְלֹא אֶגְרוֹף, אָמְרִינַן בְּסֻכָּה [רֵישׁ] פֶּרֶק הַיָּשֵׁן דְּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ נַעֲשָׂה מֵאֵלָיו מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה. וְכֵן בִּשְׁקִיפִים וּבִסְלָעִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ובסלעים – in the clefts of the stones/rocks, and the שקיפי and נקיקי הסלעים are one and the same, but one was made through the rain and the other was made on its own. But even though they were not made at the hands of man Rabbi Yehuda agrees/accepts them, for he does not derive the Gezerah Shavah/analogy of “tent”/”tent” from the Tabernacle (see above) but rather for an opening to be handbreadth alone, but more than a handbreadth, such as a fistful, we stated in [Tractate] Sukkah [at the beginning of] the chapter "הישן"/He who sleeps (Chapter 2 – folio 20b at the bottom and folio 22a at the top). For Rabbi Yehuda agrees that even if it was made of its own, it brings the uncleanness. And similarly with clefts and rocks. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:1)


**מִגְדָּל שֶׁהוּא עוֹמֵד בָּאֲוִיר.** מִגְדַּל עֵץ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ דְפָנוֹת וְהַדְּפָנוֹת עַצְמָן יֵשׁ חָלָל בְּעָבְיָן כְּגוֹן חוֹרִין הַמְפֻלָּשִׁין פְּתוּחִים לִפְנִים וְלַחוּץ אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, וּרְגִילִים לְהַצְנִיעַ שָׁם מְחָטִין וְצִנּוֹרִיּוֹת וְכֵלִים קְטַנִּים:


מגדל העומד באויר (a tower/cupboard standing isolated) – a wooden turret that has walls but the walls themselves have a cavity in their thickness, such as perforated holes that are open to the inside and the outside but they lack an opening of a handbreadth, and it is customary to hide there needles and hooks/curved pins and small vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:2)


**שֶׁהוּא עוֹמֵד בָּאֲוִיר.** תַּחַת אֲוִיר הַשָּׁמַיִם, כְּגוֹן דְּקָאֵי בֶחָצֵר:


שהוא עומד באויר—underneath the airspace of the sky, such as that it stands in the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:3)


**טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ.** בַּחֲלַל הַמִּגְדָּל עַצְמוֹ:


טומאה בתוכו – in the cavity of the turret itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:4)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבְּעָבְיוֹ.** בָּעֳבִי חֲלַל הַדְּפָנוֹת:


כלים שבעוביו – in the thickness of the cavity of the walls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:5)


**מֶחֱצָה לְמֶחֱצָה.** מֵחֶצְיוֹ שֶׁל עֳבִי מִגְדָּל וְלִפְנִים כִּלְפָנִים, מֵחֶצְיוֹ וְלַחוּץ כְּלַחוּץ. בֵּין שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בַּמִּגְדָּל וְכֵלִים בְּעָבְיוֹ, בֵּין שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בְּעָבְיוֹ וְכֵלִים בַּמִּגְדָּל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


מחצה למחצה – from its half of the thickness of the cupboard/turret and towards the inside is like the inside, from its half [of the thickness] towards the outside is like the outside. Whether the defilement is in the turret/cupboard and the vessels are in its thickness, or whether the defilement is in its thickness and the vessels are in the turret/cupboard. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:6)


**טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ.** בְּתוֹךְ הַמִּגְדָּל:


טומאה בתוכו – within the turret/cupboard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:7)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדַּלְתוֹתָיו מְגוּפוֹת. כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַטֻּמְאָה לָצֵאת מִשָּׁם לַבַּיִת:


הבית טמא – even though that its doors are sealed. As it (i.e., the Mishnah) explains the reason that the manner of the defilement/uncleanness is to depart from there to the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:8)


**טֻמְאָה בַבַּיִת מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹךְ הַמִּגְדָּל טָהוֹר.** וְהוּא שֶׁדַּלְתוֹתָיו מְגוּפוֹת. דְּאִי פְּתוּחוֹת, לֹא גָּרַע מֵחַלּוֹן שֶׁבֵּין שְׁנֵי בָּתִּים. וְטַעֲמָא דְמַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹךְ הַמִּגְדָּל טָהוֹר, שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לַטֻּמְאָה שֶׁבַּבַּיִת לְהַכְנִיסָהּ בַּמִּגְדָּל:


טומאה בבית מה שבתוך המגדל – this is with its doors sealed. For if they are opened, it is no worse than a window that is between the two houses. For the reason that what is within the turret/cupboard is ritually pure, is that there is no path for the defilement that is in the house to enter into the turret/cupboard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:9)


**אִם יֶשׁ שָׁם פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** בֵּין שׁוּלֵי הַמִּגְדָּל לָאָרֶץ אוֹ בֵּין דֹּפֶן הַמִּגְדָּל לַכֹּתֶל אוֹ בֵּין גַּג הַמִּגְדָּל לְתִקְרַת הַבַּיִת:


אם יש שם פותח טפח – between the rims of the turret/cupboard and the ground or between the wall of the turret/cupboard to the wall or between the roof the turret to the ceiling of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:10)


**טְמֵאִים.** מֵחֲמַת טֻמְאָה שֶׁבַּבַּיִת. אֲבָל אֵין שָׁם טֶפַח הָוֵי מִגְדָּל כְּלָבוּד וְאֵין טֻמְאַת הַבַּיִת נִכְנֶסֶת שָׁם:


טמאים – on account of the defilement that is in the house. But if there isn’t there [an opening] of a handbreadth, the turret/cupboard is like a לבוד/joined (i.e., that two solid surfaces are considered to be joined if there is a gap less than three handbreadths between them). And the defilement of the house does not enter there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:1:11)


**טֻמְאָה שָׁם הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** אִם הָיְתָה טֻמְאָה בֵּין שׁוּלֵי הַמִּגְדָּל לְקַרְקַע הַבַּיִת, אוֹ בֵּין גַּג הַמִּגְדָּל לְגַג הַבַּיִת, אוֹ בֵּין דָּפְנוֹת הַמִּגְדָּל לְכָתְלֵי הַבַּיִת, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בֵּינֵיהֶן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, הַבַּיִת טָמֵא, שֶׁאֵין הַמִּגְדָּל מַצִּיל עַל הַטֻּמְאָה שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת שֶׁלֹּא תְטַמֵּא כָּל אֲשֶׁר בַּבַּיִת:


טומאה שם הבית טמא – if there was defilement between the rims of the turret/cupboard to the ground of the house. Or [there was defilement] between the roof of the turret to the roof of the house or between the walls of the turret to the walls of the house, even though there isn’t between them an opening of a handbreadth. הבית טמא – for the turret/cupboard does not protect regarding the defilement that is below it within the house so that it doesn’t defile all that is in the house.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:1)


**תֵּבַת הַמִּגְדָּל.** רְגִילִים לַעֲשׂוֹת תֵּבוֹת קְטַנּוֹת בְּתוֹךְ מִגְדָּל שֶׁל עֵץ, כְּדֵי לְהַצְנִיעַ בָּהֶם כֵּלִים וָאֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִים:


תיבת המגדל -it is customary to make small chests within the wooden turret/cupboard, in order to hide in them vessels and food and liquids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:2)


**שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בַּחֲלָלָהּ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, דְּלֹא הַוְיָא טֻמְאָה טְמוּנָה:


בה פותח טפח – there is in its cavity an opening of a handbreadth, but there is no defilement hidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:3)


**וְאֵין בִּיצִיאָתָהּ.** בְּחוֹר פִּתְחָהּ אֵין בּוֹ טֶפַח:


ואין ביציאתה – in the hole of its opening, there isn’t a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:4)


**טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכָהּ הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפִּתְחָהּ קָטָן. כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ טֻמְאָה לָצֵאת, עַל כָּרְחָךְ סוֹפָהּ לָצֵאת דֶּרֶךְ פֶּתַח זֶה, לְפִיכָךְ מְטַמֵּא מִיָּד דֶּרֶךְ יְצִיאָתָהּ:


טומאה בתוכה הבית טמא – and even though that its opening is small. As it explains the reason, because it is the manner of the defilement to go out, by force, its end is to exit through the path of this opening, therefore, it defiles immediately in the path of its exiting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:5)


**מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ טָהוֹר.** הוֹאִיל וְאֵין בִּיצִיאָתָהּ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה:


שבתוכה טהור מה– since there isn’t in its removal an opening of a handbreadth, it doesn’t bring in the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:6)


**וְאֵין דֶּרֶךְ טֻמְאָה לִכָּנֵס.** שֶׁאֵין סוֹפוֹ לְהַכְנִיס לָהּ מֵת:


ואין דרך טומאה ליכנס – that its conclusion is not to bring into it a corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:2:7)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי מְטַהֵר.** דְּשֶׁמָּא אֵין סוֹפוֹ לָצֵאת דֶּרֶךְ פֶּתַח זֶה שֶׁיְּהֵא בָהּ שִׁעוּר טֻמְאָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי יָכוֹל לְהוֹצִיאָהּ לַחֲצָאִים אוֹ לְשָׂרְפָהּ בִּמְקוֹמָהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ור' יוסי מטר – for perhaps its end is not exit through the path of this opening which has the measurement of defilement/uncleanness, for it is able to be remove it by halves or to burn it in its place. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:1)


**הָיָה.** הַמִּגְדָּל עוֹמֵד כֻּלּוֹ בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת, אֶלָּא שֶׁפֶּתַח הַתֵּבָה לַחוּץ:


היה – the turret/cupboard was standing within the house, but the opening of the drawer was towards the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:2)


**טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכָהּ הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר.** דְּמִשּׁוּם מַאי אִיכָּא לְטַמּוֹיֵהּ, הָא טֻמְאָה לֵיכָּא, דְּיֵשׁ בָּהּ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח וְלָאו טְמוּנָה הִיא, וּמִשּׁוּם יְצִיאָה שֶׁל טֻמְאָה נַמִּי לֹא מִטַּמְּיָא, דְּהָא אֵין יְצִיאָתָהּ לִפְנִים בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת:


טומאה בתוכה בבית טהור – for what is there is a reason to defile it? For there is no defilement that has an opening of a handbreadth and it is not hidden within. And because the departure of defilement also does not defile, because its departure/exuding is not inside within the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:3)


**טֻמְאָה בַבַּיִת מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ טָהוֹר.** שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ טֻמְאָה לִכָּנֵס שָׁם:


טומאה בבית מה שבתוכו טהור- for it is not the manner of defilement to enter/seep in there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:4)


**מוּכְנִי.** גַּלְגַּל הֶעָשׂוּי לַמִּגְדָּל לְהוֹלִיכוֹ מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם:


מוכני (wheel-work, machine for lifting weights) – a wheel that is made for the turret/cupboard to bring it from place to place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:5)


**מְשׁוּכָה לַאֲחוֹרָיו.** מְרֻחֶקֶת מֵאֲחוֹרֵי הַמִּגְדָּל לְצַד הַבַּיִת. שֶׁהַמִּגְדָּל עוֹמֵד עַל הַפֶּתַח וְהַמּוּכְנִי מְשׁוּכָה מֵאַחֲרָיו לְצַד הַבַּיִת בִּפְנִים, וְטֻמְאָה שָׁם בְּתוֹךְ הַמּוּכְנִי כְּנֶגֶד קוֹרוֹת הַבַּיִת. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַבַּיִת מַאֲהִיל עָלָיו:


משוכה לאחוריו (if its base protruded -backwards)- distanced from the back of the turret/cupboard to the side of the house. For the turret stands on the opening and the wheel-work/base protrudes backwards towards of the house inside, but the defilement there within the wheel-work is opposite the beams of the house. Even though the house overshadows upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:6)


**הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר.** דְּנֶחֱשָׁב מוּכְנִי כַּמִּגְדָּל, וַהֲרֵי הַמִּגְדָּל עוֹמֵד בַּפֶּתַח וְנִפְתָּח לַחוּץ. וּשְׁלֹשָׁה אֶצְבָּעוֹת דְּנָקַט, לְמַעוֹטֵי אִם הָיְתָה מְשׁוּכָה טְפֵי מִשָּׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת, דְּלֹא הֲוֵי כַמִּגְדָּל, דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לְהַרְחִיק הַמּוּכְנִי מִן הַמִּגְדָּל כֻּלֵּי הַאי:


הבית טהור – for the wheel-work/base is considered like a turret/cupboard, for the turret stands at the entrance and opens outward. And the three fingers that it (i.e., the Mishnah) took, if it was protruding more than three fingerbreadths, it would not be like a turret/cupboard, for it is not the manner to distance the wheel-work/base from the turret so much.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:7)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁיֶּשׁ שָׁם פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** דַּחֲלַל הַמּוּכְנִי שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בּוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, שֶׁלֹּא תְהֵא טֻמְאָה רְצוּצָה דְבוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה:


בזמן שיש שם פותח טפח – for the cavity of the wheel-work/base that the defilement is contained in is open a handbreadth, in order that there not be pressed ritual impurity that it breaks through and rises.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:8)


**וְאֵינָהּ יוֹצְאָה.** שֶׁאֵין הַמּוּכְנִי נִשְׁמֶטֶת לְגַמְרֵי מִן הַמִּגְדָּל:


ואינה יוצאת – for the wheel-work/base is not detached completely from the turret/cupboard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 4:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/4:3:9)


**וְהַמִּגְדָּל בָּא בְמִדָּה.** שֶׁמַּחֲזִיק אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה בַּלַּח שֶׁהֵם כּוֹרַיִם בַּיָּבֵשׁ. שֶׁכְּשֶׁהוּא בְמִדָּה זוֹ אֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, וְחוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה. אֲבָל בְּפָחוֹת מִמִּדָּה זוֹ, הָוֵי כִשְׁאָר כֵּלִים הַמְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה, וְאֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה. וְכָל מִגְדָּל דִּתְנִינַן בְּפִרְקִין, בְּמִגְדָּל הַבָּא בְמִדָּה מַיְרֵי:


והמגדל בא במדה (and the cupboard comes in requisite size) – that it holds forty Se’ah in moist with are two Kors in dry. And when it is in requisite size it is not susceptible to receive defilement and it interposes before the defilement. But less than this measure, it is like the rest of the vessels that are susceptible to receive defilement and it does not interpose before the defilement. And every turret/cupboard that is taught in this chapter [of Mishnah], is a cupboard that comes in requisite size.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:1)


**תַּנּוּר שֶׁהוּא עוֹמֵד בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת וְעֵינוֹ קְמוּרָה.** נֶקֶב הֶעָשׂוּי בַּתַּנּוּר שֶׁהֶעָשָׁן יוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ מֻנָּח חוּץ לַבַּיִת:


תנור שהוא עומד בתוך הבית – the incision that is made in the oven that the smoke leaves from it is placed outside of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:2)


**וְהֶאֱהִילוּ עָלָיו נוֹשְׂאֵי הַמֵּת.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ נוֹשְׂאִים אֶת הַמֵּת לְקָבְרוֹ הֶאֱהִיל הַמֵּת עַל הָעַיִן:


והאהילו עליו נושאי המת – when they would carry the corpse to bury it, the corpse overshadowed over the “eye” (i.e., fire-place] [which is outside the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:3)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** קָסָבְרֵי טֻמְאָה נִכְנֶסֶת לַתַּנּוּר דֶּרֶךְ עֵינוֹ וְיוֹצְאָה דֶרֶךְ פִּיהָ לַבַּיִת:


בית שמאי אומרים הכל טמא – for they (i.e., the House of Shammai) hold that the defilement enters into the oven through its “eye (i.e., fire place) and departs through its mouth into the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:4)


**הַתַּנּוּר טָמֵא וְהַבַּיִת טָהוֹר.** הַתַּנּוּר שֶׁהֶאֱהִיל הַמֵּת עַל מִקְצָתוֹ טָמֵא, וְהַבַּיִת טָהוֹר, שֶׁאֵין הַטֻּמְאָה יוֹצְאָה דֶרֶךְ פִּיהָ:


התנור טמא והבית טהור – the oven which overshadowed the corpse on part of it is impure, but the house is ritually pure, for the defilement does not go out through its mouth [according to the School of Hillel].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:1:5)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר אַף הַתַּנּוּר טָהוֹר.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֹא הֶאֱהִיל עַל הַתַּנּוּר אֶלָּא עַל הָעַיִן הַיּוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ, אֵין הַתַּנּוּר טָמֵא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּבֵית הִלֵּל:


ר' עקיבא אומר אף התנור טהור – for since it (i.e., the corpse) did not overshadow over the oven but only on the “eye” (i.e., fire-place) that comes out from it, the oven is not impure. But the Halakha is according to the School of Hillel.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:2:1)


**אֲרֻבָּה.** כְּמִין חַלּוֹן שֶׁבְּגַג הַבַּיִת שֶׁהוּא קַרְקָעִית הָעֲלִיָּה:


ארובה (the opening/aperture in the roof looking to the ground floor) – like a kind of window that is on the roof of the house which is the bottom of the upper chamber.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:2:2)


**בְּכוֹנֵס מַשְׁקֶה.** כְּשֶׁמְּשִׂימִים הַכְּלִי עַל הַמַּשְׁקִים נִכְנָסִים הַמַּשְׁקִין לְתוֹכוֹ דֶּרֶךְ אוֹתוֹ הַנֶּקֶב:


בכונס משקה – when they place the vessel on the liquids that enter, the liquids enter into it through that incision.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:2:3)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** אִם יֵשׁ טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת, הַקְּדֵרָה וְכָל מַה שֶּׁבָּעֲלִיָּה טָמֵא. שֶׁאֵין הַקְּדֵרָה מַצֶּלֶת, וְטֻמְאָה עוֹלָה דֶרֶךְ אֲרֻבָּה שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


הכל טמא – if there is defilement in the house, the pot and everything that is on the upper chamber is ritually impure. For the pot does not protect, and the defilement ascends through the aperture which has an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:2:4)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים הַקְּדֵרָה טְמֵאָה.** חֻמְרָא בְּעָלְמָא הִיא שֶׁמַּחְמִירִין בֵּית הִלֵּל לְטַמֵּא אֶת הַקְּדֵרָה, דִּכְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא סְבִירָא לְהוּ שֶׁהַקְּדֵרָה מַצֶּלֶת עַל הַטֻּמְאָה, וּכְגוֹן שֶׁגַּב הַקְּדֵרָה פּוֹנֶה לְמַטָּה אֵצֶל הַטֻּמְאָה, שֶׁאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה מִגַּבּוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּבֵית הִלֵּל:


ובית הלל אומרים הקדירה טמאה – it is a mere stringency that the School of Hillel is strict to invalidate to make the pot impure, for Rabbi Akiba holds that the pot protects over the defilement, as for example that the back of the pot faces downward near the defilement, for an earthenware vessel is not susceptible to receive ritual defilement rom its back. But the Halakha is according to the School of Hillel.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:3:1)


**מַצֶּלֶת עַל הַכֹּל.** עַל כָּל מַה שֶּׁבָּעֲלִיָּה. שֶׁהִיא חוֹצֶצֶת בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


מצלת על הכל – [if affords protection] on everything that is in the upper chamber, for it interposes in front of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:3:2)


**אֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת אֶלָּא עַל הָאֳכָלִים וְעַל הַמַּשְׁקִים וְעַל כְּלֵי חָרֶס.** בִּקְדֵרָה שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ מַיְרֵי. וְטַעֲמַיְהוּ דְבֵית שַׁמַּאי מִשּׁוּם דְּכֵלָיו שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ טְמֵאִין הֵם אֵצֶל חָבֵר, וְכָל כְּלִי טָמֵא אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה. וּלְפִי שֶׁעַם הָאָרֶץ מַחֲזִיק הַכֵּלִים שֶׁלּוֹ בְטָהוֹר, אָמְרִינַן לֵיהּ שֶׁהוּא מַצִּיל עַל אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִין וּכְלֵי חֶרֶס שֶׁבָּעֲלִיָּה, דִּלְנַפְשֵׁיהּ בִּלְבַד הוּא דִּמְטַהֲרִינַן לְהוּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַחֲבֵרִים בְּדֵלִים מֵהֶם וּמִמַּגָּעָם וּבְלָאו הָכִי נַמִּי כָל מַאֲכָלָם טָמֵא. אֲבָל שְׁאָר כֵּלִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם טָהֳרָה בְּמִקְוֶה, אִם תְּטַהֲרֵם הֲרֵי הוּא מַשְׁאִילָם לְחָבֵר וְיִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהֶם בִּטְבִילָה לְחוֹדַהּ בְּלֹא הַזָּאָה שְׁלִישִׁי וּשְׁבִיעִי:


אינה מצלת אלא על האוכלים ועל המשקים ועל כלי חרס – he (i.e., the School of Shammai) is speaking about the pot of those who are illiterate. And the reason of the School of Shammai is that the vessels of the illiterate/עם הארץ are impure in regard to a person who a חבר/member of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse, and every impure vessel does not interpose in front of the defilement. And because the illiterate holds his vessels as pure, we state to him that he protects over food and drink and earthenware vessels that are in the upper chamber, that for himself alone that we declare them pure, for those who are members of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse are separate/keep aloof from them and from their contact, for without this, all of their food would be impure. But the other vessels that have purification in a ritual bath/Mikveh, if they will purify them, he (the person who is illiterate) can lend them to a חבר and he would use them with immersion [in a Mikveh] alone, without sprinkling [of ashes] on the third and seventh days.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:1)


**לָגִין.** שֶׁל עֵץ אוֹ שֶׁל מַתֶּכֶת שֶׁמֻּנָּח בַּעֲלִיָּה זוֹ שֶׁהַקְּדֵרָה מֻנַּחַת עַל פִּי אֲרֻבָּה שֶׁלָּהּ:


לגין (a vessel smaller than a pitcher but larger than a cup/flask) – of wood or of metal that is placed in this upper chamber that the pot is placed on the mouth of its aperture in the roof looking to the ground floor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:2)


**הַלָּגִין טָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה.** שֶׁאֵין הַקְּדֵרָה מַצֶּלֶת עַל כְּלֵי עֵץ וּכְלֵי מַתָּכוֹת כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


הלגין טמא טומאת שבעה – that the pot does not protect over a wooden vessel or a metal vessel as we have stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:3)


**וְהַמַּשְׁקִים טְהוֹרִים.** שֶׁהַקְּדֵרָה מַצֶּלֶת עַל אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים:


והמשקים טהורים – that the pot protects over foods and liquids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:4)


**וְאִם פִּנָּה.** הַמַּשְׁקִים הַלָּלוּ לִכְלִי אַחֵר שֶׁבְּאוֹתָהּ עֲלִיָּה, הַכְּלִי הָאַחֵר מְטַמְּאָן, שֶׁכָּל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁבָּעֲלִיָּה טְמֵאִים, חוּץ מִכְּלֵי חֶרֶס. אֲבָל הַכְּלִי הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהָיוּ הַמַּשְׁקִין בְּתוֹכוֹ אֵינוֹ מְטַמְּאָן אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, שֶׁאִם אָמַרְתָּ כֵּן, אִם כֵּן אֵין הַקְּדֵרָה שֶׁבָּאֲרֻבָּה מַצֶּלֶת עַל הַמַּשְׁקִין לְעוֹלָם, וַאֲנַן אָמְרִינַן שֶׁהִיא מַצֶּלֶת עַל אֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין:


ואם פינה (if she emptied it) – these liquids to another vessel that is the same upper chamber, the other vessel defiles them, for all of the vessels that are in the upper chamber are impure except for earthenware vessels. But the first vessel that the liquids were in are not defiled even though it (i.e., the vessel) is impure with ritual impurity for seven days. For if you said so, if so, the pot that is in the aperture in the roof looking to the ground does not protect over liquids at all, and we state that it protects over food and liquids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:5)


**הָאִשָּׁה וְהָעֲרֵבָה טְמֵאִים.** שֶׁאֵין הַקְּדֵרָה שֶׁל חֶרֶס מַצֶּלֶת עֲלֵיהֶן:


האשה והערבה טמאים (the woman and the trough are impure) – for the earthenware pot protects them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:4:6)


**וְהַבָּצֵק טָהוֹר.** שֶׁהַקְּדֵרָה מַצֶּלֶת עַל הָאֳכָלִים. וְדַוְקָא כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא פֵרְשָׁה מִמֶּנָּה, אֲבָל אִם הִגְבִּיהָה שְׁתֵּי יָדֶיהָ מִמֶּנָּה וְחָזְרָה וְלָשָׁה, הֲרֵי זוֹ מְטַמְּאָה אֶת הַבָּצֵק:


והבצק טהור – for the pot protects over the foods. And especially all the while that she did not separate from it, but if she lifted up [her] two hands from it and returned and kneaded it, this defiles the dough.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:1)


**כְּלֵי גְלָלִים.** שֶׁל צְפִיעֵי בָקָר:


כלי גללים – of excrement of cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:2)


**כְּלֵי אֲבָנִים וּכְלֵי אֲדָמָה.** כָּל הָנָךְ כֵּלִים, אֲפִלּוּ בְיַד עַם הָאָרֶץ טְהוֹרִים. וְאִם מֻנָּחִים עַל פִּי אֲרֻבָּה, מַצִּילִין עַל הַכֹּל אֲפִלּוּ עַל כְּלֵי שֶׁטֶף:


כלי אבנים וכלי אדמה – al of these are vessels, even in the hand of illiterate people they are pure, and if they are placed upon the aperture in the roof looking to the ground fall, it protects over everything even on the vessels which require only rinsing to be restored to Levitical cleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:3)


**הָיָה כְלִי טָהוֹר לַקֹּדֶשׁ וְלַחַטָּאת.** דְּהֵמְנִינְהוּ רַבָּנָן לְעַמֵּי הָאֲרָצוֹת עַל הַקֹּדֶשׁ, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא כָּל אֶחָד הוֹלֵךְ וּבוֹנֶה בָּמָה לְעַצְמוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ הַכְּלִי הַטָּהוֹר לַקֹּדֶשׁ מַצִּיל עַל הַכֹּל אֲפִלּוּ לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי. וְכֵן הֵן נֶאֱמָנִין עַל הַחַטָּאת שֶׁל אֵפֶר פָּרָה, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְהָיְתָה לַעֲדַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת, הַכֹּל נֶאֱמָנִים עַל שְׁמִירָתָהּ:


היה כלי טהור לקודש ולחטאת – for the Rabbis appointed them to the illiterate people on the Holy Things, in order that each person would not go and build an altar for himself, therefore, the vessel that is pure for Holy Things protects on everything, even according to the School of Shammai, and similarly, they are deemed faithful on the sin-offering of the ashes of the Red Heifer, as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “[A man who is pure shall gather up the ashes of the cow and deposit them outside the camp in a pure place,] to be kept [for water of lustration] for the Israelite community,” everyone is believed on its preservation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:4)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַכֵּלִים טְהוֹרִים.** כְּגוֹן כְּלֵי גְּלָלִים וּכְלֵי אֲבָנִים וּכְלֵי אֲדָמָה שֶׁהֵן טְהוֹרִים מִלְּקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה. וְכֵן כְּלֵי חֶרֶס שֶׁהֵן טְהוֹרִין כְּשֶׁהֵן לַקֹּדֶשׁ וְלַחַטָּאת. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ, נֶאֱמָן, שֶׁעַם הָאָרֶץ נֶאֱמָן לַקֹּדֶשׁ וְלַחַטָּאת כְּדַאֲמַרַן.


מפני שהכלים טהורים – as for example, vessels of excrement and vessels of stones and vessels of earth which are pure from being susceptible to receive impurity. And similarly, earthenware vessels are pure when they are for Holy Things and for purification [water]. Even though they belong to an illiterate person, he is believed, for an illiterate person is believed for Holy Things and for purification [water] as we have stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:5:5)


**כָּל אֵלּוּ מַצִּילִין עִם דָּפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים.** כְּלוֹמַר חוֹצְצִין בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה כְּשֶׁהֵם מֻנָּחִים בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ דְּפָנוֹת לָאֹהָלִים, וְהַאי אֲרֻבָּה שֶׁבֵּין בַּיִת לָעֲלִיָּה כְּדָפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים חֲשִׁיבָא. אֲבָל בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין דְּפָנוֹת, אֵין מַצִּילִים, כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל:


כל אלו מצילין עם דפנות אוהלים – that is to say, they interpose in the face of defilement when they are placed in a place where there are walls of tents, and this aperture in the roof looking to the ground floor that is between the house and the upper chamber is considered like the walls of tents. But in a place where there are no walls, it does not protect, as will be explained further on (see the next Mishnah).



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:1)


**הַבּוֹר וְהַדּוּת שֶׁבַּבַּיִת.** וְהַמֵּת בַּבַּיִת וְכֵלִים בַּבּוֹר וּבַדּוּת, וְיֵשׁ גֹּבַהּ מְחִצּוֹת סְבִיבוֹתֵיהֶן. בּוֹר וְדוּת שְׁנֵיהֶן בַּקַּרְקַע אֶלָּא שֶׁבּוֹר בַּחֲפִירָה וְדוּת בְּבִנְיָן:


הבור והדות שבבית (a cistern and a subterranean masoned cellar that are in the house) – and the corpse is in the house and vessels are in the cistern and cellar, and there is someone who seizes the partitions that are around them. Both a בור/cistern and a דות/cellar are in the ground, but the cistern [is made] through digging and the cellar [is made] through building/masonry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:2)


**וּכְפִישָׁה.** סַל גָּדוֹל שֶׁכּוֹבְשִׁים בּוֹ הַזֵּיתִים לְכָמְרָן, וְהוּא מַחֲזִיק אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה אוֹ יוֹתֵר, וְאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, וּנְתוּנָה עַל פִּי הַבּוֹר וְהַדּוּת:


וכפישה (an inverted vessel divided into two compartments by the bottom between – put on it as a tight lid) – a large basket that they press/squeeze the olives to produce shrinking and maturing of fruits by underground storage/לכומרן and it holds forty Se’ah or more, and is not susceptible to receive ritual impurity and is placed on the cistern or on the cellar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:3)


**טָהוֹר.** כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָן, הוֹאִיל וְיֵשׁ שָׁם מְחִצּוֹת:


טהור – everything that is in them is [pure] since there are partitions there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:4)


**בְּאֵר חֲלָקָה.** שֶׁפִּי הַבְּאֵר שָׁוֶה לְקַרְקַע הַבַּיִת וְאֵין לוֹ מְחִצָּה סָבִיב, דְּהַשְׁתָּא לֵיכָּא דְפָנוֹת:


באר חלקה – that the mouth of the well is level to the ground of the house and there is no partition around it, for here there are no walls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:5)


**אוֹ כַוֶּרֶת פְּחוּתָה.** כְּגוֹן כַּוֶּרֶת גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁמַּחְזֶקֶת אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה, שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה. וּפְחוּתָה, שֶׁאֵינָהּ פְּקוּקָה בְקַשׁ:


או כוורת פחותה (a broken beehive) – as for example large beehive that holds forty Se’ah, that is not susceptible to receive ritual impurity. But it is broken, and is not stuffed up with straw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:6)


**וּכְפִישָׁה נְתוּנָה עָלֶיהָ.** עַל פִּי הַבּוֹר אוֹ עַל פִּי הַכַּוֶּרֶת:


וכפישה נתונה עליו – on the mouth of the cistern or on the mouth of the beehive.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:7)


**טָמֵא.** דְּאֵין מַצִּילִים אֶלָּא עִם דָּפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים, וּבִבְאֵר אֵין דְּפָנוֹת, וְדָפְנוֹת הַכַּוֶּרֶת לָאו דָּפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים הֵן:


טמא – for it does not protect other than with the walls of tents, but a cistern lacks walls, and the walls of a beehive are not the walls of tents.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:8)


**הָיָה נֶסֶר חָלָק.** דְּאֵין זֶה כְלִי:


היה נסר חלק (if it was a smooth board) – that tis is not a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:9)


**אוֹ סְרִידָה שֶׁאֵין לָהּ גַּפַּיִם.** שֶׁאֵין לָהּ מְחִצּוֹת, דְּלָא חֲשִׁיבָא כְלִי:


או סרידה שאין לה גפיים (or stuffed matting used for stoppers of stoves which lacks handles) -which have no partitions and it is not considered a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:10)


**טָהוֹר.** אִם נְתוּנִים עַל בְּאֵר חֲלָקָה אוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי כַּוֶּרֶת פְּחוּתָה, מַצִּילִים וְטָהוֹר, דְּדַוְקָא כְפִישָׁה שֶׁהִיא כְלִי אֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת אֶלָּא עִם דָּפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים, אֲבָל נֶסֶר וּסְרִידָה שֶׁאֵין לָהּ גַּפַּיִם, דְּלָאו כֵּלִים נִינְהוּ, מַצִּילִים בְּלֹא דָפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים. סְרִידָה, כְּמִין עֲרֵבָה קְטַנָּה שֶׁל עֵץ שֶׁהַנַּחְתּוֹם מִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהּ. גַּפַּיִם, אֳגָנִים וְאָזְנַיִם:


טהור – if they are placed on a smooth well or on a broken beehive, they protect and are ritually pure. For specifically, the inverted vessel divided into two compartments by the bottom between is a vessel but does not protect other than with the walls of tents, but a board and stuffed matting which lack handles, that are not vessels, protect without the walls of tents. A stuffed matting used for stoppers of stoves, is like a small wooden trough that the baker uses. גפניים/handles are anchors and handles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:6:11)


**חֲצִי טֶפַח מִכָּאן וַחֲצִי טֶפַח מִכָּאן.** הָא דְּאֵין כְּלִי מַצִּיל בִּבְאֵר חֲלָקָה הַיְנוּ בִכְלִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ דְפָנוֹת טֶפַח, אֲבָל אִם יֵשׁ לַכְּלִי דְּפָנוֹת טֶפַח מַצִּיל עַל הַבּוֹר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין דָּפְנוֹתָיו בּוֹלְטִים כְּלָל, וְהַשְׁתָּא קָאָמַר דַּחֲצִי טֶפַח דִּכְלִי וַחֲצִי טֶפַח דְּדָפְנֵי הַבּוֹר אֵין מִצְטָרְפִים. וּכְפִישָׁה דַּאֲמַרַן לְעֵיל שֶׁאִם הִיא נְתוּנָה עַל בְּאֵר חֲלָקָה אֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת מַיְרֵי שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ דְּפָנוֹת טֶפַח:


חצי טפח מכאן וחצי טפח מכאן – for a vessel does not protect in a smooth well, that is in a vessel which lack walls one handbreadth [high], but if the vessel has walls that are a handbreadth, it protects over the cistern even though its walls do not protrude at all, and now it is stated that half of handbreadth of a vessel and half of a handbreadth of the walls of a cistern do not combine. And the כפישה/(basket) inverted vessel divided into two parts by the bottom between where they squeeze the fruits that we mentioned above, that if it is placed on a smooth well, it does not protect, we are speaking that it doesn’t have walls [at the height of] a handbreadth.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:7:1)


**כְּשֵׁם שֶׁמַּצִּילִים מִבִּפְנִים.** עִם דָּפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים, כָּךְ מַצִּילִים מִבַּחוּץ עִם דָּפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים, כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל:


כשם שמצילים מבפנים – with the walls of tents, so they protect from the outside with walls of tents as I explain further on [in this Mishnah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:7:2)


**עַל יְתֵדוֹת מִבַּחוּץ.** סָמוּךְ לַכֹּתֶל, שֶׁלֹּא תְהֵא מְשׁוּכָה מִן הַכֹּתֶל [טֶפַח], וְאָז חֲשׁוּבָה אֹהֶל לְהַפְסִיק בֵּין טֻמְאָה שֶׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ לְכֵלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ. וְדַוְקָא בְכֹתֶל בַּיִת שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה כֹּתֶל זֶה לְאֹהֶל נֶחֱשֶׁבֶת הַכְּפִישָׁה גַם הִיא אֹהֶל לְהַפְסִיק, אֲבָל בְּכֹתֶל חָצֵר וְגִנָּה שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשָׂה לְאֹהֶל לֹא תֵחָשֵׁב הַכְּפִישָׁה אֹהֶל לְהַפְסִיק:


על יתדות מבחוץ – near the wall that will not be continuous from the wall [for a handbreadth] and then it is considered a tent to interrupt between the defilement that is underneath it to the vessels that are over it. And specifically with regard to the wall of the house that this wall was made into a tent, the basket/inverted vessel is considered a tent to interrupt, but the wall of a courtyard and/or a garden is not made into a tent, the basket is not considered a tent to interrupt [the flow of defilement].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/5:7:3)


**וּקְדֵרָה תְלוּיָה בָהּ.** מוּכָח בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא שֶׁאִם שִׂפְתֵי הַקְּדֵרָה מְשׁוּכִים מִשְּׂפַת הַקּוֹרָה וְלַחוּץ בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דְאֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת, וְכֵלִים שֶׁבַּקְּדֵרָה טְמֵאִים, וְאִי פִי הַקְּדֵרָה מֻדְבָּק בַּקּוֹרָה, כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דְמַצֶּלֶת וּטְהוֹרִים. כִּי פְלִיגֵי הֵיכָא דִמְשׁוּכָה מִן הַקּוֹרָה וְלֵיכָּא פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח. רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר מַצֶּלֶת וּטְהוֹרִים, וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת וּטְמֵאִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וקדירה תלויה בה – it is proven in the Tosefta {Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 6, Halakha 5 and 6) that if the rims of the pot are pulled from the rims of the beam and outward with an opening of a handbreadth, no one disagrees that it does not protect, and vessels that are in the pot are ritually impure, but the lip of the pot is attached to the beam, no one disagrees that it protects and is ritually pure. Where they disagree is where it is drawn from the beam and there isn’t an opening of a handbreadth. Rabbi Akiba says that it protects and they are pure, whereas the Sages state that it doesn’t protect and they are impure. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:1)


**אָדָם וְכֵלִים נַעֲשִׂין אֹהָלִים לְטַמֵּא.** אֹהֶל שֶׁנִּסְמָךְ עַל יְדֵי אָדָם אוֹ עַל יְדֵי כֵּלִים נֶחְשָׁב אֹהֶל לְטַמֵּא כֵלִים שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה, כְּאִלּוּ נְתוּנִים בְּאֹהֶל הַמֵּת:


אדם וכלים עשויין אהלים לטמא – a tent that is supported by means of a person or through vessels is considered a tent to defile vessels that are underneath it, even if does not correspond to the defilement, as if they are placed in the tent of the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא לְטַהֵר.** דְּאֵין נֶחְשָׁבִים אֹהֶל לַחוֹץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


אבל לא לטמא – for they are not considered a tent outside in the presence of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:3)


**נִדְבָּךְ.** אֶבֶן גְּדוֹלָה וּרְחָבָה. לְשׁוֹן נִדְבָּכִין דִּי אֶבֶן גְּלָל:


נדבך (a course of stones) – a large and wide stone. It is the language of (Ezra 6:4): נדבכין די-אבן גלל/”With a course of timber for each three courses of hewn stones.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:4)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו טְמֵאִין.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ וּכְמַאן דְּלֵיתֵיהּ דָּמֵי, דְּאֵין עָשׂוּי אֹהֶל לְטַהֵר:


כלים שעל גביו טמאין – that it doesn’t interpose and it is like the one who doesn’t hold this opinion, for a tent is not made to make pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:5)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו טְמֵאִים.** דִּלְטַמֵּא נַעֲשִׂים אֹהָלִים:


כלים שתחתיו טמאים – for tents are made to defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:6)


**וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְטַהֵר.** בְּתַרְוַיְהוּ. דְּסָבַר נַעֲשֶׂה אֹהֶל לְטַהֵר וְחוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ורבי אליעזר מטהר – in both, for he holds that a tent is made to purify and it interposes in the face of defilement. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:7)


**נָתוּן עַל אַרְבָּעָה כֵלִים.** אִם הָיָה הַנִּדְבָּךְ נָתוּן עַל אַרְבָּעָה כֵלִים, אֲפִלּוּ כְלֵי גְלָלִים שֶׁאֵין מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה:


נתון על ארבעה כלים – if the course of stones/נדבך was placed upon the four vessels even a vessel of dung that are not susceptible to receive ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:8)


**עַל אַרְבָּעָה אֲבָנִים.** שֶׁאֵינָן כֵּלִים, נֶחְשָׁב אֹהֶל לַחוֹץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


נתון על ארבעה אבנים – which are not vessels, it is considered as a tent to the outside in the face of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:1:9)


**דָּבָר שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים.** חוּץ מֵאָדָם:


דבר שיש בו רוח חיים – except for a human.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:1)


**אַחַד מֵהֶן.** מֵאוֹתָם הַמְלַוִּים אֶת הַמֵּת לְקָבְרוֹ וְלֹא מִנּוֹשְׂאֵי הַמִּטָּה. וְהוּא הַדִּין אִם הֵגִיף אֶת הַדֶּלֶת אָדָם אַחֵר, אֶלָּא אוֹרְחָא דְמִלְּתָא נָקַט:


אחד מהן – one of those who accompany the corpse to buy it and not from those who carry the bier. And the same law applies if another person shut the door, but it (i.e., the Mishnah) took the usual incident.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:2)


**הֵגִיף אֶת הַדֶּלֶת.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִכָּנֵס הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁיַּעַבְרוּ דֶרֶךְ אַכְסַדְרָה שֶׁלִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת, דְּיֵשׁ תִּקְרָה עַל גַּבָּהּ:


הגיף את הדלת – so that the defilement should not enter the house when they pass through the portico that is in front of the house, for there is a ceiling on top of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:3)


**אִם יָכוֹל הַדֶּלֶת לַעֲמֹד.** בְּלֹא סְמִיכַת מַפְתֵּחַ, הֲוָה לֵיהּ מַפְתֵּחַ כְּמַאן דְּלֵיתֵיהּ וְהַדֶּלֶת חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


אם יכולה הדלת לעמוד – without the support of the key, he has a key like one who lacks one and the door [itself] interposes in the face of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:4)


**וְאִם לָאו טָמֵא.** דְּכָל דָּבָר הַנִּסְמָךְ בְּכֵלִים אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה, כְּדַאֲמַרַן דְּאֵין אָדָם וְכֵלִים נַעֲשִׂים אֹהָלִים לְטַהֵר. וְהַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא דְתֶבֶן, וְהַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא דִגְרוֹגָרוֹת, וְהַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא דְטִיחַ, מִשּׁוּם דַּעֲמִידָתָן עַל יְדֵי כֵלִים שֶׁהֵם חָבִית וְקֻפָּה וְקַנְקַנִּים:


ואם לאו טמא – for every thing that is supported by vessels does not interpose in the face of the defilement. For as we stated (see Mishnah 1 of this chapter) a human and vessels are not made tents to purify. And that is the reason of straw, that is the reason of dried figs and that is the reason of plaster, because their standing through [the use of] vessels which are a wine jug, a basket and cylindrical vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:5)


**שֶׁהֵן נְתוּנִים בְּחַלּוֹן.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח שֶׁמֵּבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה מִבַּיִת לְבַיִת:


שהן נתונים בחלון – that the have a handbreadth by a handbreadth [measurement] that brings the defilement from house to house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:6)


**אִם יְכוֹלִים הַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת וְהַתֶּבֶן לַעֲמֹד בְּעַצְמָן.** בְּלֹא חָבִית וְקֻפָּה:


אם יכולים הגרגרות והתבן לעמוד בעצמן – without a jug or a basket.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:7)


**טְהוֹרִים.** שֶׁהַכֵּלִים כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֵינָן, וְהַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת וְהַתֶּבֶן מַצִּילִים. וְהוּא שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת שֶׁהִסְרִיחוּ וְאֵינָן רְאוּיוֹת לַאֲכִילָה, וְכֵן הַתֶּבֶן מֻסְרָח וְאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לַאֲכִילַת בְּהֵמָה וּמְבַטֵּל לְהוּ וְאֵין דַּעְתּוֹ לְפַנּוֹתָן, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי חַיְצֵי, דְּכָל הֵיכָא דְאֵין הַתֶּבֶן וְהַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת סוֹתְמִים הַכֹּל, כְּגוֹן הָכָא דְאִיכָּא מְקוֹם קֻפָּה וְחָבִית, אִי לֹא מְבַטֵּל לְהוּ לֹא חַיְצֵי:


טהורים – that the vessels are like they are not, and the dried figs and straw protect, and this is what will be with the dried figs that decayed and are not appropriate for eating, and similarly, decayed straw which is not appropriate for the eating b cattle and he nullifies them, and it is not his intention to remove them. And because of this, they interpose, for all where the straw and the dried figs close up everything, as for example here where there is a place for a wine jug or a basket, if he doesn’t nullify them, they do not interpose.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:8)


**וְאִם לָאו טְמֵאִין.** וּבִלְבַד שֶׁתְּהֵא הֶחָבִית אִם הִיא שֶׁל חֶרֶס פִּיהָ כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה וּלְפִיכָךְ אֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת, שֶׁכָּל דָּבָר הַמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה. אֲבָל אִם גַּב הֶחָבִית כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה, חוֹצֶצֶת בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה, שֶׁאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה מִגַּבּוֹ:


ואם לאו טמאין – as long as the wine jug will be if it made of earthenware that its mouth will correspond to the defilement and therefore it does not protect, for every thing that is susceptible to receive ritual impurity does not interpose in the face of the defilement. But if the back of the wine jug corresponds to the defilement, it interposes in the face of the defilement, for an earthenware vessel is not susceptible to receive defilement from its back.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:9)


**בַּיִת שֶׁחֲצָצוֹ בְקַנְקַנִּים.** וּפִיהֶן כְּלַפֵּי הַטֻּמְאָה, דְּעַכְשָׁיו אֵין מַצִּילִים:


בית שחצצו בקנקנים – and their mouths are towards the defilement, for now they do not protect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:10)


**וְטָח בְּטִיחַ.** בֵּין מִבִּפְנִים בֵּין מִבַּחוּץ:


וטח בטיח – whether from the inside or whether from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:2:11)


**אִם יָכוֹל הַטִּיחַ לַעֲמֹד בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ טָהוֹר.** שֶׁהַטִּיחַ חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


אם יכול הטיח לעמוד בפני עצמו טהור – that the plaster interposes in the face of the defilement.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:1)


**כֹּתֶל שֶׁהוּא לָאֲוִיר.** שֶׁצִּדּוֹ אֶחָד אֲוִיר הָעוֹלָם, וְצִדּוֹ הָאַחֵר מְשַׁמֵּשׁ אֶת הַבַּיִת, כְּגוֹן כֹּתֶל שֶׁמַּפְסִיק בֵּין בַּיִת לֶחָצֵר אוֹ בֵין בַּיִת לַגִּנָּה:


כותל שהוא לאויר – that its one side is the airspace of the world, and its other side serves the house, as for example, the wall that separates between the house and the courtyard or between the house and its garden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:2)


**וְהַטֻּמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ.** שֶׁנִּסְדַּק הַכֹּתֶל וְיֵשׁ טֻמְאָה בַּחֲלָלוֹ בְּתוֹךְ הַסֶּדֶק:


וטומאה בתוכו – that the wall was split/chipped and there is defilement in its cavity within the split/chip.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:3)


**מֵחֶצְיוֹ וְלִפְנִים.** אִם הַטֻּמְאָה מִן הַחֲצִי שֶׁל כֹּתֶל לְצַד הַבַּיִת:


מחציו ולפנים – if the defilement is from the half of the wall to the side of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:4)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** כְּאִלּוּ הָיְתָה בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת, דְּהַהוּא פַלְגָּא בָּתַר בַּיִת שָׁדִינַן לֵיהּ. וְהָעוֹמֵד בַּכֹּתֶל לְמַעְלָה אֲפִלּוּ עוֹמֵד כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה:


הבית טמא – as it was within the house. And this half follows the house that we cast. But the person who stands at the wall from above even standing facing the defilement,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:5)


**טָהוֹר.** דְּלָאו כְּטֻמְאָה רְצוּצָה חֲשִׁיבָה, וְאֵינָהּ בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה, דְּהָא חָשְׁבִינַן לַהּ כְּמֻנַּחַת בָּאֹהֶל:


he is pure, for it is not considered like pressed ritual impurity/טומאה רצוצה, and it doesn’t break through and ascend, for we consider it as it is lying/placed in the tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:6)


**מֵחֶצְיוֹ וְלַחוּץ.** לְצַד הָאֲוִיר, לֹא שָׁדִינַן לֵיהּ בָּתַר בַּיִת, וּלְפִיכָךְ הָעוֹמֵד מִלְּמַעְלָה טָמֵא כֵּיוָן דְּלָאו כְּמֻנַּחַת בָּאֹהֶל הִיא הֲרֵי הִיא טֻמְאָה רְצוּצָה וּבוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה:


מחציו ולחוץ – to the side of the airspace, we do not cast it after the house, and therefore, a person who stands from above is impure, because he is not like he is lying/placed in the tent, and this is pressed ritual impurity which breaks through and ascends.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:7)


**מֶחֱצָה לְמֶחֱצָה.** שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בְּאֶמְצַע:


מחצה למחצה – and the defilement is in the middle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:8)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר מְטַמֵּא.** דְּשָׁדִינַן לֵיהּ לַכֹּתֶל בָּתַר בַּיִת וּבָתַר אֲוִיר לְחֻמְרָא:


רבי מאיר מטמא – that we cast it to the wall after the house and after the airspace as a stringency.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:9)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** דִּלְגַמְרֵי שָׁדִינַן לֵיהּ בָּתַר בַּיִת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרין – that we completely cast it after the house. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:3:10)


**כָּל הַכֹּתֶל לַבָּיִת.** אֲפִלּוּ עוֹמֶדֶת הַטֻּמְאָה בַּחֲצִי שֶׁל צַד הָאֲוִיר הֲרֵי הִיא כְאִלּוּ מֻנַּחַת בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת:


כל הכתל לבית – even if the defilement stands half of on the side of the airspace, it is as if it is ling within the house (according to Rabbi Yehuda).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:4:1)


**כֹּתֶל שֶׁבֵּין שְׁנֵי בָתִּים.** כֹּתֶל הַמַּפְסִיק בֵּין שְׁנֵי בָתִּים:


כותל שבין שני בתים- and the wall that forms partition/separates between two houses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:4:2)


**טֻמְאָה בְאַחַד מֵהֶן.** בְּאֶחָד מִן הַבָּתִּים:


טומאה באחד מהן – one of the houses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:4:3)


**מַעֲזִיבָה.** תִּקְרָה הַמַּפְסֶקֶת בֵּין בַּיִת לָעֲלִיָּה:


מעזיבה (a concrete of stone chippings/clay covering the ceiling of the lower story and serving as flooring of the upper story) - a ceiling that separates between the house and the upper chamber.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:4:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר וֽכוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר וכו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:1)


**טֻמְאָה בֵין הַקּוֹרוֹת.** בֵּין קוֹרוֹת הַתִּקְרָה שֶׁהַנְּסָרִים עֲלֵיהֶן וְהַמַּעֲזִיבָה עַל גַּבָּן. וּמַתְנִיתִין כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָמַר כָּל מַעֲזִיבָה לָעֲלִיָּה. וְהַשְׁתָּא קָאָמַר שֶׁאִם הָיְתָה טֻמְאָה בַּקּוֹרוֹת שֶׁתַּחַת הַמַּעֲזִיבָה וּמְחִצָּה שֶׁהִיא דַקָּה כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם חוֹצֶצֶת בֵּין הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת, שֶׁאֵין הַטֻּמְאָה נִרְאֵית בַּבַּיִת: **אִם יֶשׁ בִּמְקוֹם הַטֻּמְאָה פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** הַיְנוּ שֶׁיֵּשׁ חֲלַל טֶפַח בִּמְקוֹם הַטֻּמְאָה:


טומאה בין הקורות – between the beams of the ceiling that there are boards on them and the concrete of stone chippings/clay covering the ceiling of the lower story and serving as flooring of the upper story/מעזיבה of their back. And our Mishnah is according to Rabbi Yehuda who stated: All of the concrete of stone chippings/clay are with the upper chamber (see previous Mishnah). And now it states that if there was defilement in the beams that are underneath of the concrete of stone chippings/clay, and a partition that is thin like the peel of garlic, it interposes between the defilement to the house so that the defilement is not seen in the house. אם יש במקום הטומאה פותח טפח – that is the that there is a cavity of one-handbreadth in the place of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:2)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** הַבַּיִת וְהָעֲלִיָּה:


הכל טמא – the house and the upper chamber.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:3)


**רוֹאִים אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה כְּאִלּוּ הִיא אֹטֶם.** וְאֵין יוֹצְאָה הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת אֶלָּא בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה:


רואים את הטומאה כאילו היא אוטם (we regard the Levitical uncleanness – between the rafters – as if it was locked up/an obstruction) – and the defilement does not descend to the house but rather bursts through and ascends/rises.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:4)


**הָיְתָה נִרְאֵית לְתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת.** שֶׁאֵין תַּחְתֶּיהָ כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם:


היתה נראית לתוך החבית – that there isn’t under it like the peel of garlic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:5:5)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** בֵּין יֵשׁ בָּהּ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח בֵּין אֵין בָּהּ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


בין כך ובין כך – whether there is an opening of a handbreadth wide or whether there isn’t an opening of a handbreadth wide.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:1)


**בַּיִת הַמְשַׁמֵּשׁ אֶת הַכֹּתֶל.** הַמְּעָרוֹת שֶׁתַּחַת הַקַּרְקַע כְּשֶׁחוֹפְרִים כּוּךְ לְמֵת מִצַּד זֶה וְכוּךְ לְמֵת מִצַּד הַשֵּׁנִי נִמְצָא הַכֹּתֶל עָשׂוּי מֵאֵלָיו, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָרֵי לֵיהּ בַּיִת הַמְשַׁמֵּשׁ אֶת הַכֹּתֶל, שֶׁעַל יְדֵי הַחֲפִירָה נַעֲשָׂה הַכֹּתֶל, אֲבָל בִּנְיָן שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע, הַכֹּתֶל גּוֹרֵם לַבַּיִת שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בַּיִת, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָרֵי לֵיהּ [לְעֵיל מִשְׁנָה ג'] כֹּתֶל הַמְשַׁמֵּשׁ אֶת הַבַּיִת:


בית המשמש את הכותל – the caves that are under the street when they dig a sepulchral chamber for a corpse from this side and a sepulchral chamber for a corpse from the other side, it is found that the wall is made of its own, and because of this, we call it a house which serves the wall that through the digging, the wall was made, but building that is upon the ground, the wall causes the house that it will be a house, and because of this, we call it [see above, Mishnah 3], “a wall that serves the house”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:2)


**טֻמְאָה בַבָּתִּים.** בַּכּוּכִין וּבַמְּחִלּוֹת:


טומאה בבתים – in the sepulchral chambers and in the cavities/caves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:3)


**וַעֲלֵיהֶן כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם טְהוֹרִין.** דְּמַפְסִיק לַטֻּמְאָה, וְאֵין דִּינָם כְּדִין כֹּתֶל שֶׁבֵּין שְׁנֵי בָתִּים:


ועליהן כקליפת השום טהורין – because they form a partition to the defilement and their law is not like the law of a wall that is between two houses (see Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:4)


**טֻמְאָה תַחַת הָעַמּוּד.** אִם יֵשׁ עַמּוּד בְּתוֹךְ הַכּוּךְ אוֹ בְתוֹךְ הַמְּעָרָה וְטֻמְאָה תַחְתָּיו וְהִיא רוֹצֶצֶת שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח:


טומאה בוקעת ועולה בוקעת ויורדת – and all that is juxtaposed to the defilement above and/or below is impure according to the law of all covered defilement, but from the sides it is pure, and the same law applies to the pillar that is in the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:6:5)


**טֻמְאָה בוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה בּוֹקַעַת וְיוֹרָדֶת.** וְכָל שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה לְמַעְלָה וּלְמַטָּה טָמֵא כְדִין כָּל טֻמְאָה מְכֻסָּה, וּמִן הַצְּדָדִים טָהוֹר, וְהוּא הַדִּין לְעַמּוּד שֶׁבַּבַּיִת:



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:1)


**פֶּרַח.** דָּבָר בּוֹלֵט וְרָחָב שֶׁיּוֹצֵא בְּרֹאשׁ הָעַמּוּד אוֹ בְאֶמְצָעוֹ:


פרח - a thing that protrudes and is wide that comes out at the top of a column/pillar or in its middle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:2)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחַת הַפֶּרַח טְהוֹרִים.** הָכָא אַיְרֵי בְעַמּוּד הָעוֹמֵד בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אוֹ בְּגִנָּה וְטֻמְאָה תַחְתָּיו, וְהַפֶּרַח בּוֹלֵט מִן הָעַמּוּד טֶפַח לַחוּץ וּמַאֲהִיל עַל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו:


כלים שתחת הפרח טהורים – here we are speaking of a column that stands in the public domain or in a garden and the defilement is underneath it, and the “capital” protrudes from the column outward a handbreadth and overshadows over the vessels that are under it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:3)


**טְהוֹרִים.** דְּטֻמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה, וְכָל שֶׁבַּצְּדָדִים טָהוֹר:


טהורים – for the defilement bursts forth and ascends, but all that is on the sides is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי מְטַמֵּא.** דְּפֶרַח כָּעַמּוּד, וּכְשֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת חוֹזֶרֶת עַל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁהַפֶּרַח מַאֲהִיל עֲלֵיהֶם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי:


רבי יוחנן בן נורי מטמא – for the “capital” is like the column/pillar, and when the defilement bursts forth, it returns on all the vessels that the “capital” overshadows upon them. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:5)


**הַטֻּמְאָה וְהַכֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחַת הַפֶּרַח.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהַפֶּרַח מַאֲהִיל עַל הַטֻּמְאָה וְעַל הַכֵּלִים:


הטומאה והכלים שתחת הפרח – as for example, that the “capital” overshadows on the defilement and on the vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:6)


**פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** שֶׁבּוֹלֵט הַפֶּרַח חוּץ לָעַמּוּד טֶפַח:


פותח טפח – that the “capital” protrudes outside pf the column a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:7)


**פַּרְדַּסְקִים.** עַמּוּדִים חֲלוּלִים נְתוּנִים בְּכֹתֶל הַבַּיִת וּבָהֶן חַלּוֹנוֹת חַלּוֹנוֹת וְיֵשׁ לָהֶן דְּלָתוֹת, וְטֻמְאָה תַּחַת אוֹתָן עַמּוּדִים:


פרדסקים (closet, chests in the wall) – empty pillars that are placed I the wall of the house and in them are many windows/apertures and they have doors, and the defilement is under those pillars.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:8)


**נִפְתַּח אַחַד מֵהֶן הוּא וְהַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת בְּתוֹכוֹ וְיוֹצְאָה לַבַּיִת. וַאֲפִלּוּ הַטֻּמְאָה מֵחֶצְיוֹ שֶׁל כֹּתֶל וְלַחוּץ דְּאֵינוֹ נִדּוֹן כַּבַּיִת. אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי הַבַּיִת טָמֵא:


נפתח אחד מהן הבית טמא – that the defilement bursts through within and goes out to the house, And even the defilement from the half of the wall and outward is not judged to be like the house. Nevertheless, the house is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 6:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/6:7:9)


**וְרוֹאִין אֶת הַפַּרְדַּסְקִין כְּאִלּוּ הֵן אֹטֶם.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם הַדֶּלֶת נָעוּל וְלֹא נִפְתָּח רוֹאִין אוֹתָן כְּאִלּוּ אֵין שָׁם חָלָל כְּלָל, וְאִם טֻמְאָה תַחְתֵּיהֶן נִדּוֹן הַכֹּתֶל מֶחֱצָה עַל מֶחֱצָה, כְּכָל הָנֵי דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל מֵחֶצְיוֹ וְלִפְנִים הַבַּיִת טָמֵא מֵחֶצְיוֹ וּלְחוּץ הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר:
