ורואין את הפרדסקין כאילו הן אוטם – that is to say, if the door is locked and it is not opened, we view it as if there is no cavity there at all, but if the defilement is underneath them, the wall is judged half by half, like all of these that are taught about in the Mishnah above (i.e., in the previous Mishnayot of this chapter), from its middle and inwards, the house is impure; from its middle and outward, the house is pure.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:1)


**הַטֻּמְאָה.** שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ. שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַכֹּתֶל:


הטומאה. שעל גבה -that are on top of the wall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:2)


**טְמֵאוֹת.** שֶׁהַכֹּתֶל מְשַׁמֵּשׁ לָהֶן, וְרָאשֵׁי קוֹרוֹת הָעֲלִיָּה תְּחוּבוֹת בַּכֹּתֶל וְהַכֹּתֶל גָּבוֹהַּ וְהָוֵי מְחִצָּה לְכָל הָעֲלִיּוֹת שֶׁזּוֹ לְמַעְלָה מִזּוֹ. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁמְּקוֹם הַטֻּמְאָה טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח, דְּנֶחְשָׁב כָּל הַכֹּתֶל כְּקֶבֶר סָתוּם וּמֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לְכָל הָעֲלִיּוֹת, אֲבָל אֵין שָׁם טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח, בּוֹקַעַת כְּנֶגְדָּהּ, וְהַצְּדָדִים טְהוֹרִים. וְאֵין הַכֹּתֶל נִדּוֹן מֶחֱצָה עַל מֶחֱצָה כֵּיוָן דַּאֲפִלּוּ סֶדֶק לֵיכָּא לְצַד הָעֲלִיּוֹת:


טמאות – for the wall serves them, and the heads of he beams of the upper room are inserted/stuck in the wall and he wall is high and it is a partition for all of the upper rooms for this one is higher than that one. And especially when the place of defilement is a handbreadth by a handbreadth, for all of the wall is considered like a sealed grave and it brings the defilement to all of the upper rooms. But the wall is not judged half by half because it doesn’t have even a chip/split to the side of the upper room.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:3)


**עֲלִיָּה אַחַת בְּנוּיָה עַל גַּבֵּי שְׁנֵי בָתִּים.** [שֶׁכֹּתֶל זֶה מַפְסִיק בֵּין שְׁנֵי הַבָּתִּים] וַעֲלִיָּה בְּנוּיָה עַל גַּבֵּי שְׁנֵיהֶם:


עליה אחת בנויה על גבי שני בתים – [that this wall forms a partition between the two houses] and he upper room is built on top of both of them (i.e., the houses).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:4)


**הִיא טְמֵאָה.** עֲלִיָּה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה, שֶׁהַכֹּתֶל הַהוּא מַגִּיעַ עַד הַמַּעֲזִיבָה וְהַטֻּמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת בָּהּ:


היא טמאה – the first upper room, that this wall reaches until the concrete of stone chippings covering the ceiling of the lower story and serving as a flooring for the upper story but the defilement bursts forth and ascends in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:5)


**וְכָל הָעֲלִיּוֹת שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ טְהוֹרוֹת.** דְּמַעֲזִיבָה שֶׁעָלֶיהָ חוֹצֶצֶת:


וכל העליות שעל גבה טהורות – for the concrete of stone chippings covering the ceiling of the lower story and serving as flooring for the upper story/מעזיבה that is upon it interposes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:6)


**כֹּתֶל הַשּׁוּנִית.** כֹּתֶל הַבָּנוּי בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁהַיָּם מַגִּיעַ שָׁם בְּעֵת שֶׁהוֹלֵךְ וְסוֹעֵר:


כותל השונית – a wall that is built in the place that sea reaches at the time that it continually stirs up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:7)


**טֻמְאָה בוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה.** אִם יֵשׁ טֻמְאָה תַחְתָּיו אֵין הַכֹּתֶל הַהוּא מְטַמֵּא כָּל סְבִיבָיו, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין שָׁם חֲלַל טֶפַח:


טומאה בוקעת ועולה – if there is defilement underneath it, this wall does not defile all of its surroundings, because there is no cavity of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:8)


**נֶפֶשׁ אֲטוּמָה.** צִיּוּן שֶׁבּוֹנִים עַל הַקֶּבֶר, וְהוּא אָטוּם שֶׁבָּנוּי עַל הַטֻּמְאָה עַצְמָהּ וְאֵין הַמֵּת קָבוּר בְּאָרוֹן כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בוֹ חֲלַל טֶפַח:


נפש אטומה (a solid tomb-structure – to which there is no access) -a marker that they build on the grave to indicate the neighborhood of an unclean place, and that which is filled up is built upon the defilement itself but the corpse is not buried in bier in order that there will have a cavity of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:9)


**כְּקֶבֶר סָתוּם.** דִּמְטַמֵּא כָל סְבִיבָיו בְּמַגָּע:


כקבר סתום – that defiles all of its surroundings through contact.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:10)


**סָמַךְ לָהּ סֻכּוֹת.** אַנֶּפֶשׁ אֲטוּמָה דִּלְעֵיל קָאֵי, דְּאָמְרִינַן הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהּ מִן הַצְּדָדִים טָהוֹר, וְאִם עָשָׂה לְצִיּוּן זֶה הָאָטוּם סֻכּוֹת כְּעֵין מַשְׁקוֹף, הַסֻּכָּה הַזּוֹ טְמֵאָה, לְפִי שֶׁהִיא דוֹמָה לְאֹהֶל הַמֵּת:


סמך לה סוכות – it refers to the solid tomb-structure/נפש אטומה referred to above, that we state that a person who comes in contact with it from the sides is ritually pure, but if he made for this marking put up to indicate the neighborhood of an unclean place Sukkot similar to a lintel, this Sukkah is impure, because it is similar to the tent of a corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:1:11)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְטַהֵר.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִיא מִן הַצְּדָדִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ור' יהודה מטהר – since it is from the sides. Bu the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:1)


**כָּל שִׁפּוּעֵי אֹהָלִים כָּאֹהָלִים.** שֶׁאִם הָיְתָה טֻמְאָה תַּחַת הַשִּׁפּוּעַ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ תַּחַת הַגַּג שֶׁל הָאֹהֶל, טָמֵא, כְּאִלּוּ הָיְתָה מֻנַּחַת תַּחַת הַגַּג:


כל שפועי אהלים כאהלים – that if there was defilement underneath the slope even though it is not under the roof of the tent, it is impure, as if it was placed under the roof.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:2)


**וְכָלֶה עַד כְּאֶצְבַּע.** מִלְּמַעְלָה לְצַד הַגַּג. דְּלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן בְּאֹהֶל שֶׁל טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא שֶׁיְּהֵא בְתוֹכוֹ אֲוִיר כְּשִׁעוּר טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח מְרֻבָּע:


וכלה עד כאצבע (and finished off at one fingerbreadth) – from above to the side of the roof. That we aren’t concerned regarding a tent of defilement but rather that its inside will have the airspace like the measure of one cubic handbreadth squared.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:3)


**טֻמְאָה בָאֹהֶל.** רֹחַב הַגַּג שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְשֻׁפָּע קָרוּי אֹהֶל:


טומאה באוהל – the width of the roof that is not sloped is called a tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:4)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בּוֹ מִתּוֹכוֹ וֽכוּ'.** וְטַעֲמָא דְצַד פְּנִימִי שֶׁל אֹהֶל וְצַד חִיצוֹן שֶׁלּוֹ כִּשְׁנֵי כֵלִים חֲשִׁיב לְהוּ, וְצַד שֶׁכְּלַפֵּי הַטֻּמְאָה כִּכְלִי הַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּמֵּת עַצְמוֹ, וְצַד שֵׁנִי כְּכֵלִים הַנּוֹגְעִים בְּכֵלִים. הִלְכָּךְ, טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ, הַנּוֹגֵעַ מִתּוֹכוֹ טָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, דַּהֲוֵי לֵיהּ אָדָם שֶׁנָּגַע בְּכֵלִים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בְמֵת דִּתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק קַמָּא דְּטָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה:


הנוגע בו מתוכו וכו' – that the inner side of the tent and its outer side are considered like two vessels. But the side that is directed towards/opposite the defilement is like a vessel that comes in contact with the corpse itself, and the second side is like vessels that come in contact with vessels. Therefore, defilement that is within, a person who comes in contact/touches from the inside, has ritual impurity for seven days, for he is a person that touched vessels that came in contact with a corpse as it is taught in the Mishnah in the first chapter (see Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 3) that he has ritual impurity for seven days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:5)


**אֲבָל הַנּוֹגֵעַ מֵאֲחוֹרָיו טָמֵא טֻמְאַת עָרֶב.** כְּדִתְנַן (לעיל פ"א מ"ב) כֵּלִים בְּכֵלִים טְמֵאִים טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, בֵּין אָדָם בֵּין כֵּלִים טְמֵאִים טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב. וּבְטֻמְאָה מֵאֲחוֹרָיו הָוֵי אִפְּכָא, דְּצַד חִיצוֹן כְּכֵלִים הַנּוֹגְעִים בְּמֵת, וְצַד פְּנִימִי כְּכֵלִים בְּכֵלִים. וְטַעֲמָא דְצַד פְּנִימִי וְצַד חִיצוֹן נֶחְשָׁבִים כִּשְׁנֵי כֵלִים, דִּכְשֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ כֵּיוָן דְּמַצִּיל מִלִּהְיוֹת הַטֻּמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה אֵין לוֹ כֹּחַ לְטַמֵּא הַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּחוּץ טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, וּכְשֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בַּחוּץ כֵּיוָן דְּמַצִּיל מִלִּהְיוֹת הַטֻּמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת וְיוֹרֶדֶת אֵין לוֹ כֹּחַ לַפְּנִימִי לְטַמֵּא הַנּוֹגֵעַ בּוֹ טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה. וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם הָאֹהֶל עַצְמוֹ בֵּין בִּפְנִים בֵּין בַּחוּץ טָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, דַּהֲוֵי לֵיהּ כֵּלִים בְּכֵלִים. וְכֻלַּהּ מַתְנִיתִין מַיְרֵי לְאַחַר שֶׁיָּצָא הַמֵּת מִן הָאֹהֶל:


אל הנוגע מאחוריו טמא טומאת ערב – as is taught in the Mishnah regarding vessels [that come in contact] with impure vessels are defiled with ritual impurity for seven days. The third, whether a person or vessels is unclean with defilement that passes in the evening (see Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 2). But regarding the defilement from the outside, it is the opposite, for the outer side is like vessels that come in contact with a corpse, and the inner side is like vessels that come in contact with [impure] vessels. And the reason that the is that the inner side and the outer side are considered like two vessels, for the defilement that is within it, because it protects from being the defilement bursting forth and ascending, it lacks the power/strength to defile the person who touches it outside a ritual impurity of seven days, for when the defilement that is outside, since it protects from becoming the defilement that breaks forth and descends, it lacks the strength/power for the inside to defile a person who comes in contact with it for a defilement of seven days. But anyway, the tent itself, whether inside or whether outside is impure a ritual impurity of seven days, for it is like vessels [coming in contact] with [unclean] vessels, and all of our Mishnah is speaking about after the corpse has departed from the tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:6)


**כַּחֲצִי זַיִת מִתּוֹכוֹ וְכַחֲצִי זַיִת מֵאֲחוֹרָיו.** הָאֹהֶל טָמֵא מִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, שֶׁמִּצְטָרֵף חֲצִי זַיִת שֶׁבְּתוֹכוֹ עִם הַחֲצִי זַיִת שֶׁבַּאֲחוֹרָיו. אֲבָל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בֵּין מִתּוֹכוֹ בֵּין מֵאֲחוֹרָיו אֵין בּוֹ אֶלָּא טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב, דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֵיכָּא כַּזַּיִת בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְכָל צַד לֵחָשֵׁב אֶלָּא כְּכֵלִים בְּכֵלִים, הִלְכָּךְ הַנּוֹגֵעַ טָמֵא טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב:


כחצי זית מתוכו וכחצי זית מאחוריו – Whichever way you turn, the tent is impure a defilement of seven days, for the half of an olive’s bulk that is inside combines with a half of an olive’s bulk from the outside. But a person who comes in contact/touches whether from the inside or from the outside, he has naught other than ritual impurity until nightfall, for since there isn’t an olive’s bulk in one place, it is impossible for each side to be considered other than vessels [coming into contact] with unclean vessels, therefore, the person who comes in contact is impure until nightfall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:7)


**מִקְצָתוֹ מְרֻדָּד עַל הָאָרֶץ.** לְאַחַר שֶׁפֵּרְשׂוּ הָאֹהֶל, סֶרַח הָעוֹדֵף בִּירִיעוֹת הָאֹהֶל פֵּרְשׂוּ אוֹתָם בָּאָרֶץ:


מקצתו מרודד על הארץ ((part of the tent canvas is flat on the ground) – after they explained the tent, they explained the overlapping excess of the curtains of the tent [that are spread] on the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:8)


**מְרֻדָּד.** תַּרְגּוּם וַיְרַקְּעוּ אֶת פַּחֵי הַזָּהָב (שמות לט) וְרַדִּידוּ:


מרודד – it is the Aramaic translation of (Exodus 39:3): “They hammered out sheets of gold” – beaten, hammered metal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:9)


**טֻמְאָה תַחְתָּיו.** תַּחַת אוֹתוֹ רִדּוּד:


טומאה תחתיו – the defilement that is under that beaten, hammered metal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:10)


**אוֹ עַל גַּבָּיו.** עַל גַּבֵּי הָרִדּוּד:


או על גביו – on top of the beaten, hammered metal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:11)


**בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה בּוֹקַעַת וְיוֹרָדֶת.** וְנִטְמָא הָאֹהֶל. אֲבָל הַנִּכְנָס לְתוֹכוֹ טָהוֹר אִם לֹא נָגַע בּוֹ:


בוקעת ועולה בוקעת ויורדת – and the tent is defiled. But a person who enters inside of it is ritually pure if he didn’t come in contact with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:12)


**מִקְצָתוֹ.** שֶׁל אֹהֶל:


מקצתו – of the tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:13)


**מְרֻדָּד עַל אֲרֻבָּה שֶׁבֵּין בַּיִת לָעֲלִיָּה.** וְטֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת:


מרודד על ארובה שבית בית לעליה – and the defilement is in the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:14)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר מַצִּיל.** עַל הָעֲלִיָּה:


רבי יוסי אומר מציל – upon the upper chamber/room.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:2:15)


**כִּנְטִיַּת הָאֹהֶל.** שֶׁהָאֲרֻבָּה בַּאֲוִיר הָאֹהֶל, אָז מַצִּיל עַל כָּל הָעֲלִיָּה שֶׁחוּץ לָאֹהֶל:


כנטיית אוהל -that the aperture in the roof looking to the ground floor is in the airspace of the tent, then it protects over the entire upper chamber that is outside of the tent.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:1)


**כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִים.** כָּל הַכֵּלִים הַמֻּנָּחִים תַּחַת הַמַּשְׁקוֹף שֶׁל פֶּתַח בַּחֲלַל הַפְּתָחִים מִבַּחוּץ כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִים אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַפְּתָחִים נְעוּלִים, דְּגָזְרוּ חֲכָמִים טֻמְאָה עַל הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁהוּא דֶּרֶךְ יְצִיאַת טֻמְאָה, שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ שֶׁל מֵת לָצֵאת דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם:


כולן טמאים – all of the vessels that are lying underneath the lintel of the opening in the empty space of the openings from the outside, all are ritually impure, even though that the openings are locked, for the Sages decreed defilement/impurity on the place which is the path for the removal of the defilement, for ultimately the corpse will leave through that path.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:2)


**חָשַׁב לְהוֹצִיאוֹ בְאַחַד מֵהֶן.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִפְתַּח:


חשב להוציאו באחד מהן – even though it was not opened.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:3)


**אוֹ בְחַלּוֹן שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה.** דִּבְהָכִי שִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים שֶׁרָאוּי לְהוֹצָאַת הַמֵּת:


או בחלון שיש בו ארבעה – that through this the Sages estimated that it is appropriate for the removal of the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:4)


**הִצִּיל עַל כָּל הַפְּתָחִים.** הַנְּעוּלִים:


הציל על כל הפתחים – that are locked.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:5)


**יַחְשֹׁב עַד שֶׁלֹּא יָמוּת הַמֵּת.** דְּמִשֶּׁיָּרְדָה תּוֹרַת טֻמְאָה לַפְּתָחִים בְּמִיתַת הַמֵּת שׁוּב אֵינָהּ עוֹלָה מֵהֶן בְּמַחֲשָׁבָה אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי מַעֲשֶׂה כְגוֹן נִפְתַּח:


יחשוב עד שלא ימות המת – that from when the designation of defilement descended to the openings through the death of the corpse, it further does not ascend from them through intention but rather through action, such as when it is opened.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:6)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אַף מִשֶּׁמֵּת.** דְּמַחְשָׁבָה מַפְקַעַת תּוֹרַת טֻמְאָה מִן הַפְּתָחִים מִכָּאן וּלְהַבָּא. אֲבָל כֵּלִים שֶׁהָיוּ בַּפְּתָחִים קֹדֶם מַחְשָׁבָה מוֹדוּ בֵית הִלֵּל שֶׁהֵם טְמֵאִים:


ובית הלל אומרים אף משמת – for intention releases the designation of defilement from the openings from here and onwards. But vessels that were in the openings prior to the intention, the School of Hillel agrees that they are impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:7)


**הָיָה.** אֶחָד מִן הַפְּתָחִים סָתוּם בַּאֲבָנִים:


היה – one of the openings was blocked up with stones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:8)


**סתום.** באבנים:


סתום – with stones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:9)


**כְּשֶׁיִּפְתַּח אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים.** הֻצְּלוּ שְׁאָר פְּתָחִים:


כשיפתח ד' טפחים – the rest of the openings were protected.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:10)


**כְּשֶׁיַּתְחִיל.** לִפְתֹּחַ מִיָּד הֻצְּלוּ שְׁאָר פְּתָחִים:


כשיתחיל – to open them, the remainder of the openings were protected.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:3:11)


**בְּפוֹתֵחַ בַּתְּחִלָּה.** כְּגוֹן כֹּתֶל שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ פֶּתַח וּבָא לַעֲשׂוֹת פֶּתַח חָדָשׁ, לֹא הֻצְּלוּ הַפְּתָחִים עַד שֶׁיִּפְתַּח אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה:


בפותח תחלה – as for example a wall that lacks an opening and one comes to make a new opening, the other openings are not protected until he opens it four [handbreadths] by four [handbreadths].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:4:1)


**וְהוֹצִיאוּהָ מִבַּיִת לְבַיִת.** וּבְבַיִת שֵׁנִי יָלְדָה וָלָד מֵת, וּמְסַפְּקָא לָן אִם [יָצָא הַוָּלָד מֵת מִן הַבַּיִת] הָרִאשׁוֹן:


והוציאוה מבית לבית – and in the second house she gave birth to a dead offspring, but it is doubtful to us if [the offspring came out dead from first house].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:4:2)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא נִטֶּלֶת בַּגַּפַּיִם.** שֶׁחַבְרוֹתֶיהָ נוֹטְלוֹת אוֹתָהּ בִּזְרוֹעוֹתֶיהָ, שֶׁאֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לֵילֵךְ, חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יָצָא הַוָּלָד מֵת מִן הַבַּיִת הָרִאשׁוֹן:


בשמן שהיא ניטלת בגפים (at the time that she is carried by her arms- put around the neck of her supporters) – that her colleagues/friends take it with her arms for she is not able to walk, we are concerned that the offspring came out dead from the first house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:4:3)


**הַקֶּבֶר.** רֶחֶם הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁנִּפְתָּח כְּשֶׁהַוָּלָד יוֹצֵא:


הקבר – the womb of the woman that was opened when the offspring leaves it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:4:4)


**אֵין לַנְּפָלִים פְּתִיחַת הַקֶּבֶר וֽכוּ'.** סִיּוּמָא דְמִלְּתָא דְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה הִיא. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, הָא דְַאֲמַרַן שֶׁמִּשֶּׁנִּפְתַּח הַקֶּבֶר אֵין פְּנַאי לְהַלֵּךְ לֹא אֲמַרַן אֶלָּא בְּנֵפֶל שֶׁעִגֵּל רֹאשׁ כַּפִּיקָה, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ עִגּוּל רֹאשׁ גָּדוֹל כַּפֶּלֶךְ הַזֶּה שֶׁהַנָּשִׁים טוֹוֹת בּוֹ הַחוּט שֶׁל שְׁתִי, אֲבָל אִם לֹא הָיָה לַנֵּפֶל עִגּוּל רֹאשׁ כְּפִיקָה אֶלָּא קָטָן מִזֶּה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּפְתַּח הַקֶּבֶר וְיָצָא רֹאשׁ הַוָּלָד לַחוּץ, יֵשׁ לָהּ פְּנַאי לְהַלֵּךְ וְחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יָצָא רֹאשׁוֹ מִן הַבַּיִת הָרִאשׁוֹן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אין לנפלים פתיחת הקבר וכו' – this is the conclusion of the matter of Rabbi Yehuda. And this is what he said: that which we said that when the womb opened, there is no time to walk we didn’t say this other than regarding a non-viable birth until the fetus is (on leaving the vagina) forms a round head like a coil, meaning to say that he has a large round head like this spindle that the women sew on it a thread of warp/longitudinal direction, but if the non-viable birth/fetus did not have a round head like a coil but rather smaller than this, even though the womb was opened and the head of the fetus exited outside, she has time to walk and we are concerned lest his head came out from the first house. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:1)


**יָצָא הָרִאשׁוֹן מֵת.** הַוָּלָד הָרִאשׁוֹן יָצָא מֵת וְהַוָּלָד הַשֵּׁנִי יָצָא חַי:


יצא האשון מת – the first fetus came out dead and the second fetus came out alive.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:2)


**טָהוֹר.** הַשֵּׁנִי הַחַי מִטֻּמְאַת מֵת, אִם הוֹצִיאוּ הַמֵּת מִן הַבַּיִת קֹדֶם לֵידַת הַשֵּׁנִי. דִּבְעוֹדוֹ בִמְעֵי אִמּוֹ לֹא נִטְמָא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּפְתַּח הַקֶּבֶר, דְּטָהֳרָה בְּלוּעָה אֵינָהּ מִטַּמְּאָה:


טהור – the second living [fetus] is [pure] from defilement through contact with a corpse. If they removed the dead [fetus] from the house prior to the second birth, that while it was still in the womb of its mother, it is not defiled, even though the womb was opened, for a pure object that has been swallowed does not defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:3)


**הָרִאשׁוֹן חַי וְהַשֵּׁנִי מֵת טָמֵא.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּ הַחַי מִן הַבַּיִת קֹדֶם שֶׁנּוֹלַד הַמֵּת. דְּמִכֵּיוָן שֶׁיָּצָא הַחַי נִפְתַּח הַקֶּבֶר וְנִטְמָא מִיָּד, דְּסוֹף טֻמְאָה לָצֵאת:


הראשון חי והשני מת טמא – and even though they removed the living [first child] from the house prior to the birth of the dead [second child], for since the live child came out, the womb was opened and became defiled immediately, for the defilement leaves at the en


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:4)


**בְּשָׁפִיר אֶחָד טָמֵא.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם יָצְאוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם כְּעֵין שָׁפִיר אֶחָד, שֶׁיָּצְאוּ בְּבַת אַחַת, טָמֵא. שָׁפִיר, שִׁלְיָא שֶׁהַוָּלָד נָתוּן בָּהּ:


בשפיר אחד טמא – meaning to say, if both of them came out similar to one membrane (i.e., sack of a fetus), that they came out at the same time, it is impure. שפיר/sack of a fetus is the placenta that the fetus is placed on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:5:5)


**בִּשְׁנֵי שְׁפִירִין.** כְּלוֹמַר כְּעֵין שְׁנֵי שְׁפִירִין זֶה אַחַר זֶה, אֲפִלּוּ רִאשׁוֹן חַי וְשֵׁנִי מֵת: **טָהוֹר.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּפְתַּח הַקֶּבֶר אֵין מְטַמֵּא עַד שֶׁיֵּצֵא לַאֲוִיר הָעוֹלָם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


בשני שפירין- meaning to say, similar to two membranes one after the other, even if the first is alive and the second is dead. טהור – for Rabbi Meir holds that even though the womb was opened, it doesn’t defile until it (i.e., the fetus) exists to the airspace of the world. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:6:1)


**יָצָא רֻבּוֹ.** שֶׁל וָלָד. וּמִשֶּׁיָּצָא רֹב רֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁהוּא מִשֶּׁתֵּצֵא פַדַּחְתּוֹ אֵין נוֹגְעִים בּוֹ:


יצא רובו – of the offspring/child. But once most of his head departs [the womb) which when his forehead exits (see Tracate Niddah 3b), we don’t touch him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:6:2)


**שֶׁאֵין דּוֹחִים נֶפֶשׁ.** הַוָּלָד:


שאין דוחים נפש -the offspring/child.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/7:6:3)


**מִפְּנֵי נָפֶשׁ.** הָאִשָּׁה:


מפני נפש – the woman (i.e., the mother).



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:1)


**יֵשׁ מְבִיאִין אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** שֶׁאִם הֶאֱהִילוּ עַל הַטֻּמְאָה וְאָדָם וְכֵלִים מְבִיאִין אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה עַל הָאָדָם וְהַכֵּלִים, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגְעוּ בַטֻּמְאָה:


יש מביאין את הטומאה – that if they overshadowed over the defilement and humans and/or vessels bring the defilement on a person and on the vessels, and even though they didn’t come in contact/touch the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:2)


**וְחוֹצְצִים.** אִם הַטֻּמְאָה תַחְתֵּיהֶן מַצִּילִין אָדָם וְכֵלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבָּן, לְפִי שֶׁהֵן חוֹצְצִים בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה. וְכֵן אִם טֻמְאָה עַל גַּבָּן מִבַּחוּץ, מַצִּילִין כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתֵּיהֶן:


וחוצצים – if the defilement is below them, they protect persons and vessels that are upon them. Because they do not interpose in the face of the defilement. And similarly if the defilement is upon them from the outside, it protects vessels that are underneath them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:3)


**שִׁדָּה.** תֵּבָה גְדוֹלָה שֶׁל עֵץ כְּמִין אָרוֹן:


שידה (chest) a large wooden box like a kind of ark.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:4)


**תֵּבָה.** קְטַנָּה כְעֵין שֻׁלְחָנִים:


תיבה – small similar to tables.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:5)


**מִגְדָּל.** שֶׁל עֵץ עָשׂוּי לְאוֹצָר:


מגדל (turret/cupboard) – of wood made for storage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:6)


**כַּוֶּרֶת הַקַּשׁ.** קֻפּוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת עֲשׂוּיוֹת מִקַּשׁ אוֹ מֵעֲרָבָה אוֹ כַפּוֹת תְּמָרִים:


כוורת הקש – large baskets made from straw or from willow or branches of palm trees.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:7)


**כַּוֶּרֶת הַקָּנִים.** כְּמִין סַלִּים עֲשׂוּיִין מִקָּנִים:


כוורת הקנים – like baskets that are made from reeds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:8)


**וּבוֹר סְפִינָה אֲלֶכְּסַנְדְּרִית.** הַסְּפִינוֹת הַגְּדוֹלוֹת שֶׁמַּפְלִיגִים בָּהֶם בַּיָּם הַגָּדוֹל, עוֹשִׂין בְּתוֹכָן כְּלִי גָדוֹל שֶׁל עֵץ כְּמִין בּוֹר שֶׁמְּשִׂימִים בּוֹ הַמַּיִם הַמְּתוּקִין הָרְאוּיִין לִשְׁתִיָּה:


ובור ספינה אלכסנדרית (tank of sweet water in a ship of Alexandrian merchantmen) – the large ships that they go to sea on the large ocean (i.e., Mediterranean Sea), they make within it a large wooden vessel like a cistern that the place in it sweet water that is appropriate for drinking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:9)


**וְהֵן מַחֲזִיקִים אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה בְלַח.** דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁהֵן גְּדוֹלִים בְּמִדָּה זוֹ שׁוּב אֵינָן מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה, וְחוֹצְצִים בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


והן מחזיקין ארבעים סאה כלה – for since they are large in this measure, they furthermore are not susceptible to receive defilement, and they interpose in the face of defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:10)


**סְקוֹרְטְיָא.** עוֹר שֶׁלּוֹבְשִׁים הָעַבְּדָנִים בִּשְׁעַת מְלַאכְתָּן:


סקורטיא (a leather bed-sheet) – a hide that the tanners wear at the time of their work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:11)


**קְטָבֹלְיָא.** עוֹר שֶׁמַּצִּיעִין לְמִטָּה וְשׁוֹכְבִין עָלָיו, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי נט"ע:


קטבליא (hide used as a spread) - a hid that they spread over the bed and lie upon it, and we call it NETA in Arabic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:12)


**שֶׁהֵן עֲשׂוּיִין אֹהָלִים.** נְטוּיִין כְּעֵין אֹהֶל. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּירִיעָה וּסְקוֹרְטְיָא וּקְטָבֹלְיָא וְסָדִין וּמַפָּץ וּמַחֲצֶלֶת כֵּלִים נִינְהוּ וּמְקַבְּלֵי טֻמְאָה, מִכָּל מָקוֹם כְּשֶׁהֵן נְטוּיִין כְּאֹהָלִים חוֹצְצִים בִּפְנֵי טֻמְאָה, דְּאֹהֶל אַף עַל גַּב דִּמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


שהן עשויין אהלים – hanging over similar to a tent. And the leather-bed sheet/סקורטיא and the קטבליא/hide used as a spread and the sheet and the matting and the mat are vessels and susceptible to receive ritual defilement, anyway when they are hanging over like tents they interpose before the defilement, for a tent even though it receives defilement interposes in front of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:13)


**וְעֵדֶר בְּהֵמוֹת.** כְּשֶׁהֵן עוֹמְדוֹת וְלֹא מְהַלְּכוֹת וְרֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁל זֶה בֵּין רַגְלָיו שֶׁל זֶה:


ועדר בהמות – when they are standing and not walking and the head of this one is between the legs of that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:14)


**וּמְכוֹנוֹת חַיָּה וָעוֹף.** קִנֵּי חַיָּה וָעוֹף. לְשׁוֹן מָכוֹן לְשִׁבְתְּךָ (שמות טו):


ומכונות חיה ועוף – nests of wildlife and birds. It is the language of (Exodus 15:27): “The place You made to dwell in.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:15)


**וְהָעוֹף שֶׁשָּׁכַן.** שֶׁהָיָה קָשׁוּר עַל הַכֹּתֶל אוֹ עַל הָאִילָן:


והעוף ששכן – that was tied on the wall or on the tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:16)


**וְהָעוֹשֶׂה מָקוֹם לְבֵנָה בַּשִּׁבֳּלִים.** שֶׁמְּחַפֶּה לַשִּׁבֳּלִים מָקוֹם דּוֹמֶה לְבַיִת:


והעושה מקום לבינה בשבלים – that cover the ears of corn/spike (i.e., the sheaves), a place that is similar to a house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:17)


**הָאֵירוּס וְהַקִּסּוֹם וּדְלַעַת יְוָנִית וְיַרְקוֹת חֲמוֹרִים.** כָּל אֵלּוּ מִינֵי יְרָקוֹת שֶׁעָלִים שֶׁלָּהֶן רְחָבִים:


האירוס והקיסום ודלעת יונית וירקת חמורים (the lily with an aromatic root, the ivy, the Greek gourd and the ass-herbs) – all these are kinds of vegetables whose leaves are wide.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:1:18)


**חוּץ מִן הָעִגּוּל שֶׁל דְּבֵלָה.** לְפִי שֶׁתַּחַת צֵל עִגּוּל שֶׁל דְּבֵלָה הָיוּ רְגִילִים לֵישֵׁב יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר אֳכָלִים. וְהָכִי תַנְיָא בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי:


חוץ מן העיגול של דבילה – because that underneath the shade of the pressed cake of figs, they would regularly sit more than the rest of the foods. And this is taught in the Tosefta (Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 9, Halakha 5). But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:1)


**הַזִּיזִין.** עֵץ אוֹ אֶבֶן יְחִידִי הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַכֹּתֶל קָרוּי זִיז:


הזיזין (projections from door frames serving as a shed over the entrance – or a molding projecting from a window-sill serving as a bracket) – a piece of wood or an individual stone that protects out from the wall is called a זיז.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:2)


**גִּזְרִיּוֹת.** כְּמוֹ גְּזוּזְטְרָאוֹת. בִּנְיָן שֶׁל אֲבָנִים אוֹ שֶׁל עֵצִים הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַכֹּתֶל:


גזריות – like [the word]גזוזטראות /balconies). A building of stones or of wood that project from the wall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:3)


**וְהַשּׁוֹבָכוֹת.** שֶׁל יוֹנִים:


והשובכות – of doves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:4)


**וְהַשְּׁקִיפִים וְהַסְּלָעִים.** פֵּרַשְׁנוּ לְעֵיל סוֹף פֶּרֶק ג':


והשקיפים והסלעים – we explained this above at the conclusion of chapter 3 [of this tractate] (i.e., clefts in the stones and rocks – one made by the rain and the other made on its own).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:5)


**גְּהָרִים.** מִין חוֹרִים שֶׁמִּשָּׁם בָּא הָאוֹר. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים מִין מְעָרוֹת וַחֲוָחִים:


גהרים (projection, jetty) - kind of holes that from there comes the light. And there are those who explain it as kinds of caves and thorns.-


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:6)


**וְהַשְּׁנָנִים.** שִׁנֵּי הַסֶּלַע:


והשננים (pointed rocks/cliffs) – teeth of the rock.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:7)


**מַעֲזִיבָה רַכָּה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מֵעֲרָבָה וּמִקָּנִים וּמְשִׂימִים עֲלֵיהֶם טִיט מֻעָט:


מעזיבה רכה – as for example that they make from willow and reeds and place upon them a bit of plaster.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:8)


**מַעֲזִיבָה בֵינוֹנִית.** אֲבָל אִם אֵינָן יְכוֹלִים לִסְבֹּל אֶלָּא מַעֲזִיבָה רַכָּה, אֵין מְבִיאִים אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה וְלֹא חוֹצְצִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מעזיבה בינונית – but if they are not able to endure other than a soft concrete of stone chippings, they don’t bring the defilement and do not interpose. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:9)


**אִילָן הַמֵּסֵךְ.** לְשׁוֹן סוֹכְכִים בְּכַנְפֵיהֶם:


אילן מיסך -the language of (Exodus 25:20): “shielding the cover with their wings.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:2:10)


**וְהַפְּרָעוֹת הַיּוֹצְאוֹת מִן הַגָּדֵר.** דֶּרֶךְ גָּדֵר שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בְּרֹאשׁוֹ קוֹצִים וּבַרְקָנִים, וְעוֹדְפִים עַל הַגָּדֵר וּבוֹלְטִים לַחוּץ. וּפְרָעוֹת, לְשׁוֹן גַּדֵּל פֶּרַע:


והפרעות היוצאות מן הגדר (the case of protruding boughs on one of which is an uncleanness – which protrudes from the hedge) – it is the manner of a fence that we place on its top thorns and thistles, and they are more abundant on the fence and protrude outward. And [the word] פרעות/long, entangled growth (i.e., protruding boughs). The language of (Numbers 6:5): “[the hair of his head] left to grow untrimmed.”



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:3:1)


**וְאֵין מַחֲזִיקִים אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה בְלַח.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּמְקַבְּלֵי טֻמְאָה, אֵין חוֹצְצִין:


ואין מחזיקים ארבעים סאה בלח – for since they are susceptible to receive ritual defilement, they do not interpose.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:3:2)


**שֶׁאֵין עֲשׂוּיִין אֹהָלִים.** אֵין נְטוּעִים כְּעֵין אֹהֶל, אֶלָּא מְרֻדָּדִים עַל פִּי אֲרֻבָּה:


שאין עושיין אהלים – they are not insert/planted similar to a tent, but rather are flattened/spread according to the aperture in the roof looking to the ground floor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:3:3)


**מוּסָף עֲלֵיהֶם הָרֵחַיִם שֶׁל אָדָם.** הַיְנוּ רֵחַיִם דְּיָד, שֶׁהֵן מִטַּלְטְלִין וּמְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה. לְמַעוֹטֵי רֵחַיִם שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה דְּלֹא מִטַּלְטְלִין וְלֹא מְקַבְּלֵי טֻמְאָה:


מוסף עליהם הריחים של אדם – that is the hand millstone, which are carried and susceptible to receiving ritual impurity. This excludes the millstone of cattle that are not carried and are not susceptible to receiving ritual impurity.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:4:1)


**מַסֶּכֶת פְּרוּסָה.** חוּטֵי הַשְּׁתִי כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵן פְּרוּסִים בִּכְלִי הָאוֹרֵג קֹדֶם שֶׁיֶּאֱרֹג הַבֶּגֶד, אִם הָיוּ פְּרוּסִים עַל פְּנֵי אֲרֻבָּה שֶׁבֵּין בַּיִת לָעֲלִיָּה וְטֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ, מַצֶּלֶת עַל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁלְּמַעְלָה. אֲבָל אִם מַאֲהֶלֶת עַל הַמֵּת וְעַל הַכֵּלִים אֵין מְבִיאָה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, דְּלָא חֲשִׁיבָא אֹהֶל עַד שֶׁתֵּאָרֵג:


מסכת פרוסה (the spread web – i.e., the web hanging from the transverse beam) – the threads of the warp/longitudinal direction like they are spread out in a weaver’s vessel prior to weaving the cloth. If they were spread out over an aperture in the roof looking to the ground floor that is between the house and the upper room and the defilement is below it, it protects upon the vessels that are above, but if it overshadows the corpse or on the vessels, it does not bring the defilement, for it is not considered a tent until it is woven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:4:2)


**וַחֲבִילֵי הַמִּטָּה.** חֲבָלִים שֶׁל מִטָּה כְּשֶׁהִיא מְסֹרֶגֶת:


וחבילי המטה (the rope-work of the bed) – ropes of he better when it the straps are drawn in bedsteads.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:4:3)


**וְהַמִּשְׁפָּלוֹת.** קֻפּוֹת שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִים בָּהֶן אֶת הַזֶּבֶל:


והמשפלות (wicker baskets carried on a pole over the shoulder) – baskets that they remove the manure In them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:4:4)


**וְהַסְּרִיגוֹת שֶׁבַּחֲלוֹנוֹת.** סְבָכוֹת שֶׁעוֹשִׂים בַּחַלּוֹנוֹת, חוֹצְצִים, שֶׁאֵין הַטֻּמְאָה עוֹבֶרֶת לְצַד אַחֵר. וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה בְכֻלָּן נֶקֶב כְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, שֶׁנֶּקֶב פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה:


והסריגים שבחלונות (and the window lattices) – network/matting that they make in the windows, interposes, for the defilement does not pass to one side. And as long as there will not be in all of them an incision like is open to a handbreadth, for an incision/hole that is open to a handbreadth brings in the defilement.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:1)


**יְרָקוֹת שֶׁמָּנוּ.** הָנָךְ דִּתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ פִּרְקִין, אֵירוּס וְקִסּוֹם וְיַרְקוֹת חֲמוֹרִים וּדְלַעַת יְוָנִית. וְהַנְהוּ בִמְחֻבָּרִים. דְּאִי בִתְלוּשִׁים, אִי בְּשֶׁהֻכְשְׁרוּ לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, לֹא חַיְצֵי, דְּדָבָר הַמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה. וְאִי בְשֶׁלֹּא הֻכְשְׁרוּ, הַיְנוּ אֳכָלִים טְהוֹרִים:


ירקות שמנו – those that are taught in the Mishnah at the beginning of this chapter (Mishnah 1), The lily with the aromatic root/אירוס, and the ivy and the vegetables of ass-herbs and Greek gourd. And these are when attached, for if they are detached, if they became susceptible to receive ritual impurity, they don’t interpose, for something that is susceptible to receive ritual impurity does not interpose in the face of defilement. But if they were among those that were not susceptible, that is pure foods.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:2)


**כִּפַּת הַבָּרָד.** חֲתִיכַת הַבָּרָד. לְשׁוֹן מַשְׁלִיךְ קַרְחוֹ כְפִתִּים (תהלים קמז). אִי נַמִּי, בָּרָד הֶעָשׂוּי כְּכִפָּה. ארקוול"ט בְּלַעַ"ז:


כפת הברד (hailstones) – a piece of the hail. It is the language of (Psalms 147:17): “He tosses down hail like crumbs.” Alternatively, hail that is made like an arch.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:3)


**וְהַכְּפוֹר.** מַיִם הַנּוֹזְלִים וְיוֹרְדִים שֶׁקָּפְאוּ:


והכפור(frost) – water that flows/runs and comes down when it is frozen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:4)


**גְּלִיד.** מֵי נְהָרוֹת קְפוּאִים:


גליד – the frozen water of rivers (i.e., ice).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:5)


**וְהַמֶּלַח.** כָּל הָנֵי אֵין מְבִיאִים וְלֹא חוֹצְצִים. לְפִי שֶׁהֵן נִמּוֹחִים וְאֵינָם מִתְקַיְּמִים:


והמלח – all of these they don’t bring and they don’t interpose. Because they melt and do not endure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:6)


**דּוֹלֵג.** בְּהֵמָה וְחַיָּה. וְקוֹפֵץ, בָּאָדָם. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים, דּוֹלֵג וְקוֹפֵץ תַּרְוַיְהוּ בָּאָדָם, אֶלָּא דוֹלֵג שֶׁרַגְלוֹ אַחַת מֻנַּחַת בָּאָרֶץ וְדוֹלֵג בַּשְּׁנִיָּה, וְקוֹפֵץ, בִּשְׁתֵּי רַגְלָיו:


דולג (which hops/skips) – animals and wild life, and jump at humans. But there are those who say, that hopping and jumping – both of them refer to humans, but rather hopping where his one foot is placed on the ground and he hops with the second; and jumps, with both feet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:7)


**וְהַטַּלִּית הַמְנַפְנֶפֶת.** שֶׁמְּנִיפָתָהּ הָרוּחַ וְעוֹמֶדֶת בָּאֲוִיר כְּמוֹ קֻבָּה:


וטלית המפנפת (a sheet suspended as a banner) – that when the wind waves it and it stands in the air like an arched compartment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:8)


**כָּבַשׁ אֶת הָאֶבֶן עַל גַּבֵּי הַטַּלִּית.** שֶׁהָיְתָה אֶבֶן מֻנַּחַת בְּקָצֶה אֶחָד שֶׁל טַלִּית וְהֵנִיף הָרוּחַ הַקָּצֶה הַשֵּׁנִי וְנַעֲשָׂה כְמִין אֹהֶל:


כבש את האבן על גבי הטלית (presses the stone on top of the cloak) – that there was a stone placed on one end of the cloak and the wind blew the second end and it became like a kind of tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:9)


**מְבִיאָה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** אִם הֶאֱהִילָה עַל הַמֵּת וְעַל הַכֵּלִים מְבִיאָה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה עַל הַכֵּלִים:


מביאה את הטומאה – if it overshadowed over the corpse or on vessels, it brings the defilement o the vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:5:10)


**הַבַּיִת שֶׁבַּסְּפִינָה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהַבַּיִת בְּרֹאשׁ הַסְּפִינָה וּמַאֲהֶלֶת עַל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁבַּסְּפִינָה וְעַל הַמֵּת שֶׁבַּיָּם בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהִיא מְהַלֶּכֶת. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, הַבַּיִת שֶׁבַּסְּפִינָה וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לַעֲמֹד בְּרוּחַ מְצוּיָה, אֲפִלּוּ הֶאֱהִיל עַל הַמֵּת וְהַכֵּלִים שֶׁבַּסְּפִינָה אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, דְּלָא חֲשִׁיב אֹהֶל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


הבית שבספינה – as for example, that the house at the top of a ship and it overshadows on the vessels that are on the ship and on the corpse that is in the sea at the time that it is travelling. Another explanation, the house that is on the ship and it is not able to withstand the wind that is found, and even if it overshadowed on the corpse and the vessels that are on the ship do not bring the defilement, and it is not considered a tent. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:6:1)


**כִּשְׁנֵי חֲצָאֵי זֵיתִים.** חֲצִי זַיִת בְּזוֹ וַחֲצִי בְזוֹ:


בשני חצאי זיתים – half an olive’s bulk in this one, and half in that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:6:2)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** דְּאֵין צָמִיד פָּתִיל מַצִּיל עַל הַטֻּמְאָה שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו שֶׁלֹּא תְטַמֵּא הַבַּיִת, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן בִּשְׁתֵּי חָבִיּוֹת, הוֹאִיל וּשְׁתֵּיהֶן בַּבַּיִת מִצְטָרְפִים לְכַשִּׁעוּר לְטַמֵּא הַבַּיִת. וְהֶחָבִיּוֹת טְהוֹרוֹת, דְּאֵין טֻמְאַת חֲצִי זַיִת שֶׁבַּחֲבֶרְתָּהּ נִכְנֶסֶת לְתוֹכָהּ הוֹאִיל וּמֻקֶּפֶת צָמִיד פָּתִיל:


הבית טמא – that a tightly-fitting cover does not protect on the defilement that is under it so that the house would defile the house, but even though that they are in two jars, for since both of them are in the house they combine to the measurement to defile the house. But the jars are ritually pure, for the defilement of one-half an olive’s bulk that is in its companion jar enters into it, sine it is surrounded with a tight-fitting cover.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/8:6:3)


**וְכֵן שְׁנֵי חֲדָרִים שֶׁהֵן פְּתוּחִים לַבָּיִת.** וְדַלְתוֹתֵיהֶן נְעוּלוֹת, וַחֲצִי זַיִת בְּחֶדֶר זֶה וַחֲצִי זַיִת בְּחֶדֶר זֶה. הַבַּיִת טָמֵא, דְּסוֹף טֻמְאָה לָצֵאת. וַחֲדָרִים טְהוֹרִים, דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ טֻמְאָה לִכָּנֵס:


וכן שני חדרים שהן פתוחים לבית – and their doors are locked, and there is a half-olive’s bulk in this room and a half-olive’s bulk in that room, the house is impure, for ultimately the defilement exits, but the rooms are ritually pure, for there is no pay for the defilement to enter in.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:1)


**כַּוֶּרֶת.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ שׁוּלַיִם, וּמַחְזֶקֶת אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה בְּלַח שֶׁהֵן כּוֹרַיִם בְּיָבֵשׁ שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה, וּמֻטָּה עַל צִדָּהּ בְּתוֹךְ הַפֶּתַח וּמִקְצָתָהּ בַּחוּץ וּמִקְצָתָהּ בַּבַּיִת:


כוורת – that it has rims [at the bottom] and holds forty Se’ah in wet which are two KOR in dry, that it is susceptible to receive ritual defilement, and reclines on its side within the opening and part of it is outside and part of it is in the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:2)


**וּפִיהָ לַחוּץ.** וְשׁוּלֶיהָ לִפְנִים:


ופיה לחוץ – and its rim is inside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:3)


**כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת נָתוּן תַּחְתֶּיהָ.** מִבַּחוּץ:


כזית מן המת נתון תחתיה – from outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:4)


**כֹּל שֶׁהוּא כְנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת.** אִם הַזַּיִת תַּחְתֶּיהָ וְכֵלִים עַל גַּבָּהּ כְּנֶגְדּוֹ, אוֹ הַזַּיִת עַל גַּבָּהּ וְכֵלִים תַּחְתֶּיהָ כְנֶגְדּוֹ:


כל שהוא כנגד הזית – if the olive’s bulk [of the corpse] is underneath it and vessels are on top of it opposite it, or the olive’s bulk [of the corpse] is upon it and vessels are underneath it opposite it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:5)


**תַּחְתֶּיהָ וְגַבָּהּ טָמֵא.** אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ טָהוֹר. דְּאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַכַּוֶּרֶת מַצֶּלֶת עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ, אֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת עַל מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ וְגַבָּהּ כְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת שֶׁל טֻמְאָה. דְּעַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ דִּין הוּא שֶׁתַּצִּיל, דַּאֲפִלּוּ מֻנַּחַת בְּאֹהֶל הַמֵּת מַצֶּלֶת בְּצָמִיד פָּתִיל, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה, אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ וְשֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ אֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת כֵּיוָן דִּכְלִי הִיא, כְּדִתְנַן לְעֵיל פֶּרֶק ו'‏ אָדָם וְכֵלִים נַעֲשִׂים אֹהָלִים לְטַמֵּא אֲבָל לֹא לְטַהֵר:


תחתיה וגבה טמא – but what is within it is ritually pure. For even though the hive protects over what is within it, it does not protect on what is underneath it or on top of it opposite the olive’s bulk of defilement, for on what is within it, it is the law that it protects, for even if it is placed in the tent of a corpse it protects with a tightly-sealed cover/airtight lid, since it is not susceptible to receive ritual defilement, but what is underneath it and on its back it does not protect since it is a vessel, as is taught in the Mishnah above in Chapter 6 {Mishnah 1]: “Humans and vessels are made into tents to defile, but not to purify.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:6)


**וְכֹל שֶׁאֵינוֹ כְנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת תּוֹכָהּ וְהַבַּיִת טָהוֹר.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ וְגַבָּהּ שֶׁאֵינָן כְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת שֶׁל טֻמְאָה, וְתוֹכָהּ כְּלוֹמַר כֵּלִים שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן כְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת, וְכֵלִים נַמִּי שֶׁבַּבַּיִת, הַכֹּל טָהוֹר:


וכל שאינו כנגד הזית תוכה והבית טהור – this is what he said: vessels that are underneath it and on top of it that are not opposite the olive’s bulk of defilement, and within it, meaning to say, vessels that are within it and even if hey are opposite the olive’s bulk [of defilement], and vessels also that are in the house, everything is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:7)


**בַּבַּיִת אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא הַבַּיִת.** אִם הַכַּזַּיִת בַּבַּיִת אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא הַבַּיִת, אֲבָל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַכַּוֶּרֶת טְהוֹרִים, דְּפִיהָ לַחוּץ:


בבית אין טמא אלא הבית – if the olive’s bulk [of defilement] is in the house, there is nothing ritually impure other than the house, but the vessels that within the hive are pure, for its mouth is towards the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:1:8)


**בְּתוֹכָהּ הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** אִם הַכַּזַּיִת בְּתוֹךְ הַכַּוֶּרֶת, הַכֹּל טָמֵא. דִּבְכַוֶּרֶת שֶׁהִיא נְקוּבָה מִן הַדְּפָנוֹת בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח עָסְקִינַן, כִּדְבָעִינַן לְמֵימַר לְקַמָּן [מִשְׁנָה ג'], אֶלָּא שֶׁהַנְּקָבִים פְּקוּקִים בְּקַשׁ וְאֵין הַסְּתִימָה מְהֻדֶּקֶת, וְדֶרֶךְ הַנְּקָבִים יוֹצְאָה הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא מִהַדְּקֵי, וַאֲפִלּוּ כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ וְשֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ מִבַּחוּץ טְמֵאִים, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁהַזַּיִת בְּתוֹכָהּ רוֹאִין אוֹתָהּ כְּאִלּוּ הִיא מְלֵאָה טֻמְאָה, וְאֵין כְּלִי מַצִּיל עַל מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתָּיו וְעַל גַּבָּיו. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בַּבַּיִת, אֲמַרַן לְעֵיל דְּאֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא הַבַּיִת, שֶׁאֵין הַטֻּמְאָה נִכְנֶסֶת בַּכַּוֶּרֶת דֶּרֶךְ נְקָבִים אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, הוֹאִיל וְהֵן פְּקוּקִים, וְדֶרֶךְ פִּיהָ אֵינָהּ נִכְנֶסֶת שֶׁהֲרֵי פִּיהָ לַחוּץ:


בתוכה הכל טמא – if the olive’s bulk [of defilement] is within the hive, everything is ritually defiled, for we are dealing with a hive which is perforated from the walls with an opening of a handbreadth, as we are required to state further on [Mishnah 3] but rather that the incisions/holes are stopped up with straw, but the closing is not fastened, and it is the manner of the holes/incisions that the defilement goes out to the house since they are not fastened, and even vessels that are under it and on top of from the outside are ritually impure, for since the olive’s bulk [of defilement] we see it as is if it is filled with defilement, and a vessel cannot protect what is underneath it or on top of it. But when the defilement is in the house, we stated above [in this Mishnah] that there is no defilement other than the house, for the defilement does not enter in the hive through the incisions/holes even though they have an opening of a handbreadth, for since they are stuffed up, but it (i.e., the defilement) does not enter through its mouth for its mouth is towards the outside.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:2:1)


**הָיְתָה גְבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח.** דְּעַכְשָׁיו יֵשׁ תַּחְתֶּיהָ אֹהֶל טֶפַח:


היתה גבוהה מן הארץ טפח – that now there is a tent of a handbreadth underneath it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:2:2)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** דִּמְבִיאָה טֻמְאָה לַבַּיִת כֵּיוָן דִּגְבוֹהָה טֶפַח, וְכֵלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה נַמִּי טְמֵאִין וַאֲפִלּוּ בַּחוּץ, דְּתַחְתֶּיהָ כְּמַאן דְּמָלְיָא טֻמְאָה. וְאֵין מַצֶּלֶת עַל גַּבָּהּ, דְּאֵין כְּלִי מַצִּיל. וְכֵן אִם טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת נַמִּי הַכֹּל טָמֵא מֵהַאי טַעֲמָא, דְּאֹהֶל הַבַּיִת מֵבִיא טֻמְאָה לְתַחְתֶּיהָ וְאֵין מַצֶּלֶת עַל גַּבָּהּ. וְאִם טֻמְאָה עַל גַּבָּהּ, כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין מַצֶּלֶת עַל מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ, רוֹאִים תַּחְתֶּיהָ כְּמַאן דְּמָלְיָא טֻמְאָה, הִלְכָּךְ מַה שֶּׁבַּבַּיִת וּמַה שֶּׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ וּמַה שֶּׁעַל גַּבָּהּ הַכֹּל טָמֵא:


הכל טמא – that it brings the defilement into the house since it is a handbreadth high, and vessels that are on top of it, even those that are not opposite the defilement are also impure and even outside, for underneath it is like one that is full of defilement. And it doesn’t protect what on top of it, for the vessel does not protect. And similarly, if the defilement is also in the house everything is ritually impure from this reason, for the tent of the house brings the defilement to what is underneath it and doesn’t protect that which is on top of it. But if the defilement is on top of it, since it doesn’t protect over what is underneath it, we view what is beneath it as one that is filled with defilement, therefore, whatever is in the house and what is underneath it and what is on top of it -everything is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:2:3)


**אֶלָּא תוֹכָהּ.** כְּלוֹמַר הַכֹּל טָמֵא חוּץ מִמַּה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ שֶׁטָּהוֹר:


אלא תוכה -that is to say, everything is impure except for what is within it (i.e., the house) which is pure.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:1)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא כְלִי.** שֶׁלֹּא נִפְחֲתָה וְלֹא נִתְבַּטְּלָה מִתּוֹרַת כְּלִי:


בזמן שהוא כלי – that it was not hollowed out and was not nullified from the category of being a vessel. -


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:2)


**מְחֻלְחָלֶת.** שֶׁהַדְּפָנוֹת נְקוּבוֹת בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח. שֶׁרְגִילִים לַעֲשׂוֹת נְקָבִים בְּדָפְנוֹת הַכַּוֶּרֶת כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּכָּנְסוּ הַדְּבוֹרִים וְיֵצְאוּ, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהַנְּקָבִים פְּקוּקִים בְּקַשׁ, אֶלָּא דְהַפְּקָק לֹא מִהַדַּק:


מחולחלת (hollowed out – not packed entirely) – that the walls are perforated with an opening of a handbreadth. They normally make incisions in the walls of the hive in order the bees can enter and depart, and sometimes that the incisions/holes are stopped up with straw but that the stopper is not fastened tightly.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:3)


**הָיְתָה פְחוּתָה.** שֶׁנִּפְחֲתָה וְנִתְבַּטְּלָה מִתּוֹרַת כְּלִי:


היתה פחותה (if it was damaged) – that it was broken through/hollowed out and voided from the category of a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:4)


**וּפְקוּקָה בְקַשׁ.** בִּמְקוֹם הַפְּחִיתָה. וְדָפְנוֹתֶיהָ שְׁלֵמוֹת שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְחֻלְחֶלֶת:


ופקוקה בקש – in the place of the hollowing out. But its walls are complete that they are not hollowed out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:5)


**אוֹ אֲפוּצָה.** אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ אֵין דָּפְנוֹתֶיהָ שְׁלֵמוֹת אֶלָּא שֶׁאֲפוּצָה, כְּלוֹמַר סְתוּמָה בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁהִיא מְחֻלְחֶלֶת וְאֵין בָּהּ חִלְחוּל טֶפַח:


או אפוצה (or made narrower by squeezing the parts together – i.e., pressing the lid down) – or even if its walls were not complete but rather that it was made narrowed, meaning to say, that it was closed in the place where it is hollowed out/not packed entirely, and there isn’t a handbreadth of hollowed out area.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:6)


**כְּנֶגְדּוֹ עַד הַתְּהוֹם טָמֵא.** וְכָל הַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר. דְּכֵיוָן דְּלָאו כְּלִי הוּא חֲשׁוּב כְּאֹהֶל לְהַצִּיל תּוֹכָהּ וְעַל גַּבָּהּ:


כנגדו עד התהום טמא – but all of the rest is pure. For since it is not considered a vessel like a tent to protect what is inside it and what is on top of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:7)


**עַל גַּבָּהּ.** אִם כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת נָתוּן עַל גַּבָּהּ:


על גבה – if an olive’s bulk from a corpse is placed on top of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:8)


**כְּנֶגְדּוֹ עַד הָרָקִיעַ טָמֵא.** וְכָל הַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר, דְּמַצֶּלֶת תּוֹכָהּ וְתַחְתֶּיהָ. כֵּיוָן דְּלָאו כְּלִי הִיא:


כנגדו עד הרקיע טמא – but all of the rest of pure, for it protects what is inside of it and what is below it. Since it is not a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:9)


**בַּבַּיִת.** אִם כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת בַּבַּיִת:


בבית – if an olive’s bulk from the corpse is in the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:10)


**אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא הַבַּיִת.** וְכָל הַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר, דְּפִיהָ לַחוּץ:


אין טמא אלא הבית – and all of the rest is pure, for its opening/mouth is outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:11)


**בְּתוֹכָהּ.** אִם כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת בַּבַּיִת:


בתוכה – if the olive’s bulk from the corpse is within it (i.e., inside).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:3:12)


**אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא תוֹכָהּ.** וְכָל הַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר. דְּאֵין טֻמְאָה נִכְנֶסֶת לַבַּיִת כֵּיוָן דְּאֵינָהּ מְחֻלְחֶלֶת וּפִיהָ לַחוּץ. וּמַצֶּלֶת נַמִּי עַל מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ וְעַל גַּבָּהּ, כֵּיוָן דְּלָאו כְּלִי הִיא:


אין טמא אלא תוכה – and all the rest is pure, for the defilement does not enter into the house because it is not hollowed out and its mouth is towards the outside, and it protects also over what is underneath it and what is on top of it, since It is not a vessel.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:4:1)


**תַּחְתֶּיהָ וְהַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** דִּמְבִיאִין טֻמְאָה מִזֶּה לָזֶה:


תחתיה והבית טמא – for they bring the defilement from this (i.e., underneath the house) to that (i.e., the house).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:4:2)


**תּוֹכָהּ טָהוֹר.** מַה שֶּׁבִּפְנִים, כֵּיוָן דְּפִיהָ לַחוּץ. וְגַבָּהּ נַמִּי טָהוֹר, דְּמַצֶּלֶת עַל מַה שֶּׁבַּחוּץ כֵּיוָן דְּלָאו כְּלִי הִיא:


תוכה טהור – that which is inside [the house], since the its walls are outside, and what is on top of it is also pure, for it protects on what is outside, for it is not a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:4:3)


**בְּתוֹכָהּ.** אִם הַטֻּמְאָה בְתוֹכָהּ:


בתוכה – if the defilement is within it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:4:4)


**אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא תוֹכָהּ.** וְכָל הַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר:


אין טמא אלא תוכה – and all the rest is ritually pure.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:5:1)


**הָיָה פִיהָ לִפְנִים.** שֶׁהָיְתָה מֻטָּה עַל צִדָּהּ וְשׁוּלֶיהָ לַחוּץ וּפִיהָ בִּפְנִים. וְהַשְׁתָּא מַיְרֵי בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא כְּלִי מְחֻלְחֶלֶת וְאֵינָהּ גְּבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח. וְאֵין חִלּוּק בֵּין פִּיהָ לַחוּץ לְפִיהָ לִפְנִים, אֶלָּא דְפִיהָ לַחוּץ קָתָנֵי לְעֵיל בַּבַּיִת אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא הַבַּיִת, וְהָכָא דְּפִיהָ לִפְנִים קָתָנֵי הַכֹּל טָמֵא, מִשּׁוּם דְּנִכְנֶסֶת טֻמְאָה דֶּרֶךְ פִּיהָ:


היה פיה לפנים – that it was leaning on its side and its rims were outside and its mouth was inside. For now, we are speaking of the time when it is a hollowed-out vessel and is not higher from the ground a handbreadth. But there is no distinction whether its mouth is outside or whether its mouth is inside, but that when its mouth is outside as is taught above (i.e., Mishnah 4) regarding a house, nothing is impure other than the house, but here, when its mouth is inside, it (i.e., the Mishnah) teaches that everything is impure, because the defilement enters through its mouth.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:6:1)


**הָיְתָה גְבוֹהָה.** לְעֵיל דְּפִיהָ לַחוּץ קָתָנֵי טֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ אוֹ בַבַּיִת [אוֹ עַל גַּבָּהּ, הַכֹּל טָמֵא אֶלָּא תוֹכָהּ], וְהָכָא דְפִיהָ לִפְנִים, הַכֹּל טָמֵא:


היתה גבוהה – above (see Mishnah 1), its walls are outside as its teaches [in the Mishnah] the defilement that is under it or in the house [or on top of it, everything is impure, other than what is within it,] but here its walls are within, everything is impure.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:7:1)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא כְלִי מְחֻלְחָלֶת.** בְּחִנָּם נָקַט מְחֻלְחֶלֶת, דְּלֹא מַהֲנֵי מְחֻלְחֶלֶת אֶלָּא בְּפִיהָ לַחוּץ לְעִנְיַן הוֹצָאַת טֻמְאָה מִתּוֹכָהּ לַבַּיִת, וְהָכָא דְּפִיהָ לִפְנִים הֲרֵי יוֹצְאָה דֶּרֶךְ פִּיהָ. אֶלָּא אַיְדֵי דְתָנָא רֵישָׁא מְחֻלְחֶלֶת בַּהֲדֵי כְלִי נַקְטֵיהּ נַמִּי הָכָא:


בזמן שהוא כלי מחולחלת – freely, it (i.e., the Mishnah) took [the term] מחולחלת/hollow, loosely put in (i.e., not packed entirely) for it does not benefit being loosely put in other than its mouth being outside regarding the matter of removal of the defilement from within it to the house, but here, where its walls are inside, it (i.e., the defilement) goes out through its mouth. But since the first clause of the Mishnah taught מחולחלת/loosely put in (i.e., not packed entirely) while it is a vessel, it also took it (i.e., teaches it) here.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:7:2)


**כְּנֶגְדּוֹ עַד הַתְּהוֹם טָמֵא.** וְהַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר. וּבְשֶׁאֵינָהּ גְּבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח אַיְרֵי:


כנגדו עד התהום טמא – but the rest is ritually pure. And we are speaking when it is not a handbreadth high from the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:7:3)


**בְּתוֹכָהּ אוֹ בַבַּיִת תּוֹכָהּ וְהַבַּיִת טְמֵאִים.** מִשּׁוּם דְּפִיהָ לִפְנִים. אֲבָל לְעֵיל דְּפִיהָ לַחוּץ קָתָנֵי טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא הַבַּיִת וְהַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר:


תוכה או בבית תוכה והבית טמאים – because its walls are inside. But above (see Mishnah 1), where its walls are outside, it is taught that the defilement is in the house, there is no impurity other than in the house, but the rest is pure.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:8:1)


**אֶלָּא גַבָּהּ.** וְתוֹכָהּ טָמֵא כְּשֶׁהַטֻּמִאָה בַּבַּיִת, מִשּׁוּם דְּפִיהָ לִפְנִים. אֲבָל לְעֵיל דְּפִיהָ לַחוּץ, תּוֹכָהּ וְגַבָּהּ טָהוֹר:


אלא גבה – but inside is impure when the defilement is in the house, because its walls are inside. But above (see Mishnah 1), its walls are outside, its inside and on top of it are pure.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:9:1)


**הָיְתָה מְמַלְּאָה אֶת כָּל הַבַּיִת.** שֶׁכֻּלָּהּ בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת לִפְנִים וְיוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל שׁוּלֶיהָ וּפִיהָ מַגִּיעַ לִשְׁמֵי קוֹרָה, שֶׁאֵין בֵּינָהּ לַתִּקְרָה פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


היתה ממלאה את כל הבית – for all of it (i.e., the defilement) is within the house inside and it sits on its rims and its mouth reaches the top of the beam, for there isn’t between it and the ceiling an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:9:2)


**טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכָהּ הַבַּיִת טָמֵא טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ טָהוֹר.** מִידִי דַּהֲוָה אַבִּיב שֶׁהוּא קָמוּר תַּחַת הַבַּיִת שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח וְאֵין בִּיצִיאָתוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק ג':


טומאה בתוכה הבית טמא אמא בבית מה שבתוכה טהור – since this resembles a pipe/gutter that is arched/bent over underneath the house, that has an opening of a handbreadth, and there isn’t in its exiting an opening of a handbreadth as is taught in the Mishnah above in Chapter 3 [Mishnah 7].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:9:3)


**בֵּין עוֹמֶדֶת.** כְּלוֹמַר, דִּין זֶה נוֹהֵג בֵּין עוֹמֶדֶת וּמַגַּעַת לַקּוֹרוֹת בֵּין מֻטָּה עַל צִדָּהּ וְאֵין בֵּין פִּיהָ לְדֹפֶן הַבַּיִת פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


בין עומדת – that is to say, this law is in vogue/practice whether it is standing and it reaches the beams, or whether it is lying on its side and there isn’t between its mouth and the wall of the house an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:9:4)


**בֵּין אַחַת בֵּין שְׁתָּיִם.** זוֹ עַל זוֹ וְאֵין בֵּין פִּי הַשְּׁנִיָּה לַקּוֹרוֹת פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, וְכֵן אֵין בֵּין שׁוּלֵי הַשְּׁנִיָּה לְפִיהָ שֶׁל רִאשׁוֹנָה פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


בין אחת בין שתים – this over that and there isn’t between the second opening to the beams an opening of a handbreadth, and similarly, there isn’t between the second rim and its mouth of the first an opening of a handbreadth.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:10:1)


**הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר.** שֶׁהַמַּשְׁקוֹף יוֹרֵד וְסוֹתֵם אֶת פִּי הַכַּוֶּרֶת. וּבְכַוֶּרֶת שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְחֻלְחֶלֶת אַיְרֵי. דְּאִי בִמְחֻלְחֶלֶת, דֶּרֶךְ הַחִלְחוּל הָיְתָה טֻמְאָה יוֹצְאָה לַבַּיִת כְּכָל הָנֵי דִּלְעֵיל:


הבית טהור – that the lintel descends and seals/stops up the mouth of the hive, and we are speaking of a case where the hive is not hollowed out. For if it is hollowed out, it is the manner of the hollowing out that the defilement would go out to the house like those mentioned above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:10:2)


**טֻמְאָה בַבַּיִת מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ טָהוֹר.** גָּרְסִינַן. שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה יוֹצְאָה לַחוּץ וְאֵינָהּ נִכְנֶסֶת לַכַּוֶּרֶת. וּבְרֹב סְפָרִים גּוֹרְסִין, מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ טָמֵא, לְפִי שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה יוֹצְאָה מִן הַבַּיִת דֶּרֶךְ פִּתְחָהּ וּמְטַמֵּא מַה שֶּׁבַּכַּוֶּרֶת, וְהִיא עוֹמֶדֶת בְּתוֹךְ הַפֶּתַח:


טומאה בבית מה שבתוכה טהור – we have this reading. That the defilement goes outside and doesn’t enter in into the hive. But in most of the books we have the reading, that what is within it is impure because the defilement leaves from the house via its opening that defiles what is in the hive, and it stand within the doorway.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:11:1)


**בָּאֲוִיר.** בְּחָצֵר אוֹ בְגִנָּה שֶׁאֵין שָׁם אֹהֶל, וְדִינָהּ כְּדִין כַּוֶּרֶת שֶׁהִיא לְתוֹךְ הַפֶּתַח וּפִיהָ לַחוּץ כְּדִתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ פִּרְקִין. וְכֵן נַמִּי גְּבוֹהָה טֶפַח דְּהָכָא כִגְבוֹהָה טֶפַח דְּרֵישׁ פִּרְקִין, אֶלָּא דְּכָאן אֵין שַׁיָּךְ לְהַזְכִּיר בַּיִת, דִּבְעוֹמֶדֶת תַּחַת הָאֲוִיר עָסְקִינַן:


באויר – in the courtyard or in the garden, where there isn’t a tent there. And its law is like of the hive that is in the midst of the doorway and its mouth is outside, as it is taught in the Mishnah at the beginning of the chapter (see Mishnah 1). And similarly, also if it is above the ground by a handbreadth, like it is above the ground a handbreadth of here at the beginning of the chapter (see Mishnah 2). But that of here, it doesn’t belong to mention a house, for we are dealing with a case where one is standing underneath the airspace.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:12:1)
