**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא כֶלִי.** הֵיכָא דְעוֹמֶדֶת בָּאֲוִיר אֵין שַׁיָּךְ לְהַזְכִּיר מְחֻלְחֶלֶת, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי לֹא תָנָא הָכָא אוֹ אֲפוּצָה כִּדְתָנֵי לְעֵיל, דְּאֵין שַׁיָּךְ אֲפוּצָה אֶלָּא בִּמְחֻלְחֶלֶת:


בזמן שהיא כלי – where it stands in the airspace it doesn’t belong to mention that which is loosely put in/hollow, and because of this, it does not teach here, “or it was made narrower by squeezing the parts together,” as is taught above (see Mishnah 3), for the issue of making it narrower by squeezing the parts together (i.e., pressing the lid down) only applies with something that is put in loosely.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:12:2)


**אוֹ מַחֲזֶקֶת אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה כְּדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים.** דִּפְלִיגֵי אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא וְסָבְרֵי דְּמַחְזֶקֶת אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה, כְּנִפְחֲתָה וְנִתְבַּטְּלָה מִתּוֹרַת כְּלִי דָמְיָא, וּמַצֶּלֶת אַתַּחְתֶּיהָ וְאַתּוֹכָהּ וְעַל גַּבָּהּ:


או מחזקת ארבעים סאה כדברי חכמים – which is in conflict with the first Tanna/teacher as he holds that it holds forty Se’ah. When it is damaged, it is voided from the status of being like a vessel, but it protects what is underneath it and within it and above it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:12:3)


**כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת נָתוּן תַּחְתֶּיהָ.** דִּינָהּ כְּנִפְחֲתָה וְאֵינָהּ גְּבוֹהָה וּפִיהָ לַחוּץ דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל:


כזית מן המת נתון תחתיה – its law is like it is damaged and it is not [a handbreadth] high and its mouth is towards the outside, as it is taught in the Mishnah (see Mishnah 3) above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:12:4)


**הָיְתָה גְבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח.** הַךְ נַמִּי דִּינָהּ כְּדִין כַּוֶּרֶת שֶׁפִּיהָ לַחוּץ וּפְחוּתָה וּפְקוּקָה וּגְבוֹהָה טֶפַח דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל:


היתה גבוהה מן הארץ טפח – these also, its law is like that of a hive whose mouth is towards the outside and it is damaged and stuffed up [with straw] and above [the ground] a handbreadth as it is taught in the Mishnah (see Mishnah 5) above.



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:1)


**הָיְתָה יוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל שׁוּלֶיהָ וֽכוּ'‏ טֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ אוֹ בְתוֹכָהּ אוֹ עַל גַּבָּהּ.** כָּל עַל גַּבָּהּ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ גַּבֵּי כַּוֶּרֶת כְּשֶׁהִיא יוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל שׁוּלֶיהָ וּפִיהָ לְמַעְלָה, הַיְנוּ שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה מַאֲהִיל חוּצָה [לָהּ] לְמַעְלָה מִפִּיהָ:


היתה יושבת על שוליה וכו' טומאה תחתיה או בתוכה או על גבה – everything that is on top of it. As we taught concerning the hive that is sitting on its rims and its mouth is above, that is, that the defilement overshadows outward [to it] above rom its mouth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:2)


**בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה.** אַף עַל גַּב דִּלְעֵיל בְּמֻטָּה עַל צִדָּהּ וְטֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ אוֹ עַל גַּבָּהּ מַצֶּלֶת עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ, הָכָא דְפִיהָ לְמַעְלָה מֵאַחַר דְּאֵין מַצֶּלֶת עַל מַה שֶּׁחוּצָה לָהּ אֵין מַצֶּלֶת עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ, כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין הֶפְסֵק בֵּין אֲוִיר שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ לָאֲוִיר שֶׁל מַעְלָה בְּחוּצָה לָהּ. וְדַוְקָא כְנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת טָמֵא וְלֹא מִן הַצְּדָדִים, כֵּיוָן דִּפְחוּתָה וְאֵין גְּבוֹהָה טֶפַח, שֶׁאֵין שָׁם אֹהֶל לְהָבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לַצְּדָדִין.


בוקעת ועולה – even though above (see Mishnah 11) when it is lying on its side, and the defilement is underneath it, or on top of it, it protects what is inside of it, here its wall is above for it doesn’t protect over what is outside of it [also] doesn’t protect on what is within it, since there is no interruption between the airspace that is within it to the airspace that is above and outside of it. But specifically, opposite the olive’s bulk of impurity and not from the sides, since it is damaged and is not a handbreadth above the ground, for there is no tent there to bring the defilement to the sides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:3)


**אֲבָל הָיְתָה גְבוֹהָה טֶפַח אוֹ מְכֻסָּה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ גְּבוֹהָה טֶפַח.


אבל היתה גבוהה טפח או מכוסה – even though it is not a handbreadth above [the ground].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:4)


**אוֹ כְפָאָהּ עַל פִּיהָ.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ אֹהֶל לְהָבִיא טֻמְאָה:


או כפאה על פיה (or turned upside down on its mouth) – where there is a tent to bring the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:13:5)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** וְאֵין הַכִּסּוּי חָשׁוּב לְהַפְסִיק בֵּין אֲוִיר תּוֹכָהּ לָאֲוִיר שֶׁלְּמַעְלָה, דְּלֹא דָמֵי כְלָל לְמֻטָּה עַל צִדָּהּ. וְדַוְקָא דִמְכֻסָּה בִכְפִישָׁה אוֹ בִשְׁאָר כְּלִי אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, אֲבָל מְכֻסָּה בְנֶסֶר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁטֻּמְאָה עַל גַּבָּהּ אֵין הַטֻּמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת אֶת הַנֶּסֶר, כְּדִתְנַן לְעֵיל פֶּרֶק ה'‏ [מִשְׁנָה ו'] גַּבֵּי כַוֶּרֶת שֶׁהִיא בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת:


הכל טמא – and the covering is not considered to interrupt/form a partition between the airspace within it to the airspace that is above it, and it is not similar at all to lying on its side. And specially when it is covered with an inverted vessel – divided into two vessels by the bottom between or with other vessels even though it is not susceptible to receive ritual impurity, but when it is covered by a board, even though the defilement is above it, the defilement does not break through the board, as is taught in the Mishnah above in Chapter 5 [Mishnah 6] concerning a hive which is within the house.



### Mishnah 14

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:1)


**הָיְתָה פְחוּתָה.** שֶׁנִּפְחֲתָה וּבָטְלָה מִתּוֹרַת כְּלִי:


היתה פחותה – that it became damaged and it is voided from the category of being a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:2)


**כְּדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים.** דִּפְלִיגֵי אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא וּמַשְׁווּ מַחְזֶקֶת אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה לִפְחוּתָה וּפְקוּקָה בְקַשׁ:


כדברי חכמים – which is in conflict with the first Tanna/teacher and compares something that holds forty Se’ah to something that is damaged and stuffed with straw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:3)


**אֵין טֻמְאָה עוֹלָה לָהּ.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ אֵינָהּ עוֹלָה לְתוֹכָהּ:


אין טומאה עלוה לה – at the time that the defilement is below it, it does not ascend into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:4)


**אַף לֹא יוֹרֶדֶת מִמֶּנָּה.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה לְתוֹכָהּ אֵינָהּ יוֹרֶדֶת תַּחְתֶּיהָ:


אף לא יורדת ממנה - at the time that the defilement is within it, it doesn’t descend underneath it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:5)


**תַּחְתֶּיהָ טָמֵא.** וְתוֹכָהּ וְגַבָּהּ טָהוֹר:


תחתיה טמא – but inside it/within it and on top of it, it is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:14:6)


**כְּנֶגְדּוֹ עַד הָרָקִיעַ טָמֵא.** אֲבָל תַּחְתֶּיהָ טָהוֹר:


כנגדו עד הרקיע טמא – but underneath it is pure.



### Mishnah 15

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:1)


**אָרוֹן שֶׁהִיא רְחָבָה.** הַאי אָרוֹן הוּא שֵׁן סֶלַע שֶׁבּוֹלֵט מִן הָהָר, וְנֶחְצַב בּוֹ קֶבֶר רָחָב מִלְּמַטָּה וְצַר מִלְּמַעְלָה וּפִיו לְמַעְלָה:


ארון שהיא רחבה – this bier/casket is a cliff/peak of a rock that protrudes from the mountain and is hewn/chiseled into a bier that is wide from the bottom and narrow from the top and its mouth is above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:2)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהּ מִלְּמַטָּן טָהוֹר.** דְּמַה שֶּׁכְּנֶגֶד פִּיו נֶחְשָׁב כְּאָרוֹן, אֲבָל שֶׁבַּצְּדָדִים לֹא:


הנוגע בה מלמטה טהור – for what is opposite its mouth is considered like the bier/casket, but not that which is on the sides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:3)


**רָחָב מִלְּמַעְלָה וְצַר מִלְּמַטָּה.** אוֹ שֶׁהָיָה שָׁוֶה, הַנּוֹגֵעַ בּוֹ מִכָּל מָקוֹם טָמֵא, דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר. שֶׁהַכִּסּוּי מְבַקֵּעַ הַטֻּמְאָה בְכֻלּוֹ, שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה עוֹלָה עַד הַכִּסּוּי וְחוֹזֶרֶת וְיוֹרֶדֶת עַל פְּנֵי כֻלָּהּ:


‘רחב מלמעלה וצר מלמטה – or that it was equivalent, a person who touches it from any place is impure, according to the words of Rabbi Eliezer. For the covering splits the defilement in all of it, that the defilement ascends up to the covering and returns and descends over the face of all of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מְטַהֵר מִטֶּפַח וּלְמַטָּה.** דְּאֵין נֶחְשָׁב כְּאָרוֹן אֶלָּא טֶפַח הַסָּמוּךְ לַחֲלַל הָאָרוֹן, הִלְכָּךְ אֵינָהּ בּוֹקַעַת מִטֶּפַח וּלְמַטָּה:


רבי יהושע מטהר – that it is not considered like a bier/coffin other than the handbreadth that is nearest to the cavity of the bier/coffin. Therefore, it does not burst forth from a handbreadth and below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:5)


**הָעֲשׂוּיָה כְמִין קַמְטְרָא.** הוּא כְלִי שֶׁכִּסּוּיוֹ מְכַסֶּה עֳבִי הַדְּפָנוֹת. תַּרְגּוּם לַאֲשֶׁר עַל הַמֶּלְתָּחָה (מלכים ב י), לְדִמְמַנָּא עַל קֻמְטְרַיָּא:


העשויה כמין קמטרא (that is made like a chest/box – for clothes, books) – it is vessel whose covering covers the thickness of the walls. It is the Aramaic for (II Kings 10:22): “He [Jehu] said to the man in charge of the wardrobe: [“Bring out the vestments for all the worshippers of Baal],” for the one appointed over the chest/box.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:6)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהּ מִכָּל מָקוֹם טָמֵא.** דְּכָל שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַכִּסּוּי טָמֵא, וְכִסּוּיוֹ מְכַסֶּה אֶת כֻּלּוֹ:


הנוגע בה מכל מקום טמא – for all that is opposite the covering is impure, for its covering covers all of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:7)


**כְּמִין גְּלוֹסְקוֹס הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהּ מִכָּל מָקוֹם טָהוֹר.** אֵין כִּסּוּי שֶׁלּוֹ אֶלָּא כְנֶגֶד חֲלָלוֹ, כְּיָתֵד הַתָּחוּב בְּנֶקֶב בְּדֹחַק:


כמין גלוסקוס הנוגע בה מכל מקום טהור – its covering is not other than opposite its cavity like a peg that is inserted in a hole/incision in an emergency.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:15:8)


**חוּץ מִמְּקוֹם פְּתִיחָתָהּ.** בַּכִּסּוּי שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד חֲלָלָהּ:


חוץ ממקום פתחתה – with a covering that is opposite its cavity.



### Mishnah 16

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:1)


**חָבִית שֶׁהִיא יוֹשֶׁבֶת.** חָבִיּוֹת שֶׁלָּהֶן צָרוֹת לְמַעְלָה לְצַד פִּיהֶן, וּלְמַטָּה לְצַד הַשּׁוּלַיִם, וּרְחָבוֹת בְּאֶמְצָעָן, וּכְמִין בֶּטֶן רָחָב בּוֹלֵט מֵהֶן בְּאֶמְצָעָן מִבַּחוּץ:


חבית שהיא יושבת – their jars are narrow above to the side of the their mouths, and below to the side of the rims and wide in their middles, and they are like a kind of wide stomach that protrudes from them in their middles from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:2)


**בָּאֲוִיר.** הַיְנוּ בַּאֲוִיר הָעוֹלָם. לַאֲפוֹקֵי תַחַת הָאֹהֶל:


באויר – that is, in the airspace of the world, to exclude that which is under the tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:3)


**קוּרְקוּרָתָהּ.** כְּמוֹ קַרְקָעִיתָהּ, וְהֵן הַשּׁוּלַיִם:


קורקורתה (rimmed bottom of a vessel – corresponding to the bottom – under or on the rim of its bottom) – like its bottom and they are its rims.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:4)


**בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה בּוֹקַעַת וְיוֹרֶדֶת.** וְכָל כֵּלִים שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת טְמֵאִים, בֵּין תַּחְתֶּיהָ בֵּין עַל גַּבָּהּ בֵּין בְּתוֹכָהּ, מִידִי דַהֲוֵי אַכַּוֶּרֶת דִּלְעֵיל [מִשְׁנָה י"ג] הַיּוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל שׁוּלֶיהָ. אֲבָל שֶׁבַּצְּדָדִים שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת טְהוֹרִים:


בוקעת ועולה בוקעת ויורד – and all the vessels that are opposite the olive’s bulk [of defilement] are ritually impure, whether undeath it or whether above it or whether within it, something that is like the hive that is above [Mishnah 13] that sits o its rims. But that which is on the sides which is not opposite the olive’s bulk [of defilement] are pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:5)


**וְהֶחָבִית טְמֵאָה.** לֹא שֶׁתְּהֵא הֶחָבִית עַצְמָהּ טְמֵאָה, דְּהָא הָכָא בְחָבִית שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה עָסְקִינַן כִּדְמוֹכַח בְּסֵיפָא, אֶלָּא הֶחָבִית טְמֵאָה כְּלוֹמַר אַף כֵּלִים שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הֶחָבִית כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טְמֵאִים, מִשּׁוּם דִּסְתָם חָבִית פִּיהָ רָחָב כְּשִׁעוּר קוּרְקוּרָתָהּ, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁאֵין הֶפְסֵק בֵּין אֲוִיר תּוֹכָהּ לָאֲוִיר שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ לְמַעְלָה חוּצָה לָהּ, מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁאֵינָהּ מַצֶּלֶת חוּצָה לָהּ אֵין מַצֶּלֶת בְּתוֹכָהּ:


והחבית טמאה – not that the jar itself is impure but we are dealing here with a jar that is not susceptible to receive ritual defilement as will be proven in the concluding clause [of the Mishnah], but that the jar is impure, meaning to say, even vessels that are outside of the jar opposite the defilement are impure, because a mere vessel,its mouth is wide like the measurement of its rimmed bottom and because there is no interruption between the airspace of its inside to the airspace that is on top of it above it [and] outside of it, because it doesn’t protect what is outside of it, it doesn’t protect what is inside/within it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:6)


**תַּחַת דָּפְנָהּ מִבַּחוּץ טֻמְאָה בוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה בּוֹקַעַת וְיוֹרֶדֶת.** וְטָמֵא חוּץ לֶחָבִית כָּל שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת:


תחת דופנה מבחוץ טומאה בוקעת ועולה בוקעת ויורדת – and anything that is opposite the olive’s bulk [of defilement] is impure outside of the jar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:7)


**וְהֶחָבִית טְהוֹרָה.** הַיְנוּ מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹךְ הֶחָבִית אֲפִלּוּ כְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת. דְּדָפְנֵי הֶחָבִית בּוֹלְטוֹת בָּאֶמְצַע, וּכְשֶׁיֵּשׁ כַּזַּיִת תַּחַת הַדֹּפֶן דָּמֵי דָפְנָהּ לְכַוֶּרֶת שֶׁהִיא מֻטָּה עַל צִדָּהּ דְּתוֹכָהּ טָהוֹר, לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ הֶפְסֵק בֵּין אֲוִיר תּוֹכָהּ שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת לָאֲוִיר שֶׁחוּצָה לָהּ כְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת:


והחבית טהורה – that is, what is within the jar, even opposite the olive’s bulk [of defilement] for the walls of the jar protrude in the middle and when there is an olive’s bulk [of defilement] under the wall, its wall is similar to the hive which is lying on its side, that is inside is pure, because there is an interruption between the airspace of the inside that is opposite the olive’s bulk [of defilement] to the airspace that is outside of opposite the olive’s bulk [of defilement].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:8)


**בְּתוֹכָהּ וְתַחַת דָּפְנָהּ.** כְּלוֹמַר בִּפְנִים בַּחֲלַל דָּפְנָהּ. וְקָרֵי לַהּ תַּחַת דָּפְנָהּ, לְפִי שֶׁהַדֹּפֶן הוֹלֵךְ וְצַר לְצַד פִּיהָ. וְלַאֲפוֹקֵי עַל שׁוּלֶיהָ הַמְכֻוָּן כְּנֶגֶד פִּיהָ:


בתוכה ותחת דופנה – that is to say inside the cavity of its wall. We call it underneath its wall, because the wall goes and becomes narrow to the side of its mouth. And this excludes on its rim that is directed corresponding/opposite to its mouth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:9)


**אִם יֵשׁ בַּדְּפָנוֹת פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** שֶׁבֶּטֶן הֶחָבִית בּוֹלֵט כָּל כָּךְ לַחוּץ שֶׁיֵּשׁ שָׁם בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד עַד כְּנֶגֶד פִּיהָ שֶׁל חָבִית טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח:


אם יש בדפנות פותח טפח – that the belly of jar protrudes so much outward that there is in one place up until corresponding with the mouth of the jar a cubic handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:10)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** שֶׁהָאֹהֶל מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה בְּכָל הֶחָבִית חוּץ מִכְּנֶגֶד פִּיהָ שֶׁאֵין שָׁם אֹהֶל, הִלְכָּךְ כְּנֶגֶד פִּיהָ טָהוֹר:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:11)


**וְאִם לָאו.** דְּאֵין שָׁם טֶפַח:


ואם לאו – but if there isn’t there a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:12)


**טֻמְאָה בוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה.** וְאֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא כְּנֶגֶד הַזַּיִת:


טומאה בוקעת ועולה – and it is not impure other than opposite the olive’s bulk [of defilement].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:13)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים בִּטְהוֹרָה.** בְּחָבִית שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה:


במה דברים אמורים בטהורה – with a jar that is not susceptible to receiving ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:14)


**אֲבָל אִם הָיְתָה טְמֵאָה.** כְּגוֹן סְתָם חָבִית שֶׁנִּצְרְפָה בְּכִבְשָׁן שֶׁהִיא מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה:


אבל אם היתה טמאה – as for example, a mere jar that was smelted in a kiln/furnace, which does receive defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:15)


**אוֹ גְבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח אוֹ מְכֻסָּה אוֹ כְפוּיָה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא טְהוֹרָה:


או גבוהה אן הארץ טפח או מכוסה או כפויה – and even if it is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 9:16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/9:16:16)


**וְטֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ אוֹ בְתוֹכָהּ אוֹ עַל גַּבָּהּ, הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** דְּאִי בַּת קַבּוֹלֵי טֻמְאָה הִיא וְיוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל שׁוּלֶיהָ וְטֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ אוֹ בְתוֹכָהּ אוֹ עַל גַּבָּהּ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מִטַּמֵּא מִגַּבּוֹ נִטְמֵאת כָּאן הֶחָבִית עַצְמָהּ, מִידִי דַהֲוֵי אַכֵּלִים שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ, מֵאַחַר שֶׁאֵינָהּ מֻקֶּפֶת צָמִיד פָּתִיל, דְּאֵין כְּלִי מַצִּיל אֲפִלּוּ עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ אֶלָּא עִם דָּפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין הֶפְסֵק מֵאֲוִיר תּוֹכוֹ לָאֲוִיר שֶׁחוּצָה לוֹ. וְהַאי תַּחְתֶּיהָ הַיְנוּ תַּחַת שׁוּלֶיהָ, דְּלֹא תָנֵי תַחַת דָּפְנָהּ מִבַּחוּץ. וְנִטְמֵאת הֶחָבִית עַצְמָהּ, וְכֵיוָן דְּחָבִית עַצְמָהּ טְמֵאָה הִיא נִטְמְאוּ כָּל כֵּלִים שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּקַיְמָא לָן דְּאֵין אָדָם וְכֵלִים מִטַּמְּאִין מֵאֲוִיר כְּלִי חֶרֶס, הָכָא כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהַזַּיִת תַּחְתֶּיהָ מִטַּמְּאִין מִטַּעַם חִבּוּרִים, מֵאַחַר דְּחָבִית עַצְמָהּ טְמֵאָה. וְכֵן גְּבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח אֲפִלּוּ טְהוֹרָה חֲזִינַן תַּחְתֶּיהָ כְּאִלּוּ הוּא מָלֵא טֻמְאָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ טֻמְאָה עַל גַּבָּהּ. וְהַשְׁתָּא כָּל הָנֵי אֵין דִּינָם שָׁוֶה, דְּאִם הֶחָבִית מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה, מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טָמֵא. וְהַמַּאֲהִיל עַל גַּבָּהּ, כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טָמֵא, שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טָהוֹר. אֲבָל גְּבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח אוֹ מְכֻסָּה אוֹ כְפוּיָה עַל פִּיהָ, הַמַּאֲהִיל עַל גַּבָּהּ אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טָמֵא. וְאֵין לִתְמֹהַּ, כְּפוּיָה עַל פִּיהָ וְטֻמְאָה עַל גַּבָּהּ תַּצִּיל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ כְּכֵלִים טְהוֹרִים הַמֻּקָּפִים צָמִיד פָּתִיל, דְּשָׁאנֵי הָכָא דְלֵיכָּא מֵרוּחַ:


וטומאה תחתיה או בתוכה או על גבה, הכל טמא – that if it susceptible to receive ritual impurity and sits on its rims and the defilement is underneath it or within it or on top of it, even though that an earthenware vessel is not defiled from above it, the jar itself is defiled here. Since this refers to vessels that are within after they are not surrounded by a tight-fitting lid, for a vessel does not protect even on wat is ithin it other than with walls of tents in a place where there isn’t an interruption from the airspace within to the airspace outside of it. And that which is below which is under its rims, and it doesn’t teach under its wall from the outside. But the jar itself is defiled, and since the jar itself is impure, all of the vessels that are within are defiled even that which is not opposite the defilement, for even though we hold that humans and vessels are not defiled from the airspace of an earthenware vessel, here, all the while that the olive’s bulk [of defilement] is underneath it, it defiles from the reason of attachments, since after the jar itself is impure. And similarly, something a handbreadth above from the ground even that is pure we view that which is underneath it as if it filled with defilement, and even the defilement above it. But now, all of these, their law is not equivalent (i.e., the same), for if the jar is susceptible to receive ritual defilement, whatever is within it, even if it is not opposite the defilement is impure. And one who overshadows over it from above, opposite the defilement is impure, but that which is not opposite the defilement is pure. But if it is a handbreadth above the ground or it is covered or turned upside down on its mouth, one who overshadows over it even that which is not opposite the defilement is impure. And we should not be astonished, that which is turned upside downs on its mouth and the defilement is above it, whatever is within it is like the pure vessels that are surrounded with a tight-fitting lid, but it is different here for there is no open space.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:1)


**אֲרֻבָּה שֶׁהִיא בְתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת.** וּמְגֻלָּה לָאֲוִיר, שֶׁאֵין עָלֶיהָ גַּג מִלְּמַעְלָה:


ארובה שהיא בתוך הבית (a hatchway which is in the midst of the house) - and it is uncovered to the airspace, for there is no upper chamber roof from above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:2)


**כְּנֶגֶד אֲרֻבָּה טָהוֹר.** כֵּלִים הַמֻּנָּחִים לְמַטָּה בָּאָרֶץ כְּנֶגֶד הָאֲרֻבָּה וְרוֹאִים פְּנֵי הָרָקִיעַ, טְהוֹרִים:


כנגד ארובה טהור (that which is opposite the hatchway) – vessels that are placed below on the ground opposite the hatchway and they see the face of the sky are ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:3)


**מִלְמַעְלָן.** עַל פִּי הָאֲרֻבָּה:


מלמעלן (from above) – on the garret window in the wall projecting above the flat roof.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:4)


**עֵרֵב אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** וְהַכֹּל טָמֵא. הַבַּיִת טָמֵא וְהַנּוֹתֵן רַגְלוֹ גַּם כֵּן טָמֵא:


עירב את הטומאה (he combined the defilement) – and everything is impure. The house is impure and the person who places his foot in it is also impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:1:5)


**וּכְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טָמֵא.** שֶׁהַזַּיִת נִמְשַׁךְ לְכָאן וּלְכָאן:


וכנגד הטומאה טמא – that the olive’s bulk [of defilement] is stretched to here and to there.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:1)


**אֵין בָּאֲרֻבָּה פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** הָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דַּאֲפִלּוּ בְּפָחוֹת מִטֶּפַח לֹא אָמְרִינַן בְּטֻמְאָה לָבוּד:


אין בארבה פותח פתח – surely it comes to tell us that with less than a handbreadth we don’t say that with defilement there is a joining/לבוד (i.e., that two solid surfaces are considered to be joined if there is a gap of less than three handbreadths between them).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:2)


**נָתַן אֶת רַגְלוֹ.** טָהוֹר הַנּוֹתֵן, דִּכְסָתוּם דָּמִי לְגַבֵּיהּ. שֶׁאֵין טֻמְאָה נִכְנֶסֶת לְאֹהֶל וְלֹא יוֹצְאָה מִמֶּנּוּ בְּפָחוֹת מִפּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


נתן את רגלו – the person who sets [his foot] is pure, for it resembles something concealed concerning it, for the defilement does not enter into the tent and doesn’t leave from it with less than an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:3)


**אִם טֻמְאָה קָדְמָה אֶת רַגְלוֹ טָמֵא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי בְעֵת שֶׁנָּתַן רַגְלוֹ הֶאֱהִיל עַל הַטֻּמְאָה:


אם טומאה קדמה את רגלו טמא – that surely at the time when he set his foot, he overshadowed over the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:4)


**וְאִם רַגְלוֹ קָדְמָה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה טָהוֹר.** שֶׁקֹּדֶם שֶׁבָּאָה הַטֻּמְאָה כְּבָר נַעֲשָׂה אֹהֶל סָתוּם, וּבְשָׁעָה שֶׁנִּכְנָס בּוֹ הַטֻּמְאָה אֵין שָׁם בָּאֲרֻבָּה פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח כְּדֵי שֶׁתֵּצֵא מִמֶּנָּה הַטֻּמְאָה:


ואם רגלו קדמה את הטומאה טהור – that before the defilement came, a concealed tent had already been made, and at the time that the defilement enters there is no garret window in the wall projecting above the flat roof that is open a handbreadth in order that the defilement can leave from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:2:5)


**שְׁתֵּי רַגְלַיִם זוֹ עַל גַּב זוֹ.** שֶׁל שְׁנֵי אֲנָשִׁים, וְנָתַן הָרִאשׁוֹן רַגְלוֹ עַל פִּי אֲרֻבָּה וְרֶגֶל שֶׁל שֵׁנִי מֻנָּח עַל רַגְלוֹ שֶׁל רִאשׁוֹן, וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִכְנְסָה טֻמְאָה בַבַּיִת תַּחַת הָאֲרֻבָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ הֵסִיר הָרִאשׁוֹן אֶת רַגְלוֹ וְנִמְצֵאת רֶגֶל הַשֵּׁנִי מֻנַּחַת עַל פִּי הָאֲרֻבָּה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשֵּׁנִי זֶה הֶאֱהִיל עַל הַטֻּמְאָה כְּשֶׁהֵסִיר הָרִאשׁוֹן אֶת רַגְלוֹ, הַשֵּׁנִי טָהוֹר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁקָּדְמָה רַגְלוֹ שֶׁל רִאשׁוֹן אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן וְלֹא כְרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


שתי רגלים זו על גב זו – of two people, and the first placed his foot on top of the garret window in the wall and the foot of the second [person] is lying on the foot of the first [person], and afterwards, the defilement enters the house underneath the garret window in the wall near the roof, and after that, the first [person] removes his leg and it is found that the foot of the second [person] is placed on the garret window, and even though this second [foot] overshadows over he defilement when the first [person] removed his foot, the second [person] is ritually pure, because the foot of the first [person] preceded the defilement. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon nor according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:3:1)


**כְּנֶגֶד אֲרֻבָּה.** בַּאֲרֻבָּה שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


כנגד ארובה – in the garret window in the wall projecting above the flat roof that doesn’t have an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:3:2)


**וּכְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טָמֵא.** הַיְנוּ הַמַּאֲהִיל עַל הַטֻּמְאָה:


וכנגד הטומאה טמא – that is it overshadows over the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:3:3)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁתֵּחָלֵק.** שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בְּמִקְצָת הַטֻּמְאָה שִׁעוּר כְּדֵי לְטַמֵּא בְּאֹהֶל, דְּהַיְנוּ כְשֶׁיִּהְיֶה בְכֻלָּהּ שְׁנֵי זֵיתִים אוֹ יוֹתֵר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


כדי שתחלק – that there will be in the part of uncleanness a measure in order to defile the tent, that is, that there will be in all of it two olive’s bulk or more. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:1)


**אֲרֻבּוֹת זוֹ עַל גַּב זוֹ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁיֵּשׁ אֲרֻבָּה בֵּין בַּיִת לָעֲלִיָּה וְיֵשׁ עוֹד אֲרֻבָּה אַחֶרֶת בְּגַג הָעֲלִיָּה מְכֻוֶּנֶת כְּנֶגְדָּהּ, וּבְכָל אַחַת וְאַחַת פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


ארובות זו על גב זו – as for example, here is a garret window in the wall projecting above the flat roof between the house and the upper chamber and there is another turret window in the roof of the upper chamber directed against it, and in each of them there is an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:2)


**בֵּין מִלְמַעְלָן.** בָּאֲרֻבָּה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה:


בין מלמעלה – in the upper garret window/aperture in the roof looking to the ground floor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:3)


**בֵּין מִלְּמַטָּן.** בָּאֲרֻבָּה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה:


בין מלמטה – in the lower garret window/aperture looking to the ground floor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:4)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** אֲפִלּוּ מַה שֶּׁבָּעֲלִיָּה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנָּתַן בַּתַּחְתּוֹנָה וְלֹא בָּעֶלְיוֹנָה. דְּכֵיוָן דְּדָבָר הַמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה הוּא וְאֵין חוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה שֶׁבַּבַּיִת, רוֹאִין אוֹתוֹ כְאִלּוּ הוּא נָתוּן בְּפִי הָאֲרֻבָּה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה וְעֵרֵב אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה:


הכל טמא – even what is in the upper chamber. Even though he placed [his foot] in the lower part and not the upper part, for since it is something that is susceptible to receive defilement, it does not interpose before the defilement in the house, and we see it as if it is placed in the mouth of the upper garret window in the wall projecting above the flat roof and mixing/combining the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:5)


**מִמֶּנּוּ וּלְמַטָּן טָמֵא.** דְּנַעֲשָׂה אֹהֶל סָתוּם וְכָל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ טָמֵא:


ממנו ולמטן טמא – that it became a concealed tent and everything that is within it is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:4:6)


**מִמֶּנּוּ וּלְמַעְלָן טָהוֹר.** דְּחוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


ממנו ולמעלן טהור – and it interposes in front of the defilement.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:5:1)


**אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא הַתַּחְתּוֹן.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּטֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד אֲרֻבָּה, אֵין טֻמְאָה יוֹצְאָה בְּפָחוֹת מִטֶּפַח, וּטְהוֹרָה הָעֲלִיָּה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֲרֻבָּה שֶׁל עֲלִיָּה סְתוּמָה:


אין טמא אלא התחתון – for since the defilement is in the house but not opposite the garret window, the defilement does not leave/go out with less than a handbreadth [of an opening], and the upper chamber is pure even though the garret window of the upper chamber is concealed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:5:2)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּטֻמְאָה כְנֶגֶד אֲרֻבּוֹת וְדָבָר טָמֵא אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ, רוֹאִין אוֹתוֹ כְאִלּוּ הוּא נָתוּן בְּפִי הָאֲרֻבָּה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה וְעֵרֵב אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה:


הכל טמא – for since the defilement is opposite the garret windows and an impure thing does not interpose, we see it as if it is placed in the mouth of the upper garret window and it combines/mixes the defilement.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:1)


**אֲרֻבָּה.** שֶׁהִיא רוֹאָה אֶת הָאֲוִיר:


ארובה – that it sees the airspace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:2)


**וּקְדֵרָה נְתוּנָה תַחְתֶּיהָ.** בָּאָרֶץ כְּנֶגֶד הָאֲרֻבָּה. וְאֵינָהּ רְחָבָה כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁאִם תַּעֲלֶה כְנֶגֶד הָאֲרֻבָּה שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שִׂפְתוֹתֶיהָ נוֹגְעוֹת בָּאֲרֻבָּה, אֶלָּא יוֹצֵאת וְנִכְנֶסֶת בְּרֶוַח:


וקדירה נתונה תחתיה – on the ground opposite the garret window. But it is not so wide that if it rises opposite the garret window that its rims would touch the garret window, but rather it goes out and enters with wide space.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:3)


**טֻמְאָה בוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה.** וְהַבַּיִת טָהוֹר, וְכָל הַמַּאֲהִיל עָלֶיהָ שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טָהוֹר. אֲבָל כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טָמֵא, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מִטַּמֵּא מִגַּבּוֹ, הָכָא דְטֻמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת תּוֹכָהּ נַמִּי טָמֵא, אַף עַל גַּב דְּטֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ. וְכָל מַה שֶּׁבַּקְּדֵרָה טָמֵא וַאֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה:


טומאה בוקעת ועולה – but the house is ritually pure, and everything that overshadows it, which is not opposite the defilement is pure. But opposite the defilement is impure, for even though an earthenware vessel is not defiled from above it, here, that the defilement bursts forth within it is also impure, even though the defilement is below it. And all that is in the pot is impure, and even that which is not opposite the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:4)


**תַּחְתֶּיהָ וְהַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** שֶׁהַקְּדֵרָה מַאֲהֶלֶת עַל הַטֻּמְאָה, הוֹאִיל וּגְבוֹהָה טֶפַח:


תחתיה והבית טמא – that the pot overshadows over the defilement, since it is a handbreadth higher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:5)


**תּוֹכָהּ וְגַבָּהּ טָהוֹר.** שֶׁהַקְּדֵרָה חוֹצֶצֶת בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה וּמַצֶּלֶת עִם דָּפְנוֹת אֹהָלִים:


תוכה וגבה טהור – that the pot interposes in front of the defilement and protects with the walls of the tents.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:6:6)


**בְּתוֹכָהּ אוֹ עַל גַּבָּהּ הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** תּוֹכָהּ וְגַבָּהּ וְתַחְתֶּיהָ וְהַבַּיִת. דְּטֻמְאָה שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ בּוֹקַעַת תַּחְתֶּיהָ, וַהֲרֵי גְּבוֹהָה טֶפַח, וְשׁוּב אֵין מַצֶּלֶת כֵּיוָן דִּקְדֵרָה טְמֵאָה:


בתוכה או על גבה הכל טמא – within it and on top of it and below it and the house. For the defilement that is upon it bursts forth underneath it, for it is a handbreadth higher, and further it doesn’t protect since the pot is impure.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:1)


**הָיְתָה נְתוּנָה.** אַקְּדֵרָה קָאֵי:


היתה נתונה – it refers to the pot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:2)


**בְּצַד הָאַסְקֻפָּה.** שֶׁהָיְתָה מֻנַּחַת עַל הָאַסְקֻפָּה מִבַּחוּץ וּמְשׁוּכָה לִפְנִים כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁאִם תַּעֲלֶה יֵשׁ מִמֶּנָּה טֶפַח תַּחַת הַמַּשְׁקוֹף. וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהַקְּדֵרָה רְחָבָה מִלְּמַטָּה הַרְבֵּה וְצָרָה מִלְּמַעְלָה כְלַפֵּי פִּיהָ, דְּכִי קַיְמָא טֶפַח תַּחַת הַמַּשְׁקוֹף עֲדַיִן כָּל פִּיהָ לַחוּץ:


בצד אסקופה (at the side of the lintel/threshold, lower door-sill) – that it was placed on the threshold from the outside and stretched inwards so much that if you would ascend there is from it a handbreadth underneath the lintel. And as for example, that the pot was very wide from the bottom and narrow from the top towards its mouth, for when there exists a handbreadth underneath the lintel, still all of its mouth is outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:3)


**טֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ.** בִּרְצוּצָה אַיְרֵי:


טומאה תחתיה – it speaks of pressed ritual impurity (i.e., when there is less than a handbreadth between the corpse and the roof or covering, and the ritual impurity passes through the roof and rises upward indefinitely).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:4)


**בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה.** וְהַבַּיִת טָהוֹר. וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה מִן הַמַּשְׁקוֹף וְלַחוּץ, דְּאִי בְטֶפַח הַמָּשׁוּךְ לִפְנִים הָיָה הַבַּיִת טָמֵא:


בוקעת ועולה – but the house is pure. And for example, that the defilement is from the lintel and outward. For if it was stretched a handbreadth inside, the house is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:5)


**תַּחְתֶּיהָ וְהַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** כֵּיוָן דְּאִיכָּא טֶפַח מִמֶּנָּה לִפְנִים שֶׁמֵּבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת:


תחתיה והבית טמא – since that there is a handbreadth from it inside that brings the defilement into the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:6)


**תּוֹכָהּ וְגַבָּהּ טָהוֹר.** דְּנִצּוֹלֶת עִם דֹּפֶן הַבַּיִת:


תוכה וגבה טהור – that it (i.e., the inside and on top of it) is protected with the wall of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:7)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** דְּנִטְמְאָה הַקְּדֵרָה וְאֵינָהּ חוֹצֶצֶת, וְרוֹאִין אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה כְאִלּוּ הִיא תַחְתֶּיהָ, וְהַבַּיִת טָמֵא:


הכל טמא – that the pot was defiled and it doesn’t interpose. But we view the defilement as if it is beneath it and that the house is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:8)


**אוֹ מֻדְבֶּקֶת.** שֶׁאֵין הַקְּדֵרָה תַחַת הַמַּשְׁקוֹף כְּלָל אֶלָּא לְמַעְלָה דְּבוּקָה בְצַד הַמַּשְׁקוֹף, וְאֵין רֹחַב דְּבִיקָתָהּ טֶפַח:


מודבקת – that the pot is not underneath the lintel at all, but rather above it joined at the side of the lintel, and the width of its joining is not a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 10:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/10:7:9)


**אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא תַחְתֶּיהָ.** כֵּיוָן דְּלֵיכָּא טֶפַח תַּחַת הַמַּשְׁקוֹף אֵין מֵבִיא טֻמְאָה לַבַּיִת, וְהַבַּיִת וְתוֹכָהּ וְגַבָּהּ הַכֹּל טָהוֹר:


אין טמא אלא תחתיה – since there isn’t a handbreadth underneath the lentil, it doesn’t bring defilement to the house, and the house, and what is within it and what is above it, all are ritually pure.



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:1:1)


**הַבַּיִת שֶׁנִּסְדַּק.** גַּג הַבַּיִת שֶׁנֶּחֱלַק עַל פְּנֵי כֻלּוֹ לִשְׁנֵי חֲלָקִים:


הבית שנסדק – the roof of the house that was divided on the face of all of it to two parts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:1:2)


**טֻמְאָה בַחוּץ.** לַצַּד הַחִיצוֹן שֶׁבּוֹ הַפֶּתַח:


טומאה בחוץ – to the outer side where the opening is.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:1:3)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבִּפְנִים טְהוֹרִים.** שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ טֻמְאָה לִכָּנֵס אֶלָּא לָצֵאת:


כלים שבפנים טהורים – for it is not the manner of defilement to enter but only to leave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:1:4)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא מְפָרֵשׁ כָּל שֶׁהוּא דְבֵית הִלֵּל וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּהֵא כְּחוּט הַמִּשְׁקֹלֶת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּבֵית הִלֵּל:


ובית הלל אומרים כל שהוא – In the Tosefta (see Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 12, Halakha 1) that “any amount” which is the opinion of the School of Hillel is like the [breadth of the] plumb-line. And the Halakha is according to the School of Hillel.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:2:1)


**אַכְסַדְרָה שֶׁנִּסְדְּקָה.** תִּקְרַת הָאַכְסַדְרָה שֶׁנֶּחְלְקָה לִשְׁנַיִם:


אכסדרה שנסדקה (a covered place in front of the house that was split) – the ceiling of the portico that was divided into two.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:2:2)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבַּצַּד הַשֵּׁנִי טְהוֹרִים.** שֶׁאֲוִיר הַסֶּדֶק מַפְסִיק, וְגוֹרֵם שֶׁאֵין הַטֻּמְאָה עוֹבֶרֶת לַצַּד הַשֵּׁנִי:


כלים שבצד השני טהורים – for the airspace of the split interrupts, and causes that the defilement does not pass to the second side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:2:3)


**עֵרֵב אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** לְפִי שֶׁנִּסְתַּם הָאֲוִיר:


עירב את הטומאה – because the airspace was closed/concealed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:2:4)


**בָּאָרֶץ.** בְּרִצְפַּת הָאַכְסַדְרָה כְּנֶגֶד הַסֶּדֶק שֶׁבַּתִּקְרָה:


בארץ – on the floor of the portico opposite the split that is in the ceiling.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:1)


**סָגוֹס.** בֶּגֶד צֶמֶר עָב מְאֹד:


סגוס (a coarse woolen blanket, mattress to sleep on) - a very thick woolen cloth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:2)


**וְכֹפֶת.** קוֹרָה עָבָה. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגֹּבַהּ עָבְיָן יוֹתֵר מִטֶּפַח:


וכוסת – a thick beam. Even though it is high, its thickness is more than a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:3)


**אֵינָן מְבִיאִין אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** אִם נְתָנָן בָּאָרֶץ כְּנֶגֶד הַסֶּדֶק שֶׁבְּתִקְרַת הָאַכְסַדְרָה, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יֵשׁ תַּחְתֵּיהֶן חָלָל טֶפַח:


אין מביאין את הטומאה – if he placed them on the ground opposite the split that is in the ceiling of the portico, unless there is underneath them a cavity of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:4)


**קְפוּלִין.** כְּמוֹ טַלִּית מְקֻפֶּלֶת. בְּגָדִים הַרְבֵּה מְקֻפָּלִים זוֹ עַל זוֹ:


קפולין – like a folded cloak/Tallit. Many clothes are folded one upon the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:5)


**עַד שֶׁתִּהְיֶה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה גְבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַבְּגָדִים נְתוּנִים תַּחְתֶּיהָ הָעֶלְיוֹנָה מְבִיאָה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, הוֹאִיל וּגְבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח:


עד שתהא העליונה גבוהה מן הארץ טפח – and even though that the clothes are placed underneath it, the upper one brings the defilement, since it is a handbreadth above the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:6)


**הָיָה אָדָם נָתוּן שָׁם.** בָּאָרֶץ כְּנֶגֶד הַסֶּדֶק:


היה אדם נתון שם – on the ground opposite the split.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:3:7)


**חָלוּל.** חֲלַל הַגּוּף נֶחֱשָׁב חָלָל אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּנֵי מֵעָיו לְתוֹכוֹ:


חלול – avity of the body is considered as a cavity, even though that his intestines are within it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:4:1)


**וְהֶאֱהִיל עַל קוֹבְרֵי הַמֵּת.** הַיְנוּ שֶׁהֶאֱהִיל עַל הַמֵּת:


והאהיל על קוברי המת – that is that he overshadowed over the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:4:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** בֵּית שַׁמַּאי לְטַעֲמַיְהוּ, דַּחֲלַל גּוּפוֹ לָא חֲשִׁיב חָלָל, הִלְכָּךְ אֵין מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת. וּבֵית הִלֵּל לְטַעֲמַיְהוּ דְחָשְׁבֵי לֵיהּ חָלָל, הִלְכָּךְ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגּוּפוֹ דָבוּק בַּחַלּוֹן, צַד עֶלְיוֹן שֶׁבּוֹ מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת, דְּהָא אִיכָּא חֲלַל גּוּפוֹ דַהֲוֵי טֶפַח:


בית שמאי אומרים אינו מביא את הטומאה – The School of Shammai according to its reasoning, that the cavity of is body is not considered an [empty] cavity. Therefore, it does not bring the defilement to the house. But the School of Hillel, according to its reasoning that considers it an [empty] cavity, therefore, even though that his body Is connected to the window, the upper side therein brings the defilements to the house, for there is the cavity of his body that is a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:4:3)


**וּמוֹדִים.** בֵּית שַׁמַּאי בְּאָדָם לָבוּשׁ:


ומודים – The School of Shammai [agrees with the School of Hillel] concerning a person who is dressed.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:5:1)


**הָיָה מֻטָּל עַל הָאַסְקֻפָּה.** מִקְצָתוֹ בְתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת וּמִקְצָתוֹ חוּץ לַבַּיִת:


היה מוטל על האסקופה – part of him was within the house and part of him was outside of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:5:2)


**אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** דְּאֵין כָּאן חָלָל:


אינו מביא את טומאה – for there is no cavity/empty space [here].



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:6:1)


**וְהֶאֱהִילוּ עָלָיו טְהוֹרִים.** עַל מִקְצָת גּוּפוֹ שֶׁבַּחוּץ:


והאהילו עליו טהורים – on part of his body that is outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:6:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מְטַהֲרִין.** דְּאֵין בָּאָה תַחְתָּיו טֻמְאָה שֶׁבַּבַּיִת, דְּאֵין כָּאן חָלָל:


בית שמאי מטהרין – that the defilement that is in the house doesn’t come underneath him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:6:3)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מְטַמְּאִין.** דְּסָבְרֵי אָדָם חָלוּל הוּא וּבָאָה תַחְתָּיו טֻמְאָה, וְאוֹתָן שֶׁהֶאֱהִילוּ עָלָיו בַּחוּץ נִטְמְאוּ:


ובית הלל מטמאין – for they hold that a person is an empty cavity and the defilement comes underneath him, and those that overshadowed upon him outside were defiled.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:1)


**וּמֵת הַכֶּלֶב.** דְּאִלּוּ חַי אֲפִלּוּ כֻלּוֹ בַבַּיִת, הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר, דְּטֻמְאָה בְּלוּעָה בְּבַעֲלֵי חַיִּים אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה:


ומת הכלב – for if it (i.e., the dog) lived, even if all of it was in the house, the house is pure, for absorbed defilement [found] in living creatures is not defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:2)


**וּמֻטָּל עַל הָאַסְקֻפָּה.** וְצַוָּארוֹ לִפְנִים:


ומוטל על האסקופה – and its neck is inside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:3)


**אִם יֵשׁ בְּצַוָּארוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** רֹחַב הַצַּוָּאר טֶפַח. וּמְשַׁעֲרִין אוֹתוֹ עַל יְדֵי חוּט הַמַּקִּיף אֶת הַצַּוָּאר. דְּכָל שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּרָחְבּוֹ טֶפַח יֵשׁ בְּהֶקֵּפוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים:


אם יש בצוארו פותח טפח – the width of the neck is a handbreadth. And they estimate it through a thread that surrounds the neck. For everything that is within the width of a handbreadth, there is in its circumference three handbreadths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:4)


**מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** דְּצַד הָעֶלְיוֹן שֶׁבּוֹ מַאֲהִיל עַל הַטֻּמְאָה וּמַמְשִׁיךְ אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת:


מביא את הטומאה – to the upper side that within it (i.e., the dog), it overshadows over the defilement and the defilement and conducts it into the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:5)


**וְאִם לָאו.** דְּאֵינוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


ואם לאו – that there isn’t an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:6)


**אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** דִּלְעִנְיַן הֲבָאַת טֻמְאָה בָּעִינַן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


אינו מביא את הטומאה – that concerning the matter of bringing the defilement we require an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:7)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר רוֹאִים אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** בּוֹדְקִים בְּגוּפוֹ שֶׁל כֶּלֶב אֶת מְקוֹם הַטֻּמְאָה. אִם הִיא מִן הַמַּשְׁקוֹף וְלִפְנִים, הַבַּיִת טָמֵא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בְּצַוָּארוֹ רֹחַב טֶפַח, דְּטֻמְאָה טְמוּנָה בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁהַבַּיִת הֶאֱהִיל עַל הַטֻּמְאָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר רואים את הטמאה – we check the place of the defilement on the body of the dog. If it (i.e., the defilement in the belly of the dog) is from the lintel and inside, the house is impure even though its neck does not have the thickness of a handbreadth, for hidden defilement bursts forth and ascends, and it is found that the house overshadowed over the defilement. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:8)


**פִּיו לִפְנִים.** וְנֶקֶב זְנָבוֹ מִבַּחוּץ:


פיו לפנים – and the hole of his tail (i.e., the dog’s rectum) is from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:9)


**הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר.** אִם טֻמְאָה מִבַּחוּץ. דְּמִשּׁוּם הַמְשָׁכָה לֹא מְטַמְּאִינַן לֵיהּ, שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ מַאֲכָל לָצֵאת דֶּרֶךְ פִּיו אֶלָּא דֶרֶךְ נֶקֶב בֵּית הָרְעִי:


הבית טהור – if the defilement is from the outside. For because of the conducting water through a channel, we do not defile him, for here is no path for the food to leave through its motuh, but rather through the hole in the privy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:10)


**פִּיו לַחוּץ.** וְשׁוּלָיו לִפְנִים:


פיו לחוץ – and through its lower part of the body/abdomen is on the inside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:11)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי דֶרֶךְ פֶּתַח קָטָן זֶה הָיָה סוֹפָהּ לָצֵאת:


הבית טמא – for this is the path of the small opening which was at its conclusion to exit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:12)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** בֵּין פִּיו לִפְנִים בֵּין פִּיו לַחוּץ, בֵּין טֻמְאָה מִכְּנֶגֶד הַמַּשְׁקוֹף וְלִפְנִים בֵּין טֻמְאָה לַחוּץ, בֵּין יֵשׁ בְּצַוָּארוֹ טֶפַח בֵּין אֵין בְּצַוָּארוֹ טֶפַח, בְּכָל עִנְיָן הַבַּיִת טָמֵא, דְּטֻמְאָה יוֹצְאָה בֵּין דֶּרֶךְ כְּנִיסָתָהּ בֵּין דֶּרֶךְ יְצִיאָתָהּ:


בין כך ובין כך – whether its (i.e., the dog’s) mouth is inside or whether its mouth is outside, whether the defilement is opposite the lintel and inside or whether the defilement is outside, whether there is in its neck a handbreadth or whether there isn’t in its neck [the space of] a handbreadth, in every matter the house is impure, for the defilement goes whether through its entrance or whether through its exit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:13)


**כַּמָּה תִשְׁהֶה.** בְּשַׂר הַמֵּת:


כמה תשהה – the flesh of the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:14)


**בְּמֵעָיו.** שֶׁל כֶּלֶב קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּמוּת הַכֶּלֶב וְשׁוּב לֹא יְטַמֵּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה חָשׁוּב כִּמְעֻכָּל:


במעיו – of the dog prior to the dog dying and further would not defile because it would be considered as consumed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:15)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים מֵעֵת לְעֵת.** מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁאֲכָלָהּ. כִּדְאָמְרִינַן [שַׁבָּת דַּף קנה] גָּלוּי וְיָדוּעַ לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁמְּזוֹנוֹתָיו שֶׁל כֶּלֶב מֻעָטִין, לְפִיכָךְ מַשְׁהֶה אֲכִילָתוֹ בְתוֹךְ מֵעָיו שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים:


שלשה ימים מעת לעת – from the time it (i.e., the dog) ate it. As we state in [Tractate Shabbat folio 155b] that it is revealed and known before the Holy One, blessed be He that the food of a dog is small, therefore, it leaves its food remains in its intestines for three days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:16)


**בְּעוֹפוֹת וּבְדָגִים.** אִם אָכְלוּ בְשַׂר הַמֵּת, כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּפֹּל לָאוּר וְתִשָּׂרֵף:


בעופות ובדגים – if they (i.e., birds and/or fish) ate from the flesh of the corpse, in order that it fall into the fire and be burned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:7:17)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה בֶן בְּתֵירָא אוֹמֵר וֽכוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה בֶּן בְּתֵירָא:


ר' יהודה בן בתירא אומר וכו' – And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda ben Beteira.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:1)


**הֶחָדוּת.** כְּמוֹ הַדּוּת. וְהַיְנוּ בּוֹר וְהַיְנוּ דּוּת, אֶלָּא שֶׁבּוֹר בַּחֲפִירָה וְדוּת בְּבִנְיָן:


החדות (the cellar) – like [the word] דות/cellar, that is the same as a בור/cistern, and that is the same as a דות/cellar, but that the בור/cistern is something [made by] digging and דות/cellar is [something made] by building.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:2)


**וּמְנוֹרָה בְתוֹכוֹ.** בְּתוֹךְ הַדּוּת:


ומנורה בתוכה – within the cellar/דות.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:3)


**וְהַפֶּרַח שֶׁל מְנוֹרָה יוֹצֵא וּכְפִישָׁה נְתוּנָה עָלָיו גָּרְסִינַן.** כְּלוֹמַר עַל הַפֶּרַח. וּפֶרַח הוּא הָרָחָב שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁ הַמְּנוֹרָה שֶׁהַנֵּר יוֹשֵׁב עָלָיו. וּכְפִישָׁה, סַל גָּדוֹל שֶׁכּוֹבְשִׁים בּוֹ הַזֵּיתִים לְכָמְרָן:


והפרח של מנורה יוצא וכפישה נתונה עליו (the calyx/cup of the Menorah projects and its inverted vessel – divided into two compartments by the bottom - is put over it [that it would remain in position]) – we have the this reading. Meaning to say, on the calyx/cup/flower. And the cup/calyx is wide at the top of the Menorah that the candle sits upon it. And the כפישה/inverted vessel is a large basket that they press the olives in it in order to produce shrinking and maturing of the frits by underground storage or by exposure to the sun.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:4)


**עַל פִּי הֶחָדוּת.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ נוֹפֶלֶת לְתוֹכוֹ:


על פי חדות – that doesn’t fall into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:5)


**טָהוֹר.** דְּנִצּוֹל עִם דָּפְנֵי הָאֹהֶל, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק ה'‏ [מִשְׁנָה ו']:


טהור – that it is preserved with the walls of the tent, as we stated above in Chapter 5, [Mishnah 6].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:6)


**וְהַמְּנוֹרָה טְמֵאָה.** מִפְּנֵי הַפֶּרַח הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַדּוּת וְאֵין הַכְּפִישָׁה מַצֶּלֶת עָלָיו. וְאִם תֹּאמַר, כֵּיוָן דִּמְנוֹרָה טְמֵאָה הֵיאַךְ הַדּוּת טָהוֹר, וַהֲלֹא הִיא מְטַמְּאָה כַמֵּת עַצְמוֹ דְחֶרֶב הֲרֵי הוּא כֶּחָלָל. יֵשׁ לוֹמַר, דְּמַיְרֵי כְגוֹן דְּלֵיכָּא מֵת בַּבַּיִת אֶלָּא חֶרֶב שֶׁנָּגַע בַּמֵּת, וְעַל יָדוֹ מִטַּמֵּא הַבַּיִת וְהַמְּנוֹרָה, וְקַיְמָא לָן דְּאֵין כְּלִי עוֹשֶׂה כְלִי אַחֵר כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ, כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא:


המנורה טמאה – because of the calyx/flower that protrudes from the cellar, the inverted vessel does not protect it. But if you should say, since the Menorah is impure, how can the cellar be pure? For doesn’t it become defiled like the corpse itself that a sword is like that the slain person? One can say, that we are speaking as for example that there isn’t a corpse in the house but rather the sword that touched/came in contact with the corpse, and through it defiles the house and the Menorah, and we hold that the a vessel does not make another vessel something else as was explained in the first chapter (see Mishnah 2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:7)


**אַף הַמְּנוֹרָה טְהוֹרָה.** דְּבָתַר דִּידַהּ אָזְלִינַן וְלֹא בָתַר פִּרְחָהּ, וְהִיא בְּתוֹךְ הַדּוּת קַיְמָא וְנִצּוֹלֶת עִם הַדּוּת:


אף המנורה טהורה – for we follow after it (i.e., the Menorah) and not its calyx. And it (i.e., the Menorah) is within the cellar exists and is protected with the cellar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:8:8)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** בֵּין הַדּוּת בֵּין הַמְּנוֹרָה. כֵּיוָן דְּאִם תִּנָּטֵל מְנוֹרָה כְּפִישָׁה נוֹפֶלֶת לְתוֹךְ הַדּוּת:


הכל טמא- whether the cell or whether the Menorah, since if you take the Menorah, the inverted vessel falls into the cellar [and everything is unclean].



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:1)


**טֻמְאָה שָׁם.** בְּתוֹךְ הַדּוּת, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַכְּפִישָׁה עַל גַּבָּיו:


טומאה שם- within the cellar, even though the inverted vessel is on top of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:2)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** דְּאֵין צָמִיד פָּתִיל לַטֻּמְאָה:


הבית טמא – for there is no airtight fitting lid for defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:3)


**טֻמְאָה בַבַּיִת.** הַשְׁתָּא מַיְרֵי בְדוּת פָּתוּחַ שֶׁאֵין עָלָיו כְּפִישָׁה:


טומאה בבית – now we are speaking of the open cistern which lacks an inverted vessel divided into two compartments by the bottom between.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:4)


**טְהוֹרִים.** הַכֵּלִים. כֵּיוָן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בִּמְקוֹמָן טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח, דְּנִצּוֹלִין שָׁם כְּבִיב שֶׁהוּא קָמוּר תַּחַת הַבַּיִת דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק ג'‏ [מִשְׁנָה ז'] טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ טָהוֹר:


טהורים – the vessels [are pure]. Since there is in their place a cubic square handbreadth, that are protected there like a gutter/pipe which is bent/arched over under the house, as is taught in the Mishnah above in Chapter 3 [Mishnah 7] the defilement is in the house and what is within it is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:5)


**רְחָבִים מִשֶּׁל בַּיִת.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁחֲלַל הַדּוּת מַרְחִיב וְהוֹלֵךְ תַּחַת כֹּתֶל הַבַּיִת וְכֹתֶל הַדּוּת בָּנוּי מִחוּץ לְכֹתֶל הַבַּיִת וְהַכֵּלִים שָׁם:


רחבים משל בית (if the walls of the cistern are wider than the walls of the house) – as for example that the cavity of the cistern is broader/extended continuously underneath the wall of the house and the wall of the cistern is built from outside of the wall of the house and the utensils are there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 11:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/11:9:6)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ דְאֵין בִּמְקוֹמָן טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח טְהוֹרִין:


בין כך ובין כך - even if there isn’t in their place a handbreadth by a handbreadth at the height of a handbreadth, they are pure.



## Chapter 12


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:1)


**נֶסֶר שֶׁהוּא נָתוּן עַל פִּי תַנּוּר חָדָשׁ.** שֶׁלֹּא הֻסַּק, דְּלֹא מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה. וּבְתַנּוּר הָעוֹמֵד בֶּחָצֵר אוֹ בַגִּנָּה אַיְרֵי:


נסר שהוא נתון על פי תנור חדש – that was not heated/kindled, that is not susceptible to receive ritual defilement, And we are speaking of an oven that stands in the courtyard or in the garden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:2)


**בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** שֶׁהַנֶּסֶר רָחָב וְיוֹצֵא חוּץ לַתַּנּוּר טֶפַח סָבִיב מִכָּל צְדָדָיו:


בפותח טפח – that the board is wide and extends outward from the oven a handbreadth around it from all its sides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:3)


**שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו.** שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַנֶּסֶר:


שעל גביו – on top of the board.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:4)


**טְהוֹרִים.** דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁהַתַּנּוּר טָהוֹר, נִצּוֹלִים הַכֵּלִים. וְכֵן אִם טֻמְאָה עַל גַּבֵּי הַנֶּסֶר וְכֵלִים תַּחַת הַנֶּסֶר:


טהורים – for since the oven is pure, the vessels are protected, and similarly, if defilement is on top of the board and the vessels are underneath the board.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:5)


**וּבְיָשָׁן.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהֻסַּק הַתַּנּוּר דִּמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


ובישן – as for example, that the oven was heated that is susceptible to receive ritual defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:6)


**טָמֵא.** הַתַּנּוּר. שֶׁאֵין הַנֶּסֶר מַצִּילוֹ. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחַת הַנֶּסֶר וְשֶׁעַל הַנֶּסֶר. וְטַעֲמָא דְמַתְנִיתִין, דְּכֵלִים נַעֲשִׂים אֹהָלִים לְטַמֵּא אֲבָל לֹא לְטַהֵר, הִלְכָּךְ חָדָשׁ דְּלָאו כְּלִי הוּא מַצִּיל, יָשָׁן דִּכְלִי הוּא אֵינוֹ מַצִּיל:


טמא – the oven [is impure]. The board does not protect it. And all the more so, thew vessels that are under the board and that are on the board. And the reason of our Mishnah is that vessels become tents to defile but not to purify, therefore, a new [oven] which is not a vessel protects. An old [oven] which is a vessel does not protect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:7)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי מְטַהֵר.** דַּוְקָא בְתַנּוּר. אֲבָל בְּכֵלִים מוֹדֶה דִּטְמֵאִין. וְהָכִי אִיתָא בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא:


ור' יוחנן בן נורי – specifically with an oven. But regarding vessels, he (i.e., Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri) agrees that they are impure. And this is brought in the Tosefta (Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 13, Halakha 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:1:8)


**נָתוּן עַל שְׁנֵי תַנּוּרִים.** רֹאשׁ הַנֶּסֶר עַל תַּנּוּר זֶה וְרֹאשׁוֹ הַשֵּׁנִי עַל תַּנּוּר אַחֵר וְטֻמְאָה בֵּין שְׁנֵי הַתַּנּוּרִים. וּבְתַנּוּרִים יְשָׁנִים אַיְרֵי. וְאָזְדוּ רַבָּנָן לְטַעֲמַיְהוּ וְרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי לְטַעֲמֵיהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי:


נתון על פי שני תנורים – the head of the board on this oven and the other head of the board on the other oven and there is defilement between the two ovens, and we are speaking of old ovens. But the Rabbis go according to their reasoning and Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri according to his reasoning. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:2:1)


**סְרֵידָה.** כְּמִין שְׂבָכָה שֶׁל חֶרֶס. תַּרְגּוּם מַעֲשֵׂה רֶשֶׁת, עוֹבַד סְרַדְתָּא. וְהִיא חֲלָקָה וְעוֹדֶפֶת חוּץ לַתַּנּוּר טֶפַח מִכָּל צַד:


סרידא (coarse web or matting/stuffed matting used for stoppers of stoves) – like a kind of earthenware lattice-work/grid/mesh. It is the Aramaic translation of (Exodus 27:4, 38:4): “a grating of meshwork”/מעשה רשת – making mesh. And it is smooth and the leftover is outside of the oven a handbreadth from every side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:2:2)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ וְשֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ חוּץ מִכֵּלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ כְּנֶגֶד אֲוִירוֹ שֶׁל תַּנּוּר, לְפִי שֶׁהָאֹהֶל מֵבִיא טֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ כָּל סְבִיב הַתַּנּוּר וְחוֹזֶרֶת וּבוֹקַעַת לְמַעְלָה. וְאַף כְּשֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה לְמַעְלָה, בּוֹקַעַת לְמַטָּה וְנִמְשֶׁכֶת סָבִיב וְחוֹזֶרֶת וּבוֹקַעַת לְמַעְלָה:


הכל טמא – vessels that are underneath it and that are above it, except from vessels that are on top of it opposite the airspace of the oven, because the tent brings defilement underneath it all around the oven and returns and bursts forth above. But even the defilement that is above bursts forth downwards and it is stretched around and returns and bursts forth above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:2:3)


**כְּנֶגֶד אֲוִירוֹ שֶׁל תַּנּוּר טָהוֹר.** שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא מֻקָּף צָמִיד פָּתִיל וּמַפְסִיק:


כנגד אוירו של תנור טהור – for it is surrounded by an airtight seal and interrupts it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:2:4)


**כְּנֶגְדּוֹ עַד הָרָקִיעַ טָמֵא.** אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתָּיו טָהוֹר:


כנגדו עד הרקיע טמא – but what is underneath it is ritually pure.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:1)


**נֶסֶר.** שֶׁהוּא אָרֹךְ וָצַר, וּכְשֶׁהוּא נָתוּן עַל פִּי הַתַּנּוּר יוֹצֵא טֶפַח מִן הַמִּזְרָח וְטֶפַח מִן הַמַּעֲרָב, וְדָרוֹם וְצָפוֹן אֵינוֹ יוֹצֵא:


נסר – [a board] which is long and narrow, and when it is placed on the mouth of the oven, it extends outward a handbreadth from the east and a handbreadth from the west but from the south and the north it does not extend outward.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:2)


**טֻמְאָה בְצַד זֶה.** תַּחַת הַנֶּסֶר בַּמִּזְרָח אוֹ בַמַּעֲרָב:


טומאה בצד זה – underneath the board in the east or in the west.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:3)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבַּצַּד הַשֵּׁנִי טְהוֹרִים.** דְּאֵין מָקוֹם לַטֻּמְאָה לַעֲבֹר, שֶׁאֵין הַנֶּסֶר עוֹדֵף מִן הַצָּפוֹן וּמִן הַדָּרוֹם:


כלים שבצד השני טהורים – that there Is no room for the defilement to pass, for the board doesn’t have leftover from the north or from the south.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:4)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי מְטַמֵּא.** קָסָבַר אֵין תַּנּוּר מַפְסִיק. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ורבי יוסי מטמא – for he holds that the oven does not form a partition. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:5)


**הַבְּטַח.** יֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁגּוֹרְסִים אַבְּטַח. וְהוּא בִּנְיָן עָשׂוּי כְּעֵין אַמְבַּטִי שֶׁבַּמֶּרְחֲצָאוֹת שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בָּהֶן מַיִם לְרַחֲצָה, וְנֶסֶר נָתוּן עָלָיו לְתִקְרָה, וְהַנֶּסֶר עוֹדֵף מִשְּׁנֵי צְדָדִים. וְאִם הַטֻּמְאָה מִצַּד זֶה אֵין יוֹצְאָה לְצַד אַחֵר, לְפִי שֶׁהַבְּטַח חוֹצֵץ:
