הבטח (a hollow column-like receptacle of rain water near the house; Maimonides holds that it is a projection in front of the window to stand upon in climbing) – There are books that have the reading of אבטח, and it is a building that is made similar to a bathtub in bathhouses that they place water in them for washing, and a board is placed upon it for the ceiling and board has leftover from two sides. But if there is defilement from this side, it doesn’t go out to the other side because the hollow column-like receptacle of rain water near the house interposes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:6)


**הָיָה בוֹ זִיז.** הָיְתָה קוֹרָה יוֹצְאָה לְמַעְלָה מִן הַבְּטַח מִן הַמִּזְרָח לְמַעֲרָב:


היה בו זיז (if it had an attachment – a projection from the door frame serving as a shed over the entrance, or a molding projecting from a window-sill as a bracket) – there was a beam going out above from the hollow column-like receptacle of rain water from the east to the west.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:3:7)


**רוֹאִין אֶת הַבְּטַח כְּאִלּוּ אֵינוֹ.** שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַבְּטַח חוֹצֵץ בַּנֶּסֶר, גַּבֵּי זִיז אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


רואין את הבטח כיאלו אינו – for even though the hollow column-like receptacle of rain water near the house interposes with the board, in connection with the attachment/a projection from the door frame serving as a shed over the entrance – it does not interpose. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:1)


**סַנְדָּל שֶׁל עֲרִיסָה.** עֲרִיסָה שֶׁל תִּינוֹק רְגִילִים לְהַנִּיחַ תַּחַת רַגְלֶיהָ כְּמִין סַנְדָּל שֶׁל מַתֶּכֶת לְנוֹי אוֹ בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָקְבוּ:


סנדל של עריסה – the cradle of a baby that regularly is placed underneath its feet like a metal sandal for beauty or in order that it not rot/decay.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:2)


**שֶׁפְּחָתוֹ בְתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת.** שֶׁנִּפְחַת הַגַּג שֶׁל עֲלִיָּה שֶׁהָעֲרִיסָה נְתוּנָה עָלָיו וְנִרְאֶה סַנְדַּל הָעֲרִיסָה בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת מִן הַפְּחָת שֶׁבַּגַּג, וְטֻמְאָה בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת:


שפחתו בתוך הבית (that was broken through within the house) – that the roof of the upper chamber was broken through/reduced that the crib is placed upon it and the sandal of the cradle is seen within the house from the reduction that is in the roof, and defilement is within the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:3)


**אִם יֶשׁ.** בִּמְקוֹם הַפְּחָת.


אם יש – in the place of the breach.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:4)


**פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה מִן הַבַּיִת לָעֲלִיָּה:


פותח טפח – it brings the defilement from the house to the upper chamber.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:5)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁאֵין שָׁם פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


ואם לאו – that there isn’t there an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:4:6)


**מוֹנִין בּוֹ כְדֶרֶךְ שֶׁמּוֹנִין בְּמֵת.** שֶׁהַסַּנְדָּל וְהָעֲרִיסָה טְמֵאִים טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה כְדִין כֵּלִים בְּכֵלִים, וְהַתִּינוֹק טָמֵא טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב, כְּדִתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא, הַשְּׁלִישִׁי בֵּין אָדָם בֵּין כֵּלִים טְמֵאִים טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב:


מונין בו כדברך שמונין במת – that the sandal and the crib are impure with a ritual impurity for seven days like the law concerning impure vessels that come in contact with vessels, but the baby is impure with ritual impurity until the evening, as it is taught in the Mishnah in the first chapter (Mishnah 2).



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:1)


**מַעֲזִיבָה.** טִיט וּצְרוֹרוֹת שֶׁרְגִילִים לָשׂוּם עַל גַּבֵּי קוֹרוֹת:


מעזיבה (concrete of stone chippings, clay, used for paving floors, pavement covering the ceiling of the lower story and serving as flooring of the upper story) – plaster and stones that they regularly place on top of the beams.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:2)


**וּמְכֻוָּנוֹת.** קוֹרָה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה כְנֶגֶד הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה וְכָל אַחַת יֵשׁ בָּהּ רֹחַב טֶפַח:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:3)


**תַּחְתֶּיהָ טָמֵא.** וְשֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ טָהוֹר, שֶׁהַקּוֹרָה חוֹצֶצֶת. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ בַּקּוֹרוֹת פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח שֶׁהֵן רְחָבוֹת טֶפַח, אֲבָל אֵין רְחָבוֹת טֶפַח לֹא מְבִיאוֹת טֻמְאָה וְלֹא חוֹצְצוֹת וּכְמַאן דְּלֵיתַנְהוּ דָמֵי, וְנִמְצָא טֻמְאָה כְאִלּוּ עוֹמֶדֶת בָּאֲוִיר:


תחתיה טמא – and that which is on top of it is ritually pure, for the beam interposes. And these words [apply] when there the beams have an opening of a handbreadth when they are a handbreadth wide, but if they are not a handbreadth wide, they do not bring the defilement and they do not interpose, and it is like one that they don’t have it. And it is found that defilement is like it stands in the airspace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:4)


**בֵּינֵיהֶן טָמֵא.** וְשֶׁתַּחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה וְשֶׁעַל הָעֶלְיוֹנָה טְהוֹרִים, דִּשְׁתֵּיהֶן חוֹצְצוֹת:


ביניהן טמא – and that underneath the lower [beam] and on top of the upper [beam] they are pure, for both of them interpose.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:5)


**כְּנֶגְדּוֹ עַד הָרָקִיעַ טָמֵא.** וְשֶׁתַּחַת כֻּלָּן טָהוֹר:


כנגדו עד הרקיע טמא – for underneath all of them (i.e., the beams) it is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:6)


**כְּבֵין הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת.** כְּנֶגֶד אֲוִיר הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת. וְרָחְבָּן כְּשִׁעוּר אֲוִיר שֶׁבֵּינֵיהֶן, שֶׁאִם תַּעֲלֶה אֶת הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת אוֹ תוֹרִיד אֶת הָעֶלְיוֹנוֹת נִמְצָא הַכֹּל סָתוּם:


כבין התחתונות – opposite the airspace of the lower ones. And their widths is like the measure of the airspace that is between them, for if they should raise up the lower [beams] or lower he upper [beams], it is found that everything is concealed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:5:7)


**תַּחַת כֻּלָּן טָמֵא.** כְּאִלּוּ הָיְתָה תִקְרָה שָׁוָה. וְעַל גַּבָּן טָהוֹר:


תחת כולן טמא – it is as if the ceiling was level, and on top of them is pure.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:1)


**תַּחַת כֻּלָּהּ.** כָּל כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ טְמֵאִים, דִּמְבִיאָה טֻמְאָה לְכֻלָּהּ. וְשֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ טְהוֹרִים, דְּחוֹצֶצֶת בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


תחת כולה – all the vessels that are under it (i.e., the beam) are impure, for it brings defilement to everything. But on top of it is pure, for it interposes in the face of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:2)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְחָבָה טֶפַח:


ואם לאו – that it (i.e., the beam) is not a handbreadth wide.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:3)


**בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה.** דְּאִם טֻמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ כֵּלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טְמֵאִים:


בוקעת ועולה – for if defilement is underneath it, the vessels that are on top of it opposite the defilement are impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:4)


**בּוֹקַעַת וְיוֹרָדֶת.** דְּאִם טֻמְאָה עַל גַּבָּהּ, כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתֶּיהָ כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה טְמֵאִים:


בוקעת ויורדת – that if defilement is on top of it, the vessels that are under it opposite the defilement are impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:6:5)


**הֶקֵּפָהּ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים.** דְּכָל שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּרָחְבּוֹ טֶפַח יֵשׁ בְּהֶקֵּפוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (ב ד) בַּיָּם שֶׁעָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה עֶשֶׂר בָּאַמָּה מִשְּׂפָתוֹ אֶל שְׂפָתוֹ עָגוֹל סָבִיב וְקָו שְׁלֹשִׁים בָּאַמָּה יָסֹב אֹתוֹ סָבִיב:


היקפה שלשה טפחים – that all that there is in its width a handbreadth, there is in its circumference three handbreadths, for such we found in [The Second Book of] Chronicles [Chapter 4, Verse 2]: “He made the sea of cast metal ten cubits across from brim to brim, perfectly round; it was five cubits high, and its circumference thirty cubits.”



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:7:1)


**עַמּוּד שֶׁהוּא מֻטָּל לָאֲוִיר.** עַמּוּד עָגֹל שֶׁמֻּשְׁלָךְ בָּאָרֶץ וְשׁוֹכֵב עַל עִגּוּלוֹ. בָּאֲוִיר, כְּגוֹן בֶּחָצֵר אוֹ בַגִּנָּה. וּבְאֶמְצַע הָעִגּוּל הוּא מַתְחִיל לְהַאֲהִיל עַל הָאָרֶץ וְהוֹלֵךְ וּמִתְקַצֵּר מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן עַד תַּחְתִּיתוֹ שֶׁסָּמוּךְ לָאָרֶץ, וּלְעִנְיַן הֲבָאַת טֻמְאָה בָּעִינַן טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח מְרֻבָּע, וְאִם יֵשׁ כָּאן לְצַד הַחוּץ מִתַּחְתָּיו רוּם טֶפַח אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלְּמַטָּה בְּסָמוּךְ בִּמְקוֹם שְׁכִיבָתוֹ לָאָרֶץ אֵין רוּם טֶפַח כֻּלּוֹ מִטַּמֵּא בְּאֹהֶל, דְּנִדּוֹן כְּשִׁפּוּעֵי אֹהָלִים. וְעַמּוּד עָגֹל כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּהֶקֵּפוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים נִמְצָא רָחְבּוֹ שְׁמֹנָה טְפָחִים, דְּכָל שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּרָחְבּוֹ טֶפַח יֵשׁ בְּהֶקֵּפוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, וְנִמְצָא דְיָכוֹל אָדָם לְרַבֵּעַ תַּחַת דָּפְנוֹ שֶׁמִּכָּאן וּמִכָּאן טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח, שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ כָּאן רִבּוּעַ שֶׁל שְׁמֹנָה עַל שְׁמֹנָה אִם תַּעֲשֶׂה בְּתוֹכוֹ עִגּוּל שֶׁל שְׁמֹנָה, נִמְצָא הַזָּוִיּוֹת עוֹדְפִים שְׁמֹנָה חֳמָשִׁים לְכָל קֶרֶן וְקֶרֶן, דְּכָל אַמְּתָא בְרִבּוּעַ אַמְּתָא וּתְרֵי חֻמְשֵׁי בַאֲלַכְסוֹנָהּ, נִמְצָא הָאֲלַכְסוֹן עוֹדֵף עַל הָרֹחַב שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר חֳמָשִׁין, תֵּן מֵהֶם מֶחֱצָה לְקֶרֶן זֶה וּמֶחֱצָה לְקֶרֶן זֶה, הֲרֵי לְכָל אֶחָד שְׁמֹנָה חֳמָשִׁין, וְאִם בָּאתָ לַעֲשׂוֹת בְּקֶרֶן זָוִית שֶׁחוּץ לָעִגּוּל רִבּוּעַ שֶׁל טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח, נִמְצָא אֲלַכְסוֹנוֹ עוֹדֵף עַל רָחְבּוֹ שְׁנֵי חֳמָשִׁים, וְנִמְצָא מִדַּת אֲלַכְסוֹנוֹ שִׁבְעָה חֳמָשִׁים, לֹא נִשְׁתַּיֵּר לָנוּ יוֹתֵר עַל הַצֹּרֶךְ אֶלָּא חֹמֶשׁ אֶחָד, וּמִשּׁוּם חֹמֶשׁ אֶחָד לֹא דָק. וְעוֹד, דְּכָל אַמְּתָא בְרִבּוּעַ אַמְּתָא וּתְרֵי חֻמְשֵׁי בַאֲלַכְסוֹנָהּ אֵינוֹ מְכֻוָּן לְגַמְרֵי, שֶׁחָסֵר מִמֶּנּוּ מְעַט:


עמוד שהוא מוטל לאויר (a column which lay [on the ground] in the open air) – a round column that is cast on the ground and lies on its circle. In the open air, as for example, in the courtyard or in the garden. And from the middle of the circle it begins to overshadow on the ground and gradually becomes shortened from here and there until its bottom is next to/adjacent to the ground, and regarding the mater of bringing defilement we require a handbreadth by a handbreadth at the height of a handbreadth squared, but if there is here to the side that is outward from underneath the height of a handbreadth even though that which is below is adjacent/next to in the place of his lying down on the ground, the height is not a handbreadth completely which defiles in the tent, and it is judged like the sloping of tents. But the round pillar which has in its circumference twenty four handbreadths is found that its width is eight handbreadths, that all that has in its width a handbreadth there is in its circumference twenty-four handbreadths, and it is found that a person is able to make a quadrangle underneath his partition/wall that is from here to and from there a handbreadth by a handbreadth. At the height of a handbreadth, there is here a square of eight [handbreadths] by eight [handbreadths] if you make within it a circle of eight [handbreadths], it is found that the corners overlap eight-fifths [handbreadth] for each and every corner, for each cubit in a square is a cubit and two-fifths crosswise/in a diagonal line, it is found that the diagonal line is in excess of the width by sixteen-fifths, take from them half for this corner and half for that corner, there is for each one eight-fifths, and if you come to make in the corner that is outside of the circle a square of a handbreadth by a handbreadth, it is found that his diagonal line/crosswise is in excess over its width by two-fifths, and it is found that the measure of the diagonal line is seven-fifths, there would not remain for us more than the need other than one-fifth, but because the one-fifth is not exact. And furthermore, that each cubit in a square is a cubit and two-fifths in a diagonal line – which is not completely lying exactly in line, for it is missing a bit from it.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:1)


**מֻדְבָּק לָאַסְקֻפָּה.** שֶׁהָאַסְקֻפָּה רְחָבָה וְטֻמְאָה מֻדְבֶּקֶת בָּאַסְקֻפָּה מִן הַשְּׁקוֹף וְלַחוּץ, רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר חֲשִׁיב כָּל הָאַסְקֻפָּה כַּבַּיִת, הִלְכָּךְ מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַבַּיִת. וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ סָבַר מִן הַשְּׁקוֹף וְלַחוּץ אֵין הָאַסְקֻפָּה חֲשׁוּבָה כַּבַּיִת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


מודבק לאסקופה – that the threshold is wide and defilement is attached on the threshold from the arched gateway with posts and outward, Rabbi Eliezer holds that all of the threshold is like the house, therefore, it defiles the house. But Rabbi Yehoshua holds from he arched gateway with posts and outward, the threshold is not considered like the house. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:2)


**יִדּוֹן מֶחֱצָה לְמֶחֱצָה.** מֵחֶצְיָהּ שֶׁל אַסְקֻפָּה וְלִפְנִים כְּלִפְנִים וְהַבַּיִת טָמֵא, מֵחֶצְיָהּ וְלַחוּץ כְּלַחוּץ וְהַבַּיִת טָהוֹר, מֶחֱצָה עַל מֶחֱצָה הַבַּיִת טָמֵא:


ידון מחצה למחצה – from half of the threshold and inward is like inside, and the house is impure, from half and outward is like outside and the house is pure, half and half, the house is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:3)


**מֻדְבָּק לַמַּשְׁקוֹף.** תַּחַת הַמַּשְׁקוֹף מִן הַשְּׁקוֹף וְלַחוּץ:


מודבק למשקוף – underneath the lintel from the arched gateway and posts outward.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:4)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ מִן הַשְּׁקוֹף וְלַחוּץ חָשׁוּב בַּיִת:


הבית טמא – that even from the arched gateway and posts and outward it is considered a house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:5)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי מְטַהֵר.** דְּלָא חֲשִׁיב לֵיהּ כַּבַּיִת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ור' יוסי מטהר – for it is not considered to him like a house. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:6)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְטַמֵּא.** דַּחֲשִׁיב כָּל הָאַסְקֻפָּה כַּבַּיִת:


רבי אליעזר מטמא – that he considers all of the threshold like a house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:7)


**מִטֶּפַח וּלְמַטָּן טָהוֹר.** דִּכְקַרְקַע דָּמֵי:


מטפח ולמטן טהור – for it is similar to the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 12:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/12:8:8)


**מִטֶּפַח וּלְמַעְלָן טָמֵא.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּכְשֶׁמֻּדְבָּק לָאַסְקֻפָּה מְטַהֵר רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אֶת הַבַּיִת, הָכָא כְשֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בַּבַּיִת מְטַמֵּא רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בָּאַסְקֻפָּה מִטֶּפַח וּלְמַעְלָה, מִשּׁוּם דְּדֶרֶךְ טֻמְאָה לָצֵאת וְלֹא לִכָּנֵס. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


מטפח ולמעלן טמא – even though it is attached to the threshold, Rabbi Yehoshua purifies the house, here when then defilement is in the house, Rabbi Yehoshua declares impure the threshold from a handbreadth and higher, because it is the manner of defilement to leave and not to enter. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



## Chapter 13


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:1)


**הָעוֹשֶׂה מָאוֹר.** חַלּוֹן הֶעָשׂוּי לְאוֹרָה:


העושה מאור ((he who leaves a light-hole/opening – in the wall – from the start – when building) – a window that is made for its light.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:2)


**בַּתְּחִלָּה.** מִשּׁוּם דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנָא סֵיפָא שְׁיָרֵי הַמָּאוֹר תָּנָא רֵישָׁא בִתְחִלָּה:


בתחילה – because they need to need the concluding clause: "שיירי המאור"/”remnants of the light,” it teaches in the first clause, “from the start”/"בתחילה".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:3)


**מַקְדֵּחַ גָּדוֹל שֶׁל לִשְׁכָּה.** הוּא כְפוּנְדְיוֹן הָאִטַּלְקִי וּכְסֶלַע נֵרוֹנִית וְיֶשְׁנוֹ כִמְלֹא נֶקֶב שֶׁל עוֹל. אִם יֵשׁ בַּחַלּוֹן כַּשִּׁעוּר הַזֶּה, מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לְצַד אַחֵר:


מקדח גדול של לשכה (the full capacity of a large borer/drill of the chamber) – It is like the Italian Dupondium and like the Neronian Sela and it has the full capacity of a hole of a yoke/pole. If it has in the window like this measure, it brings the defilement to the other side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:4)


**שְׁיָרֵי הַמָּאוֹר.** אִם נִשְׁאַר מִן הַחַלּוֹן שֶׁלֹּא נִסְתַּם:


שיירי המאור – if thee remains from the window that is not concealed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:5)


**חֲרָרוּהוּ מַיִם.** שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשָׂה הַחוֹר עַל יְדֵי אָדָם אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי מְרוּצַת הַמַּיִם:


חררוהו מים- that the cave was not made at the hand of a person but rather through the running water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:6)


**אוֹ שְׁרָצִים.** כְּגוֹן הַחֹלֶד וְהָעַכְבָּר:


או שרצים – as for example the mole/weasel and the mouse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:7)


**אוֹ שֶׁאֲכָלַתּוּ מַלַּחַת.** שֶׁהָאָרֶץ מְלֵחָה וּמִתְבַּקַּעַת מֵאֵלֶיהָ:


או שאכלתו מלחת (or formed through corrosion produced by saline corrosion) – that the land is filled with saline corrosion and it burst forth on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:8)


**מְלֹא אֶגְרוֹף.** אֶגְרוֹפוֹ שֶׁל בֶּן אֲבַטִּיחַ. אָדָם גָּדוֹל שֶׁהָיָה הָאֶגְרוֹף שֶׁלּוֹ כְרֹאשׁ גָּדוֹל שֶׁל כָּל אָדָם:


מלא אגרוף – the fist of Ben Battiah (see Tractate Kelim, Chapter 17, Mishnah 12 – Ben Battiah is the nephew of Rabban Yohanan ben Zakkai, one of the leaders of the terrorists during the siege of Jerusalem by the Romans) – a large person whose fist was like the large head of all people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:9)


**חָשַׁב עָלָיו לְתַשְׁמִישׁ.** חַלּוֹן שֶׁחֲרָרוּהוּ מַיִם אוֹ שְׁרָצִים, שֶׁחִשֵּׁב לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בּוֹ וּלְהַנִּיחַ שָׁם חֲפָצָיו:


חישב עליו לתשמיש – a window that that the running waters or the reptiles made, that he gave thought to use it and to leave there his belongings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:10)


**לְמָאוֹר.** לְהַכְנִיס אוֹרָה:


למאור – to bring in light.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:11)


**הַסְּרִיגוֹת.** מְחִצָּה כְמִין סְבָכָה מְסֹרֶגֶת שֶׁעוֹשִׂים בַּחַלּוֹנוֹת שֶׁל אוֹצָרוֹת:


הסריגות (the lattice In the windows) – the partitions which are like net-work in zig-zag that they make in widows of storehouses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:12)


**וְהָרְפָפוֹת.** מְחִצָּה מְסֹרֶגֶת שֶׁעוֹשִׂים בַּחַלּוֹנוֹת שֶׁל בָּתֵּי הַקַּיִץ שֶׁיּוֹשְׁבִים שָׁם לְהִתְקָרֵר:


והרפפות (loose lattice work with wide meshes – used for sheds) – partitions that are zig-zag that they make in windows of summer houses where they sit there to cool off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:13)


**מִצְטָרְפוֹת כִּמְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ.** אֲוִיר שֶׁבֵּין הַסְּרִיגוֹת, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד כִּמְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ מִצְטָרְפִין בֵּין כֻּלָּם לְכִמְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ:


מצטרפות כמלוא מקדח – the airspace that is between the lattice in the windows, even though there isn’t in one place like the breadth of a hole of a borer/drill, they combine amongst all of them to the size of a borer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:1:14)


**לְהָבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה וּלְהוֹצִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** לָאו אַפְּלוּגְתָּא דְבֵית שַׁמַּאי וּבֵית הִלֵּל קָאֵי, אֶלָּא אַשִּׁעוּרִים דְּרֵישָׁא קָאֵי. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, מְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ וּמְלֹא אֶגְרוֹף וּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח דַּאֲמַרַן, כָּל הַשִּׁעוּרִין הַלָּלוּ בֵּין לְהָבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת בֵּין לְהוֹצִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה מִן הַבַּיִת. וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פָּלֵיג, וְסָבַר בֵּין לְהָבִיא בֵּין לְהוֹצִיא כֻּלָּן שִׁעוּרָן בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, וְלֵית לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שִׁעוּר שֶׁל מְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ וּמְלֹא אֶגְרוֹף כְּלָל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


להביא את הטומאה ולהוציא את הטומאה – this is not referring to the dispute between the School of Shammai and the School of Hillel, but it refers to the measurements of the first clause of the Mishnah. And this is what he said: The size of the hole of the large drill/borer and the size of the fist [of Ben Battiah] and the opening of a handbreadth that we stated, all of these measurements, whether to bring in the defilement into the house or whether to remove the defilement from the house. But Rabbi Shimon disputes this, and holds, whether to bring in or whether to remove, for all of them their measurement is the opening of a handbreadth, and Shimon does not have the measurement of the size of the hole of the borer or the size of the fist at all. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:1)


**חַלּוֹן הֶעָשׂוּי לַאֲוִיר.** לְהַכְנִיס בּוֹ אֲוִיר הָעוֹלָם, וְהַיְנוּ חַלּוֹן הֶעָשׂוּי לְמָאוֹר. וּמִשּׁוּם דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי בָּנָה בַּיִת חוּצָה לָהּ, הֲדַר תַּנְיֵהּ:


חלון העשוי לאויר – o bring in the air of the world, that is the window that is made for its light. And because it was necessary to teach that he built a house outside of it, he went back and repeated the teaching.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:2)


**בָּנָה בַיִת חוּצָה לָהּ.** כְּנֶגֶד הַחַלּוֹן, וְנִתְבַּטֵּל אֲוִיר שֶׁל חַלּוֹן:


בנה בית חוצה לה – opposite the window, and the airspace of the window was voided.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:3)


**שִׁעוּרָהּ בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** כְּחַלוֹן הֶעָשׂוּי לְתַשְׁמִישׁ:


שיעורה בפותח טפח – like the window that is made for usage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:4)


**נָתַן אֶת הַתִּקְרָה בְאֶמְצַע הַחַלּוֹן.** שֶׁנִּמְצָא הַחַלּוֹן חֶצְיוֹ לְמַעְלָה מִן הַתִּקְרָה וְחֶצְיוֹ לְמַטָּה:


נתן את התקרה באמצע החלון – that the window is found half it of it above the ceiling and half of it below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:5)


**הַחַלּוֹן הַתַּחְתּוֹן בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** כְּדִין חַלּוֹן הֶעָשׂוּי לְתַשְׁמִישׁ, דְּהָא אֵין כָּאן אֲוִיר:


החלון התחתון בפותח טפח – like the law of the window made for usage, for here there is no air.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:2:6)


**וְהָעֶלְיוֹן מְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ.** דִּלְמַעְלָה מִן הַתִּקְרָה הוּא לַאֲוִיר:


והעליון מלוא מקדח – that above from the ceiling is for the air.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:1)


**הַחוֹר שֶׁבַּדֶּלֶת.** בְּאֶמְצַע הַדֶּלֶת, דְּאֵינוֹ עָשׂוּי לַאֲוִיר:


החור שבדלת – in the middle of the door, that it is not made for the air.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:2)


**שִׁיֵּר בּוֹ הֶחָרָשׁ.** הָאֻמָּן שֶׁתִּקֵּן אֶת הַדֶּלֶת:


שייר בו החרש – the artisan who prepared/fixed the door.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:3)


**מִלְּמַטָּן.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁשִּׁיֵּר סָמוּךְ לָאַסְקֻפָּה:


מלמטן – as for example that he left [a hole] near the threshold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:4)


**אוֹ מִלְמַעְלָן.** סָמוּךְ לַמַּשְׁקוֹף:


או מלמעלן – near the lintel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:5)


**הֱגִיפָהּ.** סְגָרָהּ:


הגיפה (he filled the hole out but not entirely)– he closed it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:6)


**וְלֹא מֵרְקָהּ.** לֹא גְמָרָהּ:


ולא מירקה – but didn’t complete it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:7)


**אוֹ שֶׁפְּתָחַתּוּ הָרוּחַ.** כְּלוֹמַר, אוֹ שֶׁמֵּרַק אֶת הַסְּגִירָה אֶלָּא שֶׁבָּאתָה הָרוּחַ וּפְתָחַתּוּ:


או שפתחונו הרוח – meaning to say, or that he competed the closure but the wind came and opened it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:3:8)


**שִׁעוּרוֹ מְלֹא אֶגְרוֹף.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּנִפְתַּח מִמֵּילָא הָוֵי כַּחֲרָרוּהוּ מַיִם אוֹ שְׁרָצִים. וּבְהַךְ סֵיפָא לֹא פָלֵיג רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן, מִשּׁוּם דְּחוֹר שֶׁבָּאֶמְצַע עָשָׂה הֶחָרָשׁ בְּמִתְכַּוֵּן, וְלֹא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לַחוֹר אֶלָּא קְצָרִים הָיוּ הַדַּפִּין שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה מֵהֶן הַדֶּלֶת וְעָלְתָה בְדַעְתּוֹ לְהַנִּיחַ הַנֶּקֶב בָּאֶמְצַע יוֹתֵר מִלְּהַנִּיחַ לְמַטָּה אוֹ לְמַעְלָה, וְדָמֵי לְעָשָׂה בְמִתְכַּוֵּן, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהִנִּיחוֹ מִלְּמַטָּה אוֹ מִלְּמַעְלָה אֵין זֶה כְעוֹשֶׂה בַיָּדַיִם, אֶלָּא מֵחֲמַת שֶׁהַנְּסָרִים קְצָרִים וְלֹא נִתְמַלֵּא הַנֶּקֶב:


שיעורו מלוא אגרוף – for since it opened on its own, it is like the running water or reptiles. But in this concluding clause [of the Mishnah], Rabbi Tarfon does not disagree because the hole that is in the middle the carpenter made with intention, but not because he needs the hole but rather the boards were short and a door was made from them and he had the intention to place a hole/incision in the middle more than to place it below or above. And it is similar to someone who makes with intention, but when he placed it from below or from above, this is not like doing it with one’s hands, but rather because the boards are short, and the hole wasn’t filled.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:1)


**הָעוֹשֶׂה מָקוֹם.** שֶׁנִּקֵּב נֶקֶב מְפֻלָּשׁ בַּכֹּתֶל לְהַנִּיחַ שָׁם קָנֶה שֶׁל אוֹרְגִים:


העושה מקום – that he made a puncture going all the way through in the wall to place there a reed of the weavers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:2)


**וּלְאִסְפָּתִי.** חֶרֶב בִּלְשׁוֹן יָוָן קוֹרִין אִסְפְּתִי. וְיֵשׁ לָאוֹרְגִים כְּלִי כְּתַבְנִית חֶרֶב:


ולאספתי (and for the executioner’s sword/weaver’s stave) – a sword in the Greek language they call ASPATI. And weavers have a vessel like the shape of a sword.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:3)


**שִׁעוּרוֹ בְכָל שֶׁהוּא.** לְהָבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לַצַּד הָאַחֵר:


שעורו בכל שהוא – to bring in the defilement to the other side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:4)


**לָזוּן אֶת עֵינָיו.** לְהִסְתַּכֵּל מִשָּׁם לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אוֹ לֶחָצֵר:


לזון את עיניו – to look out from there to the public domain or to the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:4:5)


**וּלְתַשְׁמִישׁ.** לְהַצְנִיעַ שָׁם חֲפָצָיו:


ולתשמיש – to hide there his belongings.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:1)


**אֵלּוּ מְמַעֲטִים אֶת הַטֶּפַח.** חַלּוֹן שֶׁשִּׁעוּרוֹ בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח וְהָיָה אֶחָד מִכָּל הָנֵי דַּחֲשִׁיב הָכָא מֻנָּח בּוֹ וּמְמַעֵט אֶת הָאֲוִיר מִטֶּפַח, חוֹצֵץ, שֶׁאֵין הַטֻּמְאָה עוֹבֶרֶת לְצַד אַחֵר:


אלו ממעטים את הטפח – a window that its measure is when it is open a handbreadth, and one of all of these that is considered here is placed in it and it reduces the airspace from a handbreadth, it interposes, that the defilement doesn’t pass to the other side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:2)


**עַל יְדֵי רֹבַע עֲצָמוֹת.** אִם יֵשׁ בַּבַּיִת רֹבַע עֲצָמוֹת דִּמְטַמֵּא בְאֹהֶל, וּפָחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת מִבְּשַׂר הַמֵּת מֻנָּח בַּחַלּוֹן, מְמַעֵט. וְדַוְקָא פָּחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת, דְּאִלּוּ כַּזַּיִת, הוּא עַצְמוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְאֹהֶל. וְדַוְקָא עַל יְדֵי רֹבַע עֲצָמוֹת, אֲבָל אִי אִיכָּא בַּבַּיִת כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר מִן הַמֵּת, אֵין פָּחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת זֶה מְמַעֵט, דְּאַדְּרַבָּה בָּשָׂר מִצְטָרֵף עִם בָּשָׂר. וְהַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא נַמִּי דְדַוְקָא בָשָׂר מְמַעֵט עַל יְדֵי עֲצָמוֹת וְלֹא עֲצָמוֹת עַל יְדֵי עֲצָמוֹת, דַּעֲצָמוֹת אַעֲצָמוֹת מִצְטָרְפֵי:


על ידי רובע עצמות – if there is in the house a quarter [KAB] of bones that defile in a tent, and less than an olive’s bulk from the flesh of a corpse that is placed in the window, it lessens it. And specifically, less than an olive’s bulk, for if it were an olive’s bulk, it itself defiles in a tent. And specifically, through a quarter [Kab] of bones. But if there is in the house an olive’s bulk of flesh from a corpse, nothing less than this olive’s bulk can lessen it, for on the contrary, flesh combines with flesh. And that is the reason also that specifically flesh reduces through bones, but not bones through bones, for bones with bones combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:3)


**פָּחוֹת מֵעֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה.** אֲבָל עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה לֹא מְמַעֵט, מִשּׁוּם דִּמְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא:


פחות מעצם כשעורה -but a bone like [the size of] a barleycorn does not reduce/lessen the size, because it defiles through contact and through carrying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:4)


**פָּחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת.** הַשְׁתָּא הֲדַר תַּנְיֵהּ אַף עַל גַּב דְּתָנָא לֵיהּ רֵישָׁא, לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן דִּמְמַעֵט עַל יְדֵי כָּל הַמְטַמְּאִין בְּאֹהֶל, כְּגוֹן רְבִיעִית דָּם וּמְלֹא תַרְוַד רָקָב וְכָל אוֹתָן הַשְּׁנוּיִין בְּפֶרֶק ב'‏:


פחות מכזית מן המת – now [the Tanna/teacher of the Mishnah] has repeated his teaching, even though he taught it in the first clause [of the Mishnah] to inform us/teach us that he lessens/reduces [the size] through all that defile in a tent, such as a quarter [LOG] of blood and a spoonful of mass of earth from a grave containing parts of a decayed human body if of a certain quantity and all of those that are taught in Chapter Two [of Tractate Ohalot].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:5)


**פָּחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת מִן הַנְּבֵלָה וֽכוּ'.** כָּל הָנֵי דַּחֲשִׁיב מִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ, מְמַעֲטֵי בֵּין עַל יְדֵי עֲצָמוֹת בֵּין עַל יְדֵי בָּשָׂר:


פחות מכזית מן הנבלה וכו' – all of these that are considered from here and onward, that they reduce whether through bones or whether through flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:6)


**וּפָחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה אֳכָלִים.** שֶׁאֵין אֹכֶל פָּחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה מְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים וְלֹא מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה מִן הַתּוֹרָה, הִלְכָּךְ מְמַעֵט וְחוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


ופחות מכביצה אוכלים – that foods less than an egg’s bulk do not defile others and are not susceptible to receive ritual defilement from the Torah, therefore it reduces and interposes in the face of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:7)


**וְהַתְּבוּאָה.** שֶׁהִשְׁרִישָׁה רָחוֹק מִן הַכֹּתֶל וְנִתְפַּשְּׁטוּ עֲנָפֶיהָ וְסָגְרוּ אֶת הַחַלּוֹן, דִּמְחֻבָּר לַקַּרְקַע הִיא:


והתבואה – that took root far from the wall and its foliage straightened and closed the window, for it is attached to the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:8)


**כָּכַי שֶׁיֶּשׁ בָּהֶן מַמָּשׁ.** קוּרֵי עַכָּבִישׁ נִקְרָאִים כָּכַי. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי כְמִין קוּרֵי עַכָּבִישׁ נִמְצָא בְתוֹךְ הַקָּנֶה. וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהוּא דַק מְאֹד וְהוּא כָּכַי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מַמָּשׁ, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהוּא כְּעֵין אֲרִיגַת בֶּגֶד וְהוּא כָּכַי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ מַמָּשׁ:


וככי שיש הן ממש (the web-like marrow of reeds/substantial spiderweb in which there is substance) – spider’s web that are called the web-like marrow of reeds. But I heard that they are a species of spider’s web that is found within the reed. And sometimes it is very thin and it is web-like marrow of reeds and there is no substance to them, and sometimes it is similar to the weaving of cloth and it is like a web-like marrow of reeds that has substance to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:9)


**וְנִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר שֶׁלֹּא חִשַּׁב עָלֶיהָ.** שֶׁנִּבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה עַד שֶׁיַּחֲשֹׁב עָלֶיהָ לַאֲכִילָה, וְאָז מְטַמְּאָה טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים בְּכַבֵּיצָה וְאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה הֶכְשֵׁר:


ונבלת עוף טהור שלא חישב עליה – that the carcass of fowl/birds is ritually pure and doesn’t defile until one considers it/given it thought for eating, and then it defiles with the ritual impurity of foods like an egg’s bulk and does not require certification [to be susceptible to ritual uncleanness].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:5:10)


**נִבְלַת עוֹף טָמֵא שֶׁחִשַּׁב עָלֶיהָ וְלֹא הִכְשִׁירָהּ.** דְּנִבְלַת עוֹף טָמֵא צְרִיכָה מַחֲשָׁבָה וּצְרִיכָה הֶכְשֵׁר, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁיָּבֹא עָלֶיהָ מַיִם אוֹ אֶחָד מִשִּׁבְעָה מַשְׁקִין, וְאִם חָסֵר מַחֲשָׁבָה אוֹ הֶכְשֵׁר, טְהוֹרָה, וּמְמַעֶטֶת וְחוֹצֶצֶת בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


נבלת עוף טמא שחשב עליה ולא הכשירה – for the carrion of an impure bird/fowl requires intention/though and requires certification [to be susceptible to ritual uncleanness, that is to say, that when water comes upon it or one of the seven liquids (i.e., dew, water, wine, olive oil, blood, milk and honey – see Tractate Makhshirin, Chapter 6, Mishnah 4), but if it is lacking thought/intention or certification of [to be susceptible to ritual uncleanness], it is pure and it reduces/lessens [the handbreadth] and interposes in the face of defilement.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:6:1)


**וְלֹא תְבוּאָה שֶׁבַּחַלּוֹן.** שֶׁהִשְׁרִישָׁה בַּחַלּוֹן מַמָּשׁ. מִשּׁוּם דְּדַעְתּוֹ לְפַנּוֹתָהּ שֶׁהִיא מְקַלְקֶלֶת אֶת הַכֹּתֶל, וְכָל דָּבָר שֶׁדַּעְתּוֹ לְפַנּוֹתוֹ אֵינוֹ מְמַעֵט בַּחַלּוֹן. וּלְעֵיל אַיְרֵי שֶׁהִשְׁרִישָׁה רָחוֹק מִן הַכֹּתֶל, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֵין דַּעְתּוֹ לְפַנּוֹתָהּ:


ולא תבואה שבחלון – that actually took root in the window. Because it was his intention to remove it when it ruins the wall, and every thing that is his intention to remove, does not reduce in the window. But above (i.e., the previous Mishnah), it speaks about something that took root far from the wall, that now it is not his intention to remove it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:6:2)


**הַשְּׁתִי וְהָעֵרֶב הַמְנֻגָּעִים.** דִּשְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב מִטַּמֵּא בִנְגָעִים, וְדָבָר טָמֵא אֵינוֹ מְמַעֵט:


השתי והערב המנוגעים (but not – the warp and woof threads that have contracted leprosy) – that the warp and woof [threads] become defiled through plagues/leprosy, and something that is impure does not reduce/lessen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 13:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/13:6:3)


**וְלֹא לְבֵנָה מִבֵּית הַפְּרָס.** הַלְּבֵנָה הָעֲשׂוּיָה מֵעֲפַר בֵּית הַפְּרָס. רַבִּי מֵאִיר סָבַר שֶׁהִיא טְמֵאָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁעֲפַר בֵּית הַפְּרָס טָמֵא, הִלְכָּךְ אֵינָהּ מְמַעֶטֶת בַּחַלּוֹן. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי לֹא טִמְּאוּ אֶלָּא גוּשׁ כִּבְרִיָּתוֹ הַבָּא מִבֵּית הַפְּרָס, וְלֹא הֶעָפָר אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁגִּבְּלוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. וְשִׁעוּר הַגּוּשׁ, כְּפִיקָה גְדוֹלָה שֶׁל סַקָּאִים, שֶׁהוּא כְּחוֹתַם הַמַּרְצוּפִין:


ולא לבינה מבית הפרס – the brick is made from the dust of the an area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse [surrounded by one-hundred cubits]. Rabbi Meir holds that it is impure just as the dust of an area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse, therefore, it does not lessen in the window. But the Rabbis hold that they only defiled a clod/lump-like creature that comes from an area where in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse, and not the dust even after it was kneaded. And the Halakha is according to the Sages. And the measure of a clod/lump is like a large lump of sacks which is like the sealing clay as required for a seal on bags.



## Chapter 14


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:1)


**הַזִּיז.** שֶׁפָּנָיו לְמַטָּה. כְּמִין לוּחַ יוֹצֵא מִן הַכֹּתֶל לַחוּץ וְרֹאשׁוֹ כָפוּף לְמַטָּה לָאָרֶץ לְצַד הַפֶּתַח, הִלְכָּךְ מִצְטָרֵף אָהֳלוֹ עִם הַפֶּתַח וּמֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא וַחֲשִׁיב כְּאִלּוּ יֵשׁ בּוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, וְאִם טֻמְאָה תַחְתָּיו הַבַּיִת טָמֵא, שֶׁרוֹאִין רֹאשׁוֹ הַכָּפוּף לְמַטָּה כְּאִלּוּ הִגִּיעַ לָאָרֶץ, וּמֵבִיא טֻמְאָה שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו לְצַד הַבַּיִת:


הזיז שפניו למטה (the projection from the door frame serving as a shed over the entrance, or a molding projecting from a window-sill serving as a bracket --- the finished side of which faces the ground) – like a tablet/board that projects out from the wall outward and its head is bent to the land to side of the opening. Therefore, its tent combines with the opening and brings the defilement in any amount and it is considered as if it has an opening of a handbreadth, but if the defilement is underneath it, the house is impure, that we see its head bent downward as if it reached the ground and it brings defilement to the side of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:2)


**וְגִזְרָה.** שֶׁפָּנֶיהָ לְמַעְלָה. רֹאשָׁהּ כָּפוּף לְמַעְלָה וְאֵין אֹהֶל שֶׁלָּהּ מִצְטָרֵף עִם הַפֶּתַח, הִלְכָּךְ בָּעְיָה פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


וגיזרה – that it faces upward. Its head is bent upward and its tent does not combine with the opening, therefore, it requires an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:3)


**וְגַבְלִית.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא כְּלִי שֶׁמֵּנִיקָה מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן וּמְשַׁיֵּר מִן הָאֶמְצַע:


וגבלית (a Giblean balcony) – The Tosefta (Tosefta Ohalot, Chapter 14, Halakha 9) explains that it is vessel which sucks from (is girded to the wall on) both sides and left alone (without support) in the middle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:4)


**שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר טְפָחִים.** דְּשִׁעוּר כָּל נִדְבָּךְ אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים. וְנִדְבָּךְ הוּא שׁוּרָה שֶׁל בִּנְיַן אֲבָנִים:


שנים עשר טפחים – for the measurement each נדבך/course of stones or tier/layer is four handbreadths, and a נדבך is a row of the stones of the building.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:5)


**וְהָעֲטָרוֹת.** אֲבָנִים סְדוּרוֹת בְּעִגּוּל בּוֹלְטוֹת מִן הַקִּיר לְמַעְלָה מִן הַפֶּתַח כְּעֵין כִּפָּה לְנוֹי, וְהַיְנוּ עֲטָרָה:


והעטרות (cornice, molding, wreaths -above the entrance) – stones arranged in a circle that protrude from the wall above from the opening similar to an archway for beauty.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:1:6)


**וְהַפִּתּוּחִים.** שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מֵאֶבֶן עַל הַפֶּתַח אוֹ עַל הַחַלּוֹן לְנוֹי, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁבּוֹלְטִים מִן הַחַלּוֹן וּמִן הַפֶּתַח טֶפַח אוֹ יוֹתֵר:


והפתוחים (and the engravings -above the entrance)- that they make from stone on the opening or on the window for beauty, and sometimes they protrude from the window or from the opening a handbreadth or more.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:2:1)


**זִיז שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַפֶּתַח וֽכוּ'.** הָכָא מַיְרֵי שֶׁנִּסְתַּם הַפֶּתַח, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי בָּעִינַן שֶׁיְּהֵא בַזִּיז פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח. וְהָכִי מַשְׁמַע בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא:


זיז שעל גבי הפתח וכו' – here we are speaking where the opening is concealed. And because of this, we require that the projection will have an opening of a handbreadth. And this is implied in the Tosefta (see Tosefta Ohalot, Chapter 14, Halakha 9).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:2:2)


**וְשֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַחַלּוֹן רוּם אֶצְבָּעַיִם.** כְּלוֹמַר בֵּין חַלּוֹן שֶׁשִּׁעוּרוֹ רוּם אֶצְבָּעַיִם כְּגוֹן שְׁיָרֵי הַמָּאוֹר, בֵּין חַלּוֹן שֶׁשִּׁעוּרוֹ מְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ כְּגוֹן הָעוֹשֶׂה מָאוֹר בִּתְחִלָּה, זִיז שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא:


ושעל גבי החלון רום אצבעיים – that is to say, whether a window whose measure is a height of two fingerbreadths, such as the remnants of the light-hole (i.e., opening in the wall from the start – see Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 13, Mishnah 1) or whether its measurement is as large as the hole made with the [large carpenter’s] borer/drill, as for example, that makes the light-hole at the outset, the projection that on top of both of this is whatever the amount.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:2:3)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר מְלֹאוֹ.** חַלּוֹן שֶׁשִּׁעוּרוֹ רוּם אֶצְבָּעַיִם, זִיז שֶׁלּוֹ שִׁעוּרוֹ בְאֶצְבָּעַיִם. וְחַלּוֹן שֶׁשִּׁעוּרוֹ מְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ זִיז שֶׁלּוֹ נַמִּי מְשַׁעֲרִינַן בִּמְלֹא מַקְדֵּחַ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ורבי יוסי אומר מלואו (only if its height is of equal measure/of full capacity) – a window whose measure is a height of two fingerbreadths, its projection it’s measure is with two fingerbreadths. But a window whose measurement is as large as the hole made by a borer/drill, its projection we also estimate/assess to be as large as the hole made by a borer/drill. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:3:1)


**אֲפִלּוּ גָבֹהַּ מֵאָה אַמָּה.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּזִיז שִׁעוּר גָּבְהוֹ מִן הַפֶּתַח שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר טְפָחִים, בְּקָנֶה הֶחְמִירוּ מִשּׁוּם דְּמִטַּלְטֵל. וְרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי סָבַר דְּאֵין לְהַחֲמִיר בְּקָנֶה יוֹתֵר מִן הַזִּיז, וּלְמַעְלָה מִשְּׁנֵים עָשָׂר טְפָחִים שִׁעוּרוֹ בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי:


אפילו גבוה מאה אמה – even if the projection, the measurement of its height from the opening is twelve handbreadths, with a reed they were stringent because it is carried. But Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri holds that one should not be stringent with a reed more than with the wall projection, and higher than twelve handbreadths with an opening of one handbreadth. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:4:1)


**זִיז.** שֶׁהוּא רָחָב טֶפַח וְסוֹבֵב אֶת כָּל כָּתְלֵי הַבַּיִת מִבַּחוּץ, וּכְנֶגֶד הַפֶּתַח אוֹכֵל שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת וְעַל שְׁאָר הַפֶּתַח אֵין זִיז. שֶׁאִם הָיָה אוֹכֵל כְּנֶגֶד הַפֶּתַח טֶפַח הָיָה הַבַּיִת טָמֵא לְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל:


זיז – which is a handbreadth wide and surrounds all the walls of the house from the outside, and opposite the opening/door extends three fingerbreadths, but on the rest of the opening/door, there is no projection. For if there was an extension opposite the opening/door of a handbreadth, the house would be impure according to the words of everyone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:4:2)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו טְמֵאִים.** בְּהָא לֹא פָלֵיג רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ כִּדְפָלֵיג בְּסֵיפָא, לְפִי שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ טֻמְאָה לָצֵאת:


כלים שתחתיו טמאים – in this Rabbi Yehoshua does not dispute as he disputes in the concluding clause [of the Mishnah], because it is the manner of defilement to depart/go out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:4:3)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַבַּיִת.** כֵּיוָן דְּזִיז חָמוּר שֶׁמְּטַמֵּא בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא, מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לַבַּיִת בְּשָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת:


רבי אליעזר מטמא את הבית – because the projection is stringent that it defiles in any amount, it brings defilement to the house with three fingerbreadths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:4:4)


**וְכֵן בְּחָצֵר שֶׁהִיא מֻקֶּפֶת אַכְסַדְרָה.** וְהָאַכְסַדְרָה אוֹכֶלֶת בַּפֶּתַח שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת, טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת, כֵּלִים שֶׁבָּאַכְסַדְרָה טְמֵאִים. טֻמְאָה בָאַכְסַדְרָה, רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְטַמֵּא הַבַּיִת, וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מְטַהֵר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


וכן בחצר שהיא מוקפת אכסדרה – but the covered place in front of the house/parlor extends three finger breadths, [if] defilement is in the house, vessels that are in the parlor are impure. [If] defilement is in the parlor, Rabbi Eliezer declares the house to be impure. But Rabbi Yehoshua declares it to be pure. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:1)


**שְׁנֵי זִיזִין זֶה עַל גַּבֵּי זֶה.** וּמְכֻוָּנִים זֶה כְנֶגֶד זֶה:


שני זיזין זה על גב זה – and they are corresponding this one opposite that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:2)


**יֵשׁ בָּהֶן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד מִן הַזִּיזִים רָחְבּוֹ טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח:


יש בהן פותח פתח – that each one of the wall projections – its width is a handbreadth by a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:3)


**וּבֵינֵיהֶן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** הַתַּחְתּוֹן גָּבֹהַּ מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח וְהָעֶלְיוֹן גָּבֹהַּ מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹן טֶפַח:


וביניהן פותח טפח – the bottom one is above from the ground a handbreadth and the upper one is a handbreadth above from the lower one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:4)


**וְטֻמְאָה תַחְתֵּיהֶן.** תַּחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן. כָּל מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתָּיו טָמֵא וּמַה שֶּׁעַל גַּבָּיו טָהוֹר, דְּחוֹצֵץ בִּפְנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


וטומאה תחתיהן – underneath the bottom one. Everything that is below it is impure and what is above it is pure, for it interposes in the face of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:5)


**בֵּינֵיהֶן.** אִם טֻמְאָה בֵּין תַּחְתּוֹן לָעֶלְיוֹן כָּל מַה שֶּׁבֵּינֵיהֶן טָמֵא, אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁתַּחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן וְשֶׁעַל הָעֶלְיוֹן, טָהוֹר, דְּחוֹצְצִים:


ביניהן – if defilement is between the bottom one (i.e., projection) and the upper one, everything that is between them is impure. But what is under the bottom one and that which is above the higher one, is pure, for they interpose.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:6)


**עַל גַּבֵּיהֶן.** עַל גַּבֵּי הָעֶלְיוֹן. כְּנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה עַד הָרָקִיעַ, טָמֵא, וְכָל הַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר:


על גביהן – on top of the upper projection. And what is opposite the defilement until the firmament, is impure. And all the rest is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:7)


**הָיָה הָעֶלְיוֹן עוֹדֵף עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹן וֽכוּ'‏ תַּחְתֵּיהֶן וּבֵינֵיהֶן טָמֵא.** דְּבֵין שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה תַחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן בֵּין שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בֵינֵיהֶן, אוֹתוֹ הָעֹדֶף שֶׁהָעֶלְיוֹן יָתֵר עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹן טֶפַח מְעָרֵב אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה בְּכָל מָקוֹם. אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁעַל הָעֶלְיוֹן טָהוֹר, דְּחוֹצֵץ:


היה העליון עודף על התחתון וכו' תחתיהן וביניהן טמא – that whether the defilement is under the lower [projection] or whether that the defilement is between them, the very excess that the upper projection is greater than the bottom [projection] by a handbreadth combines the defilement in every place. But what is above the upper [projection] is pure, for it interposes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:8)


**עַל גַּבֵּיהֶן.** עַל גַּבֵּי הָעֶלְיוֹן:


על גביהן – on top of the upper [projection].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:9)


**הָיָה הָעֶלְיוֹן עוֹדֵף עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹן וֽכוּ'‏ טֻמְאָה תַחְתֵּיהֶן.** תַּחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן:


היה העליון עודף על התחתון וכו' טומאה תחתיהן – that is under the lower [projection].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:10)


**תַּחְתֵּיהֶן וּבֵינֵיהֶן טָמֵא.** דְּאוֹתוֹ עֹדֶף מְעָרֵב אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה בֵּינֵיהֶן וּמֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לְמַה שֶּׁתַּחְתָּיו:


תחתיהן וביניהן טמא – for that surplus combines/mixes the defilement and brings the defilement to what is below it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:11)


**בֵּינֵיהֶן.** אִם הַטֻּמְאָה בֵּינֵיהֶן אוֹ לְמַטָּה בָּאָרֶץ תַּחַת הַמּוֹתָר, דְּהַיְנוּ כְנֶגֶד הָעֹדֶף וְלֹא תַחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן:


ביניהן – if the defilement is between them or blow on the ground underneath the supplement, that is opposite the excess/surplus but not below the lower [projection].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:12)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר תַּחְתֵּיהֶן וּבֵינֵיהֶן טָמֵא.** דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֵיכָּא טֶפַח בָּעֹדֶף מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לְתַחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן, הוֹאִיל וְעוֹדֵף עָלָיו:


רבי אליעזר אומר תחתיהן וביניהן טמא – for even though there isn’t a handbreadth in the surplus, it brings the defilement under the lower [projection], since the surplus is over it [as a tent].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:13)


**בֵּינֵיהֶן וְתַחַת הַמּוֹתָר טָמֵא.** מַה שֶּׁבֵּין שְׁנֵי הַזִּיזִין וּמַה שֶּׁתַּחְתֵּיהֶן בָּאָרֶץ כְּנֶגֶד הַמּוֹתָר, טָמֵא:


ביניהן ותחת המותר טמא – what is between the two projections and what is underneath them on the ground opposite the surplus is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:5:14)


**וְתַחְתֵּיהֶן טָהוֹר.** מַה שֶּׁתַּחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן. דְּכֵיוָן דְּאֵין בַּמּוֹתָר טֶפַח אֵין הַטֻּמְאָה בָאָה תַחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ותחתיהן טהור – what is underneath the lower [projection], for since there isn’t in the surplus a handbreadth, the defilement does not come underneath the lower [projection]. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:6:1)


**וְאֵין בֵּינֵיהֶם פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** שֶׁאֵין הַתַּחְתּוֹן גָּבֹהַּ מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח וְאֵין הָעֶלְיוֹן גָּבֹהַּ מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹן טֶפַח:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:6:2)


**בֵּינֵיהֶן וְתַחְתֵּיהֶן טָמֵא.** דְּאֵין הַתַּחְתּוֹן חוֹצֵץ, כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין תַּחְתָּיו פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח וְלֹא עַל גַּבָּיו פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, וְהָעֶלְיוֹן מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה לְכָל מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתָּיו וְתַחַת הַתַּחְתּוֹן, וְחוֹצֵץ לְמַה שֶּׁעַל גַּבָּיו, כֵּיוָן דְּעֶלְיוֹן גָּבֹהַּ מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:6:3)


**כְּנֶגְדּוֹ עַד הָרָקִיעַ טָמֵא.** וּמַה שֶּׁתַּחְתָּיו טָהוֹר. דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין גָּבֹהַּ מֵחֲבֵרוֹ טֶפַח, הֲרֵי גָּבֹהַּ מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח וְחוֹצֵץ:



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:7:1)


**אֵין בָּהֶן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** שֶׁהַזִּיזִין אֵין בְּרָחְבָּן טֶפַח, הִלְכָּךְ בֵּין שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה לְמַטָּה בֵּין שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה לְמַעְלָה לֹא חַיְצֵי כְּלָל:


אין בהן פותח טפח – that the [wall] projection do not have the width of a handbreadth, therefore, whether the defilement is below or whether the defilement is above, it does not interpose at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 14:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/14:7:2)


**וְכֵן שְׁתֵּי יְרִיעוֹת שֶׁהֵן גְּבוֹהוֹת מִן הָאָרֶץ בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** וּגְבוֹהוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ טֶפַח, דִּינָן כִּשְׁנֵי זִיזִין:


וכן שתי יריעות שהן גבוהות מן הארץ בפותח טפח – and they higher than each other by a handbreadth, and their law is like that of two [wall] projections.



## Chapter 15


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:1:1)


**סָגוֹס עָבֶה.** הַךְ מַתְנִיתִין פֵּרַשְׁנוּהָ לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק הַבַּיִת שֶׁנִּסְדַּק [מִשְׁנָה ג']. וְאַיְדֵי דְבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי טַבְלָיוֹת, הֲדַר תַּנְיֵהּ הָכָא:


סגוס עבה (a thick coarse woolen blanket) – This Mishnah was explained above (i.e., Chapter 11) in the chapter "הבית שנסדק"/The house that was split [Mishnah 3]. And since it is necessary to teach about boards/planks, it is taught once again here.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:1:2)


**וְאִם הָיוּ שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ.** אֲפִלּוּ עֶלְיוֹנָה גְבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח לָא חֲשִׁיבֵי אֹהֶל לְהָבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, כֵּיוָן דְּהַתַּחְתּוֹנָה מְמַעֶטֶת אֶת הָאֲוִיר לְפִי שֶׁהֵן נֶחְשָׁבוֹת כָּאָרֶץ. אֲבָל טַבְלָאוֹת שֶׁל עֵץ אֵינָן חֲשׁוּבוֹת כְּקַרְקַע בִּזְמַן שֶׁהָעֶלְיוֹנָה גְּבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַתַּחְתּוֹנָה מְמַעֶטֶת אֶת הָאֲוִיר, הָעֶלְיוֹנָה מְבִיאָה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה:


ואם היו של שיש – even if the larger one was a handbreadth above the ground, it is not considered a tent to bring the defilement, since the lower one reduces the airspace because they are considered like the ground. But boards/planks of wood are not considered like the ground at the time when the upper one is a handbreadth higher than the ground, even though the lower one reduces the airspace, the upper one brings the defilement.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:2:1)


**טַבְלָיוֹת שֶׁל עֵץ.** שֶׁהֵן סְמוּכוֹת זוֹ אֵצֶל זוֹ וְכָל אַחַת מֵהֶן גְּבוֹהָה מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח וְנוֹגְעוֹת בְּקַרְנוֹתֵיהֶן כָּל אַחַת בַּחֲבֶרְתָּהּ שֶׁאֶצְלָהּ, וְדֶרֶךְ קַרְנֵי הַטַּבְלָאוֹת שֶׁאֵין בְּרָחְבָּן טֶפַח, הִלְכָּךְ כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחַת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ, טְמֵאִים, וְשֶׁתַּחַת הַשְּׁנִיָּה טְהוֹרִים:


טבליות של עץ – that are adjacent to one another and each of them are a handbreadth above the ground and touch at their corners – each other with its neighbor that is near it, and it is the manner of the corners of the boards that they are not a handbreadth wide, therefore, vessels that are underneath the first that the defilement is under it are impure, but that which is under the second are pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:2:2)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּשְּׁנִיָּה טָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא כֵּלִים הַנּוֹגְעִים בְּמֵת וְכֵלִים בְּכֵלִים טְמֵאִים טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, הַשְּׁלִישִׁי בֵּין אָדָם בֵּין כֵּלִים טְמֵאִים טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב, הָא תְנַן הָתָם אֵין הָאֹהֶל מִתְחַשֵּׁב, הִלְכָּךְ טַבְלָא שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ אֵינָהּ מִן הַמִּנְיָן:


הנוגע בשניה טמא טומאת שבעה – and even though it is taught in the Mishnah in the first chapter [Mishnah 2] that: “Utensils which touch the corpse and utensils [which touch other] utensils are unclean with the uncleanness of seven [days]. The third, whether man or utensils, is unclean with the uncleanness [that passes at] evening.” But it is taught in the Mishnah there (i.e., at the conclusion of Mishnah 3): “The tent does not come under consideration.” Therefore, the board that the defilement is underneath it is not part of the count (i.e., is not included).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:2:3)


**עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בוֹ רִבּוּעַ בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** כְּמִין תֵּבָה פְּרוּצָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ אַרְבַּע דְּפָנוֹת הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים לַשֻּׁלְחָן שֶׁהַשֻּׁלְחָן יוֹשֵׁב עָלֶיהָ, וְאוֹתָהּ תֵּבָה פְּרוּצָה קָרוּי רִבּוּעַ. וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהַשֻּׁלְחָן בּוֹלֵט וְיוֹצֵא חוּץ מֵאוֹתוֹ רִבּוּעַ טֶפַח אוֹ פָּחוֹת אוֹ יוֹתֵר, וְהַשְׁתָּא קָאָמַר דְּצָרִיךְ שֶׁיְּהֵא הַשֻּׁלְחָן בּוֹלֵט חוּץ מֵאוֹתוֹ רִבּוּעַ בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, דְּמַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹךְ הָרִבּוּעַ אֵינוֹ מִצְטָרֵף. וְאִם טֻמְאָה נַמִּי בְתוֹךְ הָרִבּוּעַ מִבִּפְנִים, הָרִבּוּעַ חוֹצֵץ וּמוֹנֵעַ הַטֻּמְאָה מִלַּעֲבֹר לַחוּץ:


עד שיהא בו ריבוע בפותח טפח (until it has a squared handbreadth) – it is a kind of breached/broken-through chest that has four walls that they would make for a table when the table sits upon it, and that breached chest is called a square. And sometimes, that the table protrudes and extends outside from that square a handbreadth or less or more, and now it states that it needs that the table protrudes outward from that square with an opening of handbreadth, for just as what is within the square does not combine, if defilement is also within the square from the inside, the square interposes and prevents the defilement from passing to the outside.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:1)


**חָבִיּוֹת.** שׁוּרוֹת הַרְבֵּה שֶׁל חָבִיּוֹת, זוֹ אַחַר זוֹ וְזוֹ עַל זוֹ:


חביות – many rows of jars [of clay], this one after that one and this over that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:2)


**שֶׁהֵן יוֹשְׁבוֹת עַל שׁוּלֵיהֶן.** וּפִיהֶן לְמַעְלָה:


שהן יושבות על שוליהן – and their mouths are [facing] upward.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:3)


**אוֹ מֻטּוֹת עַל צִדֵּיהֶן.** וּפִיהֶן מִן הַצַּד:


או מוטות על צדיהן – and their mouths are from the side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:4)


**בָּאֲוִיר.** בַּגִּנָּה אוֹ בֶחָצֵר:


באויר – in a garden or in a courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:5)


**בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** בְּרֹחַב טֶפַח נוֹגְעוֹת זוֹ בָזוֹ:


בפותח טפח – with the width of a handbreadth they touch one another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:6)


**טֻמְאָה בוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה.** לַצְּדָדִין קָתָנֵי לַהּ. דְּמֻטּוֹת עַל צִדֵּיהֶן אֲפִלּוּ כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתֵּיהֶן וְעַל גַּבֵּיהֶן, אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָן טָהוֹר, כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מִטַּמֵּא מִגַּבּוֹ, עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ מַצִּיל. וּמַה שֶּׁאֵינוֹ כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁתַּחְתֵּיהֶן וְעַל גַּבֵּיהֶן טָהוֹר. וּבְיוֹשְׁבוֹת עַל שׁוּלֵיהֶן אֲפִלּוּ עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ אֵין מַצִּילוֹת, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין הֶפְסֵק מֵאֲוִיר תּוֹכוֹ לָאֲוִיר שֶׁחוּצָה לוֹ, וְחָבִית עַצְמָהּ נִטְמְאָה וְנִטְמְאוּ כָל כֵּלִים שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ וַאֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד הַטֻּמְאָה, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן טַעֲמָא בְּסוֹף פֶּרֶק כַּוֶּרֶת:


טומאה בוקעת ועולה – to the sides it teaches [in the Mishnah]. That they lie on their sides, even facing the defilement, nothing is impure other than what is below them and on top of them, but what is within them is pure, since that an earthenware vessel does not become impure on top of it, it protects what is inside of it, and what is not facing the defilement, even that it is under them or above them, is pure. And when they sit on their rims, even what is within is not protected, because there is no interruption from the airspace inside it to the airspace that is outside of it, but the jar itself is defiled, and all of the vessels that are in it are defiled, and even if it is not facing the defilement, as we stated the reason at the conclusion of the chapter הכוורת/The Hive (Chapter 9 of Tractate Ohalot, Mishnah 16).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:7)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים בִּטְהוֹרוֹת.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּטְהוֹרוֹת הֵן דָּמוּ לְטַבְלָאוֹת שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ שֶׁהַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת סוֹתְמוֹת אֶת חֲלַל טֶפַח וְטֻמְאָה בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה:


במה דברים אמורים בטהורות – for since they are pure, they are similar to boards of marble whose bottoms conceal the empty space/cavity of a handbreadth and the defilement bursts through and ascends.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:8)


**אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ טְמֵאוֹת.** אֲפִלּוּ אֵין גְּבוֹהוֹת מִן הָאָרֶץ, הָעֶלְיוֹנוֹת שֶׁגְּבוֹהוֹת מִן הָאָרֶץ טֶפַח מְבִיאוֹת אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, שֶׁאֵין הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת סוֹתְמוֹת כֵּיוָן דִּטְמֵאוֹת מִידִי דַהֲוֵי אַקְּפוּלִין זוֹ עַל זוֹ וְטַבְלָאוֹת זוֹ עַל זוֹ:


אבל אם היו טמאות – even if they are not above the ground, the upper ones which are a handbreadth above the ground bring the defilement, for the lower ones do not conceal/stop up, since they are impure, this resembles the ones folded over/doubled on each other and boards on top of each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:3:9)


**אוֹ גְבוֹהוֹת מִן הָאָרֶץ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** מְבִיאוֹת אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ טְהוֹרוֹת, כֵּיוָן דְּנוֹגְעוֹת זוֹ בָזוֹ בְּרֹחַב טֶפַח נֶחְשָׁבוֹת כֻּלָּן כְּאֹהֶל אֶחָד:


או גבוהות מן הארץ פותח טפח – they bring the defilement, and even the pure ones since they touch each other in a width of a handbreadth they are all considered like one tent.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:1)


**שֶׁחֲצָצוֹ.** שֶׁעָשָׂה בוֹ מְחִצָּה:


שחצצו – that he made in it a partition (i.e., a room which one partitioned off with boards or tapestry on the sides – walls – or on the ceiling).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:2)


**מִן הַצְּדָדִין.** שֶׁהַיְרִיעוֹת וְהַנְּסָרִים נְתוּנוֹת כְּנֶגֶד הַכְּתָלִים שֶׁל הַבַּיִת:


מן הצדדין – that the curtains and the boards were placed opposite/corresponding to the walls of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:3)


**אוֹ מִן הַקּוֹרוֹת.** שֶׁנְּתוּנוֹת תַּחַת תִּקְרַת הַבַּיִת:


או מן הקורות – that they were placed underneath the ceiling of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:4)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבַּחֲצָץ טְהוֹרִים.** דִּירִיעוֹת וּנְסָרִים חוֹצְצִים, וְנֶחְשָׁבִים כְּכֵלִים שֶׁעַל הַגַּג וּכְכֵלִים שֶׁחוּץ לַכֹּתֶל:


כלים שבחצץ טהורים (vessels that are in the space between two partitions/the vacuum) – that the curtains and the boards interpose, and are considered like vessels that are on the roof and like vessels that are outside of the wall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:5)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבַּבַּיִת טְמֵאִים.** דְּאֵין חֲצִיצָה לַטֻּמְאָה, מִידִי דַהֲוָה אַכְּלִי חֶרֶס הַמֻּקָּף צָמִיד פָּתִיל, דְּאִם טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת מַצִּיל עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ, וְאִם טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ אֵין צָמִיד פָּתִיל לַטֻּמְאָה:


כלים שבבית טמאים – for there is no interposition for defilement, for this resembles an earthenware vessel that is surrounded with an airtight lid, for if the defilement that is in the house it protects over what is within it, but if the defilement is within it, there is no airtight lid for defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:6)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבַּחֲצָץ.** הַכְּלִי שֶׁעִם הַטֻּמְאָה בְּתוֹךְ הַמְּחִצָּה:


כלים שבחצץ – the vessel that is with the defilement with in the partition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:7)


**אִם יֶשׁ שָׁם פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ שָׁם חָלָל רָחָב טֶפַח:


אם יש פותח טפח – that there is an empty space/cavity which is a handbreadth wide.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:4:8)


**וְאִם לָאו טְהוֹרִים.** הַכֵּלִים שֶׁבַּחֲצָץ. אֲבָל בַּיִת מִיהָא טָמֵא:


ואם לאו טהורים – the vessels that are in the space between the two partitions/the vacuum [are pure]. But a house however is impure.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:5:1)


**חֲצָצוֹ מֵאַרְצוֹ.** שֶׁכִּסָּה קַרְקָעִית הַבַּיִת בִּנְסָרִים אוֹ יְרִיעוֹת:


חצצו מארצו (if one partitioned it off from the floor - laying an additional floor with a vacuum between) – that he covered the bottom [of the floor] of the house with boards or curtains.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:5:2)


**טֻמְאָה בַחֲצָץ.** אֲפִלּוּ יֵשׁ בּוֹ טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח:


טומאה בחצץ – even if it has a handbreadth [in length] by a handbreadth [in width] at the height of a handbreadth [vessels that are in the house are impure].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:5:3)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבַּבַּיִת טְמֵאִים.** כְּבִיב שֶׁהוּא קָמוּר תַּחַת הַבַּיִת, דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק ג', יֵשׁ בּוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח וְאֵין בִּיצִיאָתוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, טֻמְאָה בְתוֹכוֹ הַבַּיִת טָמֵא, טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ טָהוֹר:


כלים שבבית טמאים – like a gutter/canal that is arched over underneath the house, as is taught in the Mishnah above in Chapter 3 [Mishnah 7]: “If it is a handbreadth wide, and its outlet is not a handbreadth-wide, the uncleanness is in in it - the house is unclean. Uncleanness is in the house – what is in it is clean.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:5:4
