[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:5:4)


**שֶׁאַרְצוֹ שֶׁל בַּיִת כָּמוֹהוּ.** כְּשֶׁאֵין בּוֹ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח:


שארצו של בית כמוהו – when it lacks an opening of a handbreadth.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:1)


**טֻמְאָה בִפְנִים.** בִּמְקוֹם הַתֶּבֶן:


טומאה בפנים- in the place of the straw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:2)


**שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַיְצִיאָה.** מָקוֹם פָּנוּי יֵשׁ כְּרֹחַב אַמָּה מִן הַפֶּתַח עַד כֹּתֶל שֶׁכְּנֶגְדּוֹ שֶׁנִּכְנָסִים וְיוֹצְאִים דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם לְהוֹצִיא הַתֶּבֶן וּלְהַכְנִיס. וְכֵלִים שֶׁבְּאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם הַפָּנוּי טְמֵאִים, מִידִי דַהֲוָה אַטֻּמְאָה בַּחֲצָץ דְּכֵלִים שֶׁבַּבַּיִת טְמֵאִים:


שכנגד היציאה – a vacant place which is like the width of a cubit from the opening until the wall that is opposite it that they (i.e., people) enter and depart on that path there to remove the straw and to bring it in. But the vessels that are within the vacant space are impure, which resembles the defilement in the vacuum/space of the two partitions of the vessels that are in the house are impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:3)


**טֻמְאָה בַחוּץ.** הַיְנוּ בַּמָּקוֹם הַפָּנוּי מִן הַפֶּתַח עַד כֹּתֶל שֶׁכְּנֶגְדּוֹ:


טומאה בחוץ – that is in the vacant space from the opening until the wall that is opposite it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:4)


**כֵּלִים שֶׁבִּפְנִים.** בִּמְקוֹם הַתֶּבֶן:


כלים שבפנים – in the place of the straw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:5)


**אִם יֵשׁ בִּמְקוֹמָן טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח טְהוֹרִים וְאִם לָאו טְמֵאִים.** דְּבַחֲצָץ נַמִּי אִם טֻמְאָה בַּבַּיִת כֵּלִים שֶׁבַּחֲצָץ שֶׁאֵין בִּמְקוֹמָן טֶפַח טְמֵאִים:


אם יש במקומן טפח על טפח ברום טפח טהורים ואם לאו טמאים – that is in the space between two partitions/in the vacuum also if defilement is in the house, vessels that are in the vacuum where there isn’t in their place a handbreadth are impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:6:6)


**וְאִם יֵשׁ בֵּין הַתֶּבֶן וְלַקּוֹרוֹת פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** לֹא נִתְבַּטֵּל הַתֶּבֶן הַהוּא לֵחָשֵׁב כַּחֲצָץ וַהֲרֵי הוּא כִשְׁאָר חֲפָצִים שֶׁבַּבַּיִת, וְאֵין מַצִּיל עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ בֵּין יֵשׁ בִּמְקוֹמוֹ טֶפַח בֵּין אֵין בִּמְקוֹמוֹ טֶפַח:


ואם יש בין התבן ולקורות פותח טפח - that this straw was not nullified to be considered like a vacuum/space of the two partitions and it is like the rest of the belongings that are in the house. But it doesn’t protect on what is inside, whether there is in its place a handbreadth or whether there isn’t in its place a handbreadth [of space].



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:7:1)


**וְכֵן כְּרִי שֶׁל תְּבוּאָה.** שֶׁבִּטְּלוֹ:


וכן כרי של תבואה – that they abandoned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:7:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ טֻמְאָה בְצַד הַכֵּלִים.** שֶׁהָיְתָה הַטֻּמְאָה בְּתוֹךְ הַגַּל, וְכֵלִים שָׁם סְמוּכִים לַטֻּמְאָה בְּתוֹךְ הַגַּל אֲבָל אֵינָן נוֹגְעִים בַּטֻּמְאָה:


אפילו טומאה בצד הכלים – that the defilement was within the heap of stones, and vessels there were adjacent/near to the defilement within the heap but ae not touching the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:7:3)


**טֻמְאָה בוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה.** וּמַה שֶּׁבְּצִדָּהּ טָהוֹר. וְאִם יֵשׁ בִּמְקוֹם הַטֻּמְאָה טֶפַח עַל טֶפַח בְּרוּם טֶפַח, הֲרֵי הוּא כְקֶבֶר סָתוּם וּמְטַמֵּא כָּל סְבִיבָיו:


טומאה בוקעת ועולה – but whatever is at its side is pure. And if there is in the place of the defilement a handbreadth [length) by a handbreadth [width] at a height of a handbreadth, it is indeed like a concealed grave and it defiles everything that is around it.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:1)


**חֲצַר הַקֶּבֶר.** חָצֵר הַמֻּקֶּפֶת מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹתֶיהָ אַרְבַּע מְעָרוֹת שֶׁל קְבָרוֹת:


חצר הקבר – he courtyard that surrounds from its four directions four caves of graves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:2)


**טָהוֹר עַד שֶׁיְהֵא בָהּ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת טָהוֹר הָעוֹמֵד בָּהּ. אֲבָל פְּחוּתָה מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת, טָמֵא הָעוֹמֵד בָּהּ. וְאִם אֵין שָׁם אֶלָּא שָׁלֹשׁ מְעָרוֹת מִשָּׁלֹשׁ רוּחוֹת וְרוּחַ רְבִיעִית שֶׁל חָצֵר פְּתוּחָה לַאֲוִיר הָעוֹלָם, אֲפִלּוּ אֵינוֹ מְרֻחָק מִפֶּתַח הַמְּעָרָה אֶלָּא כָּל שֶׁהוּא, טָהוֹר, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִגַּע לַמַּשְׁקוֹף שֶׁל פֶּתַח הַמְּעָרָה. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי חֲצַר הַקֶּבֶר דִּמְסַיְּמָא מְחִצָּתָהּ, אֲבָל מֵת בְּעָלְמָא תּוֹפֵס אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת לְטֻמְאָה:


טהור עד שיהא בה ארבע אמות – all the while that there are four cubits the person who stands in its midst is ritually pure. But less than four cubits, the person who stands in its midst is impure, but if there aren’t there other than three caves from three directions and the fourth direction of the courtyard is open to the airspace of the world, even if he isn’t is not distanced from the opening of the cave other than whatever the amount, he is pure, as long as he doesn’t touch the lintel of the opening of the cave. And these words [apply] when the courtyard of the grave is marked with a partition, but for a mere corpse [it takes only] four cubits for defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:3)


**גּוֹלֵל לְקֶבֶר.** כִּסּוּי עַל פֶּתַח הַקֶּבֶר:


גולל לקבר – a covering on the opening of the burial cave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:4)


**אֶלָּא כְנֶגֶד הַפֶּתַח.** שֶׁל הַקֶּבֶר. וְהַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּקָּצֶה הַמֻּנָּח חוּץ לַקֶּבֶר, טָהוֹר:


אלא כנגד הפתח (only the part directly opposite the opening) – of the burial crave. But a person who touches the end that is placed outside of the grave, is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:5)


**עָשָׂה רֹאשָׁהּ גּוֹלֵל לְקֶבֶר.** וַהֲרֵי הִיא עוֹמֶדֶת עַל הַקֶּבֶר כְּמוֹ אִילָן. הַנּוֹגֵעַ מִמֶּנָּה בְּאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים סָמוּךְ לַקֶּבֶר, טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם גּוֹלֵל, וּמֵאַרְבָּעָה וּלְמַעְלָה טָהוֹר:


עשה ראשה גולל לקבר – and behold it stands over the burial cave like a tree. A person who comes in contact with it at four handbreadths near the burial cave is impure because of the stone placed on top of a burial cave/top stone, but from four [handbreadths] and above, he is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:6)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהוּא עָתִיד לָגֹד.** לָקֹץ. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא קִצֵּץ עֲדַיִן. לָגֹד, כְּמוֹ גֹּדּוּ אִילָנָא (דניאל ד):


בזמן שהוא עתיד לגוד – to cut it off. And even though he did not cut it yet. [The word] לגוד is like (Daniel 4:20): “[The holy Watcher whom the king saw descend from heaven and say,] Hew down the tree [and destroy it].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:8:7)


**כֻּלָּהּ חִבּוּר.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא קִצֵּץ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעָתִיד לָקֹץ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


כולו חיבור – all he while that he did not cut it, and even though he will ultimately cut it down. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:9:1)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהּ.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהִיא גוֹלֵל:


הנוגע בה – all the time that it is a covering on the opening of a burial cave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:9:2)


**טָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע עַל פְּנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה, לְרַבּוֹת גּוֹלֵל וְדוֹפֵק:


טמא טומאת שבעה – as it is written (Numbers 19:16): “And in the open, anyone who touches a person [who was killed or who died naturally, or human bone, or a grave, shall be unclean seven days],” including the stone placed on top of a burial cave/גולל and the frame supporting the movable stone of a tomb/דופק.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:9:3)


**וְהֶחָבִית וְהַמַּשְׁקִין טְהוֹרִים.** דִּכְלִי חֶרֶס הַמֻּקָּף צָמִיד פָּתִיל טָהוֹר מִלִּטַּמֵּא בְאֹהֶל הַמֵּת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) וְכֹל כְּלִי פָתוּחַ אֲשֶׁר אֵין צָמִיד פָּתִיל עָלָיו טָמֵא הוּא, הָא יֵשׁ צָמִיד פָּתִיל עָלָיו טָהוֹר, וּבִכְלִי חֶרֶס הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר, שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה מִגַּבּוֹ:


והחבית והמשקין טהורים – that an earthenware vessel that is surrounded with an air-tight lid is pure from being defiled in the tent of a corpse, as it states (Numbers 19:15): “And every open vessel, with no lid fastened down, shall be unclean,” but if it is has an air-tight lid upon it, it is pure, and an earthenware vessel that Scripture speaks of is not susceptible to receive defilement from outside it/above it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:9:4)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר וֽכוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר, אֶלָּא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין לְךָ דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים וּמְטַמֵּא, חוּץ מֵאָדָם, מִכָּל מָקוֹם מְטַמְּאִים הֵן מִשּׁוּם גּוֹלֵל כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהֵן גּוֹלֵל לַקֶּבֶר:


רבי מאיר אומר וכו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir, but rather even though you don’t have a thing that is a living, breathing creature and it defiles, outside of a human being, nevertheless, they defile because of the top-fitting cover all the while that they are top-fitting covers for a burial cave.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:1)


**הַמַּאֲהִיל עַל הַמֵּת וְנוֹגֵעַ בְּכֵלִים.** הַכֵּלִים טְמֵאִין טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה מִטַּעַם חִבּוּרִים, כְּגוֹן דִּבְשָׁעָה שֶׁנָּגַע בַּמֵּת אוֹ מַאֲהִיל עָלָיו, נוֹגֵעַ כְּמוֹ כֵן בַּכֵּלִים:


המאהיל על המת ונוגע בכלים – the vessels are impure for a period of seven days because of attachments, as for example, that at the time that he touched the corpse or overshadowed it, a person who touches likewise with vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:2)


**וְאִם יֵשׁ בְּיָדָיו פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח טְמֵאִים.** כֵּיוָן דְּיָדָיו מַאֲהִילוֹת עַל הַמֵּת וְעַל הַכֵּלִים, מְבִיאוֹת אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה:


ואם יש בידיו פותח טפח טמאים – since his hands overshadow over the corpse and over the vessels, they bring the defilement in.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:3)


**שְׁנֵי בָתִּים.** וּפִתְחֵיהֶן מְכֻוָּנִים זֶה כְנֶגֶד זֶה:


שני בתים – and their openings correspond with one another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:4)


**מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** וְנִשְׁאֲרוּ שְׁנֵי הַבָּתִּים טְמֵאִים. דְּאֹהֶל שֶׁל יָדָיו מְצָרֵף אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה כְּאִלּוּ שְׁנֵי הַחֲצָאִים מֻנָּחִים בָּזֶה וּבָזֶה:


מביא את הטומאה – and they remained as two impure houses. For a tent of his hands combines the defilement as if two halves ae placed in this one (i.e., hand) and that one (i.e., hand).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 15:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/15:10:5)


**וְאִם לָאו.** דְּאֵין בְּיָדָיו פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, גַּבְרָא מִיהָא טָמֵא הָוֵי, כְּדִתְנַן לְקַמָּן וְעַל עַצְמָן בְּכָל שֶׁהֵן, אֲבָל שְׁנֵי הַבָּתִּים טְהוֹרִים:


ואם לאו – that there isn’t an opening of a handbreadth in his hands. A person, however, is impure, as it is taught in the Mishnah further on [Chapter 16, Mishnah 1] and on themselves with whatever amount, but the two houses are ritually pure.



## Chapter 16


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:1)


**כָּל הַמִּטַּלְטְלִין מְבִיאִין אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** מִשּׁוּם אֹהֶל. שֶׁאִם הֶאֱהִיל רֹאשׁוֹ אֶחָד עַל הַמֵּת וְרֹאשׁוֹ אֶחָד עַל הַכֵּלִים, מֵבִיא לָהֶם הַטֻּמְאָה:


כל המיטלטלין מביאין את הטומאה – because of the tent. For if it overshadows its one head over the corpse and other head over the vessels, it brings the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:2)


**כָּעֳבִי הַמַּרְדֵּעַ.** אֲפִלּוּ אֵין בּוֹ רֹחַב טֶפַח אֶלָּא שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּהֶקֵּפוֹ טֶפַח, דְּחוּט שֶׁל טֶפַח מַקִּיפוֹ, דְּגָזְרוּ עַל הֶקֵּפוֹ מִשּׁוּם עָבְיוֹ:


כעובי המרדע (as thick as an ox-goad) – even if it lacks the width of a handbreadth but rather that it has in its circumference a handbreadth. For a thread of a handbreadth surrounds it, for they (i.e., the Rabbis) decreed on its circumference because of is thickness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:3)


**אֲקַפַּח אֶת בָּנַי.** אֶקְבְּרֵם. קִפּוּחַ לְשׁוֹן כְּרִיתָה וְחִתּוּךְ. וּשְׁבוּעָה הִיא, אֲקַפַּח אֶת בָּנַי אִם אֵין דָּבָר זֶה אֱמֶת שֶׁהֲלָכָה זוֹ מְקֻפַּחַת קְטוּעָה וּמְחֻתֶּכֶת, דְּהָא פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח בָּעִינַן:


אקפח את בני (May I bury my children -an oath frequently used by Rabbi Tarfon) – I will bury them. [The word] קיפוח/deprivation – is the language of mutilation/excision or cutting. And it is an oath, “may I bury my children if this thing is not true” that this Halakha is forced/prevented, defected/lopped and cut piece-meal. For we require an opening of a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:4)


**שֶׁשָּׁמַע הַשּׁוֹמֵעַ.** כְּשֶׁנִּשְׁאֲלָה שְׁאֵלָה עַל יְדֵי מַעֲשֶׂה בְּבֵית הַמִּדְרָשׁ וְשָׁמַע שֶׁטִּמְּאוּהוּ וְלֹא יָדַע מִשּׁוּם מָה טִמְּאוּהוּ. הֵם טִמְּאוּ אֶת הַמַּרְדֵּעַ מִשּׁוּם כֵּלִים הַמַּאֲהִילִים עַל הַמֵּת, שֶׁהַכְּלִי עַצְמוֹ הַמַּאֲהִיל עַל הַמֵּת טָמֵא בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא, דְּלֹא בָעִינַן פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח אֶלָּא לְהָבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה תַּחַת רֹאשׁוֹ הַשֵּׁנִי, וְטִמְּאוּ אֶת הָאִכָּר אוֹ מִטַּעַם חִבּוּרִים אוֹ מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲוֵי לֵיהּ כֵּלִים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בַמֵּת וְאָדָם בַּכֵּלִים וְטָמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה. וְהַשּׁוֹמֵעַ טָעָה לוֹמַר דְּטִמְּאוּהוּ מִטַּעַם שֶׁהַמַּרְדֵּעַ מֵבִיא עָלָיו טֻמְאָה מִדִּין אֹהֶל:


ששמע השומע – when the question was asked through an actual event/incident in the Bet Midrash/academy and he heard that they defiled and he did not know for what reason they defied it. They defiled the ox-goad because of the utensils that overshadow over the corpse, that the vessel itself that overshadows the corpse is impure regardless of the amount, for we don’t require an opening of a handbreadth other than to bring the defilement under its second head, and they defiled the farmer either because of either for the reason of connections or because he had vessels that touched the corpse and a person [who came in contact] with vessels and is impure for a period of seven days. But the listener erred to state that they defiled him for the reason that the ox-goad brings upon him defilement from the law of the tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:5)


**שֶׁיְּהוּ דִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים מְקֻיָּמִים.** שֶׁאוֹמְרִים דְּמַרְדֵּעַ מֵבִיא טֻמְאָה.


שיהו דברי חכמים מקויימים (that the words of the Sages may endure) – that they say that the ox-goad brings the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:6)


**עַל הָאָדָם הַנּוֹשְׂאָן.** זִמְנִין דְּאָדָם הַנּוֹשְׂאָן אֵין עָלָיו כִּי אִם טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב, שֶׁבִּגְדוֹ מַפְסִיק בֵּין כְּתֵפוֹ לַמַּרְדֵּעַ וַהֲוֵי לֵיהּ דְּיִקְרַב בִּדְיִקְרַב לִדְיִקְרַב, וְהָרוֹאֶה שֶׁמְּטַמְּאִים אוֹתוֹ טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב יִטְעֶה לוֹמַר מִשּׁוּם אֹהֶל טִמְּאוּהוּ לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּהֶקֵּפוֹ טֶפַח, וְאָתֵי לְמֵימַר דְּטֻמְאַת אֹהֶל אֵין בָּהּ כִּי אִם טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב. הִלְכָּךְ גָּזְרוּ עָלָיו בָּעֳבִי הַמַּרְדֵּעַ לִטַּמֵּא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה מִדִּבְרֵיהֶם מִשּׁוּם אֹהֶל:


על האדם הנושאן – there are times when a person who carries them does not have upon him anything other than defilement for one night, for his clothing interrupts between his shoulder and the ox-goat and it is for him “that he will offer with that he will offer to that he will offer,” (see Leviticus 21:17, 18 and 22:3) that a person who sees that they defile him a defilement of an evening will err to state that it is because of the tent that they defiled him, because it has a circumference of a handbreadth, and it comes to inform us that the defilement of a tent has naught other than the defilement of an evening. Therefore, they (i.e., the Sages) decreed upon him “like the thickness of an ox-goad” to defile for a period of seven days according to their words (i.e., of the Rabbis) because of the tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:7)


**וְעַל עַצְמָן בְּכָל שֶׁהֵן.** דִּכְלִי הַמַּאֲהִיל עַל הַמֵּת טָמֵא אֲפִלּוּ כָּל שֶׁהוּא:


ועל עצמן בכל שהוא – for a vessel that overshadows over a corpse is impure, even of any amount.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:8)


**וְעַל שְׁאָר אָדָם וְכֵלִים.** שֶׁתַּחַת הַמַּרְדֵּעַ.


ועל שאר אדם וכלים – that is underneath the ox-goad.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:1:9)


**בְּפוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין לָהֶם טֻמְאָה כְּלָל אֵין שַׁיָּךְ לִגְזֹר עַל הֶקֵּפוֹ מִשּׁוּם עָבְיוֹ:


בפותח טפח – because they don’t defilement at all, it doesn’t belong to defile on its circumference because of its thickness.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:1)


**כּוּשׁ.** פֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהַנָּשִׁים טוֹוֹת בּוֹ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּפְשׁוּטֵי כְּלִי עֵץ הוּא מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, מִשּׁוּם צִנֹּרֶת שֶׁל מַתֶּכֶת הַקְּבוּעָה בּוֹ:


כוש (reed used a spindle) – a spindle that women spin on. And even though they are flat wooden vessels, it is susceptible to receive Levitical defilement, because of the metal hook/curved pin that is fixed in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָן מְכֻוָּנִין.** שֶׁאֵין שְׁנֵי חֲצָאֵי זֵיתִים מְכֻוָּנִים זֶה כְּנֶגֶד זֶה:


אע"פ שאינן מכוונין (even though they are not directly opposite one another)- for two halves of an olive’s bulk [of a corpse] are not directly opposite each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:3)


**טָמֵא.** הַכּוּשׁ. דְּמֵבִיא הַטֻּמְאָה עַל עַצְמוֹ בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא:


טמא – the spindle. That it brings the defilement upon itself with any amount.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:4)


**הַקַּדָּר.** מוֹכֵר הַקְּדֵרוֹת:


הקדר – who sells pots.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:5)


**וְהַסַּל.** הַמּוֹט שֶׁנּוֹשֵׂא בּוֹ אֶת הַקְּדֵרוֹת. וּבְמַסֶּכֶת כֵּלִים קוֹרֵהוּ אֵסֶל, דִּתְנַן הָתָם [פי"ז מט"ז] הָאֵסֶל שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ בֵּית קִבּוּל מָעוֹת:


והסל – the balancing pole/staff that he carries the pots. And in the Tractate Kelim (Chapter 17, Mishnah 16) they call it an איסל /pole or yoke carried on two, or more commonly, one shoulder and it is taught in the Mishnah there: "האיסל שיש בו בית קיבול מעות"/a carrying yoke in which there is a (hidden) receptacle for money.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:6)


**וְאִם יֵשׁ בַּסַּל פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** מֵבִיא טֻמְאָה עַל הַקְּדֵרוֹת שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו לַצַּד הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁתְּלוּיוֹת בּוֹ:


ואם יש באיסל פותח טפח – it brings the defilement on the pots that are underneath it to the second side where they are suspended on it (i.e., the yoke).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:7)


**הַתְּלוּלִיּוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן תֵּל:


התלוליות (the mounds) – it is the language of a תל/a hill.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:8)


**הַקְּרוֹבוֹת בֵּין לָעִיר.** הַסְּמוּכָה לְבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת:


הקרובות בין לעיר – that are adjacent/near to the cemetery.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:9)


**בֵּין לַדֶּרֶךְ.** הַסָּמוּךְ לְבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת:


בין לדרך – that is near the cemetery.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:10)


**אֶחָד חֲדָשׁוֹת וְאֶחָד יְשָׁנוֹת טְמֵאוֹת.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַנָּשִׁים קוֹבְרוֹת שָׁם נִפְלֵיהֶן, דְּעַד חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה אָזְלָא אִשָּׁה לְחוֹדַהּ וְקוֹבֶרֶת שָׁם הַנֵּפֶל שֶׁלָּהּ. אֲבָל רְחוֹקוֹת לֹא אָזְלָא לְחוֹדַהּ לִקְבֹּר בַּתֵּל, וְהוֹאִיל וּצְרִיכָה לְאִישׁ שֶׁיֵּלֵךְ עִמָּהּ, הוֹלֶכֶת לְבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת וְאֵינָהּ קוֹבֶרֶת בַּתֵּל. וְהָא דִמְטַמְּאִינַן בִּישָׁנוֹת וַאֲפִלּוּ רְחוֹקוֹת, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא קְרוֹבוֹת הָיוּ מִתְּחִלָּתָן וְחָרְבָה הָעִיר וְנִשְׁתַּכַּח הַדָּבָר:


אחד חדשות ואחד ישנות – because that the women bury their non-viable births, that up to fifty cubits a woman goes alone and buries her non-viable birth. But the ones far away, a woman doesn’t go alone to bury [the non-viable birth] on the mound/hill, and since she needed a man that would go with her, she goes to the cemetery but doesn’t bury on the hill. And that which we defile the old ones and even the far-off ones, for we are concerned lest the near ones were from their beginnings and the city was destroyed and the matter would be forgotten.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:2:11)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ור' יהודה אומר כו' – And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:1)


**הַמּוֹצֵא מֵת בַּתְּחִלָּה.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה יָדוּעַ שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם קֶבֶר. וְהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא בְּנָזִיר [דַּף סד] הַמּוֹצֵא, וְלֹא שֶׁהָיָה מָצוּי. מֵת, פְּרָט לְהָרוּג, דְּהָרוּג אֵין לוֹ תְבוּסָה וְאֵין לוֹ שְׁכוּנַת קְבָרוֹת, וְהָכִי גְמִירֵי לַהּ.


המוצא מת בתחלה – for it was not known that there was a graveyard there, and this what we say in the Gemara in [Tractate] Nazir [folio 64b]: "המוצא"/He who finds a corpse, but not that it was accessible; dead, excluding one that was killed, for a killed person has no ground which is pressed by a body in the grave (and which is considered the dead person’s property) and he lacked a neighborhood of graves, and this is what is derived.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:2)


**מֻשְׁכָּב.** וְלֹא שֶׁהָיָה יוֹשֵׁב:


מושכב – and not that he was sitting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:3)


**כְּדַרְכּוֹ.** וְלֹא שֶׁהָיָה רֹאשׁוֹ מֻנָּח בֵּין יַרְכוֹתָיו. דְּכָל הָנֵי חָיְשִׁינַן לְהוּ שֶׁמָּא נָכְרִים הֵן, שֶׁאֵין דַּרְכָּן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל לִקְבֹּר מֵתִים כָּךְ:


כדרכו – and not that his head was lying between his thighs. For all of these, we are concerned/worried lest they are heathens, for it is not manner of Israelites to bury their dead as such.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:4)


**נוֹטְלוֹ וְאֶת תְּפוּסָתוֹ.** מֻתָּר לְפַנּוֹתוֹ מִשָּׁם וּלְקָבְרוֹ בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר. וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּטֹּל מִן הֶעָפָר שֶׁל הַקֶּבֶר עִמּוֹ, כְּדֵי תְפִיסָה, שֶׁהוּא כָּל הֶעֲפַר תִּחוֹחַ שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו, וְחוֹפֵר בְּקַרְקַע בְּתוּלָה שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת. דִּכְתִיב (בראשית מז) וּנְשָׂאתַנִי מִמִּצְרַיִם וּקְבַרְתַּנִי בִּקְבֻרָתָם, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר מִמִּצְרַיִם, אֶלָּא הָכִי קָאָמַר, מֵעָפָר שֶׁל מִצְרַיִם טֹל עִמִּי. וְרַמְבַּ"ם גָּרֵיס תְּבוּסָתוֹ, הֶעָפָר הַמְעֹרָב מִדָּם וְלֵחָה שֶׁל הַמֵּת. לְשׁוֹן מִתְבּוֹסֶסֶת בְּדָמָיִךְ (יחזקאל טז):


נוטלו ואת תבוסתו (if one finds in his field a body in the usual position of a buried man, the first time, he may remove it with the ground which it occupies – for burial in another place) – it is permissible to remove him from there and to bury him in another place. And he must take from the dust of the grave with him, the ground which is pressed by the body in the grave (and which is considered the dead man’s property), which is all the crushed, loose earth that is underneath him, and he digs in virgin earth three fingerbreadths as it is written (Genesis 47:30): “[When I lie down with my fathers,] take me up from Egypt and bury me in their burial-place,” for he didn’t need to say, “from Egypt”, but rather, this is what he said: “from the dust of Egypt take with me.” But Maimonides had the reading "תבוסתו"/his ground that is pressed by a body in a grave (as opposed to the reading in the Mishnah"תבוסתן"/their ground that is pressed by a body in a grave, the dust that is mixed from blood and the moisture/secretion of the corpse. It is the language of (Ezekiel 16:6): “[When I passed by you] and saw your wallowing in your blood, [I said to you: ‘live in spite of your blood.’].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:5)


**מָצָא שְׁלֹשָׁה.** אִם יֵשׁ בֵּין זֶה לָזֶה מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת וְעַד שְׁמֹנֶה. כְּלוֹמַר מִקֶּבֶר רִאשׁוֹן עַד שְׁלִישִׁי אֵין פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת וְלֹא יוֹתֵר עַל שְׁמֹנֶה:


מצא שלשה. אם יש בין זה לזה מארבע אמות ועד שמונה – meaning to say, from the first grave until the third, there is no less than four cubits and not more than eight [cubits].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:6)


**הֲרֵי זֶה שְׁכוּנַת קְבָרוֹת.** וְנִכָּר שֶׁלְּשֵׁם קְבוּרָה נְתָנוּם שָׁם, וְאָסוּר לְפַנּוֹתָם, דְּקָנוּ מְקוֹמָם. וַאֲפִלּוּ מֵת אֶחָד אִם נִכָּר שֶׁלְּשֵׁם קְבוּרָה נְתָנוּהוּ שָׁם אָסוּר לְפַנּוֹתוֹ. אֶלָּא שֶׁבְּאֶחָד אוֹ בִשְׁנַיִם אָנוּ תוֹלִים שֶׁלֹּא נִקְבְּרוּ שָׁם אֶלָּא לְפִי שָׁעָה וְהָיָה דַעְתָּם לְפַנּוֹתָם, אֲבָל בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה מוּכָח שֶׁזֶּה מָקוֹם מְיֻחָד לִקְבָרוֹת. וְאֹרֶךְ הַמְּעָרָה דַּרְכָּהּ לִהְיוֹת שֵׁשׁ וְרָחְבָּהּ אַרְבַּע וַאֲלַכְסוֹנָהּ עוֹדֵף שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁמֹנֶה, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי תְנַן מֵאַרְבָּעָה וְעַד שְׁמֹנֶה:


הרי זה שכונת קבורות (behold, this is a graveyard) – and it is recognized that to there burial is provided to them there, and it is prohibited to remove them, for they have acquired their place. And even,one corpse if it is known that to there burial was given to him, it is forbidden to remove him. But that for one or two we suspend [this view] that they were not buried there other than on a temporary basis and it was their intention to have them removed, but with three, it is proven that this is a special place for burials. And the length of the cave is its manner to be six [cubits] long and four [cubits] wide with a diagonal line of the surplus of two cubits which is eight. And because of this, it is taught [in our Mishnah]: “from four cubits and until eight.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:7)


**כִּמְלֹא מִטָּה וְקוֹבְרֶיהָ.** לֹא גָרְסִינַן לַהּ הָכָא:


כמלוא מטה וקובריה – we don’t have this reading here.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:8)


**בּוֹדֵק מִמֶּנּוּ וּלְהַלָּן עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה.** שֶׁהַמְּעָרָה אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת רֹחַב עַל שֵׁשׁ אֹרֶךְ, וְהֶחָצֵר שֶׁהַמְּעָרוֹת פְּתוּחוֹת לוֹ מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן, שֵׁשׁ עַל שֵׁשׁ, דְּהָכִי סְבִירָא לְהוּ לְרַבָּנָן בְּבָבָא בַתְרָא פֶּרֶק הַמּוֹכֵר פֵּרוֹת [דַּף קא], נִמְצָא אֹרֶךְ שְׁתֵּי מְעָרוֹת וְחָצֵר שֶׁבֵּינֵיהֶן שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה אַמּוֹת, וּלְפִי שֶׁפְּעָמִים חוֹפְרִים מְעָרָה אַחַת בַּאֲלַכְסוֹנָהּ, וַאֲלַכְסוֹן שֶׁל מְעָרָה עוֹדֵף עַל רִבּוּעָהּ שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת, נִמְצְאוּ עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה, שְׁמֹנֶה דִּמְעָרָה רִאשׁוֹנָה בַאֲלַכְסוֹנָהּ וְשֵׁשׁ דְּחָצֵר שֶׁבֵּין שְׁתֵּי הַמְּעָרוֹת וְשֵׁשׁ דִּמְעָרָה שְׁנִיָּה, דְּחַד אֲלַכְסוֹן אָמְרִינַן תְּרֵי אֲלַכְסוֹנֵי לֹא אָמְרִינַן, וְהַיְנוּ דְקָתָנֵי עֶשְׂרִים. וְעוֹד צָרִיךְ לִבְדֹּק מִלְּמַעְלָה וּמִלְּמַטָּה עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה, דְּהַיְנוּ אַרְבָּעִים אַמָּה, דְּשֶׁמָּא זוֹ הִיא מְעָרָה שֶׁבְּמִזְרַח הֶחָצֵר וְיֵשׁ עֲדַיִן אַחֶרֶת כְּנֶגְדָּהּ בְּמַעֲרַב הֶחָצֵר, אִי נַמִּי זוֹ הִיא שֶׁבְּמַעֲרַב הֶחָצֵר וְיֵשׁ עֲדַיִן אַחֶרֶת בְּמִזְרַח הֶחָצֵר:


בודק ממנו ולהלן עשרים אמה – that the cave is four cubits wide by six in length, and the courtyard and the caves are open to him from here and from there, six [cubits] by six [cubits], for this is what the Rabbis hold in [Tractate] Bava Batra, in the chapter: "המוכר פירות" /He who sells fruit (Chapter 6) [folio 101a, see also the Gemara on folio 102a], it is found that the length of two caves and a courtyard that is between them is eighteen cubits, and because that sometimes they dig one cave in a diagonal, and the diagonal of a cave has a surplus on its square of two cubits, they are found to be twenty cubits, eighteen of the first cave with a diagonal and six [cubits] of the courtyard that is between the two caves and six [cubits] of the second cave, for one diagonal we have stated, two diagonals we have not stated. And that is what is taught [in the Mishnah] regarding twenty. And further one needs to check from above and below twenty cubits, and that is forty cubits, for perhaps this is a cave that is in the eastern part of the courtyard and there still another [cave] that is opposite on the western side of the courtyard; alternatively, this one is on the western side of the courtyard and there is still another on the eastern part of the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:9)


**מָצָא מֵת אֶחָד בְּסוֹף עֶשְׂרִים בּוֹדֵק הֵימֶנּוּ וּלְהַלָּן עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה.** דְּמִי יֵימַר דְּמִבֵּית הַקְּבָרוֹת זֶה הַוְיָא הַהִיא מְעָרָה, שֶׁמָּא קֶבֶר אַחֵר הוּא וְחָצֵר אַחֶרֶת שֶׁל אָדָם אַחֵר, וְצָרִיךְ לַעֲשׂוֹת גַּם לְשָׁם כָּל בְּדִיקוֹת דִּלְעֵיל, דִּכְמוֹ שֶׁיֵּשׁ שָׁם קֶבֶר כְּמוֹ כֵן יֵשׁ אֲחֵרִים:


מצא מת אחד בסוף עשרים בודק הימנו ולהלן עשרים אמה – for who would say that from this cemetery there is this cave, perhaps there is another cave and another courtyard of another person, and one must also make to there all of the examinations mentioned above, for just as there is a grave there, just like this, there are others.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:10)


**שֶׁרַגְלַיִם לַדָּבָר.** אַחֲרֵי שֶׁנִּמְצָא כְּבָר בְּשָׂדֶה זוֹ שְׁכוּנַת קְבָרוֹת:


שרגלים לדבר – after that is found already in this field a graveyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:3:11)


**שֶׁאִלּוּ מִתְּחִלָּה מְצָאוֹ.** לְקֶבֶר זֶה קֹדֶם שֶׁמָּצָא הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה הָרִאשׁוֹנִים, הָיָה נוֹטְלוֹ וְאֶת תְּפוּסָתוֹ, כְּדִתְנַן בָּרֵישָׁא הַמּוֹצֵא מֵת בִּתְחִלָּה כוּ'‏ נוֹטְלוֹ וְאֶת תְּפוּסָתוֹ:


שאילו מחלה מצאו – for this grave prior to his having found the first three, he removes it and its soil that it occupies, as it is taught in the first clause of the Mishnah: "המוצא מת בתחלה כו' נוטלו ואת תפוסתו"/a person finds a corpse in first instance, he removes it and the soil that is with it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:4:1)


**הַבּוֹדֵק.** הָא דִתְנַן וּבוֹדֵק הֵימֶנּוּ וּלְהַלָּן עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה, אֵין צָרִיךְ לַחְפֹּר אֶת כֻּלָּן, אֶלָּא חוֹפֵר אַמָּה עַל אַמָּה וּמַנִּיחַ אַמָּה שֶׁאֵינוֹ חוֹפֵר, וְעוֹשֶׂה כֵן עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לְסוֹף עֶשְׂרִים. וְאוֹתָהּ אַמָּה שֶׁהוּא חוֹפֵר צָרִיךְ לַחְפֹּר עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לְסֶלַע, אוֹ לִבְתוּלָה, שֶׁהִיא קַרְקַע שֶׁלֹּא נֶחְפְּרָה מֵעוֹלָם:


הבודק – that which is taught in the [previous] Mishnah: "ובודק הימנו ולהלן עשרים אמה"/And he examines [the ground] from it and onward for twenty cubits, there is no need to dig it all up, but rather, he digs a cubit by a cubit and leaves a cubit hat he doesn’t dig, and he does this until he reaches the end of twenty [cubits]. And that cubit that he digs he must dig until he reaches the rock or the virgin/untouched soil, which is ground that had never ever been dug.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:4:2)


**הַמּוֹצִיא אֶת הֶעָפָר מִמְּקוֹם הַטֻּמְאָה.** מֵאוֹתָם הָאַמּוֹת שֶׁהוּא חוֹפֵר:


המוציא את העפר ממקום הטומאה – from those cubits that he digs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:4:3)


**אוֹכֵל בְּדִמְעוֹ.** אִם כֹּהֵן הוּא אוֹכֵל בִּתְרוּמָתוֹ. לְשׁוֹן מְלֵאָתְךָ וְדִמְעֲךָ לֹא תְאַחֵר (שמות כב). וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין כֹּהֲנִים רַשָּׁאִין לִבְדֹּק שֶׁהֲרֵי הֵן מֻזְהָרִין עַל הַטֻּמְאָה, מִכָּל מָקוֹם אִי אִתְרְמֵי כֹּהֵן וּבָדַק אֵינוֹ אָסוּר לֶאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה הֵיכָא דְּלֹא אִתְחַזַּק שָׁם טֻמְאָה:


אוכל בדמאו – if he is a Kohen, he eats of his heave-offering. It is the language of (Exodus 22:28): “You shall not put off the skimming of the first yield of your vats.” But even though the Kohanim are not permitted to check, they are warned about the defilement, nevertheless, if the Kohen happens by chance to check, he is not forbidden to eat heave-offering where it is not under the presumption of defilement being there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:4:4)


**הַמְפַקֵּחַ אֶת הַגַּל.** שֶׁל אֲבָנִים שֶׁנָּפַל עַל הָאָדָם וָמֵת, אִם כֹּהֵן הוּא אֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל בְּדִמְעוֹ, דְּכֵיוָן דְּהֻחְזְקָה שָׁם טֻמְאָה חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא הֶאֱהִיל, וַאֲפִלּוּ סָפֵק חַי סָפֵק מֵת חָיְשִׁינַן:


המפקח את הגל – of stones that fell upon a person and he died. If he was a Kohen, he does not eat of his doubtful heave offering. For since it is under the presumption that there is defilement there, we are concerned that perhaps [the corpse] was overshadowed, and even we are concerned even if he is doubtfully alive or doubtfully dead.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:1)


**הָיָה בוֹדֵק.** לְהַשְׁלִים עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה:


היה בודק- to complete the twenty cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:2)


**לְנַחַל.** נָהָר:


לנחל – river.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:3)


**לִשְׁלוּלִית.** אַמַּת הַמַּיִם שֶׁמְּחַלֶּקֶת שָׁלָל לַאֲגַפֶּיהָ שֶׁאַמּוֹת אֲחֵרוֹת שֶׁל מַיִם שׁוֹתוֹת מִמֶּנָּה:


לשלולית (to a pool, pond, rivulet) – a rivulet/canal which imparts booty (alluvium) to its banks and other canals of what drink from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:4)


**אוֹ לְדֶרֶךְ הָרַבִּים.** שֶׁאֵין קוֹבְרִים בָּהּ אֶת הַמֵּת:


או לדרך הרבים – and they don’t bury the dead/corpse in it (i.e., the public road).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:5)


**מַפְסִיק.** וְאֵינוֹ בוֹדֵק עוֹד לְהַשְׁלִים עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה:


מפסיק - and he doesn’t examine further to complete the twenty cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:6)


**וְהַכֹּל טָהוֹר.** וְאֵין לָהֶם תְּפוּסָה. אֶלָּא מְלַקֵּט הָעֲצָמוֹת בִּלְבַד וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לִטֹּל יוֹתֵר:


והכל טהור – and they don’t have the ground which it occupied (i.e., the earth surrounding the buried corpse), but rather he collects the bones alone and he doesn’t have to take more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 16:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/16:5:7)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון אומר כו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



## Chapter 17


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:1)


**הַחוֹרֵשׁ.** בֵּית הַפְּרָס. מָקוֹם שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה פְּרוּשָׂה וּמִתְפַּשֶּׁטֶת שָׁם. וְרַבּוֹתַי פֵרְשׁוּ, פְּרָס לְשׁוֹן דָּבָר פָּרוּס וְשָׁבוּר, שֶׁעֲצָמוֹת שֶׁל מֵת [שְׁלֵמוֹת] נִפְרְסוּ שָׁם. וּמִפִּי אֲחֵרִים שָׁמַעְתִּי, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁפַּרְסוֹת בְּנֵי אָדָם נִמְנָעִים לָלֶכֶת שָׁם מִפְּנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


החורש. בית הפרס – a place where the defilement is separated and flattened/straightened there. But my Rabbis explained that [the word] פרס is "דבר פרוס ושבור" (see also the Bartenura commentary to Tractate Niddah, Chapter 7, Mishnah 5 where the same phrase appears) something that is broken and injured, that the bones of the corpse were broken/divided there. And from the mouth of others I heard that as a result that the feet of human beings are prevented from walking there because of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:2)


**מְלֹא מַעֲנָה.** שֶׁל הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה. מִמְּקוֹם הַקֶּבֶר שֶׁהִתְחִיל לַחֲרֹשׁ בּוֹ עַד מֵאָה אַמָּה בְּאֹרֶךְ הַשָּׂדֶה עַל מֵאָה בְּרֹחַב, חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא הוֹלִיכָה הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה מִמֶּנּוּ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה. וְאִם הָיָה חוֹרֵשׁ וְהוֹלֵךְ חוּץ לְמֵאָה אַמָּה, מִמֵּאָה אַמָּה וְלַחוּץ טָהוֹר, שֶׁאֵין הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה מוֹלֶכֶת עַצְמוֹת הַקֶּבֶר אֶלָּא עַד מֵאָה אַמָּה:


מלא מענה (a furrow’s length) – of a plough. From the place of he grave that he began to plow until one-hundred cubits along the length of the field by one-hundred [cubits] in width, we are concerned lest the plough carries from him a [piece of human] bone the size of a barleycorn. But if he would continue ploughing outside of the one-hundred cubits, from one-hundred cubits and outward, it is ritually pure, for the plough does not carry bones of a grave other than up until one-hundred cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:3)


**בֵּית אַרְבַּעַת סְאִין.** מֵאָה אַמָּה אֹרֶךְ עַל מֵאָה אַמָּה רֹחַב הוּא מְקוֹם זְרִיעַת אַרְבַּע סְאִין, שֶׁהֲרֵי חֲצַר הַמִּשְׁכָּן שֶׁהָיָה מֵאָה עַל חֲמִשִּׁים הוּא בֵית סָאתַיִם, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּעֵרוּבִין [כג:], נִמְצָא מֵאָה עַל מֵאָה בֵּית אַרְבַּע סְאִין:


בית ארבעה סאין (a space of four Se’ah) – one-hundred cubits in length by one-hundred cubits in width is the place of seeding four Se’ah. For the courtyard of the Tabernacle that was one-hundred by fifty is Bet Se’atayim, as we state in [Tractate] Eruvin [23b], it is found that one-hundred cubits by one-hundred cubits is a Bet Arbah Se’ah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר בֵּית חָמֵשׁ סְאִין.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר בית חמש – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:5)


**וּבְמוֹרָד.** כְּלוֹמַר, שִׁעוּר זֶה שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ לְתַנָּא קַמָּא וּלְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי לְמַר כִּדְאִית לֵיהּ וּלְמַר כִּדְאִית לֵיהּ, הָנֵי מִלֵּי כְשֶׁמּוֹלִיךְ הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה בְּמוֹרָד, דִּמְגַלְגַּלְתּוֹ לְמֵרָחוֹק, וְהוּא הַדִּין כְּשֶׁהַקַּרְקַע שָׁוָה בְּמִישׁוֹר. אֲבָל בְּמַעֲלֶה, כְּשֶׁחוֹרֵשׁ וְעוֹלֶה, לְדִבְרֵי תַנָּא קַמָּא נוֹתֵן רֹבַע קַב כַּרְשִׁינִין עַל בֹּרֶךְ הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה, הוּא עֵץ חָלוּל כָּפוּף עָשׂוּי כְעֵין בֶּרֶךְ שֶׁהַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה תְּקוּעָה בוֹ, וְעוֹשֶׂה בְּשׁוּלָיו נֶקֶב קָטָן כְּשִׁעוּר שֶׁיֵּצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ גַּרְעִין אֶחָד מִגַּרְעִינֵי הַכַּרְשִׁינִים, וּמוֹלִיךְ הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה וְהַגַּרְעִינִים נוֹפְלִים מְעַט מְעַט, וּבְמָקוֹם שֶׁכָּלוּ הַכַּרְשִׁינִים שֶׁאֵין נוֹפֵל מֵהֶם הַרְבֵּה בְּיַחַד אֶלָּא שְׁלֹשָׁה גַרְעִינִים זֶה בְּצַד זֶה, שָׁם כָּלֶה בֵּית הַפְּרָס:


ובמורד (and in descent) - meaning to say, this measure that we stated to the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] and to Rabbi Yossi, to him as he holds and to the other (i.e., Rabbi Yossi) as he holds, these words [refer to] when the plough carries in descent, that it [looks like he] is rolling it from afar, and the same law applies when the land is on a plain level. But in an ascent, when he plows and ascends, according to the words of the first Tanna/teacher, he gives a quarter of a Kab of vetches on the knee-shaped pole of the plough- as far as the knee of the plough enters the soil, which is a bent wooden cavity made like a knee that the plough is affixed to, and it makes in its rim/bottom of the vessel a small incision like the measure that went out from it one globule/stone or kernel of a stone fruit, nut from the globules of the vetches, and it brings the plough and the globules fall off bit by bit, and in a place where the vetches have run out, not much falls from them together other than three globules each one by the side of the other and there was completed the Bet HaPras (i.e., the place declared - unclean on account of crushed bones carried over it from a ploughed grave).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:1:6)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר בְּמוֹרָד וְלֹא בְמַעֲלֶה.** לֹא אָמְרוּ דִין בֵּית הַפְּרָס אֶלָּא בְמוֹרָד, אֲבָל כָּשֶׁהַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה עוֹלָה בַּגֹּבַהּ אֵין שָׁם בֵּית הַפְּרָס כְּלָל, שֶׁמִּיָּד הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה נִנְעֶרֶת וְאִם יֵשׁ שָׁם עֶצֶם נוֹפֵל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר במורד ולא במעלה – they did not say the law of the Bet HaPras other than in descent, but when the plough ascends to at a height where there is no Bet HaPras at all, for immediately the hand of the plough is emptied and if there is a bone there it falls. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:1)


**וְהִטִּיחַ בְּסֶלַע.** הִכָּה הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה בְּסֶלַע וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ נִנְעֲרָה הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה:


והטיח בסלע (and he struck – with the plough against the rock) – the plough hit the rock and through it, the plough emptied/poured out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:2)


**אוֹ שֶׁנִּעֵר אֶת הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה.** מִן הֶעָפָר שֶׁעָלֶיהָ. וְהָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא לֹא נִעֵר יָפֶה:


או שניער את המחרישה (or he shook off the plough) – from the dirt that was upon it. And it comes to teach us that we are not concerned that he did not shake it off well.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:3)


**עַד שָׁם הוּא עוֹשֶׂה בֵית הַפְּרָס.** וְלֹא יוֹתֵר, וַאֲפִלּוּ תוֹךְ שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת אוֹ חָמֵשׁ:


עד שם הוא עושה בית הפרס – and no further, and even within six cubits or five [cubits].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:4)


**בֵּית פְּרַס עוֹשֶׂה בֵית פְּרָס.** הַמַּתְחִיל לַחֲרֹשׁ בְּסוֹף בֵּית הַפְּרָס אוֹ בְאֶמְצַע. מוֹנֶה מֵאָה אַמָּה מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁהִתְחִיל לַחֲרֹשׁ, וְהוּא נַעֲשָׂה בֵּית הַפְּרָס כְּאִלּוּ חָרַשׁ מִמְּקוֹם הַקֶּבֶר:


בית הפרס עושה בית הפרס – One who begins to plough at the end of a Bet HaPras or in the middle, he counts one-hundred cubits from the place that he began to plough, and it becomes a Bet HaPras as if he ploughed from the place of the grave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:2:5)


**חָרַשׁ חֲצִי מַעֲנָה.** חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה מִמְּקוֹם הַקֶּבֶר, וְחָזַר אַחַר כָּךְ וְחָרַשׁ מִשָּׁם עוֹד חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה אֲחֵרוֹת, כָּל הַמֵּאָה נַעֲשׂוּ בֵּית פְּרָס. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


חרש חצי מענה (he ploughed half a furrow) – fifty cubits form the place of the grave and he returned afterwards and ploughed from there another fifty cubits, all the one-hundred became/were made into a Bet HaPras. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:1)


**מְלָטַמְיָא.** כְּמוֹ מָלֵא טַמְיָא. חֲפִירָה מְלֵאָה מֵתִים זֶה עַל גַּבֵּי זֶה. טַמְיָא בְלָשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי עֲצָמוֹת שֶׁל מֵתִים:


מלטמיא (quarry; he who starts ploughing from a quarry – where chips of stones, bones, etc. are deposited, or from a deposit of bones) – like full of bones. The dig is filled with corpses one on top of the other. [The word] טמיא – in the Aramaic language are bones of corpses/dead people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:2)


**וּמִצְּבִירַת הָעֲצָמוֹת.** מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁהָעֲצָמוֹת צְבוּרִים שָׁם. דְּזֶה דָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מָצוּי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה אָדָם חוֹרֵשׁ עַל חֲפִירָה שֶׁהִיא מְלֵאָה מֵתִים וְעַל צְבִירַת עֲצָמוֹת גְּלוּיִים, וְלֹא גָזְרוּ אֶלָּא בְקֶבֶר שֶׁנֶּחֱרַשׁ שֶׁהוּא דָבָר הַמָּצוּי:


מצבירת העצמות (from a pile of bones) – from the place that the bones are piled up there. And this is a matter that is not accessible/frequent that a person will plough on a pit/dig that is filled with corpses and on the pile of bones they are revealed, and they (i.e., the Rabbis) did not decree other than with a grave that was ploughed that is a thing that is accessible.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:3)


**וּמִשָּׂדֶה שֶׁאָבַד בָּהּ קֶבֶר.** דַּהֲוֵי לֵיהּ סְפֵק סְפֵקָא, שֶׁמָּא לֹא חָרַשׁ בִּמְקוֹם הַקֶּבֶר, וְאִם תִּמְצֵי לוֹמַר חָרַשׁ שֶׁמָּא לֹא הוֹלִיךְ מִמֶּנָּה עֶצֶם:


ומשדה שאבד הקבר מתוכה – that this is a compound uncertainty, lest he did not plough in the place of he grave, and if you can say, he ploughed, but perhaps he did not carry from it a bone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:4)


**אוֹ שֶׁנִּמְצָא בָהּ קֶבֶר.** שֶׁחָרַשׁ וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִמְצָא. דְּבֵית הַפְּרָס דְּרַבָּנָן, וְלֹא קָנְסוּ לֵיהּ כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא יָדַע:


או שנמצא בה קבר- that he ploughed and afterwards it was found, that this is a Rabbinic Bet HaPras, and they did not fine him since he did not know.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:5)


**הַחוֹרֵשׁ אֶת שֶׁאֵינוֹ שֶׁלּוֹ.** לֹא קָנְסוּ רַבָּנָן לְמִהְוֵי אַרְעָא דְחַבְרֵיהּ בֵּית הַפְּרָס. וְכֵן נָכְרִי לֹא שַׁיָּךְ בֵּיהּ קְנָס:


החורש את שאינו שלו – the Rabbis did not fine him for the land is to be that of his colleague is the Bet HaPras. And similarly, regarding a heathen, a fine is not appropriate for him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:3:6)


**שֶׁאֵין בֵּית הַפְּרָס לַכּוּתִיִּים.** כְּלוֹמַר, שֶׁהֲרֵי אַף עַל הַכּוּתִיִּים לֹא קָנְסוּ, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁלֹּא קָנְסוּ עַל הַנָּכְרִי:


שאין בת הפרוס לכותיים – that is to say, that even on the Cutheans they (i.e., the Rabbis) did not fine, all the more so, that they didn’t fine the heathen.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:4:1)


**עַל גַּבֵּי טְהוֹרָה.** שֶׁשְּׂדֵה בֵּית הַפְּרָס לְמַעְלָה וּטְהוֹרָה לְמַטָּה:


על גבי טהורה – that the field of the Bet HaPras (i.e., the area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse) is above and it is pure below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:4:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ אֲדֻמָּה.** שֶׁהָיְתָה עֲפָרָהּ שֶׁל טְהוֹרָה אֲדֻמָּה:


אפילו אדומה – that the dirt of the pure area is red.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:4:3)


**וְהִלְבִּינוּהָ.** מֵחֲמַת עֲפַר בֵּית הַפְּרָס שֶׁהָיָה לָבָן:


והלבינה – on account of the dirt of the Bet HaPras which was white.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:4:4)


**אֵין עוֹשִׂין אוֹתָהּ בֵּית הַפְּרָס.** שֶׁלֹּא גָזְרוּ טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא עַל גּוּשׁ כִּבְרִיָּתוֹ, וְלֹא גָזְרוּ עַל הֶעָפָר הַנִּשְׁטָף עִם הַמַּיִם:


אין עושין אותה בית הפרס – they (i.e., the Rabbis did not decree defilement other than on a clod/clump of earth in its natural condition, but they did not decree on dirt that was swept away with the water.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:1)


**עֲלִיָּה טְהוֹרָה.** מִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ, אִם הַקֶּבֶר בְּתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת, הַבַּיִת טָמֵא וְהָעֲלִיָּה טְהוֹרָה. וְאִם הַקֶּבֶר תַּחַת אַסְקֻפַּת פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת, טֻמְאָה נִכְנֶסֶת לַבַּיִת וַעֲלִיָּה טְהוֹרָה. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁאֵין פֶּתַח הָעֲלִיָּה מְכֻוָּן כְּנֶגֶד פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת, חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא קָאֵי קֶבֶר תַּחַת הַכֹּתֶל מְכֻוָּן כְּנֶגֶד פִּתְחָהּ שֶׁל עֲלִיָּה, וּבוֹקַעַת הַטֻּמְאָה עַד שֶׁמַּגַּעַת לְפִתְחָהּ שֶׁל עֲלִיָּה, וְנִכְנֶסֶת לָעֲלִיָּה דֶּרֶךְ הַפֶּתַח:


עליה טהורה -whichever way you turn, if the grave is within the house, the house is impure and the upper chamber is ritually pure. If the grave is under the threshold of the entrance of the house, the defilement enters the house and the upper chamber is ritually pure. But when the opening of the upper chamber is not corresponding opposite the opening of the house, we are concerned that perhaps the grave exists underneath the wall that is corresponding to the opening of the upper chamber, and the defilement bursts forth until it reaches to the opening of the upper chamber, and enters into upper chamber through the opening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:2)


**וַעֲפַר חוּצָה לָאָרֶץ.** כְּלוֹמַר אוֹ עֲפַר חוּץ לָאָרֶץ. דְּאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים וּבֵית הַפְּרָס תַּרְוַיְהוּ מְטַמּוּ בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא:


ועפר חוצה לארץ – that is to say, or the dirt of outside of the Land of Israel. For the Land of the Nations and the Bet HaPras together defile through touching and through lifting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:3)


**שֶׁבָּא בְיָרָק.** כְּגוֹן אֲגֻדָּה שֶׁל יָרָק שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ קְלָחִים הַרְבֵּה שֶׁנֶּעֶקְרוּ בְגוּשֵׁיהֶן, וּבְכָל קֶלַח בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ לֵיכָּא עָפָר כְּחוֹתַם הַמַּרְצוּפִין שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר הַגּוּשׁ לְטַמֵּא:


שבא ברק – as for example a bunch of greens that have many stalks/stems that were uprooted in their clods of earth, for in each stalk/stem on its own, there isn’t dirt like the seal of the packing bags (especially adapted for ship-loads) which is the measure of clods of earth to defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:4)


**חוֹתַם הַמַּרְצוּפִין.** אַמְתָּחוֹת שֶׁל עוֹר כְּעֵין שַׂקִּין שֶׁהוֹלְכֵי דְרָכִים נוֹתְנִים כְּלֵיהֶם בְּתוֹכָם וְחוֹתְמִים עֲלֵיהֶם בְּחוֹתָם שֶׁל טִיט:


חותם המרצופין – leather bags, like sacks, that those who travel place their vessels into them and seal them with a seal of plaster.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 17:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/17:5:5)


**כִּסְאָה וּכְסָאתַיִם חוֹתָמוֹת.** שֶׁל טִיט. וְהַאי עֻבְדָּא מַיְתֵי רַבִּי יְהוּדָה לְסַיּוֹעֵי לְרַבָּנָן דְּלִסְאָה וְסָאתַיִם מֵעָפָר שֶׁבָּא מִחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ לֹא חָשְׁשׁוּ, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא הָיָה בְחוֹתָם אֶחָד בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ כְּחוֹתַם הַמַּרְצוּפִין. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


כסאה וכסאתים חותמות – of plaster. And this fact brings Rabbi Yehuda to the assistance of the Rabbis that they didn’t worry about a Se’ah or two Se’ah from the dirt that comes from outside the Land of Israel, for since there wasn’t the seal of one of them on its own like the seal of the leather bags. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



## Chapter 18


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:1)


**כֵּיצַד בּוֹצְרִין בֵּית הַפְּרָס.** וְיִהְיוּ הָעֲנָבִים שֶׁגָּדְלוּ בוֹ טְהוֹרִים:


כיצד בוצרים בית הפרס (how do they cut grapes in an area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse or crushed bones carried over it from a ploughed grave)- so that the grapes that grew in it would be ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:2)


**מַזִּין עַל הָאָדָם וְעַל הַכֵּלִים.** בַּשְּׁלִישִׁי:


מזין על האדם ועל הכלים – on the third day [they would sprinkle].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:3)


**וְשׁוֹנִין.** בַּשְּׁבִיעִי:


ושונין – on the seventh day [they repeat it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:4)


**וּמוֹצִיאִין חוּץ לְבֵית הַפְּרָס.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהַבּוֹצֵר לַגַּת הֻכְשַׁר לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה וְהוּא מִשְּׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר דָּבָר שֶׁגָּזְרוּ בוֹ בַיּוֹם, הָנֵי מִלֵּי לְטֻמְאָה דְאוֹרַיְתָא, אֲבָל לְטֻמְאַת בֵּית הַפְּרָס דְּרַבָּנָן הִיא לֹא חָשְׁבִינַן לַהּ הֶכְשֵׁר. וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי נָקַט וְתָנֵי מַזִּין עַל הָאָדָם וְעַל הַכֵּלִים, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ טְהוֹרִים מִטֻּמְאָה דְאוֹרַיְתָא, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחוֹזְרִין וּמִטַּמְּאִין בְּבֵית הַפְּרָס בִּשְׁעַת בְּצִירָה, תּוּ לֹא מִטַּמּוּ עֲנָבִים בִּנְגִיעָתָם מִשּׁוּם טֻמְאַת בֵּית הַפְּרָס כָּל כַּמָּה דְּלֹא הֻכְשַׁר הֶכְשֵׁר גָּמוּר:


ומוציאין חוץ לבית הפרס – and even though that one cuts grapes for the vat for wine-pressing are fit for receiving Levitical uncleanness and this is one of eighteen matters that they decreed on the day [that Rabban Gamaliel was deposed as head of the Sanhedrin (see Tractate Berakhot 28a)], these words [refer to] defilement according to the Torah, but concerning the defilement of the a field where uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or corpse or crushed bones carried over it from a ploughed grave is Rabbinic, and we don’t consider it a fitness to become unclean. And because of this, it (i.e., the Mishnah) took [the term] and taught: “they sprinkle on the man and on the vessels [and repeat the process],” in order that they would be pure from defilement according the Torah (see Numbers 19:19, and even though they go back and become defiled in the field declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed grave at the time of the harvesting of grapes, further, the grapes are not defiled through their contract because of the defilement of the field declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed grave all the while that they did not have a complete fitness to become unclean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:5)


**וַאֲחֵרִים.** שֶׁלֹּא נִכְנְסוּ בְּבֵית הַפְּרָס:


ואחרים – who did not enter into the field declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed grave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:6)


**מְקַבְּלִין.** מִן הַבּוֹצְרִים:


מקבלין – from those who cut/harvest the grapes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:7)


**וּמוֹלִיכִין לַגָּת.** בְּכֵלִים אֲחֵרִים. אֲבָל הַבּוֹצְרִים אֵין מוֹלִיכִין אוֹתָם לַגַּת, לְפִי שֶׁהַגַּת מָלֵא מַשְׁקִין וּמִתְכַּשְּׁרֵי בְהוּ הֶכְשֵׁר גָּמוּר:


ומוליכין לגת – in other vessels. But those who cut/harvest the grapes do not bring them to the vat for wine-pressing, because the vat is filled with liquids and they are pronounced fit [for Levitical uncleanness] for a complete fitness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:8)


**וְאִם נָגְעוּ אֵלּוּ וָאֵלּוּ.** הַבּוֹצְרִים נָגְעוּ בַּאֲחֵרִים הַמְקַבְּלִין: **טְמֵאִים.** הַמְקַבְּלִים וּמְטַמְּאִין כָּל הָעֲנָבִים. שֶׁהָאָדָם שֶׁנִּטְמָא בְּבֵית הַפְּרָס נַעֲשָׂה אַב הַטֻּמְאָה וּמְטַמֵּא אָדָם וְכֵלִים:


ואם נגעו אלו באלו – those who cut/harvest the grapes came in contact with/touched the others who receive [the grapes]. טמאים – those who receive [the grapes] and they all of the grapes. For a person that is defiled in a field declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed grave become a primary source of ritual defilement/ אב הטומאהand it defiles people and vessels [according to the School of Hillel]


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:9)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים אוֹחֵז אֶת הַמַּגָּל.** דְּסָבְרֵי הַבּוֹצֵר לַגַּת הֻכְשַׁר אֲפִלּוּ לְטֻמְאַת בֵּית הַפְּרָס, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי צָרִיךְ שֶׁיֹּאחַז הַמַּגָּל שֶׁהוּא בוֹצֵר בּוֹ בְּסִיב שֶׁל דֶּקֶל דְּלֹא מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


. בית שמאי אומרים אוחז את המגל – that they (i.e., the School of Shammai) holds that the person who harvests [grapes] in the vat becomes fit for Levitical uncleanness even for the defilement of a field declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed grave, and because of this, he must hold on to a sickle wrapped in a fibrous substance/bast of a palm tree when he harvests that does not receive defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:10)


**אוֹ בוֹצֵר בְּצוֹר.** וְהוּא אֶבֶן חַדָּה, דְּלָאו כְּלִי הִיא:


או בוצר בצור (or he harvests with a flint) – and it is a sharp stone that is not a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:11)


**וְנוֹתֵן לְתוֹךְ כְּפִישָׁה.** כְּלִי גָדוֹל שֶׁכּוֹבְשִׁים בּוֹ זֵיתִים, דְּלָאו בַּת קַבּוֹלֵי טֻמְאָה הוּא:


ונותן לתוך הכפישה (places it into an inverted vessel/a vessel divided into two compartments by the bottom between) – a large vessel that one harvests olives in, that it doesn’t receive ritual defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:12)


**וּמוֹלִיךְ לַגָּת.** אֲפִלּוּ הוּא עַצְמוֹ. דְּכֵיוָן דְּזָהִיר וְאִית לֵיהּ הֶכֵּרָא שֶׁמַּנִּיחַ בִּכְפִישָׁה לֹא נָגַע:


ומוליך לגת – even he himself. For since he is careful and he has recognition that he places it in an inverted vessel and hasn’t touched it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:1:13)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים בְּכֶרֶם הַנַּעֲשָׂה בֵית הַפְּרָס.** שֶׁהָיוּ גְּפָנִים וְאִילָנוֹת נְטוּעִים בּוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה בֵּית הַפְּרָס: **אֲבָל הַנּוֹטֵעַ.** לְכַתְּחִלָּה בְּבֵית הַפְּרָס: **יִמָּכֵר לַשּׁוּק.** דְּקָנְסִינַן לֵיהּ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לוֹ לִטַּע בְּבֵית הַפְּרָס. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


במה דברים אמורים כברם הנעשה בית הפרס – that the vines and trees were planted in it prior to it becoming a field declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed grave. But a person who touches it ab initio in the field declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed grave [should sell them in the market]. ימכר לשוק – that we fine him, because he should not have planted in a field declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed grave. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:1)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה בֵית הַפְּרָס הֵן.** כִּדְקָא חֲשִׁיב לְהוּ וְאָזֵיל. שָׂדֶה שֶׁנֶּחְרַשׁ בָּהּ קֶבֶר, וְשָׂדֶה שֶׁאָבַד בָּהּ קֶבֶר, וּשְׂדֵה בוֹכִים:


שלשה בית הפרס הן – that he considers them and leaves. A field in which a grave is ploughed up, and a field that lost a grave, and a field of mourners/tomb niches.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:2)


**נִטַּעַת כָּל נֶטַע.** נוֹטֵעַ בָּהּ כָּל אִילָנוֹת שֶׁיִּרְצֶה. וּמַתְנִיתִין דְּלֹא כְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי דְּאָסַר לְעֵיל. וְאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר דְּאַף רַבִּי יוֹסֵי לֹא אָסַר אֶלָּא לִטַּע בָּהּ כֶּרֶם בִּלְבַד, מִשּׁוּם דְּהַבּוֹצֵר לַגַּת הֻכְשַׁר, אֲבָל לֹא שְׁאָר אִילָנוֹת:


ניטעת כל נטע (planted with any kind of tree) – he plants in it all the trees that he wishes. But our Mishnah is not according to Rabbi Yossi who forbid this above (see Mishnah 1). But one can say that even Rabbi Yossi did not forbid other than to plant in it a vineyard alone, because the person who harvests grapes to the vat makes it susceptible [to receive ritual defilement], but not to the rest of the trees.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:3)


**חוּץ מִזֶּרַע הַנִּקְצָר.** אֲבָל זֶרַע הַנֶּעֱקָר עִם שָׁרָשָׁיו חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יָבִיא עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה עִם הֶעָפָר הָעוֹלֶה עִם הַשָּׁרָשִׁים הַנֶּעֱקָרִים:


חוץ מזרע הנקצר – but seed that is uprooted with its roots, we are concerned/worried lest he bring a bone like the size of a barleycorn with the irt that comes up with the roots that are uprooted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:4)


**וְאִם עֲקָרוֹ.** אוֹתוֹ זֶרַע הַנִּקְצָר:


ואם עקרו – that seed that is sown.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:5)


**צוֹבֵר אֶת גָּרְנוֹ לְתוֹכוֹ.** לְתוֹךְ הַשָּׂדֶה עַצְמָהּ. וְדָשׁ אוֹתוֹ שָׁם. וְלֹא יְבִיאֶנּוּ לַבַּיִת עַד אַחַר דִּישָׁה, שֶׁלֹּא לְהַרְבּוֹת אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה:


צובר את גורנו לתוכו (heaps up the threshing floor in it) – into the midst of the field itself. And he threshes it there. And he does not brig it to the house until after threshing, so as to not increase the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:6)


**וְכוֹבְרוֹ בִשְׁתֵּי כְבָרוֹת.** זוֹ אַחַר זוֹ. שֶׁמָּא נִתְעָרֵב עִמּוֹ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה:


וכוברו בשתי כברות (and he sifts it twice in two sieves) - one after another, lest a bone the size of a barleycorn become combined with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:7)


**וְהַקִּטְנִיּוֹת בְּשָׁלֹשׁ.** לְפִי שֶׁהַקִּטְנִיּוֹת יֵשׁ בָּהֶן עָפָר יוֹתֵר מִבַּתְּבוּאָה, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי חָיְשֵׁי בְהוּ טְפֵי. אֲבָל טְהוֹרִין הֵן, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא הֻכְשְׁרוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


והקטנית בשלש – because the pulse has more dirt than does the grain, because of this they are more worried. But they are ritually pure, for they have not become fit for Levitical uncleanness. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:8)


**וְשׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַקַּשׁ וְאֶת הֶעָצָה.** בְּתוֹךְ הַשָּׂדֶה. שֶׁמָּא יֵשׁ בָּהֶן עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה:


ושורף את הקש ואת העצה (and he burns the stubble and the pea-stalks) -within the field. Lest they have a bone the size of a barleycorn.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:9)


**קַשׁ.** תֶּבֶן שֶׁל תְּבוּאָה:


קשׁ – the straw of grain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:10)


**עָצָה.** תֶּבֶן שֶׁל קִטְנִיּוֹת:


עצה – the straw of legumes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:11)


**וּמְטַמְּאָה בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא.** שְׂדֵה בֵּית הַפְּרָס שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ:


ומטמאה במגע ובמשא – the sedeh beit ha‑pras we mentioned becomes defiling through touch and carrying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:2:12)


**וְאֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה בְאֹהֶל.** דְּכָל טֻמְאָתָהּ מִשּׁוּם עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה וְעֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְאֹהֶל:


ואינה מטמאה באוהל – for all of its defilement is because of a bone the size of barleycorn, but the bone the size of a barleycorn does not defile in a tent.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:3:1)


**נִזְרַעַת כָּל זֶרַע.** הָכִי גָרְסִינַן בְּרֹב הַסְּפָרִים. אֲבָל בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא קָתָנֵי אֵינָהּ נִזְרַעַת כָּל זֶרַע. וְכֵן הִיא בְעֵינַי:


נזרעת כל זרע – this is our reading in most of the books. But in the Tosefta (Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 17, Halakha 10), it teaches: אינה נזרעת כל זרע/no seed is sown there (i.e., this is the reverse of what is explicitly taught in our Mishnah). And such it is in my eyes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:3:2)


**וְאֵינָהּ נִטַּעַת כָּל נֶטַע.** וְלֹא מִבַּעְיָא דְּאֵין נוֹטְעִין לְכַתְּחִלָּה, אֶלָּא אַף הַנָּטוּעַ לֹא יְקַיֵּם, שֶׁלֹּא לְהַרְגִּיל בְּנֵי אָדָם לְשָׁם, מִשּׁוּם דִּמְטַמְּאָה בְאֹהֶל אַחְמוּר בָּהּ טְפֵי:


ואין ניטעת כל נטע (and is not planted with any kind of tree) – and needless to say, we don’t plant ab initio. But even that which is planted will not endure/be preserved, so that people will not be accustomed to this, because the defilement in a tent is more stringent in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:3:3)


**חוּץ מֵאִילַן סְרָק.** דְּמֻתָּר לִטַּע בָּהּ. וּמַהוּ אִילַן סְרָק, שֶׁאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה פֵּרוֹת:


חוץ מאילן סרק – that it is permitted to plant in it. And what is aאילן סרק ? A tree which bears no fruit).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:1)


**שְׂדֵה בוֹכִים.** מָקוֹם רָחוֹק מִבֵּית הַקְּבָרוֹת שֶׁמּוֹשִׁיבִין שָׁם הַמִּטָּה שֶׁל הַמֵּת וּמִתְוַעֲדִין וּבוֹכִין שָׁם:


שדה בוכים – a place far from the cemetery that they place there the pier of the corpse and [people] gather there and weep.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:2)


**לֹא נִטַּעַת וְלֹא נִזְרַעַת.** בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְּמוֹעֵד קָטָן בַּגְּמָרָא, מְפָרֵשׁ דְּמִשּׁוּם יֵאוּשׁ בְּעָלִים נָגְעוּ בָהּ, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁכֵּן הוּא, מִתְיָאֲשִׁים הַבְּעָלִים מִמֶּנָּה וְהָוֵי לֵיהּ כְּמֵצַר שֶׁהֶחֱזִיקוּ בוֹ רַבִּים וְאָסוּר לְקַלְקְלוֹ. וְדַוְקָא כְשֶׁנִּתְיָאֲשׁוּ הַבְּעָלִים, שֶׁהֶחֱזִיקוּ בוֹ הָרַבִּים כַּדִּין. אֲבָל לֹא נִתְיָאֲשׁוּ, דְּהַשְׁתָּא שֶׁלֹּא כַדִּין הֶחֱזִיקוּ בוֹ, מֻתָּר לְקַלְקְלוֹ:


לא ניטעת ולא נזרעת – in the first chapter of [Tractate] Moed Katan [5b] in the Gemara, it explains that because of the despair of the owners that came in contact with it, for since this is the situation, the owners despair from it and it is like he is in distress because many took possession/hold of it and it is forbidden to be upset over it. And specifically, when the owners became despaired that many took possession of it according to law. But if they did not despair, for now, it is not according to the law that they took possession of it, it is permissible to be upset over it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:3)


**וַעֲפָרָהּ טָהוֹר.** דְּלֹא מַחְזְקִינַן בָּהּ טֻמְאָה כְּלָל:


ועפרה טהור – for we don’t presume defilement to it at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:4)


**וְעוֹשִׂים מִמֶּנּוּ תַנּוּרִים לַקֹּדֶשׁ.** מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בִּשְׁנֵי בֵית הַפְּרָס הָרִאשׁוֹנִים:


ועושים ממנו תנורים לקודש – which is not the case in the first two fields of crushed bones carried over from a ploughed area.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:5)
