**שֶׁבּוֹדְקִים לְעוֹשֶׂה פֶסַח.** אַשָּׂדֶה שֶׁנֶּחֱרַשׁ בָּהּ קֶבֶר קָאֵי. דְּאִם הוֹלֵךְ שָׁם אָדָם וְצָרִיךְ לִשְׁחֹט אֶת פִּסְחוֹ, בּוֹדְקִים אוֹתָהּ כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ בְּסֵיפָא, וְאִם לֹא נִמְצָא שָׁם עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה הוֹלֵךְ וְעוֹשֶׂה אֶת פִּסְחוֹ:


שבודקים לעושה פסח – it refers to a field that a grave was ploughed up. For if a person goes there, he needs to slaughter his Passover sacrifice, they examine it as it is explained in the concluding clause [of this Mishnah], but if there is not found there a bone the size of a barleycorn, he goes and makes his Passover sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:6)


**וְאֵין בּוֹדְקִין לִתְרוּמָה.** דְּדַוְקָא לְפֶסַח סוֹמֵךְ אַבְּדִיקָה זוֹ, מִשּׁוּם דְּטֻמְאַת בֵּית הַפְּרָס דְּרַבָּנָן, וַחֲכָמִים לֹא הֶעֱמִידוּ דִבְרֵיהֶם בִּמְקוֹם כָּרֵת, שֶׁמִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַקְרִיב קָרְבַּן פֶּסַח בְּמוֹעֲדוֹ חַיָּב כָּרֵת. אֲבָל בִּתְרוּמָה דְּאֵין בַּאֲכִילָתָהּ כִּי אִם עֲשֵׂה, הֶעֱמִידוּ דִבְרֵיהֶם בִּמְקוֹם עֲשֵׂה וְאֵין מוֹעֶלֶת בְּדִיקָה זוֹ לְהַתִּירוֹ בַּתְּרוּמָה:


ואין בודקין לתרומה – that specifically for Passover that he relies upon this examination, because the defilement of a Bet HaPras/an area containing crushed bones carried over from a ploughed field which is Rabbinic. But the Sages did not stand by their words with regard to extirpation, for whomever does not offer the Passover sacrifice at the appropriate time is liable to extirpation. But regarding the heave-offering, that its consumption is not anything but a positive commandment, the Rabbis stood by their words in the place of a positive commandment and an examination is not effective to permit it for heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:7)


**וּלְנָזִיר.** שֶׁהָלַךְ שָׁם:


ולנזיר – [and to a Nazirite] that went there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:8)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים בּוֹדְקִין.** וְאִם לֹא נִמְצָא שָׁם עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה אֵינוֹ מְגַלֵּחַ:


בית שמאין אומרים בודקין – and if a bone the size of a barleycorn was not found there, he does not shave his head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:9)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אֵין בּוֹדְקִין.** דְּבֵין נִמְצָא וּבֵין לֹא נִמְצָא מְגַלֵּחַ וּמֵבִיא קָרְבָּן:


ובית הלל אומרים אין בודקין – that whether it (i.e., a bone the size of a barleycorn) is found or not found, he shaves his head and brings a sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:10)


**אֶת הֶעָפָר שֶׁהוּא יָכוֹל לַהֲסִיטוֹ.** כְּגוֹן עָפָר תִּחוֹחַ:


העפר שהוא יכול להסיטו – as for example, crushed, loose earth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:4:11)


**וּמְמַחֶה.** עוֹשֵׂהוּ דַּק דַּק בְּיָדוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּרְאֶה אִם יֵשׁ בּוֹ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה:


וממחה (and crumbles it) – make it very fine in his hand so that he can see if has a bone-fragment the size of a barley corn.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:1)


**כֵּיצַד מְטַהֲרִין בֵּית הַפְּרָס.** אַבֵּית הַפְּרָס הָרִאשׁוֹן קָאֵי, שֶׁהוּא שָׂדֶה שֶׁנֶּחֱרַשׁ בּוֹ קֶבֶר:


כיצד מטהרין בית הפרס – it (i.e., the Mishnah) refers to the first area of a square Pras declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over it from a ploughed grave, which is a field in which a grave was ploughed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:2)


**נוֹטְלִין מִמֶּנּוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים.** כְּשִׁעוּר עֹמֶק הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה שֶׁמְּגַלְגֶּלֶת הָעֲצָמוֹת:


נוטלין ממנו שלשה טפחים – like the measure of the depth of the plough that rolls/turns over the bones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:3)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר' שמעון אומר כו' – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:4)


**הָרוֹצֵף.** לְשׁוֹן רִצְפָּה שֶׁל אֲבָנִים:


הרוצף(if one paves) - the language of a floor of stones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:5)


**טָהוֹר.** כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין יָכוֹל לַהֲסִיטָן. שֶׁאִם הָיָה מְהַלֵּךְ עַל גַּבָּן וְנִסָּטוֹת, אִיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ אַהֶסֵּט עֶצֶם שֶׁתַּחְתֵּיהֶן:


טהור – since he is not able to move them, for if he would walk on top of them and, and they would be moved, there is a concern regarding movement (even without actually coming into contact with it) of a human bone that is underneath them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:5:6)


**אַף הָעוֹזֵק.** הַחוֹפֵר וּמַשְׁלִיךְ הָאֲבָנִים, אִם עָשָׂה כֵן בְּכָל בֵּית הַפְּרָס, טָהוֹר. עוֹזֵק, לְשׁוֹן וַיְעַזְּקֵהוּ וַיְסַקְּלֵהוּ (ישעיה ה). וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אף העוזק (even someone who beaks clods suspected of containing human bones – and levels it) – he who digs and casts away the stones, if he did this in all of the Bet HaPras/a square Pras declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over it from a ploughed grave, it is pure. [The word] עוזק – is the language of (Isaiah 5:2): “He broke the ground, cleared it of stones.” But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:1)


**הַמְהַלֵּךְ בְּבֵית הַפְּרָס.** בְּשָׂדֶה שֶׁנֶּחֱרַשׁ בָּהּ קֶבֶר:


המהלך בבית הפרס – in a field where a grave was ploughed up in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:2)


**עַל אֲבָנִים שֶׁהוּא יָכוֹל לַהֲסִיטָן.** כְּגוֹן עַל גַּבֵּי אֲבָנִים שֶׁאֵינָן תְּקוּעוֹת וּקְבוּעוֹת בָּאָרֶץ וְהֵן נִסָּטוֹת מֵחֲמַת הָעוֹבֵר עֲלֵיהֶן, וְעֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה נִסָּט מִכֹּחוֹ. וְרוֹכֵב עַל גַּבֵּי אָדָם אוֹ בְּהֵמָה [שֶׁכֹּחָן] רַע וְהוֹלִיכוּהוּ עֲלֵיהֶן, טָמֵא הָרוֹכֵב, דְּכֵיוָן דְּכֹחַ הַתַּחְתּוֹן רַע, מֵחֲמַת מַשָּׂא הָעֶלְיוֹן נִסְּטוּ וְהָוֵי כְּאִלּוּ הוּא מֵסִיט הָעֶצֶם. אֲבָל אִם הָיָה כֹּחַ הַנִּרְכָּב יָפֶה, לָא חֲשִׁיב הָרוֹכֵב כְּמֵסִיט הָעֶצֶם. וְהֵיכִי דָּמֵי אָדָם שֶׁכֹּחוֹ רַע, כָּל שֶׁרוֹכְבוֹ וְאַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו נוֹקְשׁוֹת. וּבְהֵמָה שֶׁכֹּחָהּ רַע, כָּל שֶׁרוֹכְבָהּ וּמַטִּילָה גְּלָלִים:


על אבנים שיכול להסיטן – as for example on top of stones that are not lodged/placed and affixed in the ground and they can be moved on account of passing over them, and a human bone the size of a barleycorn is shaken from its position of strength, or rides upon a person or an animal [which cannot endure pressure and walks/treads upon them, the rider is impure, for since the strength at the bottom is bad/poor, on account of the load above, it was moved and it is as if he shakes the bone. But if if the strength of the one being driven has a strong legal right, the rider is not considered as if he shaking the bone. But how is that to be understood – that a person has a weak legal right? All that ride it and his knees and the surrounding parts knock against each other. But an animal whose strength is weak, all that ride it cause it to empty its bowels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:3)


**בֶּהָרִים וּבַסְּלָעִים.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין קוֹבְרִין שָׁם מֵת:


בהרים ובסלעים – even though they don’t bury a corpse there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:4)


**טָמֵא.** מִפְּנֵי עֲפַר אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים שֶׁמִּתְגַּלְגֵּל שָׁם:


טמא – because of the dust of the Lands of the Nations that is rolled there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:5)


**בַּיָּם וּבַשּׁוּנִית טָהוֹר.** מִטֻּמְאַת גּוּשָׁהּ. שֶׁגָּזְרוּ עַל גּוּשָׁהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים לִשְׂרֹף תְּרוּמָה וְקָדָשִׁים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בַּעֲפָרָהּ וְאֵין שׂוֹרְפִים תְּרוּמָה וְקָדָשִׁים שֶׁבָּאוּ בַיָּם וּבַשּׁוּנִית, שֶׁאֵין קוֹבְרִים שָׁם מֵת, אֲבָל טֻמְאַת אֲוִירָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים יֵשׁ בָּהֶן, שֶׁגָּזְרוּ עַל אֲוִירָהּ לִתְלוֹת, שֶׁתְּרוּמָה וְקָדָשִׁים שֶׁנִּכְנְסוּ לַאֲוִיר אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים וְלֹא נָגְעוּ בְגוּשָׁהּ, לֹא אוֹכְלִין וְלֹא שׂוֹרְפִין:


בים ובשונית טהור (on the sea and on the beach/rocky cliffs – from the defilement of the soil/ground. That they (i.e., the Rabbis) decreed on the soil of the Land of the Nations to burn the heave-offering and Holy Things that touched its dust, for we don’t burn heave-offering or Holy Things that came to the sea or to the beach/rocky cliffs, for we don’t bury a corpse there, but they have the defilement of the Land of the Nations, for they (i.e., the Rabbis) decreed upon its airspace to suspend, that heave-offering and Holy Things that entered into the airspace of the Land of the Nations and didn’t touch its soil, we don’t eat it nor do we burn it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:6:6)


**בְּזַעְפּוֹ.** כְּשֶׁהַיָּם הוֹלֵךְ וְסוֹעֵר:


בזעפו – where the sea rages.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:1)


**הַקּוֹנֶה שָׂדֶה בְסוּרְיָא.** אֲרַם נַהֲרַיִם וַאֲרַם צוֹבָה שֶׁכָּבַשׁ דָּוִד. וַעֲפָרָהּ טָמֵא כְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ, וְחַיֶּבֶת בַּמַּעַשְׂרוֹת וּבַשְּׁבִיעִית כְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


הקונה שדה בסוריא – Aram Naharayim and Aram Tzoba that David conquered. But its dust is impure like what is outside of the Land of Israel, but one is liable for tithes and Seventh Year produce like the Land of Israel (see also Tractate Demai, Chapter 6, Mishnah 11 and Second Samuel, Chapter 10 and Second Chronicles, Chapter 19 and Tractate Gittin folio 8a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:2)


**אִם יָכוֹל לְהִכָּנֵס לָהּ בְּטָהֳרָה.** שֶׁאֵין אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים מַפְסִיק בֵּין אוֹתָהּ שָׂדֶה שֶׁקָּנָה לְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


אם יכול להכנס לה בטהרה – for the Land of the Nations does not form a partition between that field that he bought to the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:3)


**טְהוֹרָה.** אוֹתָהּ שָׂדֶה. וַאֲפִלּוּ אַגּוּשָׁהּ דִּידַהּ לֹא גָזְרוּ, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִיא שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, דְּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָתָנֵי הַקּוֹנֶה שָׂדֶה. אֲבָל אִם הָיָה רֹחַב טֶפַח מֵאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים אוֹ מִבֵּית הַקְּבָרוֹת מַפְסִיק בֵּין אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לַשָּׂדֶה שֶׁקָּנָה בְסוּרְיָא, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֵין יָכוֹל לִכָּנֵס בָּהּ בְּטָהֳרָה, עֲפָרָהּ שֶׁל אוֹתָהּ שָׂדֶה טָמֵא כְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ:


בטהרה – that field. And even on its own clod of earth they did not decree, because it belongs to an Israelite, and because of this, it is taught [in the Mishnah]: "הקונה שדה"/a person who purchases a field. But if it had the width of a handbreadth from the Land of the Nations or from the cemetery, it interrupts between the Land of Israel and a field that he purchased in Syria, for now, he is not able to enter in ritual purity, the soil of that field is impure like outside of the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:4)


**מְדוֹרוֹת הַגּוֹיִם.** שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


מדורות הגוים (Gentile dwelling places) – that are in the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:5)


**טְמֵאִים.** שֶׁקּוֹבְרִין נִפְלֵיהֶן בְּבָתֵּיהֶן:


טמאים – they bury their aborted fetuses in their homes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:6)


**אַרְבָּעִים יוֹם.** כְּשִׁעוּר יְצִירַת הַוָּלָד:


ארבעים יום – like the measure of the creation of the embryo.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:7)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין עִמּוֹ אִשָּׁה.** דְּנָכְרִים פְּרוּצִים בַּעֲרָיוֹת וּמְבִיאִין נָשִׁים לְבָתֵּיהֶן בַּלַּיְלָה:


אע"פ שאין עמו אשה – lawless/unrestrained men in the towns and they bring women to their homes at night.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:8)


**עֶבֶד.** שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


עבד – [slave] of an Israelite (i.e., a Canaanite slave that is in the hand of an Israelite – according to the commentary of Tiferet Yisrael).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:9)


**אוֹ אִשָּׁה.** יִשְׂרְאֵלִית:


או אשה – an Israelite [woman].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:7:10)


**מְשַׁמְּרִין.** הַמָּדוֹר שֶׁל הַנָּכְרִים, שֶׁלֹּא יִקְבְּרוּ שָׁם:


משמרין – the Gentile dwelling places, that they (i.e., Israelites) will not be buried there.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:8:1)


**הַבִּיבִין הָעֲמֻקִּים.** צִנּוֹרוֹת שֶׁמְּקַלְּחִין מֵהֶן הַשְּׁפָכִים לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁכָּתוּב בָּהֶם הַכּוּכִים, לְשׁוֹן כּוּךְ, מְעָרָה שֶׁתַּחַת הַקַּרְקַע:


הביבין העמוקים (the deep drains) – water pipes/ducts that they cause a gushing forth [of water] that spills out to the public domain. And there are books where it is written in them הכוכים/sepulchral chambers, the language of a כוך/a cave that is underneath the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:8:2)


**וְעָפָר הַתִּחוֹחַ.** דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ דְּמֵחֲמַת הַנֵּפֶל שֶׁקָּבְרוּ שָׁם נַעֲשָׂה הֶעָפָר תִּחוֹחַ:


ועפר התיחוח – for one can be concerned that on account of the abortion/non-viable birth that they buried there, the ground became crushed, loose earth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:8:3)


**חֲזִיר וּבַרְדְּלָס.** דַּרְכָּן לַחְפֹּר וּלְחַפֵּשׂ תַּחַת הַקַּרְקַע, וְאִם יִמְצְאוּ נֵפֶל קָבוּר יוֹצִיאוּהוּ מִשָּׁם. בַּרְדְּלָס, חַיָּה שֶׁקּוֹרִין לָהּ בַּעֲרָבִי אלצב"ע:


חזיר וברדלס (pig and spotted beast/leopard, hyena) – it is their manner to dig and search underneath the ground. But if they would find an abortion/non-viable birth buried underneath the ground, they would remove it from there. ברדלס – is a beast that they call ALTZEBAH in Arabic.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:1)


**אִצְטַבָּא.** עֲשׂוּיָה לְנוֹי לִפְנֵי הַבָּתִּים. וְאֵין קוֹבְרִים שָׁם נְפָלִים:


אצטבא (portico/colonnade, balcony) -made for beauty in front of the houses. But they don’t bury there non-viable births.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:2)


**עִיר גּוֹיִם שֶׁחָרְבָה.** עָרֵי אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁדָּרוּ בָּהּ נָכְרִים וְחָרְבָה מִן הַנָּכְרִים:


עיר נכרים שחרבה – cities of the land of Israel where heathens liveג and they were destroyed/laid waste from the heathens.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:3)


**אֵין בָּהֶן מִשּׁוּם מְדוֹר גּוֹיִם.** שֶׁחַיּוֹת מְצוּיוֹת שָׁם וּמוֹצִיאוֹת כָּל הַנְּפָלִים שֶׁנִּקְבְּרוּ שָׁם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


אין בהן משום מדור עובדי כוכבים – that wild animals are found there and they remove all the aborted births that were buried there. But the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:4)


**קְבָרוֹת.** כְּלוֹמַר יָדוּעַ שֶׁהֵם קְבָרוֹת:


קברות – it is known that they are graveyards.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:5)


**הָיָה סָפֵק.** אִם מֵאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים אִם מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


היה ספק – [there was doubt] if they are from the Land of the Nations, [or] if they are from the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:6)


**וְטִהֲרוּהוּ.** דַּחֲשָׁבוּהוּ כְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. אִי נַמִּי, הָיָה סָפֵק אִם הָיוּ שָׁם קְבָרוֹת אִם לָאו, וְטִהֲרוּהוּ שֶׁהֵעִידוּ שֶׁאֵין שָׁם קְבָרוֹת:


וטיהרוהו – They (i.e., the Sages) considered it like the Land of Israel. Alternatively, there was doubt there were graveyard there or not, and they (i.e., the Sages) made them pure/clean, since they testified that there were no graveyards there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:9:7)


**קֵינִי.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם וְהָיוּ נוֹהֲגִים בּוֹ טֻמְאָה מִסָּפֵק, עַד שֶׁנִּמְנוּ עָלָיו רַבִּי וּבֵית דִּינוֹ וְטִהֲרוּהוּ:


קיני – the name of a place. And they would have the practice there of defilement from doubt, until Rabbi [Judah the Prince] and his Bet Din decided/resolved upon it and made it pure.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:1)


**אָהֳלֵי עַרְבִיִּים.** אֵינָן קְבוּעִים בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד, וּמִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא קְבִיעֵי לֹא קָבְרֵי בֵיהּ. אִי נַמִּי, לְפִי שֶׁנּוֹסְעִים וְחוֹנִים מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם לֹא רָצוּ לְהַחֲזִיק טֻמְאָה:


אוהלי ערביים – they are not established/set up in one place, and because they are not set up, they don’t bury in it. Alternatively, because they travel and camp from place to place, they did not want to abet the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:2)


**וְהַסֻּכּוֹת.** שֶׁל שׁוֹמְרֵי פֵּרוֹת:


והסוכות – of those who guard/protect the fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:3)


**וְהַצְּרִיפִים.** מְדוֹרוֹת שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם גַּג, אֶלָּא הַדְּפָנוֹת נוֹגְעוֹת בְּרָאשֵׁיהֶן זוֹ לָזוֹ. וְדִירוֹת שֶׁל צַעַר הֵן וְלֹא קְבִיעֵי:


והצריפים – dwellings that do not have a roof. Rather the walls touch at their tops with each other. And they are dwellings of pain/disregard and not fixed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:4)


**בֻּרְגָּנִין.** סֻכּוֹת עֲשׂוּיוֹת בַּשָּׂדֶה לְהַכְנִיס בָּהֶן פֵּרוֹת מִפְּנֵי הַגְּשָׁמִים:


בורגנין (keepers or residents of a station house) – huts made in the field to bring into the fruit because of the rains.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:5)


**אַלְקְטִיּוֹת.** גַּג עוֹמֵד עַל אַרְבָּעָה עַמּוּדִים לְהִתְקָרֵר בּוֹ בַקַּיִץ, שֶׁהָרוּחַ מְנַשֶּׁבֶת בּוֹ מִכָּל צְדָדָיו. וּלְשׁוֹן אַלְקְטִיּוֹת, בָּתֵּי הַקַּיִץ, תַּרְגּוּם קַיִץ, קַיְטָא:


אלקטיות (sheds for stacks of grain in the field)- a roof that stands on four pillars to cool off in the summer when he wind lows in it from all of its sides. And the language אלקטיות, summer houses. It is the Aramaic translation of קיץ/summer. קייטא/summer. Fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:6)


**מְקוֹם הַחִצִּים.** מָקוֹם שֶׁבְּנֵי הָעִיר מַנִּיחִין בּוֹ חִצִּים לִשְׁמֹר לְעֵת מִלְחָמָה:


מקום החצים – a place where the people of the city place in it arrows to keep for the time of war.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Oholot 18:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Oholot/r/18:10:7)


**מְקוֹם לִגְיוֹנוֹת.** שֶׁחוֹנִים בּוֹ חֵילוֹת שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ:


מקום לגיונות – where the soldiers/legions of the king camp out.
